### [1] [On physical implementability of reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14131)
*Eiji Konishi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文给出一个负面结果：在带奖励外势能的物理神经网络硬件实现中，链式思维（chain of thought）的存在与系统的李雅普诺夫稳定性相矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年前后，强化学习驱动的推理过程在机器学习社区受到广泛研究；作者关注该推理过程在物理神经网络模型中的硬件实现可行性。

Method: 在给定奖励对应外势能的物理神经网络模型中，对强化学习驱动的推理过程进行硬件实现，并从动力学角度分析其与李雅普诺夫稳定性的关系。

Result: 得到负面结果：在该硬件实现中，动力学里存在链式思维与系统的李雅普诺夫稳定性相互矛盾，即二者不能同时成立。

Conclusion: 这表明此类物理神经网络硬件实现强化学习驱动推理可能存在根本性限制：链式思维动力学要求系统不满足李雅普诺夫稳定。

Abstract: In the machine learning community, the reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning process has been intensively studied since around 2025. In this article, we present a negative result on the hardware implementation of this reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning process in a physical neural network model in the presence of a reward external potential energy for a given reward. In this hardware implementation, the existence of a chain of thought in dynamics contradicts the Lyapunov stability of the system.

</details>


### [2] [Weyl's theorem and the theoretical origin of Boson peak in two-dimensional scalar spring network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15318)
*Cunyuan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作将非晶固体建模为零温标量弹簧网络，并通过数学证明无序会使振动密度态向低频移动，从而积累出反常低频部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体中普遍观测到的反常低频振动密度态（VDOS）起源长期存在争议，需要剥离复杂因素并揭示其基本机制。

Method: 采用零温标量弹簧网络作为最简模型，以网络拓扑连通性定义无序；基于厄米矩阵的Weyl定理进行严格数学推导，不依赖微扰或唯象假设。

Result: 证明无序可导致VDOS向低频移动并在低频区积累，从而形成反常部分；该机制具有普适性，可适用于真实体系。

Conclusion: 该研究在数学原理层面揭示了一个简洁而深刻的非晶固体反常低频VDOS起源机制。

Abstract: The origin of the anomalous low-frequency vibrational density of states (VDOS) universally observed in amorphous solids has been debated for decades. In this work, we model amorphous solids as a zero-temperature scalar spring network, the simplest model that captures both the amorphous structure and the features of the anomalous VDOS. Using this scalar network, we mathematically demonstrate that disorder can lead to a shift of the VDOS toward low frequencies, thereby accumulating the anomalous part there. Disorder is defined through the topological connectivity of the network. The validity and universality of the theoretical framework are general and valuable for real-world scenarios because it rests only on Weyl's theorem for Hermitian matrices and is free of perturbation and phenomenological assumptions. This work strips away complex factors and reveals, at the level of mathematical principles, a profound and clean mechanism for the origin of the anomalous VDOS in amorphous solids.

</details>


### [3] [Eigenstate transitions and critical transport in 3D Aubry-Andr\'e model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15842)
*Pragna Das,Lev Vidmar,Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作数值研究了有限尺寸共度近似下三维 Aubry-André 模型的谱、波函数和时间演化性质，通过标度分析证明存在两个临界点：一个类似三维 Anderson 模型的实空间局域-离域转变，另一个是无 Anderson 对应的对偶动量空间局域-离域转变；二者均表现出量子遍历性与局域化之间的临界特征，临界动力学指数为 z=3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三维 Aubry-André 模型中局域化转变的临界性质，特别是其与三维 Anderson 模型的异同，以及量子遍历性-局域化临界点是否具有普适的谱、波函数和动力学标度关系。

Method: 在有限尺寸共度近似下对三维 Aubry-André 模型进行数值计算；通过标度分析确定临界点；利用谱测度（如能隙比、谱形式因子）和波函数测度（如逆参与率、参与熵）估计临界值；并研究本征态、本征谱满足的压缩率-分形维数关系以及波包动力学。

Result: 明确发现两个临界点：一个为实空间局域-离域转变，类似三维 Anderson 模型；另一个为对偶动量空间局域-离域转变，在三维 Anderson 模型中无对应。两个临界点均呈现量子遍历性与局域化之间的临界标志：本征态与本征谱满足本征谱压缩率 χ 与分形维数 d^(1) 的普适互关系；波包动力学由临界动力学指数 z 控制，且与三维 Anderson 模型类似，表现为亚扩散 z=3。

Conclusion: 三维 Aubry-André 模型可支持实空间和对偶动量空间两类局域-离域临界点，且它们共享量子遍历性-局域化临界的普适谱-波函数关系与亚扩散动力学，但其对偶动量空间临界点是区别于三维 Anderson 模型的关键特征。

Abstract: We numerically study spectral and wave-function properties, as well as time evolution, in finite-size commensurate approximations of the 3D Aubry-Andr\'e model. We explicitly show, through scaling analysis, the existence of two critical points. One of the critical points is a localization-delocalization transition in real-space, similar to the critical point in the 3D Anderson model. The other is a localization-delocalization transition in a dual momentum space, which does not have any analog in the 3D Anderson model. We estimate their values from spectral measures, such as the gap ratio and the spectral form factor, as well as from wave-function measures, such as the inverse participation ratio and the participation entropy. Additionally, we demonstrate that both critical points exhibit hallmarks of critical points between quantum ergodicity and localization. First, the eigenstates and the eigenspectrum at the critical point fulfill the mutual universal relation between the eigenspectrum compressibility $\chi$ and the eigenstate fractal dimension $d^{(1)}$ [S. Jiricek et al., Proc. Nat. Acad. Sci. 123, e2518027123 (2026)]. Second, the dynamics of the wave packet at the critical point is governed by the critical dynamical exponent $z$, characterized by the spectral fractal dimension and the dimensionality of the lattice [M. Hopjan and L. Vidmar, Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 060401 (2025)]. Thus, similarly to the 3D Anderson model, the critical dynamical exponent is sub-diffusive with the value of $z=3$.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum Localization in Geometric Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15874)
*Jan Lewenstein-Sanpera,Tobias Grass,M. \`Angeles Serrano,Bruno Juli\'a-D\'iaz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在几何软配置网络模型 S^1 中，单粒子态的局域化受网络拓扑调控：小世界网络趋于扩展态，非小世界网络趋于局域化，表明复杂连接性可替代随机能量无序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无需外势或随机能量无序时，复杂网络拓扑本身能否导致量子局域化，并理解小世界到非小世界转变对单粒子态局域化的影响。

Method: 采用 S^1 几何软配置模型，调节温度与异质性参数生成不同结构模式，聚焦小世界到非小世界区域的转变，分析单粒子态局域化行为。

Result: 小世界网络倾向于形成扩展态，波函数覆盖整个系统尺寸；非小世界网络倾向于局域化。复杂连接性可扮演随机能量无序的角色。

Conclusion: 网络拓扑本身可诱导类似 Anderson 局域化的现象，复杂连通性是除随机能量无序外调控量子局域化的重要机制。

Abstract: Quantum localization, the phenomenon by which wave diffusion is halted, here understood as the superposition of propagating waves, can have many different origins, such as onsite random potentials in Anderson localization. However, some systems exhibit complex geometries and could potentially reproduce similar results without external potentials. In this work, we study how localization of single particle states is affected by the underlying topology of a simple but highly predictive complex network model: the geometric soft configuration model $\mathbb S^1$. By tuning the model's temperature and heterogeneity parameters, we are able to generate different structural patterns and focus on the transition from a small-world to a non-small-world regime. Our study suggests that small-world networks tend to have extended states, which spread the whole system's size, in contrast to non-small-world networks which tend to localize, showing how complex connectivity can play the role assigned to random energetic disorder.

</details>


### [5] [Orbital cancellation and reentrant localization in a quasiperiodic trimer ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16197)
*Xin Wang,Pei-Jie Chang,Quanfeng Lu,Muxi Zheng,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在三聚体梯形中，均匀轨道失谐可与准周期调制协同，通过避免交叉重新分配轨道权重，恢复波函数空间延展，形成两个重入高参与窗口；但残余带间调制和完整非对角调制会缩小这些窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究准周期调制增强时如何恢复空间延展，并厘清轨道失谐、多带耦合与残余带间调制对离域窗口的影响。

Method: 固定三聚体梯形的跃迁和轨道调制符号，同步增大轨道失谐与调制幅度；用全带参与度标度与波包传播检验空间恢复；用高斯求积改善闭路行列式估计收敛，并交叉验证能量分辨衰减；在同一有限尺寸参与判据下比较耦合与解耦梯形。

Result: 失谐与调制幅度共同增大引发连续避免交叉，使两个低分子带的投影在位势被抑制、第三带仍强调制；参与度标度给出两个重入高参与窗口，波包传播在代表性控制下证实空间恢复。对角相消留下残余带间调制，产生幅度依赖分子能级间距的自能。恢复完整非对角调制后，耦合与解耦梯形比较显示两个采样窗口均变窄，且第二个窗口绝对收缩更大。

Conclusion: 均匀轨道失谐可通过选择性抑制低带在位势来对抗准周期局域化，但残余带间调制及完整非对角调制会限制空间恢复；该机制为多带准周期系统中的离域调控提供依据。

Abstract: Uniform orbital detuning can restore spatial extent even as quasiperiodic modulation strengthens. In a trimer ladder with fixed hoppings and orbital modulation signs, increasing detuning and modulation amplitude together drives successive avoided crossings that redistribute orbital weights, suppressing the projected onsite potential in both lower molecular bands while leaving the third strongly modulated. Complete-band participation scaling supports two reentrant high-participation windows, with wave-packet propagation corroborating spatial recovery at representative controls over the studied sizes and times. These diagonal cancellations leave residual interband modulation, which generates a self-energy whose magnitude depends on the molecular level separation. Gaussian quadrature improves convergence of closed-walk determinant estimates used to cross-check energy-resolved attenuation. Comparing the coupled and decoupled ladders under the same finite-size participation criterion shows that restoring the full off-diagonal modulation narrows both sampled windows, with a larger absolute contraction of the second.

</details>

### [6] [SIREN: A nonlinear Rankine-source boundary element solver for the stationary motion of a marine vehicle in calm water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14027)
*Daniele Peri*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: SIREN 是一种基于 Dawson 方法的 Rankine 源面元求解器，用于船舶稳态兴波阻力；它通过在真实边界上迭代求解基流并移动自由面面元来非线性更新自由面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双体流仅适用于平自由面和 z=0 处切分的船体，且自由面面元常需去奇异化；需要一种能在实际边界上施加不可穿透性、避免这些限制并支持非线性迭代的兴波阻力计算方法。

Method: 自由面条件围绕基流线性化并迭代。基流为实际边界上的 Neumann 问题：在全部船体面元（湿/干）与自由面面元上布置源，并对二者施加不可穿透性，使自由面在当前高度充当刚性盖。非线性迭代中，自由面面元按其形心处松弛波高垂直刚体移动，船体面元相对移动后自由面标记湿/干，并在新几何上重算基流；不可穿透性仍施加于整个船体，湿/干标记仅用于选择进入力积分的面元；仅在非线性修正累积且未被重置的迭代步中使用欠松弛。辐射条件由配置点单行上游移位施加；兴波阻力分别由压力积分和横向波切分析独立求得。

Result: 摘要未给出定量验证或对比结果；所提出的表述消除了对去奇异化自由面面元的需要，并给出了压力积分与横向波切分析两种独立的兴波阻力计算途径。

Conclusion: SIREN 提供了一种可迭代、基于实际边界和移动自由面的 Rankine 源面元兴波阻力求解框架，用当前自由面作为刚性盖的基流替代经典双体流，并保留两种阻力评估方法。

Abstract: SIREN (Ship Inviscid Resistance and Elevation eNgine) is a Rankine-source panel solver for the steady wave resistance of a ship, in the tradition of Dawson's method. The free-surface condition is linearised about a base flow, and the linearisation is iterated. In the present formulation the base flow is the solution of a Neumann problem on the actual boundary: sources on the entire hull, wet and dry, and on the free surface panels, with impermeability imposed on both, so that the free surface acts as a rigid lid at the position it currently occupies. This replaces the classical double-body flow, whose image about z=0 is meaningful only for a flat surface and a hull cut at z=0, and it removes the need for desingularised free-surface panels. In the non-linear iteration the free-surface panels are moved rigidly in the vertical to the relaxed wave elevation at their centroid, the hull panels are marked wet or dry against the moved surface, and the base flow is recomputed on the moved geometry; impermeability applies to the whole hull regardless of this marking, which is used only to select which panels enter the force integral, and under-relaxation is confined to the passes where the non-linear correction accumulates without being reset. The radiation condition is imposed by a one-row upstream shift of the collocation points, and the wave resistance is obtained by pressure integration and, independently, by transverse wave-cut analysis.

</details>


### [7] [A Gauge-Inspired Coupled Fokker--Planck System for Multistate Stochastic Dynamics: Formalism and 1-D Numerical Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14052)
*Francesco Pio De Cosmo,Gianluca Di Natale,Leandro Cieri*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 首次实现SU(2)规范耦合Fokker-Planck系统的数值求解器，揭示非阿贝尔耦合导致概率交换与空间离域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非阿贝尔规范场耦合的Fokker-Planck方程从理论框架转化为可数值预测的工具，此前尚未解决；受冰晶习性演化工作启发。

Method: 采用算子分裂格式结合非振荡平流求解器，严格保证概率守恒、非负性和数值稳定性。

Result: 非阿贝尔规范耦合作为交叉反应反馈回路，驱动状态间连续概率交换，抑制局部密度峰，迫使长时空间离域；在线性与二次耦合及强相互作用(g<=1.0)下验证。

Conclusion: 为大气气候模型中冰云表示改进提供关键步骤，且物理框架与计算引擎具有通用多学科价值，适用于任何需要耦合多态随机方程的领域。

Abstract: Extending the Fokker--Planck equation to a coupled system of equations through non-Abelian symmetry groups offers a powerful theoretical framework for coupled multi-state stochastic systems. However, translating this abstract gauge field formulation into viable numerical predictions remains an unaddressed challenge. Motivated by our previous work on ice crystal habit evolution, in this work we bridge this gap by presenting the first numerical solver for an SU(2) gauge-coupled Fokker--Planck system in (1+1) dimensions. Using an operator-splitting scheme combined with a non-oscillatory advection solver, our algorithm strictly ensures probability conservation, non-negativity, and numerical stability. We demonstrate that non-Abelian gauge coupling acts as a cross-reactive feedback loop that fundamentally alters system dynamics, driving continuous probability exchange between states, suppressing localised density peaks, and forcing long-time spatial delocalisation. Validated across linear and quadratic coupling regimes up to strong interactions (g <= 1.0), this work establishes the foundational computational infrastructure needed to integrate complex multi-state physics. Overall, while our approach provides a key step toward improving ice cloud representation in atmospheric and climate models, the underlying physical framework and computational engine remain fundamentally general and multidisciplinary, offering a versatile paradigm for any domain requiring coupled multi-state stochastic equations.

</details>


### [8] [acousticHelmholtzFoam: an MPI-parallel unstructured finite-volume solver for frequency-domain acoustics in heterogeneous two-phase media with perfectly matched layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14057)
*Chuanchao Xu,Jun Liu,Dieter Bothe,Tomislav Maric*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: acousticHelmholtzFoam 是一个基于 OpenFOAM 的求解器，用于在非结构多面体网格上计算异质气液介质中的时谐声压场，并输出声辐射力相关量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在复杂气液两相介质和非结构多面体网格上高效求解时谐声压，并支持开放边界截断、声辐射压力与声力评估，同时利用并行直接求解流程。

Method: 将复声压表示为实部和虚部体积场，材料系数由 VOF 相分数及几何面分数平均构造；使用笛卡尔 PML 截断开放边界，阻尼张量由 setPMLFields 生成。两个耦合实值方程采用 OpenFOAM 单元中心有限体积离散，显式组装处理器耦合和边界贡献为分布式 PETSc 矩阵，默认使用 MUMPS 稀疏直接求解。求解器输出压力幅值、复速度、声辐射压力和时均动量通量，并文档化方程、算例接口、组装求解算法、构建流程和测试基础设施。

Result: 分层气液-PML 解析算例作为端到端回归：8000 单元时全区域复压力相对误差为 1.197e-3；串行与最多 8 个 MPI 进程的解在相对 L2 范数下差异不超过 1e-12。单轴声悬浮器高度扫描结合恒定体积椭球液滴，验证了两相材料输入和力输出，并给出了强 scaling 和问题规模 scaling 测量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 acousticHelmpholtzFoam 的实现、验证与直接求解工作流表征；数值格式及其验证详见配套论文 arXiv:2610.01593。

Abstract: acousticHelmholtzFoam is an OpenFOAM application that computes time-harmonic acoustic pressure fields in heterogeneous gas-liquid media on unstructured polyhedral meshes. The complex pressure is stored as real and imaginary volume fields, the material coefficients are constructed from a volume-of-fluid phase fraction with geometric face-fraction averaging, and open boundaries are truncated by Cartesian perfectly matched layers (PMLs) whose damping tensor is generated by the companion utility setPMLFields. The two coupled real-valued equations are discretized with the cell-centred finite-volume operators of OpenFOAM and assembled explicitly, including processor couplings and boundary contributions, into a distributed PETSc matrix that is solved by default with the MUMPS sparse direct solver. The solver writes pressure amplitude, complex velocity, acoustic radiation pressure and time-averaged momentum flux for acoustic-force evaluation. This paper documents the implemented equations, the case interface, the assembly and solution algorithm, the build procedure and the test infrastructure. A layered gas-liquid-PML case with an analytical solution serves as an end-to-end regression: on 8000 cells the whole-domain relative complex-pressure error is $1.197\times10^{-3}$, and serial and MPI solutions on up to eight ranks agree to within $10^{-12}$ in relative $L_2$ norm. A single-axis acoustic-levitator height sweep with constant-volume spheroidal drops demonstrates the two-phase material input and the force output, and strong- and problem-size-scaling measurements characterize the direct-solver workflow. The numerical formulation and its verification are presented in a companion paper (arXiv:2610.01593).

</details>


### [9] [FDTD Model of Noise Pollution from an AI Data Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14899)
*Gavin Buxton*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究用FDTD模拟AI数据中心附近地形上的声传播，发现地形显著决定低频噪声污染分布，可用于确定降噪措施的重点区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心快速增长引发环境担忧，其中低频噪声污染不仅扰民，还可能对当地居民健康造成显著影响。

Method: 建立FDTD模型，模拟声音在AI数据中心附近地形上的传播。

Result: 地形对噪声水平影响很大：AI数据中心附近河流两侧地势突然升高区域更易出现高噪声，而更远处区域则大多受地形保护而免受噪声污染。

Conclusion: 理解地形在噪声污染中的作用，并针对特定地形捕捉噪声污染情况，有助于识别最适合实施噪声缓解策略的区域。

Abstract: The sudden growth in AI data centers is raising numerous environmental concerns. Low-frequency noise pollution is a concern for local residents, that may not just be an annoyance but also carry significant health impacts. An FDTD model of sound propagation over the topography near an AI data center is presented. The topography plays a large role in the level of noise pollution, with areas of sudden elevation on either side of the river near the AI data center more susceptible to high noise levels, while areas further afield largely protected from noise pollution by the topography. Understanding the role that topography plays in noise pollution, and capturing the noise pollution in specific topographies, can help identify areas where noise mitigation strategies might be best employed.

</details>


### [10] [A quantum algorithm for meshless lattice Boltzmann method with unitary interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14943)
*Chi Zhang,Hefeng Wang,Hua Xiang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出质量加权的无网格移位不可压格子Boltzmann方法，将半拉格朗日插值流步精确酉化并结合Carleman嵌入，降低块编码成本且保持碰撞步结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无网格LBM在散点上的半拉格朗日流步是插值矩阵，非酉；通用块编码需按稀疏度归一化并使用对数多辅助比特，量子化/编码代价高。

Method: 将插值写为mimetic导数算子指数exp(aDx+bDy)；为节点赋予Voronoi质量，经质量加权相似变换，在矩约束与低波数三角差分条件下使导数算子反厄米，从而实现插值精确酉。碰撞步仅二次项缩放后不变，同一权重用于分布函数振幅编码。

Result: 对准均匀节点集，质量加权对角保持在单位阶归一化内，随Carleman截断阶增长仅轻微退化，从而降低块编码成本；Taylor-Green涡数值实验复现完整无网格LBM，速度误差随Carleman阶增加而减小。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了无网格移位不可压LBM流步的精确酉化，并与Carleman嵌入兼容，为高效块编码该算法提供了可行方案且经数值验证有效。

Abstract: We present a mass-weighted meshless formulation for the shifted incompressible lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) whose streaming step is exactly unitary, combining with the Carleman embedding. In traditional meshless LBM, on a scattered node set the semi-Lagrangian streaming step is an interpolation, which is not unitary; a generic block encoding of this matrix therefore requires a normalization proportional to its sparsity and logarithmically many ancillas. To overcome the non-unitarity, we instead express the interpolation as the exponential exp(a Dx + b Dy) of mimetic derivative operators. Endowing each node with its Voronoi mass and applying a mass-weighted similarity transform renders these derivative operators skew-Hermitian by enforcing moment constraints together with low-wave number trigonometric differentiation conditions, so that the interpolation becomes exactly unitary. Such unitarization of interpolation leave the local collision step unchanged up to a rescaling of its quadratic term, where the same weights define the amplitude encoding of the distribution functions. For quasi-uniform node sets, the mass-weighting diagonal remains within a unit-order normalization that degrades only mildly as the Carleman truncation order grows, thereby mitigating the cost of block encoding. Numerical experiments on the Taylor Green vortex confirm that the method reproduces the full meshless LBM, with the velocity error decreasing as the Carleman order increases.

</details>


### [11] [Self-Adaptive Model-Order Reduction for Shock-Dominated Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15944)
*Ali Mohaghegh,Cheng Huang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种自自适应投影模型降阶（MOR）框架，在线根据解特征动态选择并部署自适应或静态ROM，以兼顾强对流/激波主导流动的预测精度与计算效率，并在旋转爆震发动机（RDE）上开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态ROM通常依赖离线/在线训练策略，对强对流主导、物理强演化如激波的问题预测能力有限；自适应ROM通过在线更新低维子空间跟踪局部动力学，但会带来额外计算开销，频繁基与采样点更新可能显著削弱其相对静态ROM的计算优势。因此需协调自适应精度与效率之间的权衡。

Method: 基于投影MOR，提出自自适应MOR框架，在在线模型执行过程中根据解的演化特征动态构造并部署最合适的ROM形式（自适应或静态），以在保持局部物理高建模精度的同时优化计算效率，并减少对大规模离线训练的依赖。

Result: 摘要未提供定量性能结果；该框架针对旋转爆震发动机（RDE）进行开发与评估，RDE包含强对流主导、瞬态、多尺度反应流动力学，构成具有挑战性的测试环境。

Conclusion: 该自自适应MOR策略旨在利用自适应ROM与静态ROM两者优势，按需动态切换以平衡预测精度与计算效率，适用于激波主导和强演化物理问题，并降低对广泛离线训练的依赖。

Abstract: Projection-based model-order reduction (MOR) offers a promising approach for accelerating expensive full-order model (FOM) by constructing reduced-order models (ROMs) that retain the majority of FOM accuracy while significantly reducing the computational cost. However, conventional static ROMs, often following offline/online training strategy, can exhibit limited predictive capability for convection-dominated problems featuring strongly evolving physics such as shocks. Adaptive ROMs emerge as a promising alternative and address such limitation by dynamically updating the low-dimensional subspace during online simulation to track locally evolving system dynamics, thereby enabling accurate predictions even for shock-dominated flows. However, such online adaptation introduces additional computational overhead, and frequent basis and sampling-point updates can substantially reduce their computational advantage relative to their static counterpart. To reconcile this tradeoff, the current work introduces a self-adaptive MOR framework that dynamically constructs and deploys the most appropriate ROM formulation (either adaptive or static) on the fly according to the evolving characteristics of the solution. to achieve optimal computational efficiency while maintaining high modeling accuracy of the local physics during the online model execution. In this regard, the proposed framework seeks to exploit the advantages of both adaptive- and static-ROM approaches to achieve an optimal computational efficiency and predictive accuracy while reducing reliance on extensive offline training. The framework is developed and evaluated for modeling rotating detonation engines (RDEs), which provide a challenging test environment involving strongly convection-dominated, transient, and multiscale reacting-flow dynamics.

</details>


### [12] [Principles and Techniques for Agentic Research in Experimental Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17193)
*Niko R. Reed,Declan M. Daly,Andrew J. Beling,Jordan D. Giandrea,Ronald L. Walsworth,John W. Blanchard,Daniel Ang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出实验物理中协作型 AI agent 的十项要求与实施框架，并通过六个案例证明闭环 LLM agent 可有效辅助设计、控制、建模与数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 在物理研究中日益普及，但缺乏有效使用指南；需要人本、可负责、能放大而非替代人类专家能力的协作范式。

Method: 以原理驱动提出十项要求，涵盖科学规范、agent 有效使用和领域专业知识；构建框架，通过定制 scaffolding 改进现成 LLM 行为，定义通用科研工作流与技能，并采用面向明确目标的闭环迭代 campaign。

Result: 六个实验物理案例覆盖器件设计、硬件控制、神经网络优化、物理模型辨识和数据分析，产出包括：超越已发表工作的 NN 架构与固态量子缺陷光动力模型；可制造且满足实验约束的微波波导；仿真性能提升逾一个数量级的量子控制脉冲候选；在实时硬件上将 NMR 谱仪调试至约 1 Hz 频率精度；以及首次从衍射极限磁成像重建人工自旋冰阵列 Ising 微态的数据拟合流程。

Conclusion: 人本协作 AI agent 能产出有用科研成果并增强人类研究者能力，闭环工作流是实验物理中有效 AI 协作的有前景模式。

Abstract: The use of artificial intelligence (AI) in physics research is increasingly common, yet few guidelines exist for its effective use. Starting with a principles-based approach, we outline a set of ten requirements for collaborative AI agents in experimental physics, spanning scientific norms, effective agent use, and domain expertise. We take a human-centered approach in which agents act as collaborators that amplify rather than replace the expertise of a human researcher (user) who directs the work and is responsible for its results. We then present a framework designed to enforce these requirements, detailing methods for improving off-the-shelf behavior of large language models (LLMs) with custom scaffolding and describing general-purpose scientific workflows and skills. In particular, we highlight the power of closed-loop campaigns where agents work iteratively toward a well-defined goal. We demonstrate the potential of this mode of research through six case studies in experimental physics, covering device design, hardware control, neural network optimization, physical model identification, and data analysis. All six case studies result in useful work products that add utility to our research, including a neural network architecture and solid-state quantum defect photodynamic model that substantially outperform our published work; a microwave waveguide design that extends a published design concept to a fabrication-ready device meeting our experimental constraints; a quantum control pulse sequence candidate that improves simulated performance by more than an order of magnitude; commissioning of an NMR spectrometer to $\sim$1~Hz frequency precision on live hardware; and finally a data-fitting pipeline that achieves, to our knowledge, the first reconstruction of the Ising microstate of an artificial spin-ice array from diffraction-limited magnetic imaging.

</details>


### [13] [Wisevolve Potential Engine: Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics at 100 Million Atom-Steps per Second on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17366)
*Hekai Bu,Zheyong Fan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: WPE 是面向 GPUMD 的 GPU 势能执行引擎，根据势模型和模拟阶段选择并复用专用执行策略，从而显著加速多种分子动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用分子动力学软件需支持多种势函数和模拟需求，但在单个模拟阶段中，已加载的势模型与所需计算通常固定，因此可利用这一不变性进行 GPU 执行策略的模型与阶段专用化。

Method: WPE 从 GPUMD 获取模型和模拟阶段信息，选择并复用适配该模型与阶段的 GPU 执行策略；GPUMD 仍负责模拟控制和数据所有权。文中将 WPE 与 GPUMD 集成，并在多种 NEP、qNEP、经典势和 PIMD 工作负载上评估。

Result: 在单块 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上，GPUMD-WPE 在 106 个 NEP 工作负载中达到中位 109 million atom-step/s；15.1M 原子金刚石 Si 系统达 142 million atom-step/s。相对 GPUMD 的几何平均加速为：NEP 3.49x、qNEP 3.61x、经典势 1.24x、PIMD 10.5x。水结构、晶格热导率和 PIMD 热膨胀的端到端结果与 GPUMD 及已发表结果吻合；12.5M 原子 Cu 冲击压缩展示其适用于大规模非平衡动力学。

Conclusion: 模型与阶段特定的专用化是加速通用分子动力学工作流中 GPU 势能评估的实用途径。

Abstract: General-purpose molecular dynamics software supports diverse interatomic potentials and simulation requirements. Within a simulation stage, the loaded potential model and the required calculations typically remain fixed as the atomic configuration evolves. We present the Wisevolve Potential Engine (WPE), which uses this information from GPUMD to select and reuse a GPU execution policy tailored to the model and simulation stage. GPUMD retains simulation control and ownership of the simulation data. On a single NVIDIA RTX 5090, WPE-enabled GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) achieves a median throughput of 109 million atom-step/s across 106 neuroevolution potential (NEP) workloads. For a 15.1-million-atom diamond Si system, it sustains 142 million atom-step/s. Relative to GPUMD, geometric-mean speedups are 3.49x across these NEP workloads, 3.61x across 16 workloads using NEP with dynamic charges (qNEP), 1.24x across 28 classical-potential workloads, and 10.5x across 56 path-integral molecular dynamics (PIMD) configurations. End-to-end calculations of water structure, lattice thermal conductivity, and PIMD thermal expansion show close agreement with corresponding GPUMD results and published simulation results. A separate 12.5-million-atom Cu shock-compression simulation demonstrates its applicability to large-scale nonequilibrium dynamics. These results support model- and stage-specific specialization as a practical way to accelerate GPU potential evaluation in general-purpose molecular dynamics workflows.

</details>


### [14] [KINTORA: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin drift-kinetic, unstructured mesh tokamak solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17428)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种完全不依赖贴磁力线网格的连续介质漂移动理学求解器：在非结构三角极向平面上做半拉格朗日间断伽辽金离散并沿环向拉伸，平行方向按通量坐标无关（FCI）方式沿精确磁力线映射推进，配以绝热电子和长波长极化场求解；所有算子均与独立参考对比验证，并在CPU/GPU及不同MPI进程数间可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续介质回旋动理学代码依赖结构化、贴磁力线的嵌套磁面网格；粒子模拟（PIC）虽支持非结构网格与开放磁力线，却存在统计噪声下限。本文旨在构建一种不落于二者任何一端的方案：既无PIC噪声，又不要求网格与磁场对齐。

Method: 采用半拉格朗日间断伽辽金（SLDG）离散，在非结构三角极向网格上离散并沿环向拉伸成三维网格；平行推进为沿精确磁力线的映射（FCI方式），因此网格始终不与磁场对齐；物理模型包含绝热电子与长波长极化场求解。

Result: 每个算子均与独立计算的参考解对比：Landau阻尼、测地声学模根及线性离子温度梯度（ITG）特征值问题中，除测地声学阻尼误差为12%外，其余实测速率均在4%以内。在canonical-Maxwellian平衡上的线性环向ITG示范得到径向相干模式，增长率0.065 c_s/a且随时间保持稳定。无碰撞带状流残余是唯一尚未收敛的量测：随极向加密由0.72升至模型自身轨道求积预测值的0.87。求解器完全驻留设备，所有结果在CPU与GPU构建及不同MPI进程数下均可复现；代码、各表格数据与工件说明已公开于公共仓库。

Conclusion: 该SLDG-FCI漂移动理学求解器在非结构网格上实现了低噪声、可验证的模拟能力，多数线性基准在4%以内通过验证；但带状流残余尚未收敛，其网格收敛性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Continuum gyrokinetic codes use structured, field-aligned grids on nested flux surfaces; particle-in-cell codes support unstructured meshes and open field lines but carry a statistical noise floor. We present a continuum drift-kinetic solver that occupies neither position: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin discretization on unstructured triangular poloidal planes extruded toroidally, whose parallel advance is a map along the exact field line in the flux-coordinate independent manner, so the mesh is never aligned with the field, with adiabatic electrons and a long-wavelength polarization field solve. Every operator is verified against an independently computed reference, from Landau damping to the geodesic-acoustic root and a linear ion-temperature-gradient eigenvalue problem, every measured rate within 4% except the geodesic-acoustic damping, within 12%. A linear toroidal ion-temperature-gradient demonstration on a canonical-Maxwellian equilibrium recovers a radially coherent mode whose growth rate, 0.065 c_s/a, is stationary in time. The collisionless zonal-flow residual is the one measurement still moving: it rises with poloidal refinement from 0.72 to 0.87 of the model's own orbit-quadrature prediction for the measured observable and has not converged. The solver is fully device resident, and every result is reproduced between CPU and GPU builds and across MPI process counts. The code, the data behind every table and an artifact description are in a public repository.

</details>

### [15] [Revealing New Leakage Paths in GAAFETs using Predictive First-Principles Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14298)
*Denis Mamaluy,Juan P. Mendez,Coleman Cariker,Michael Titze,Alex A. Belianinov*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作利用电荷自洽的NEGF-CBR量子输运模拟，在实空间解析了GAAFET中由DIDBL增强的栅-漏直接隧穿漏电路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAAFET是FinFET的潜在后继者，但在负栅压下漏极电流偏离热电子发射行为并随栅压更负而增大；该泄漏被认为源于高k介质中的栅-漏直接隧穿，并受漏致介质势垒降低（DIDBL）增强，需从第一性原理和空间分辨角度加以验证。

Method: 采用预测性、电荷自洽的非平衡格林函数（NEGF）模拟，基于接触块约化（CBR）方法，将源、漏、栅作为载流量子力学接触处理，并在多端量子输运框架中解析器件内部局域电流密度。

Result: 空间分辨的电流密度表明，栅注入载流子优先在栅极靠近漏极一侧穿过高k介质，该处介质势垒受漏偏压降低最强，随后被漏极收集。

Conclusion: 该模拟给出了DIDBL驱动栅-漏泄漏机制的直接实空间图像，区别于主要依赖唯象泄漏模型的分析，为理解GAAFET负栅压漏电提供了量子输运层面的证据。

Abstract: Gate-All-Around Field-Effect Transistors (GAAFETs) are emerging as successors to Fin Field-Effect Transistors (FinFETs) and are central to continued scaling at advanced semiconductor nodes. Despite their improved electrostatic control, recent measurements show that the drain current in GAAFETs deviates strongly from thermionic behavior at negative gate voltages, increasing as the gate voltage becomes more negative. This leakage has been attributed to direct gate-drain tunneling through the high-$k$ dielectric, enhanced by drain-induced dielectric barrier lowering (DIDBL). Here, we use predictive, charge-self-consistent Non-Equilibrium Green Function (NEGF) simulations based on the Contact Block Reduction (CBR) method to spatially resolve this leakage pathway in GAAFETs. Unlike approaches based primarily on phenomenological leakage models, our first-principles, multi-terminal quantum-transport framework treats the source, drain, and gate as current-carrying quantum-mechanical contacts and resolves the local current density throughout the device. The spatially resolved current density shows that gate-injected carriers tunnel through the high-$k$ dielectric preferentially near the drain side of the gate, where the dielectric barrier is most strongly lowered by the drain bias, and are collected by the drain. This provides a direct real-space picture of the DIDBL-driven gate-drain leakage mechanism.

</details>


### [16] [Non-Hermitian Two-Kink Control of Corner States in Twisted Chern Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15470)
*Xiao-Ming Zhao,Su-Peng Kou*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过扭转由相反陈数陈绝缘体构成的双层，并引入非厄米层间质量相位扭结与独立虚动量扭结，可分别独立调控质量缺陷角态的能谱、面内空间分布和层极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角态通常被边界质量缺陷锚定，这种几何锚定限制了对其空间位置与内部自由度的控制；作者希望解耦并独立调控角态的谱、空间和层间特性。

Method: 研究由相反陈数陈绝缘体组成的扭转双层；构造非厄米层间质量的质量相位扭结与独立虚动量扭结；以扭转 Qi-Wu-Zhang 双层在任意包括凹边界上验证，并在 30° 扭转 Haldane 十二边形中演示。

Result: 质量相位扭结产生母体角极点并固定其能量与本征衰减，定向层间隧穿编码层极化；虚动量纹理重塑闭合边界包络并在角间重新分配稳态右态权重。QWZ 双层验证该分工；30° Haldane 十二边形出现十二个有限能量母态，带隙内谱流形保持，而归一化右子空间权重可向六、三、二或一个目标角重新分布。

Conclusion: 两个扭结可协同或竞争，同时保持谱系来源、空间权重和层特征彼此区分，为跨不同边界几何与质量纹理控制质量缺陷角态提供统一框架。

Abstract: Corner states are bound by boundary-mass defects, but this geometric anchoring restricts their spatial and internal control. Focusing on twisted bilayers assembled from Chern insulators with opposite Chern numbers, we show that a mass-phase kink carried by a non-Hermitian interlayer mass and an independent imaginary-momentum kink separate the spectral, in-plane, and interlayer control of mass-defect corner states. The mass-phase kink creates the parent corner pole and fixes its energy and intrinsic decay, while directed interlayer tunneling encodes its layer polarization. The imaginary-momentum texture instead reshapes the closed-boundary envelope and redistributes stationary right-state weight between corners. A twisted Qi--Wu--Zhang bilayer verifies this division on arbitrary, including concave, boundaries. A $30^\circ$ twisted Haldane dodecagon realizes twelve finite-energy parent states whose in-gap spectral manifold persists while boundary patterns redistribute its normalized right-subspace weight toward six, three, two, or one target corners. The two kinks therefore cooperate or compete while keeping spectral ancestry, spatial weight, and layer character distinct, thereby establishing a unified framework for controlling mass-defect corner states across disparate boundary geometries and mass textures.

</details>


### [17] [Coexistence of Multiple Distinct Corner-State Classes via Nearest-Neighbor Couplings Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15727)
*Luhong Su,Dongyi Wang,Shenglong Guo,Xinjiang Chen,Lei Zhou,Shaojie Ma,Mengyao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在不引入长程耦合、甚至次近邻耦合的情况下，仅通过调节最近邻耦合，就能在单一晶格中实现并实验观测多种局域角态的共存；作者利用拓扑电路平台验证了I型/II型角态、角局域连续域束缚态、平带态和边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种局域态（尤其是不同拓扑角态）的共存具有重要价值，但通常依赖足够强的长程耦合，导致在多种系统中难以实验实现。因此，需要寻找无需长程耦合、易于实验落地的实现方案。

Method: 研究者在纯最近邻耦合条件下设计晶格，使多种局域角模得以演化与共存；实验采用拓扑电路平台，该平台可按设计严格排除长程耦合，并直接表征能带谱及局域本征模的空间分布。

Result: 在单一晶格中同时实现了I型角态、II型角态、角局域连续域束缚态（BIC）、平带态和边缘态，并实验确认其共存；通过能带谱和本征模空间分布对局域模式进行了直接刻画。

Abstract: The ability to dynamically control topological order remains a central challenge in the exploration of quantum materials. Despite generating electronic temperatures far above the topological-to-normal transition temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$, optical excitation in the TCI phase does not induce a phase transition. Instead, it drives an ultrafast lattice contraction of $\Delta a \approx -0.45$ pm within less than 1 ps that counteracts thermal expansion and prevents the system from reaching $a_\mathrm{c}$. Abstract: We demonstrate how to build a robust phase space electronic Hamiltonian $\hat{H}_\text{PS}(\boldsymbol{R},\boldsymbol{P})$ that depends on nuclear position $\boldsymbol{R}$ and nuclear momentum $\boldsymbol{P}$ in the presence of ionic pseudopotentials. In particular, we show that in order to account for the non-local component of a pseudopotential while also conserving linear and angular momentum, one is forced to introduce a non-local one-electron operator $\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}^\text{nl}$ that couples electronic and nuclear momenta together. To test the validity of our ansatz, we derive a generalized form of Nafie's exact equality (which relates electronic momentum and electronic position) so as to include the presence of pseudopotentials. We then benchmark our proposed phase space electronic structure theory against this generalized Nafie-like result for a diverse set of diatomic molecules spanning a wide range of elements. We find excellent agreement across this ab initio test set, highlighting the strength of our method in capturing meaningful electronic momentum beyond the Born-Oppenheimer framework.

Abstract: Real-space interference of wave packets is governed by their central momentum separation and momentum width, which set the oscillation wavelength and spatial envelope, respectively. Here, we show that in crystalline systems, quantum metric distribution in an isolated band can similarly give rise to real-space interference through the evanescent tails of a localized bound state. The location and width of a quantum metric hot spot in the momentum space set a complex wavevector as one evanescent plane wave component of a bound state. Consequently, with multiple quantum metric hot spots, the density distribution of a bound state or the total density of two spatially overlapped bound states exhibits an interference pattern, where the oscillation period and long-range decay length are determined by the momentum-space separation of the hot spots and the width of the narrowest hot spots, respectively. This quantum-geometric interference is particularly pronounced in (nearly) flat bands, where competing scales from band dispersion are suppressed. Furthermore, we demonstrate that using a Josephson STM the real-space interference can be experimentally observed through the tip-position dependence of the Josephson critical current. Our results reveal a direct correspondence between momentum-space quantum geometry and real-space interference, showing how bound-state profiles encode the momentum-resolved quantum metric.

Conclusion: 全电学、可寻址的核自旋操控为可扩展的硅基量子计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The nuclear spin of a phosphorus atom in silicon is considered as a promising quantum information carrier for quantum computing due to its long coherence time. Coherent control of the nuclear spin is typically realized using nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR). In this work, we theoretically propose that with the aid of hyperfine interactions and magnetic field gradients, fast electric-dipole-induced nuclear electric resonance (EDNER) can be achieved. The stronger EDNER (compared with NMR) arises from the combined effects of hyperfine interaction between the nuclear and electron spins and coupling between the electron spin and charge degrees of freedom mediated by magnetic field gradients in this scheme. By operating at specific working points, the nuclear-spin splitting becomes less sensitive to electrical noise, enabling long coherence times and high-fidelity quantum operations. All elctrical, addressable control of nuclear spins paves the way for scalable silicon-based quantum computing.

An additional and important metric to consider is the strength of the dielectric constant. Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is the benchmark gate dielectric for two-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures, owing to its wide bandgap, and atomically flat interface. At the same time, its dielectric constant (2--4) highlights the opportunity to explore alternative higher-permittivity van der Waals dielectrics that provide stronger electrostatic control. Previous work established lanthanum oxybromide (LaOBr), a layered dielectric with a predicted dielectric constant of 12.5, as a potential dielectric for van der Waals heterostructures. Using graphene field-effect devices with LaOBr, we show that the leakage-onset and breakdown fields decrease systematically with thickness. Dual-gate capacitance measurements combined with a dead-layer model reveal a bulk dielectric constant of $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{bulk}} = 12.2$, in agreement with theory, while interfacial dead-layers cause the effective dielectric constant to vary from 6 to 11 across multiple thicknesses. Benchmarked against established gate dielectrics, LaOBr combines breakdown fields comparable to hBN with dielectric constants 2 to 3 times larger. By establishing the operating regime of devices with various thicknesses, we further confirm the advantage of LaOBr over hBN for high-capacitance gate-dielectric operation. These results validate LaOBr as a promising dielectric for integration into two-dimensional heterostructures, and establish thickness-dependent benchmarking as essential for evaluating emerging layered dielectric materials.

Here, we show that trigonal tellurium (Te) hosts an enantiomer-dependent Berry curvature dipole (BCD) that can be accessed through the measurement of cross-axial nonlinear Hall effect (NHE), unlike previously studied in-plane configurations where the BCD contribution vanishes. We studied the transport kinetics of IXs in a MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ van der Waals heterostructure with an anomalously high IX mobility, consistent with superfluidity. This high-mobility transport demonstrates that disorder-induced localization is negligible. We found a remarkable suppression of this efficient IX transport, directly indicating the Mott insulator. As the IX density increases during the excitation pulse, the IX cloud initially expands and then contracts upon entering the Mott insulator phase. Abstract: Low-energy electrons in graphene obey an emergent, rotationally invariant Dirac equation, while the honeycomb lattice enters only through higher-order, symmetry-breaking corrections, the leading one being trigonal warping. This provides a unique solid-state arena to address a cornerstone challenge in quantum gravity: how to detect ultraviolet symmetry-breaking granular corrections, suppressed by a fundamental scale, atop an emergent infrared relativistic continuum. We show that intense counter-rotating $\omega$-$2\omega$ fields and valley-resolved high-harmonic generation (HHG) provide such a probe. Within a family of models that interpolates continuously between the isotropic Dirac cone and nearest-neighbour graphene at fixed Fermi velocity, the valley contrast of the harmonic yield vanishes identically in the symmetric theory, grows linearly with the warping coupling, and its valley-even counterpart appears only at second order, as expected from effective-field-theory power counting. The stronger the laser, the farther it drives electrons from the Dirac points, so the laser intensity plays the role of the collision energy. For the fourth harmonic, the leading warping correction alone accounts for the valley contrast of real graphene up to fundamental intensities of about $10^{11}$ W/cm$^2$, below the damage threshold of monolayer graphene, whereas the fifth harmonic already reveals higher-order lattice corrections at intensities around $10^{10}$ W/cm$^2$. The two harmonics, which rotate in opposite directions, are emitted by opposite valleys. Valley-resolved harmonics are far more sensitive than the residual valley populations, making them a practical detector of lattice-scale physics hidden beneath an emergent Dirac theory.

Abstract: Recent experiments highlighted the essential role of the orbital degrees of freedom underlying current-induced magnetic dynamics. However, a microscopic picture of how orbital angular momenta are transferred between itinerant and localized electrons, and the way it feeds back into transport, remains elusive. Here we develop a theoretical framework for the orbital exchange interactions by generalizing the Anderson-Kondo model to incorporate the orbitals of conduction bands. Illustrating with the $p$-orbitals, we determine the way electrons acquire orbital polarization and its influence on the transport properties, uncovering the orbital counterparts of phenomena analogous to spin filtering and tunnel magnetoresistance. Reciprocally, we demonstrate that orbital-polarized currents can exert torques on localized magnetic moments without having to convert into spin currents, which enables electrical switching of magnetization even when spin polarization becomes inactive. These striking discoveries lay a microscopic foundation for orbitronics and reveal critical mechanisms for orbital manipulations of magnetic order.

Abstract: A solvable microscopic model of interacting electrons in a half-filled Landau level is presented. The Hamiltonian is a well-defined limit of the electron-electron interaction. The low energy sector consists of a set of independent, spatially interlaced, one-dimensional systems of non-interacting fermionic dipoles, each system with a Fermi sea ground state. The total number of dipoles in the set of Fermi seas forming the two-dimensional ground state equals the number of electrons in the system and the density of states at the Fermi level is that of a two-dimensional Fermi gas. The model is obtained by generalizing, or rather adopting, the existing exact solution on a thin, but not too thin, torus to the two-dimensional bulk case.

Abstract: The integer and fractional quantum Hall effects reveal that quantized physical responses can take integer and fractional values, respectively. This naturally raises the question of whether quantization can extend beyond rational numbers to an irrational value. Here we show that such a quantized response can emerge from the limiting behavior of a finite, experimentally realizable geometric network. We develop a method based on infinite geometric structures, in which a sequence of finite networks rapidly converges toward an infinite limit. Following this approach, we identify a planar network whose normalized resistance converges to the irrational value $\sqrt{3}$, and realize a finite approximation with high-precision geometric control. The measured normalized resistance is $1.735 \pm 0.001\, h/e^2$ for a fully occupied Landau level in the designed pattern. Our experimental results extend the concept of quantization beyond rational values and demonstrate that an irrational quantized response can be approached through a finite physical system.

Abstract: Short-wavelength spin waves are central to miniaturizing magnonic devices and to studying exchange-dominated magnetization dynamics. Yet the wave-vector range between roughly 30 and 300 radians per micrometre has remained difficult to access experimentally, posing challenges for both excitation and detection. This Perspective reviews recent advances in emerging measurement techniques and discusses prospects for their future development. Optical and microwave techniques are restricted to the vicinity of the Brillouin-zone centre. Inelastic neutron, electron and resonant hard x-ray scattering cover the full Brillouin zone but lack the energy resolution needed for low-frequency magnons at intermediate wave vectors. This Perspective examines five emerging approaches that are beginning to close this gap: Mie-enhanced Brillouin light scattering, colour-centre magnetometry, magnon momentum microscopy, extreme-ultraviolet transient-grating spectroscopy, and time-resolved scanning transmission x-ray microscopy. For each, we discuss the physical principle, demonstrated capabilities, current limitations and prospects for development. Compared side by side, the techniques give complementary access to magnon frequencies, local stray fields, real-space dynamics and momentum-space distributions, and they differ in their sensitivity to coherent and incoherent excitations. We therefore discuss the main advantages and use cases of each technique and consider the practical requirements for addressing different scientific questions. We highlight opportunities for further development, including higher sensitivity, improved spatial and temporal resolution, and broader frequency and wave-vector coverage. Together, these advances could enable, for example, systematic studies of nonlinear magnon interactions at nanoscale wavelengths and investigations of emerging magnetic materials such as altermagnets.

</details>

In this work, we study the kinetics of spin transport mediated by a quantum system of indirect excitons (IXs) in a van der Waals heterostructure composed of atomically thin transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs). This spin transfer mechanism allows for the realization of efficient spin transport characterized by a high spin mobility reaching 10$^5$ cm$^2$/(eV$\cdot$s), which is orders of magnitude higher than that achieved via standard diffusive spin transport in TMD materials. The suppression of IX scattering enables high-mobility spin transport.

TL;DR: 该文提出一个方形晶格上缓慢调制的狄拉克质量纹理模型，可精确解析且实验可及，统一实现平带与拓扑零模，并揭示微带带宽由三种不同尺度共同决定。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带能放大相互作用并导致关联相，拓扑束缚态则受原理保护；因此希望将二者结合，并避免复杂几何、格点尺度纹理或长程跃迁，同时保持模型可精确分析与实验可实现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个精确可解、实验可及的平带-拓扑零模平台，将 Jackiw-Rebbi 零模、SSH 环与平带物理联系起来，并提出可在冷原子超晶格、Kekule 有序石墨烯、STM 设计晶格和微波阵列中检验。 

Abstract: Flat bands amplify interactions into correlated phases, and topological bound states are robust by principle; combining the two in a model that is both exactly analyzable and experimentally accessible is therefore especially desirable. Here we show that a square-lattice model with a slowly modulated Dirac mass texture, $m(x)=m+m_0\cos(2\pi x/L)$ with $L$ up to hundreds of sites, provides such a setting, without engineered geometries, lattice-scale textures, or long-range hoppings. Exact nesting reduces the problem, independently at each transverse momentum $k_y$, to a $1+1$ dimensional Dirac Hamiltonian with a position-dependent mass: every simple zero of $m(x)$ binds Jackiw-Rebbi zero modes, and the two walls of a period form an effective Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ring. The model interpolates between a chirally pinned flat band at large $L$, a helical miniband whose nodes are enforced by a boundary phase of the ring, and a bias-dimerized regime in which the sign of the bias sets a winding number. A central result is that the miniband bandwidth is not a single scale: a max-min measurement conflates three widths of different origin, the helical height $W_{\rm JR}$ exponential in the period, the zone-edge upturn $W_{\rm edge}\propto L^{-2/3}$ dominating at large $L$, and a bias bump exponential in $L$, so the flat band is robust against bias in the thermodynamic limit. We analyze two lattice realizations, a two-orbital model with modulated inter-orbital hybridization and a single-orbital bond-order texture sharing the nodal physics while leaving the van Hove points gapless, and propose experiments on cold-atom superlattices, Kekule-ordered graphene, STM designer lattices, and microwave arrays.

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述有序和无序拓扑绝缘体中的自旋拓扑性质，需要在有限自旋能隙下定义自旋极化、自旋陈数，并建立动量空间与实空间、超胞框架下的 Wilson loop 表述。

Method: 通过幺正旋转推广占据或未占据子空间中的自旋矩阵以避免谱隙闭合；利用自旋本征矢量将哈密顿量本征态重组为两个自旋扇区；通过维度约化，用一维有效布里渊区上的 Wilson loop 表示各自旋扇区电荷极化，其线性组合定义自旋极化并量化动量空间自旋陈数；进一步由离散二维布里渊区上的局域 Wilson loop 直接导出自旋陈数，并推广到实空间超胞框架，用布里渊区边界周围的 Wilson loop 表述实空间自旋陈数。

Conclusion: Wilson loop 为自旋陈数提供了一种适用于动量空间和实空间、并可处理无序系统的统一拓扑表征；在 BHZ 模型中，自旋陈数与 Z2 拓扑指数一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive the spin polarization and the spin Chern number in momentum and real spaces in terms of the Wilson loop in each spin sector. A giant AHE has recently been observed in the candidate $p$-wave magnet Gd$_3$(Ru$_{1-\delta}$Rh$_{\delta}$)$_4$Al$_{12}$ [Nature 646, 837(2025)], while its theoretical interpretation invokes a spontaneous magnetization to break time-reversal symmetry ($\mathcal{T}$), although the magnetic order itself already breaks $\mathcal{T}$. Here we establish the symmetry condition and microscopic mechanism for the AHE in OPMs. Since the OPM order is classified by the spin group whereas the AHE is governed by the magnetic group, the AHE is allowed only when the magnetic point group is ferromagnetic; conversely, it is forbidden whenever the effective time reversal $\mathcal{T}\tau$ (with $\tau$ a fractional translation), which is essential to the realization of the OPM, is preserved. Microscopically, OPMs generically host a hidden Zeeman field (HZF) rooted in this intrinsic $\mathcal{T}$ breaking, and its interplay with spin-orbit coupling (SOC) generates the AHE without any external field. In particular, a square-lattice helimagnet with sublattice-resolved SOC maps exactly onto an anisotropic Rashba--Zeeman model, whose Zeeman term is nothing but the HZF and directly yields the AHE. Our results establish the symmetry condition for the AHE in OPMs and identify the HZF--SOC interplay as a general route to realize it.

</details>

Nevertheless, it produces a Berry curvature odd in the $x$-direction, yielding a finite Berry curvature dipole (BCD) along $x$ proportional to the sine of the initial phase, while the $y$-component vanishes. The hybrid scenario, by contrast, exhibits a remarkably rich phenomenology. Specifically, its gap-closure criterion becomes cubic and thus admits up to three real roots when the effective parameter along the $x$-direction is negative, thereby producing multiple light-tunable gap-closing points and a series of topological phase transitions. In particular, the BCD exhibits a double-peak structure with strong enhancement near gap-closing manifolds and synchronized sign reversal of the full vector under external tuning. The AHC and ATHC exhibit quantized plateaus that serve as signatures of Chern-insulator phases, while the ANC, being a Fermi-surface probe, provides complementary local information and can identify light-induced gaps even when global topological invariants remain unchanged. Taken together, these results offer useful insights into the topological properties of 2D type-II SDSMs and related systems.

We show that this floor is the thermal noise of the tension. By the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, the tension of a resonator with fixed ends fluctuates by an amount set by the loss angle of its material; for structural damping, these fluctuations produce $1/f$ frequency noise with a flicker coefficient $h_{-1}=E k_{\mathrm{B}} T/(2\pi V\sigma_0^2 Q_{\mathrm{el}})$, with $Q_{\mathrm{el}}$ the intrinsic loss under elongation, that contains no free parameter and is diluted by the tensile stress $\sigma_0$ in the same way as damping is in dissipation dilution. Silicon nitride strings and membranes from 30 to 340~nm thickness follow the predicted volume scaling, the predicted $\sigma_0^{-2}$ dilution across resonators and within stress-engineered strings, and the predicted thickness dependence, which places noise-active two-level systems in the volume and at the surfaces of the resonators, all at the absolute level set by the independently measured intrinsic loss. The same relation accounts for published data between 78 and 296~K; below 78~K, the loss measured at the resonance no longer represents the loss probed by the noise, marking the limit of the structural-damping assumption. Dissipation dilution therefore lowers the $1/f$ floor, and the longest useful integration time of a resonant sensor is set by its prestress, volume, temperature, and intrinsic loss angle.

Abstract: We reconstruct a geometry of thermodynamic coordinates from transport coefficients within a mesoscopic canonical model. Self-adjoint flux observables determine a compatible evolution generator containing symmetric and antisymmetric transport coefficients. When the Hermitian transport tensor is positive, an exact graded first-order factorization reproduces this generator on its scalar sector. Its commutators define a spectral distance whose metric depends only on the symmetric tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution while leaving the metric unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. In the constant Onsager regime, a Clifford representation gives the same distance and an algebra fixed by the transport matrix. Published transport data for a $\mathrm{Bi_2Te_3}$ film give a principal-distance ratio of approximately $2.12$ in normalized coordinates at $ZT\simeq0.68$. We also examine a separate quadratic entropy-production observable and distinguish its discrete spectrum from the spectrum of the evolution generator. The spinorial representation adds no physical spin degrees of freedom.

Abstract: We derive a distance between thermodynamic states from a positive Hermitian transport tensor within a mesoscopic canonical model. An exact first-order factorization of the evolution generator gives a commutator norm and the metric $G=2g^{-1}$, where $g$ denotes the symmetric transport tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution, but they leave this distance unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. For constant Onsager coefficients, a Clifford representation gives the same metric. In a two-channel thermoelectric system, the ratio of normalized principal distances is $\sqrt{1+ZT}+\sqrt{ZT}$. Abstract: Structured light provides a powerful means of controlling matter while itself exhibiting nontrivial spatial topology. Here, we show that these spatial structures can be harnessed to locally engineer the topological properties of matter. In a paradigmatic example of a topological insulator with Dirac surface states driven by an optical skyrmion beam, we show that structured light spatially tailors the effective Hamiltonian, yielding position-dependent topological gaps and Dirac velocities. The resulting gap modulation can create topological and trivial regions separated by optically defined interfaces, without introducing material boundaries or chemical modifications. We further show that the geometry of these interfaces can be controlled through the spatial structure of the light, in particular its orbital angular momentum. Our results establish structured light as a route to dynamically pattern topological phases and open a broader perspective on the interplay between topology in light and matter, including the possibility of transferring topological characteristics between the two.

Abstract: A recent experiment reported coherent parity oscillations between minimal Kitaev chains, demonstrating a Majorana parity qubit (arXiv:2607.09511). Here we show how the exponential protection in the underlying chains can nevertheless be revealed through unprotected qubit operations. To this end, we develop an analytical theory of quasistatic dephasing for two coupled $N$-site Kitaev chains at the sweet spot. In Rabi experiments, protection can be resolved by adapting the operating point with chain length: depending on the coupling architecture, it appears as an exponentially increasing optimized number of coherent oscillations or as a longer coherence time. Ramsey experiments show an exponentially increasing coherence time, provided the chains can be sufficiently decoupled. Our analytical theory takes into account the non-Gaussian splitting statistics of the Kitaev chains, allowing us to quantitatively predict the scaling with chain length. Moreover, we determine how residual noise and imperfections limit the range of chain lengths over which this scaling can be observed. Our results show how qubit dynamics can be used to probe Majorana protection in experimentally realistic devices.

Abstract: Feynman's ratchet illustrates how work can be extracted from thermal fluctuations in the presence of two heat baths at different temperatures. Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive theoretical study of the electronic states in gapped graphene in the presence of a charge impurity and a magnetic field. Special attention is paid to the screening of the Coulomb potential in the presence of metallic gates situated at some distances from graphene sheet, as is commonly the case in experiments. Abstract: Conventional theoretical descriptions of moir\'e superlattices, epitomized by the Bistritzer--MacDonald continuum model, are fundamentally constrained by low-energy Dirac cone approximations and first-shell interlayer coupling truncations. While successful for small-angle twisted bilayer graphene near charge neutrality, these frameworks fail to provide universal analytical solutions for strained lattices, high-energy band behaviors, or arbitrary commensurate geometries. Here, we establish a unified, approximation-free analytical formalism for generalized moir\'e systems based on three pillars: (i) a rigorous two-dimensional-to-one-dimensional supercell cyclic mapping, (ii) exact block-circulant diagonalization of intralayer Hamiltonians, and (iii) quasi-degenerate L\"owdin perturbation theory centered on arbitrary energy degeneracy points across the full spectrum. Unlike traditional schemes anchored to the Dirac point, our approach enables systematic perturbative expansions around any band crossing, unifying the characterization of low-energy flat bands with high-energy degeneracies. We demonstrate that this framework perfectly recovers the Bistritzer--MacDonald model in the low-energy limit while extending its reach to heterostrained systems and high-energy regimes. By incorporating high-order interlayer coupling shells, we reveal an intrinsic scale self-similarity in moir\'e lattices and predict the emergence of higher-order magic angles and tunable flat bands driven by secondary degeneracies. This work eliminates the long-standing lack of universal analytical tools for diverse moir\'e platforms, providing a full-spectrum theoretical foundation for band engineering and the exploration of exotic correlated and topological states in van der Waals heterostructures.

The workflow builds upon standard density functional theory packages to construct compact transport Hamiltonians that preserve the properties of realistic materials. Superconductivity is incorporated via an effective pairing potential within the Nambu-spinor formalism, while coupling to the STM tip and substrate electrodes is treated using the wide-band limit approximation. Within this formulation, transport quantities such as current-voltage characteristics and differential conductance are evaluated in a closed analytical form. This avoids explicit numerical energy integrations and enables rapid spectroscopic calculations over broad parameter spaces. The framework provides a unified and computationally efficient approach for modeling superconducting hybrid nanostructures with spin-orbit coupling, magnetic textures, and spin polarization. Representative applications in spin chains, two-dimensional and molecular magnets on a superconductor demonstrate its robust capabilities for both simple model systems and realistic ab initio molecular structures relevant to modern STM experiments.

Abstract: We uncover an intrinsic gyrotropic spin current (IGSC) driven solely by an oscillating magnetic field, arising from the spin version of Zeeman quantum geometry. The effect is studied in a minimal Rashba-Dresselhaus model that also supports intrinsic gyrotropic charge currents from the Zeeman Berry curvature. The spin and charge currents exhibit a $\pi/2$ phase shift and are found to be perpendicular to each other for certain parameter ranges. Abstract: Dissipation dilution has driven a revolution in the quality factors ($Q$) of top-down nanomechanical resonators. Yet it has not been leveraged for van der Waals (vdW) materials. Here we report quality factors up to $372{,}000\pm37{,}000$ in monolayer MoS$_2$ drums at 4 K, nearly an order of magnitude beyond the previous best for any vdW material at this temperature, enabled by a clean, polymer-free exfoliation technique. We develop a dissipation-dilution model for circular drums and analyze the contributing intrinsic and extrinsic dissipation mechanisms. The MoS$_2$ monolayer exhibits a dilution factor of order $1,000$, establishing sub-nanometer-thin resonators as ultimate dilution materials. The intrinsic quality factor, however, is limited by surface losses at atomic thickness. Conversely, multilayer drums show a lower dilution factor but a high intrinsic quality factor, on par with established top-down materials of similar dimensions, both resulting from the increased thickness. A two-level-system analysis of the temperature-dependent damping confirms this bulk-versus-surface picture and provides first insight into its microscopic origin. Our results identify dissipation dilution as the governing mechanism for high-$Q$ mechanical vdW resonators. Combined with strategies such as soft clamping or stress engineering, they establish atomically thin resonators as a platform for ultrahigh-$Q$ quantum transduction and sensing.

</details>

Abstract: We study collective charge excitations in pseudospin-1 triple-point semimetals within the random-phase approximation, considering both the linearised model with a flat-band and its quadratic-corrected counterpart. We analyse their density response, Drude weights, screening, plasmon dispersion and damping, particle-hole continua, and Friedel oscillations from the long-wavelength to the arbitrary-momentum regime. The quadratic correction renders the flat-band dispersive and endows it with a Fermi surface, thereby affecting the overall response. Comparisons with Weyl and Hopf semimetals reveal how bandstructure and quantum geometry jointly govern collective charge dynamics. While the Drude weights are determined by the Fermi surface and band-velocities, interband screening, plasmon dispersion, and Friedel oscillations reflect the quantum geometry of the eigenstates.

Abstract: We propose a protocol to prepare and probe dark states in a single-molecule electron spin resonance based on atomic force microscopy (ESR-AFM), thus extending existing techniques developed to address Rabi oscillations. To this end, we simulate in analogy to atomic physics the coherent manipulation of a three-level system in the V-configuration using a multifrequency magnetic field. More specifically, we consider a neutral molecule with a zero-field-split (ZFS) first excited triplet and analyze within a pump-probe scheme the dynamics of the total triplet population. In the triplet subspace, the dark state is a coherent superposition formed by two ZFS states which does not evolve in time despite a continuous resonant driving towards the third one. We characterize the properties of this state comparing it to its fully incoherent counterpart. Within a density-matrix formalism for open systems, we then compute the expected dark state signatures both for ideal scenarios and for the realistic parameterization of ESR-AFM on pentacene.

Abstract: We establish theoretically that transverse spin textures are generically induced by the spin-orbit interaction at the Fermi surface of centrosymmetric ferromagnetic metals. We confirm this prediction with {\em ab initio} computations for Fe, Co and Ni. By symmetry, this cannot be associated with spin-orbit torques under a uniform current. However, a new {\em current-gradient-induced} torque emerges in nanoscale geometries. For a series of Ni-based bilayers, the computed Ni thickness dependence of the current-gradient-induced torque is in quantitative agreement with measurements previously attributed to orbital torques. We discuss how this opens a new avenue for the electrical control of magnetic orders, possibly also including the N\'eel vector of metallic altermagnets.

</details>

Abstract: Anyons are particles with quantized fractional statistics. In theory, a single anyon can be stabilized as a localized fractional charge within the bulk of a topologically ordered two-dimensional insulator. However, experiments to date have focused on probing anyons in structures in which many anyonic excitations coexist and interact. Here, we demonstrate the trapping of a single anyon in a device consisting of a graphene-based quantum point contact with an embedded tunable quantum Hall dot. Benchmarking our device at v = 1, we demonstrate backscattering between Fermi liquid edge states mediated by the whole-electron Coulomb blockade of the dot. The cleanliness and control available in our device allow depletion of the dot to the last quasihole, and we observe spin blockade for transitions to the opposite-spin Landau level. At v = 1/3 and 2/3, we observe backscattering resonances with three times the frequency, consistent with resonant transmission of e/3 charges via a single anyon transistor. We demonstrate controlled depletion to the last quasiparticle to form a single anyon quantum dot. Tracing the charge addition spectrum for quasiparticle number Nqp = 1-5, we observe highly irregular addition energies, consistent with DMRG modeling showing a complex orbital shell-filling behavior. At higher Nqp = 10-20, the addition energies show a striking pattern: quasiholes at 1/3 (and quasiparticles at 2/3) show a regular addition spectrum while quasiparticles at 1/3 (and quasiholes at 2/3) do not. We trace the origin of this irregularity to an attractive component of the inter-anyon interaction: by experimentally tuning the confining energy, we observe complete suppression of alternating charge sectors, after which the dot charges in increments of twice the fundamental charge. This observation indicates the emergence of an anyon binding energy as large as the charging energy.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作揭示菱面石墨烯中分数陈绝缘体态的本质，并为后续研究提供新的理论框架。

Abstract: The experimental observation of the fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect in rhombohedral graphene multilayers has raised questions about the nature of the fractional Chern insulator (FCI) states and the role of the moir\'e potential. In this Letter we develop a composite fermion (CF) description and discuss how moir\'e modulation and Coulomb interactions stabilize the FCI ground states. Under a flux-attachment transformation and mean-field approximation, the CF spectrum forms Landau levels that are further split by the moir\'e potential. At moir\'e filling fractions in the Jain sequence, an integer number of CF bands are occupied, resulting in an incompressible ground state with fractionally quantized Hall response. Remarkably, when the attached flux opposes the Berry curvature of the conduction band of rhombohedral graphene, the CF Landau levels are widely spaced in energy and delocalized across layers, leading to a reduced density of states that helps protect the CF mean-field state and amplifying effects of the moir\'e potential acting on the layer away from the doped electrons. Interactions between CFs further enlarge the moir\'e-induced band gap and stabilize the FCI ground state. Our work sheds light on the nature of the FCI states in rhombohedral graphene and provides a new theoretical framework for further studies.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 Ammann–Beenker 准晶的经典 Heisenberg 模型中确定精确交错磁基态，并通过自旋分辨输运的 g 波对称性验证无平移对称性体系中的交错磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁通常依赖平移对称性和动量空间自旋劈裂来识别，而准晶缺乏平移对称性，因此需要探究准晶中能否产生交错磁序以及如何观测其交错磁特征。

Method: 以 Ammann–Beenker 镶嵌为例，利用其母体四维超立方晶格的宇称结构求解经典 Heisenberg 模型的精确基态；随后将局域自旋与 itinerant electrons 耦合，计算自旋分辨输运响应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了准晶中交错磁序的几何路径，将精确磁基态与其自旋分辨输运特征联系起来，把交错磁性拓展到无平移对称性的准周期体系。

Abstract: We identify exact altermagnetic ground states of a classical Heisenberg model on a quasicrystal. Considering the Ammann--Beenker tiling as an example, we determine the ground states by exploiting the parity structure of its parent four-dimensional hypercubic lattice. The ground state hosts coplanar altermagnetic order characterized by the preserved spin-group symmetry $[C_{2x}\parallel C_{8z}]$. Without translational symmetry, the momentum-space spin splitting that usually identifies altermagnets is not directly accessible. We instead couple the spins to itinerant electrons and show spin-resolved transport with $g$-wave symmetry, confirming the altermagnetic nature in a nonperiodic system. Our results establish a geometric route to altermagnetic order in quasicrystals, connecting exact magnetic ground states to their spin-resolved transport signatures.

</details>

Abstract: The valence-bond-glass state of the 4d^1 double perovskite Ba2YMoO6 combines Mo^5+ singlets with residual paramagnetic "orphan" spins whose coupling is hard to reach with bulk probes. We probe them by electron paramagnetic resonance of a dilute Gd^3+ ion on the Y^3+ site via broadband coplanar-waveguide transmission at 5 K. The isostructural nonmagnetic niobate Ba2YNbO6, doped with the same Gd^3+ probe, is a fixed-lattice control. The Gd^3+ linewidth nearly doubles in the magnetic host. Dipolar coupling to the sparse orphans is nearly an order of magnitude too weak; the excess is instead reproduced by a transferred f-d superexchange of J ~ 0.2 K, so the width is a static measure of orphan-spin coupling. The half-field (Delta m_S = +/-2) satellite collapses in the magnetic host, a dynamical dephasing channel, while the unchanged g-factor marks a zero-mean random internal field. Together the two channels probe the coupling and dynamics of valence-bond-glass orphan spins.

</details>


### [60] [Superlattice-Stabilized Fractional Chern Insulators in a "Berry Basin" Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15197)

Abstract: The discovery of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene, following their discovery in the isolated topological flat band in twisted MoTe$_2$, motivates a search for the conditions under which an electronic band can host a topological correlated state. In this Letter we introduce a ``Berry basin" model which captures the essential features of rhombohedral graphene: a dispersive band with a flat bottom, momentum-space winding of a ``layer" degree of freedom, and Coulomb interactions, while also including periodic potentials, enabling controlled studies of the interplay of quantum geometry, superlattice modulation, and electron-electron interactions in producing correlated topological states beyond the isolated topological band paradigm. While integer Chern insulators emerge at strong coupling without a superlattice potential, stabilizing the fractional state requires nonzero potential for all interaction strengths we access. We provide insight into the greater robustness of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene when electrons are polarized away from the moir\'e interface. Our results establish a new class of systems that host robust fractional Chern insulators and provide a controlled numerical platform to test competing theories of interacting topological phases.

Abstract: Magnetic circular dichroism (MCD) is an optical probe of orbital time reversal symmetry breaking. Although frequency integrated MCD is related to the self-rotation contribution to orbital magnetization at zero field, its magnetic response generally differs from the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility. Here, we define the MCD susceptibility and derive general expressions for multiband systems using an electromagnetic $U(1)$ gauge invariant approach. For insulators, the response contains contributions from the non-Abelian quantum metric, Berry curvature, and field induced interband mixing. For metals, a finite temperature Green function formulation separates the smooth response into Fermi sea and Fermi surface contributions. An independent Landau level calculation confirms the expressions for the zero-field MCD moment and its linear magnetic response. In a continuum model for twisted bilayer graphene, the MCD susceptibility vanishes within the charge neutrality gap, while the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility remains markedly diamagnetic. Near the gap edges, the orbital moment term dominates the response. Features near the van Hove energies are associated with density of states enhancement. These results clarify how the two susceptibilities reflect different orbital processes even for the same electronic structure and thus establish the MCD susceptibility as a distinct probe of orbital magnetic response.

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (tBLG) on H-intercalated SiC (twist angle of $9 \pm 1^{\circ}$) is studied by position\mbox{-}, time\mbox{-}, and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). The in-gap feature exhibits distinct dynamics, and its binding energy and $k$-space location are consistent with the proposed non-hydrogenic ``ghost-Fano'' exciton arising from destructive interference between two nearly degenerate excitonic channels. Abstract: Particle exchange in two dimensions gives rise to quantum statistics beyond the boson-fermion dichotomy, with central roles in fractional quantum Hall physics, topological quantum computation, and strongly correlated quantum matter. In conventional topological phases, microscopic exchange geometry is compressed into rigid braid and fusion data. We develop a theory of non-Hermitian statistical geometry in which relaxing Hermiticity softens these exchange data into a larger complex space of spectral and relative-geometric degrees of freedom. For two adjacent braid generators, a single projective exchange spectrum is insufficient: their relative eigenline geometry supplies an additional gauge-invariant modulus. On the generic irreducible two-channel branch, braid consistency locks these coordinates through an exact spectrum-geometry relation. Using Riesz projectors and Kato transport, we realize this geometry in a five-arm Kitaev-Majorana network and test spectral and relative-geometric responses in an independent two-dimensional four-vortex Kitaev model. Finite-time dynamics tests the exchange operation, microscopic fusion sewing tests its composition across fusion descriptions, and matched-spectrum controls isolate the role of relative geometry. Nonzero projective Kato curvature shows that braid-consistent, fusion-coherent exchange can depend on the control path. Non-Hermiticity thus exposes exchange geometry, braid consistency re-locks it, fusion coherence further constrains it, and projective flatness determines when geometric exchange becomes intrinsically topological.

</details>

Abstract: Magnetoelectric (ME) effects in antiferromagnets provide a fertile platform for exploring symmetry-driven cross-correlated responses. However, their microscopic origin remains elusive and is often obscured in simplified low-energy descriptions. In this study, we revisit the microscopic mechanism of the ME effect in a collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chain by employing a multi-orbital tight-binding model that explicitly includes both $s$- and $p$-orbital degrees of freedom. Using analytical and numerical calculations based on the Kubo formula, we demonstrate that the ME response is governed by orbital degrees of freedom activated through $s$--$p$ hybridization, while the spin contribution vanishes due to spin conservation. To elucidate the low-energy description, we derive an effective Hamiltonian projected onto the $s$-orbital subspace using the Schur complement. We show that a naive application of the Kubo formula within this effective model fails to capture the ME response. This issue is resolved by systematically incorporating vertex corrections in terms of orbital hybridization into the response functions. Furthermore, by introducing a quasiparticle renormalization scheme, we formulate a renormalized Kubo formula that preserves conservation laws and accurately reproduces the full multi-orbital results. Our analysis revisits the conventional low-energy perspective and reveals that the ME effect originates from virtual interorbital processes encoded in vertex corrections, rather than from the bare low-energy Hamiltonian. The effective framework developed here provides a unified microscopic understanding of orbital-driven ME responses and offers a systematic route to incorporate hybridization effects beyond simple low-energy models.

</details>
