Abstract: In order to reproduce experimental photoemission spectroscopy (PES) data by using band structure calculations with linearized augmented-plane-wave method, we developed a new method to calculate the atomic-subshell photoelectron differential cross section (PE-DCS) of the valence band. PE-DCS was calculated to be the sum of the products of the partial density of states (PDOS) and the photoionization differential cross section (PI-DCS) of each atomic subshell. To construct an appropriate PDOS beyond the conventional muffin-tin sphere (MTS) PDOS, we proposed a ``nearly-neutral atomic sphere", which can be defined as a natural extension of MTS. Although the calculational method for atomic-subshell PI-DCS was the standard Hartree-Fock-Slater method, we developed a new computer code in order to deal with the unoccupied electron shells in the ground state of isolated atoms up to $\sim$10 keV. Details of the PE-DCS calculations were examined by using two simple examples of Na metal and NaCl, and then, the method was applied to hard x-ray PES (HX-PES) data of CuAlO$_2$. The experimental spectra were satisfactorily reproduced, demonstrating the potential applications of our method to HX-PES spectra of many compounds.

Abstract: Mn$_3$Sn exhibits a complex magnetic phase diagram with multiple symmetry-breaking phenomena. Minute electron doping at the Mn site breaks the kagome lattice symmetry in Mn$_3$Sn. Abstract: A two-dimensional electron gas in a uniform neutralizing background forms a triangular Wigner crystal at low density. In a strongly mass-asymmetric electron-hole bilayer, localized heavy holes instead provide a nonuniform positive background that supports an electron crystal at densities two orders of magnitude higher than for a homogeneous electron gas in the same material. Here we show that correlated mobile electrons can overturn the preference for triangular order, stabilizing a honeycomb electron-hole crystal. Using the powerful real-space neural-network variational Monte Carlo in the Born-Oppenheimer limit with fixed holes, we find that increasing the density drives a transition from a triangular electron-hole crystal to a honeycomb crystal, which eventually melts into itinerant electrons coexisting with the honeycomb or the triangular hole lattice at fixed interlayer separation. Relative to triangular order, honeycomb order emerges when gains in electronic kinetic energy and electron-hole attraction outweigh its higher intralayer Coulomb repulsion, a threshold never reached in the rigid point-dipole model. Moreover, the honeycomb hole lattice supports paramagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and stripe-ordered spin states. Complex polarization indicates a metal-insulator transition, while the states on the honeycomb hole lattice are consistent with a stripe-ordered Dirac semimetal and paramagnetic or antiferromagnetic Mott insulators via mapping to the half-filled Hubbard model. We further propose a BLG/hBN/WS$_2$-WSe$_2$ heterostructure as a platform for the predicted honeycomb crystal, at an experimentally feasible density $n\sim10^{11}~\text{cm}^{-2}$.

Abstract: Quantum skyrmions in frustrated spin-$1/2$ magnets are bound states of a few flipped spins in a ferromagnetic background. Using exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations for the $J_1$--$J_2$ triangular-lattice model with easy-axis anisotropy $K$, we ask what happens when two such objects are brought together. The binding energy per flipped spin of a single skyrmion has a minimum at $\Nf^{*}=7$ for $K=0.02$--$0.04$, and skyrmions smaller than $\Nf^{*}$ do not exist as pairs: at any distance they coalesce into a single larger object, and pinning wells cannot prevent it. Two skyrmions of natural size, by contrast, form a bound molecule without pinning or external field, with binding energy $U\simeq-0.007J_1$ at $K=0.03$--$0.04$ and an equilibrium separation of $5.5$ lattice spacings that does not depend on $K$ or on the size of the simulation domain; at $K=0.02$, where the single skyrmion is itself marginally bound, the pair is unbound within our resolution. The two constituents keep their identity ($7+7$ flipped spins) and are coupled by a coherent exchange of magnons, visible as number fluctuations between the two halves of the domain in excess of those of two independent skyrmions and as in-plane spin correlations between the two skyrmion walls that reach $0.16$ across the gap. These correlations follow the vortex pattern of the two textures and lock their relative helicity, the quantum counterpart of the helicity-dependent interaction of classical skyrmions. We discuss the molecule as a self-assembled two-qubit element for skyrmion helicity qubits in a single layer.

</details>

Result: 得到三个结构结论：(i) 给定轨道扇区的谱与 N 和粒子统计无关；(ii) 玻色子与费米子正则配分函数满足 Z_b(-N)=(-1)^{N_b}Z_f(N)；(iii) 无阻挫跃迁时基态总是颜色铁磁体。应用包括：大 N 下少格点环上吸引 SU(N) 玻色子在任意填充下的有限温度相图；以及在 sawtooth 和 kagome 等阻挫晶格中由阻挫跃迁诱导的 singlet-cluster 态，作为费米子铁磁性的对应物。

Abstract: We show how to perform exact diagonalization of SU($N$)-symmetric Bose-Hubbard models separately in each irreducible representation of SU($N$), by exploiting the Howe duality between U($L$) and U($N$). As applications, we first derive the finite-temperature phase diagram of attractive SU($N$) bosons on few-site rings at large $N$ for arbitrary filling. We then show the emergence of singlet-cluster states from frustrated hopping, the counterpart of fermionic ferromagnetism, with the example of the sawtooth and kagome lattice.

Abstract: Fermi surface transformation accompanied by quantum phase transition is widely observed in correlated electron systems including heavy fermion materials and van der Waals quantum heterostructures. The unified Anderson lattice description for this phenomenon suggests that their interacting ground states in the case of electron half filling always destabilize the metallic phase in the strong Kondo-coupling regime. We examine this issue within the paramagnetic phase of a Kondo lattice system with a heterotic layered structure. In the weak Kondo-coupling regime, the system exhibits a fractional Dirac liquid involving the massless or massive Dirac fermions and a charge neutral spinon Fermi surface. In the strong Kondo-coupling regime, the spinons hybridize with the Dirac fermions resulting in an intermediate nearly-flat hybridizing band together with the conduction and valence heavy fermion bands, yielding a large electric Fermi surface. Absence of the Kondo gap between these bands is observed depending on the stacking configurations of the layered structure. In particular, the system remains metallic even at the exact half filling in this regime and Luttinger's theorem is violated across the critical point. Implications of these results on the van der Waals quantum heterostructures are briefly discussed.

Abstract: Bloch oscillations typically arise in periodic systems subjected to a constant force generated by a potential gradient. Abstract: We first show that, for the SU(N) Heisenberg model with isotropic two-site interactions on an arbitrary lattice of Ns sites, the full spectrum in its singlet sector (when N divides Ns) is the negative of the full spectrum in its singlet sector of the SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg model defined on the same lattice. This theorem, valid for models with the fundamental irreducible representation (irrep) at each lattice site, follows naturally from a transposition operation in the basis of Standard Young Tableaux (SYTs). Second, this basis -- previously recognized as particularly convenient for exact diagonalization (ED) of SU(N) Heisenberg models -- admits an efficient, memory-saving implementation, thereby enabling large-scale diagonalizations for unprecedented system sizes Ns and numbers of colors N>3. We apply these ideas to an ED study of the SU(4) and SU(7) Heisenberg models on the triangular lattice (TL), extending the calculations up to Ns=28 sites. In particular, starting from the SU(4) case, whose nearest-neighbor ground state remains debated, we confirm that next-nearest-neighbor couplings stabilize four-sublattice long-range order. We further find that increasing the number of colors from N=4 to N=7 provides another route toward a N\'eel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings. N = 4 to N = 7 provides another route toward a N\'eel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings.

Abstract: Optical and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) experiments in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ have revealed narrow excitonic resonances near 1.5 eV whose microscopic origin remains under debate. In this work we study the momentum dependence of these excitations using the singlet-polaron theory (SPT) previously developed for NiPS$_3$. Within its framework, we calculate the spectral function of the corresponding effective model and extract the dispersion of the resulting quasiparticles. The theory naturally predicts two singlet excitations whose dynamics arise from their propagation in the zig-zag antiferromagnetic background ($S=1$) coupled to two-magnon processes. We show that the dispersion of the lowest-energy quasiparticle agrees very well with the excitonic dispersion measured by RIXS, reproducing both its overall shape and bandwidth along high-symmetry directions of the Brillouin zone. Our results provide a consistent interpretation of the optical excitations in terms of dispersive singlet polarons, and suggest that similar mechanisms may govern excitonic dynamics in other layered van der Waals antiferromagnets.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算非厄米多体算子的多重本征态对研究相互作用与开放量子系统的激发谱、谱性质及弛豫动力学十分重要，但本征矢量通常非正交，且希尔伯特空间维度随系统尺寸指数增长，使该任务极具挑战。

Method: 作者在MPS框架下推广GPLHR方法，以复平面中给定位移为中心求解多个目标右本征态；采用SRC加速算符应用；并在transcorrelated与复Hubbard链、相互作用非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链以及Liouvillian模型上进行测试。

Result: 测试结果表明，GPLHR结合MPS能够高效且准确地计算非厄米多体算子的多重本征态。

Conclusion: GPLHR与MPS的结合为非厄米多体算子多重本征态问题提供了一种高效、精确的求解途径，适用于相互作用和开放量子系统相关谱计算。

Abstract: Computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators plays an important role in investigating excitation spectra, spectral properties, and relaxation dynamics in interacting and open quantum systems. However, this task is challenging because the eigenvectors are generally nonorthogonal and the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with system size. Here, we formulate the generalized preconditioned locally harmonic residual (GPLHR) method in a matrix product state (MPS) framework to efficiently compute multiple targeted right eigenstates whose eigenvalues lie near a specified shift in the complex plane. Successive randomized compression (SRC) is employed to accelerate operator applications. We test the method on transcorrelated and complex Hubbard chains, an interacting non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain, and a Liouvillian model. The results demonstrate that GPLHR combined with MPS provides an efficient and accurate approach for computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators.

</details>


### [77] [Dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in rotating QED$_3$: critical flavor number and the occupation-reweighting channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15568)
*Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在刚性旋转圆盘上的(2+1)维QED中，零温旋转不改变动力学手征对称破缺临界味数；有限温旋转主要通过占据重加权通道强烈压低手征凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转效应对(2+1)维QED中动力学手征对称破缺及临界味数的影响，并为该模型建立有限温度下的基准结果。

Method: 在满足ΩR<1的刚性旋转圆盘上，采用彩虹Schwinger-Dyson框架；比较零温与有限温情形，并将旋转响应在线性化层面分解为占据重加权与屏蔽通道，同时用三种操作定义确定临界味数。

Result: 零温下凝聚的旋转响应恒为零，因此N_c^SD(Ω,T=0)=N_c^SD(0)=2.60±0.01；有限温下旋转压低凝聚，主导的占据重加权通道系数κ_occ=-1.55，在参考参数下贡献线性化总响应的≥99.995%，且自洽反馈将其放大26.5倍；屏蔽通道在固定正则化ε=0.01时κ_scr=-0.97，其ε→0行为对数发散；固定温度下crossover味数N*(Ω)单调下降；零温临界味数不受旋转影响（定理）。

Conclusion: 旋转在零温不改变临界味数，但在有限温通过占据重加权通道显著抑制手征凝聚；屏蔽通道对正则化敏感且在对数发散极限下需谨慎处理，这些结果共同给出该模型的有限温度参考。

Abstract: We study dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in $(2{+}1)$-dimensional QED on a rigidly rotating disc, $\Omega R<1$, within the rainbow Schwinger--Dyson framework. At zero temperature the rotation response of the condensate vanishes identically, so $N_c^{\rm SD}(\Omega,T{=}0)=N_c^{\rm SD}(0)=2.60\pm0.01$ (using three operational definitions). At finite temperature, rotation suppresses the condensate through a dominant occupation-reweighting channel, $\hat{\kappa}_{\rm occ}=-1.55$, accounting for $\ge 99.995\%$ of the linearized total response at the reference parameters, with self-consistent feedback amplifying it by $26.5$. The screening channel is reported at fixed regularization, $\hat{\kappa}_{\rm scr}(\varepsilon = 0.01)=-0.97$; its $\varepsilon \to 0$ behavior is logarithmically divergent. The crossover flavor $N^{*}(\Omega)$ falls monotonically at fixed temperature (a corollary). The critical flavor number is unchanged by rotation at $T=0$ (theorem). Together these results set the finite-temperature reference for the model.

</details>


### [78] [Single-Particle Spectroscopy and Cross-Barrier Dynamics in Inhomogeneous Luttinger Liquids via Unified Field Bosonization Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15608)
*Soundarya P,Venkata Suryanarayana M,Joy Prakash Das*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用统一场玻色化技术（UFBT）研究含局域杂质团簇的非均匀 Luttinger 液体，非微扰地导出动力学态密度与跨势垒隧穿电导的幂律标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解含局域杂质团簇和 forward-scattering 相互作用的非均匀 Luttinger 液体的单粒子性质，并避免对杂质强度或相互作用强度作微扰展开，同时无需显式重整化群处理。

Method: 采用 UFBT，基于费米子两点关联函数推导动力学态密度（DDOS）和跨势垒隧穿电导的领头奇异贡献；该方法适用于任意杂质强度，并可从关联函数直接得到特征标度行为。

Result: DDOS 在杂质团簇附近与远场呈现不同幂律行为；隧穿电导随温度呈幂律依赖：排斥相互作用下随降温减小，吸引相互作用下随降温增大，无相互作用时与温度无关。杂质团簇改变振幅和空间结构，但幂律指数仅由相互作用参数决定。弱相互作用极限可恢复隧穿响应的领头微扰修正。

Conclusion: UFBT 可作为描述非均匀 Luttinger 液体中局域谱性质和跨势垒输运的统一框架，其标度行为直接源于费米子关联函数，无需对杂质强度进行显式重整化群处理。

Abstract: The Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT) is employed to investigate the single-particle properties of inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids containing localized clusters of impurities in the presence of forward-scattering interactions. Within this framework, fermionic two-point correlation functions are used to derive the leading-order singular contributions to the dynamical density of states (DDOS) and the cross-barrier tunneling conductance. The results reveal distinct power-law behaviours of the DDOS near the impurity cluster and in the far-field regime. The tunneling conductance exhibits a power-law temperature dependence, decreasing with decreasing temperature for repulsive interactions and increasing for attractive interactions, while remaining temperature-independent in the noninteracting limit. The impurity cluster modifies the amplitudes and spatial structure of these observables, whereas their characteristic power-law exponents are determined solely by the interaction parameters. The formulation accommodates arbitrary impurity strengths without requiring perturbative expansions in the impurity or interaction strengths. The characteristic scaling behaviour emerges directly from the fermionic correlation functions, without an explicit renormalization-group (RG) treatment of the impurity strength, while the weak-interaction limit recovers the leading perturbative correction to the tunneling response. These findings establish UFBT as a unified framework for describing local spectral properties and cross-barrier transport in inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids.

</details>


### [79] [Variational Minimization of Almost Strong Zero Modes in Parafermionic Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15748)
*Niklas Tausendpfund,Matteo Rizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种量子启发的变分算法，用于在一般 parafermionic 链模型中搜索 almost strong zero modes；在非可积 Kitaev-Hubbard 链和手性 Potts 模型中复现精确结果并找到强束缚 parafermionic 算符，还验证了 Z3 手性 Potts 模型对称极限下存在真正的 strong zero mode。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足 Z_n 对称性的强关联费米子链可具有拓扑相和高度简并基态，其简并由 parafermionic zero modes 连接；这些模式在零温下严格保持，但有限能密度下相互作用会导致其失稳。对一般 Hamiltonian，求解其寿命通常需要完整谱或长时间演化，因此难以直接研究。

Method: 作者提出一种量子启发的变分算法，通过设计变分 ansatz 在一般 parafermionic 链模型中搜索 almost strong zero modes。该 ansatz 通过解析和数值方式验证可忠实复现精确结果，并应用于非可积 Kitaev-Hubbard 链与手性 Potts 模型。

Result: 该变分 ansatz 与精确结果一致，并在非可积 Kitaev-Hubbard 链和手性 Potts 模型中找到了强束缚 parafermionic 算符；进一步验证了 Z_3 parafermions 的手性 Potts 模型在对称极限下存在真正的 strong zero mode。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可行且可扩展的变分框架，用于研究有限能密度下相互作用对 parafermionic edge modes 的影响，可识别 almost strong zero modes 与 true strong zero modes，并适用于非可积 parafermionic 链模型。

Abstract: Strongly interacting fermionic chains obeying a $Z_n$ symmetry can host topological phases with highly degenerate ground-state manifolds. The different ground states are connected by strongly bound fractionalized edge modes called parafermionic zero modes. While exactly preserved under time evolution at zero temperature, interactions lead to a destabilization of these modes at finite energy densities, and, given a generic Hamiltonian, obtaining the lifetime of these modes is often impossible as it requires knowledge of the full spectrum of the Hamiltonian or long time evolutions. Here, we propose a novel quantum-inspired algorithm that can be used to variationally search for these almost strong zero modes in generic parafermionic chain models. We demonstrate analytically and numerically that our ansatz faithfully reproduces the exact results and finds strongly bound parafermionic operators in the non-integrable Kitaev-Hubbard chain and chiral Potts model. Our results further allow us to verify the existence of a true strong zero mode in the symmetrical limit of the chiral Potts model for $Z_3$-parafermions.

</details>

*Tobias Weber*

Method: 采用高性能、可移植的 C++ 编写；采用模块化架构，便于快速开发新计算模块并可嵌入外部应用；内置代码生成器实现与其他线性自旋波库的无缝互操作。

Result: 实现了全图形化界面、三维磁模型与磁振子色散关系可视化，支持开源分发、模块扩展、外部集成及跨库互操作。

Conclusion: Magpie 通过开源、模块化和互操作设计，为线性自旋波研究与可视化提供了可扩展的软件平台。

Abstract: Magpie is a fully graphical linear spin-wave software which has its main focus on 3-d visualisation of magnetic models and magnon dispersion relations. The software is distributed under an open-source license and is written in high-performance, portable C++. Its modular architecture allows for a rapid development of new calculation modules and permits the inclusion into external applications. Included code generators ensure seamless interoperability with other linear spin-wave libraries.

</details>


### [81] [Electronic Structure of a Delafossite Oxide CuAlO$_2$ in Comparison with CuCrO$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16061)
*Kenta Takahashi,Ryo Kato,Mario Okawa,Tetsuji Okuda,Akira Yasui,Eiji Ikenaga,Kanta Ono,Noriaki Hamada,Tomohiko Saitoh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合VUV/HX-PES与LDA+U计算比较CuAlO2和CuCrO2的电子结构，证实CuAlO2中Cu为+1价，并揭示CuCrO2中Cu2+卫星峰及Cu 3d,4s-Cr 3d电荷转移，强调空3d轨道的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清铜基delafossite氧化物中Cu的价态，特别是CuCrO2中因探测方法不同而出现的矛盾结果，并理解其电子结构。

Method: 采用真空紫外光电子能谱、硬X射线光电子能谱、Cu 3p-3d共振PES和Cu 2p芯能级HX-PES，结合LDA+U能带结构计算，对比CuAlO2与CuCrO2。

Result: CuAlO2中Cu为+1价；CuCrO2的Cu 2p HX-PES存在弱Cu2+卫星峰但无明显Cu2+主峰；价带HX-PES与计算显示价带中含显著Cu 4s及Al 3s/Cr 4s态。

Conclusion: Cu 3d,4s-Cr 3d电荷转移模型可合理解释上述结果，表明空3d轨道在铜基delafossite氧化物电子结构中起关键作用。

Abstract: We investigated the electronic structure of a delafossite oxide CuAlO$_2$ in comparison with CuCrO$_2$, using vacuum ultraviolet (VUV) and hard x-ray (HX) photoemission spectroscopy (PES) and band structure calculations within the local-density approximation +$U$ scheme. Cu 3$p\! -\! 3d$ resonant PES and Cu $2p$ core-level HX-PES give concrete evidence that the Cu valence in CuAlO$_2$ is monovalent, which makes a sharp contrast to CuCrO$_2$ that showed contradictory results depending upon probing methods [Yokobori \textit{et al.}, Phys. Rev. B \textbf{87}, 195124 (2013)]. We further noticed weak but detectable satellite structures due to Cu$^{2+}$ state in the Cu $2p$ HX-PES spectrum of CuCrO$_2$, surprisingly, without an observable main peak due to the Cu$^{2+}$ state. Comparisons between the valence-band HX-PES spectra and the band structure calculations revealed a significant amount of the Cu $4s$ and Al $3s$/Cr $4s$ states in the valence band. The above results can reasonably be explained by the Cu $3d, 4s-$Cr $3d$ charge transfer model that we previously proposed in the above paper, thereby demonstrating the critical role of empty $3d$ orbitals in the electronic structure of the copper delafossite oxides.

</details>


### [82] [Thermally Enhanced Pseudo-Goldstone Spin Gap in the Isotropic Helimagnet ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16439)
*N. D. Andriushin,A. Sanders,J. Reuther,D. C. Peets,P. Steffens,A. Hiess,J. Lass,L. Prodan,V. Tsurkan,V. Felea,D. S. Inosov*

TL;DR: 非弹性中子散射发现Heisenberg螺旋磁体ZnCr2Se4的赝戈德斯通能隙随温度非单调变化：在有序相大部分温区随升温增大，直至接近TN才闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认为磁有序系统中磁振子自旋能隙随升温减小并在长程有序转变处闭合；赝戈德斯通能隙虽常与自旋波理论非线性项相关，但其确切起源和性质仍存争议。

Method: 对Heisenberg螺旋磁体ZnCr2Se4进行非弹性中子散射测量，并与基于各向同性系统有效四阶势的自旋动力学模拟结果进行对比。

Result: ZnCr2Se4的赝戈德斯通能隙表现出 atypical 非单调温度依赖：在有序相大部分区域随温度升高而增大，随后在TN附近闭合；该结果与有效四阶势的自旋动力学模拟一致。

Conclusion: ZnCr2Se4中的赝戈德斯通自旋能隙在亚开尔文温度下由量子效应主导，其随升温的特征性增长源于热涨落，支持相关有效四阶势图像。

Abstract: In magnetically ordered systems, the spin gap in the magnon spectrum is normally expected to decrease on warming and close at the transition out of the long-range order. The presence of a pseudo-Goldstone gap is commonly associated with nonlinear terms in spin-wave theory. However, its precise origin and detailed properties can vary and remain under debate. Here we show by means of inelastic neutron scattering that the pseudo-Goldstone gap in the Heisenberg helimagnet ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$ exhibits an atypical, nonmonotonic temperature dependence: the gap increases over much of the ordered phase before closing near $T_\mathrm{N}$. These results align with recent spin-dynamic simulations based on an effective quartic potential in an isotropic system~[Phys. Rev. B \textbf{113}, L220411 (2026)]. As the pseudo-Goldstone spin gap in ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$ is dominated by quantum effects at subkelvin temperatures, its characteristic growth on warming is caused by thermal fluctuations.

</details>


### [83] [Scalable Quantum Monte Carlo for Shannon-R\'enyi Entropy: An Experiment-visitable Probe of Quantum Phases and Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16494)
*Dong-Xu Liu,Zhe Wang,Zenan Liu Yi-Ming Ding,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出Shannon-Rényi熵的场论时空复制流形与重加权退火量子蒙特卡洛方法，将计算复杂度从指数降为多项式，并建立其在量子临界点附近的有限尺寸标度理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon-Rényi熵可由冷原子单次测量快照直接获得，但在相互作用系统中理论计算代价指数级，且缺乏像纠缠熵那样作为量子临界性诊断的独立标度地位。

Method: 构造q个复制在虚时边界粘接的时空流形，将SR熵表示为广义配分函数之比以适于大规模量子蒙特卡洛；发展重加权退火框架，包括虚参数方案（通过二项加权部分粘接在未粘接与全粘接间插值）和实参数方案（在随机级数展开中以物理耦合常数为退火变量），二者均将代价从指数降至多项式。

Result: 建立SR熵在量子临界点附近的有限尺寸标度理论：S_q=a(g)L^2+S_q^0((g-g_c)L^{1/ν})；其一阶导数数据坍缩给出O(3)与Ising普适类的关联长度指数ν，分别在spin-1/2柱状二聚化Heisenberg模型和双层Heisenberg-Ising模型中实现；同一模拟从次领头项提取Goldstone模式数N_G=2与Z_2基态简并ln 2。

Conclusion: 将SR熵提升为无需序参量的量子临界性诊断量，具有与纠缠熵同等的标度地位，并能对最先进量子模拟器的实验快照进行定量基准测试，从而闭合理论与量子模拟之间的循环。

Abstract: The Shannon-R\'enyi (SR) entropy characterizes the probability distribution of many-body wave functions in a chosen basis and is directly accessible from single-shot measurement snapshots in cold-atom experiments, yet its theoretical evaluation in interacting systems carries exponential cost. Here we construct a field-theoretic spacetime manifold for the SR entropy in which $q$ replicas are glued at the imaginary-time boundaries, recasting it as a ratio of generalized partition functions amenable to large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulation. On this basis we develop a reweight-annealing framework with two complementary strategies: a virtual-parameter scheme interpolating between unglued and fully glued limits via binomial-weighted partial gluing, and a real-parameter scheme using a physical coupling constant as the annealing variable within stochastic series expansion. Both reduce the computational cost from exponential to polynomial. Most importantly, we establish for the first time a finite-size scaling theory for the SR entropy near a quantum critical point, $S_q=a(g)L^2+S_q^0\left((g-g_c)L^{1/\nu}\right)$; data collapse of its first derivative yields the correlation-length exponent $\nu$ for both the O(3) and Ising universality classes, realized in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ columnar dimerized Heisenberg model and the bilayer Heisenberg-Ising model. The same simulations extract the Goldstone-mode count $N_G=2$ and the $Z_2$ ground-state degeneracy $\ln 2$ from subleading terms. These results elevate the SR entropy to a standalone, order-parameter-free diagnostic of quantum criticality with the same scaling status as the entanglement entropy, and close the loop between theory and quantum simulation by enabling quantitative benchmarking of experimental snapshots from state-of-the-art quantum simulators.

</details>


### [84] [Topological Chiral Superconductivity from Doping Correlated Charge Transfer Insulator with Orbital Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16674)
*Wen Sun,Yuan Yang,Shou-Shu Gong,Wen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过微扰分析、精确对角化和DMRG，在强关联无自旋费米子模型中发现，掺杂电荷转移绝缘体可产生具有p+ip对称性的拓扑手性超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑手性超导通常从动量空间电子结构角度研究，而强关联体系中其实空间涌现机制仍很不清楚。

Method: 研究三子格、每原胞2π磁通、强排斥无自旋费米子模型，并结合微扰分析、精确对角化与密度矩阵重正化群数值模拟。

Result: 确立了p+ip拓扑超导基态；在大电荷转移带隙极限下，手性电荷转移激发产生关联跃迁过程，为电子配对及其手性结构提供微观机制；手性超导可延续到较小带隙区域，并可能出现手性激子双粒子束缚态。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了通过掺杂具有轨道磁性的强关联电荷转移绝缘体来实现拓扑手性超导的具体实空间路径。

Abstract: Topological chiral superconductivity has been extensively studied from the perspective of electronic structures in momentum space, while its emergence from real-space processes in strongly interacting systems remains largely unexplored. Here, we study a doped charge-transfer insulator in a strongly repulsive spinless-fermion model with three sublattices and $2\pi$ flux per unit cell. Combining perturbative analysis, exact diagonalization, and density matrix renormalization group simulations, we establish a topological superconducting ground state with $p+ip$ symmetry. In the large charge-transfer gap limit, chiral charge-transfer excitations generate a correlated-hopping process that provides a microscopic mechanism for electron pairing and its chiral structure. The chiral superconductivity persists into the smaller-gap regime, where chiral excitonic two-particle bound state can emerge. Our results establish a concrete real-space route to topological chiral superconductivity upon doping strongly interacting charge-transfer insulators with orbital magnetism.

</details>


### [85] [Confinement-deconfinement transitions in the dimer-loop models on two-dimensional non-bipartite lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16676)
*Jay Pandey,Souvik Kundu,Kedar Damle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明，完全填充的 dimer-loop 模型在 Kagome、三角等非二分格子上也可存在拓扑 confinement 相变；两相均为短程关联液体，由 Z2 绕数区分，且相变仍属 Ising 普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在二分蜂窝等格子的 dimer-loop 模型中，两个 Coulomb 相由无散度极化场的 U(1) 通量及其 Z2 confinement 相变描述；本文旨在探讨无守恒 U(1) 通量的非二分格子上，此类拓扑相变是否仍能出现，并联系 S=1 阻挫磁体。

Abstract: Recent work [Kundu, Damle, Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025)] has demonstrated that a class of $S=1$ kagome lattice antiferromagnets with competing anisotropies can exhibit unusual low-temperature behavior that is best described in terms of the statistical mechanics of an interesting interpolation between the fully-packed dimer model and the fully-packed loop model on the bipartite honeycomb lattice. On this and other bipartite lattices in two and three dimension, such fully-packed dimer-loop models generically have two distinct power-law correlated Coulomb phases, both of which admit a low-energy description in terms of a fluctuating divergence-free polarization vector field [Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025); arXiv:2510.11607]. The transition between these phases corresponds to a confinement transition for the $Z_2$ part of the corresponding $U(1)$ flux [Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025); arXiv:2510.11607]. Here we demonstrate that a variant of such a topological confinement transition can also exist in dimer-loop models on non-bipartite lattices such as the kagome and triangular lattices although the low-energy description does not feature any conserved $U(1)$ flux. In these non-bipartite dimer-loop models, both phases are short-range correlated liquids, and the topological distinction between them is in terms of a $Z_2$-valued winding number instead of the flux of a divergence-free polarization field. In spite of this key difference, this transition appears to be in the same Ising universality class as the analogous transition of the bipartite dimer-loop models. These kagome and triangular lattice dimer-loop models correspond respectively to the low-energy limit of a class of $S=1$ antiferromagnets with competing anisotropies on the ruby lattice comprising a corner-sharing arrangement of fully-frustrated squares and a lattice of corner-sharing fully-frustrated hexagons.

Abstract: We investigate quantum criticality in the transverse-field Ising model on finite Cayley trees and demonstrate that their extensive boundary fundamentally changes the nature of the quantum phase transition compared with the infinite, boundary-free Bethe lattice. Using tree tensor-network simulations for systems with up to $N=12286$ sites, we identify a boundary-driven continuous transition at $h_x^c\approx1.76$, distinct from the mean-field Bethe-lattice transition at $h_x^c\approx2.23$. We characterize this transition through finite-size scaling of the magnetization and magnetic susceptibility and extract a consistent set of critical exponents. By introducing next-nearest-neighbor interactions, we demonstrate the robustness of the extracted critical exponents, providing numerical argument for the Ising boundary universality class on the Cayley tree. Furthermore, we show that the physics of the boundary-free Bethe lattice can be efficiently recovered on a finite Cayley tree by fine-tuning a local boundary field. The resulting \textit{effective Bethe line}, whose existence is tied to the mean-field nature of the bulk transition, effectively screens the extensive boundary and restores both the critical point and the mean-field critical scaling of the infinite Bethe lattice.

</details>

Magnetometry and specific heat reveal a long-range magnetic ordering transition at $T_\textrm{N} = 3.5$~K and a field-induced transition at $\mu_{0}$H$_\textrm{c}$ = 1 T. This zero-field long-range magnetic order was determined to be a commensurate $\kappa = 0$ order with an ordered moment that is reduced from the full $S = 1/2$ magnetic moment by 30%. ### [89] [Mutual information across the commensurate--incommensurate crossover and near the Kosterlitz--Thouless point of the spin-$\tfrac12$ $J_1$--$J_2$ Heisenberg chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16915)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 互信息的额外价值主要在块互信息及SU(2)通道结构中，而非两站点量；标量通道揭示键能关联并可作为相区和临界行为的敏感诊断。结果支持J1-J2链中二聚化、KT点、无序点和Lifshitz点附近的场论描述。

Abstract: What quantum mutual information adds to conventional correlation functions is often unclear. We study the spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg chain, using DMRG on infinite chains and periodic rings and an SU(2) decomposition of the mutual information into correlation channels. For singlet ground states the two-site mutual information is an exact function of the spin correlator, so any additional content must come from blocks. In the dimerized phase, blocks resolve bond-energy correlations in the SU(2)-scalar channel without an independent asymptotic length: singlet and triplet correlation lengths agree to about 1%. The scalar channel dominates block mutual information at large distances in the commensurate regime up to $J_2/J_1=0.40$, but falls to a few percent beyond the disorder point, which coincides numerically with the Majumdar-Ghosh point $J_2/J_1=1/2$; the structure-factor peak splits only at the Lifshitz point $J_2/J_1=0.520622(2)$. On the gapless side we compare block mutual information on rings extrapolated to infinite size with the two-interval prediction of SU(2)$_1$ conformal field theory across the Kosterlitz-Thouless point $J_2/J_1=0.241167$. There, for block lengths $l\le6$, the correction decays as $l^{-p}$ with $p=1.96$-$2.07$, and any logarithmic term is at least 60 times smaller than in the Heisenberg chain. Expanding in the marginal coupling (from level spectroscopy) the first-order coefficient is consistent with $l^{-2}$ scaling and the second-order coefficient is positive for $x\le1/4$, suggesting that the mutual information relative to the field-theory value becomes stationary at the KT point for large blocks. The scalar-channel share approaches 1/4 at small cross ratio $x$, as expected from SO(4) symmetry; under two stated assumptions its first-order response to the coupling is $(3/8)\ln x-5/8$ at small $x$, matched by the $l=4$ data within 1-3% at $x\le1/9$.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids are exotic states of matter in which strongly interacting spins remain disordered down to zero temperature and host fractionalized excitations. This has motivated an extensive search for their realization in materials. Among candidate systems, $\alpha$-RuCl$_3$ has drawn particular attention as a platform for the quantum spin liquid proposed by Kitaev, in which the low-energy excitations in applied magnetic fields are interpreted as itinerant Majorana fermions and local flux excitations. However, conflicting experimental signatures have cast doubt on whether a Kitaev quantum spin liquid-like state is really the right starting point to interpret the low-energy states in $\alpha$-RuCl$_3$, motivating a closer look at the nature of these excitations. Here we report an extensive high field study of specific heat up to 35 T on $\alpha$-RuCl$_3$. We find that the low-energy thermodynamics exhibit universal $B/T$ scaling with a single effective energy scale proportional to the applied field as long as the magnetic field is not close to the $c$-axis. This behavior persists continuously from the antiferromagnetic phase boundary to the highest fields, suggesting that the perturbative Kitaev limit, with its Majorana and flux excitations, may not be an appropriate starting point for describing the low-energy thermodynamics of $\alpha$-RuCl$_3$ for fields away from the $c$-axis.

</details>

Abstract: The acoustic Faraday effect probes phonon Hall viscosity through the rotation of transverse acoustic polarization, conventionally interpreted in terms of circular birefringence. We show that magnetic order breaking rotational symmetry generates anisotropic phonon responses and elliptically polarized eigenmodes, so that Faraday rotation cannot be attributed to Hall viscosity alone. To elucidate their microscopic origin and consequences, we derive spin-phonon couplings from a generic $J$-$K$-$\Gamma$-$\Gamma'$ model with interlayer coupling, identifying transverse strain couplings for propagation along the $c$-axis arising from the interplay of Kitaev $(K)$ and $\Gamma$ interactions, along with strain-induced $g$-tensor contributions. Incorporating these couplings into our theory for the Kitaev spin-liquid candidate $\alpha$-RuCl$_3$, we find that symmetry breaking modifies the Faraday response, revealing a Hall viscosity approximately twice the value inferred assuming rotational symmetry. Our results establish a microscopic framework beyond circular birefringence, refining the connection between acoustic Faraday rotation and phonon Hall viscosity in symmetry-broken spin-orbit-coupled magnets.

Abstract: Two-dimensional materials can host topological phases stabilized either by an intrinsic moir\'e potential or topological crystallization. Crucial to the topology of these phases is the interplay between the (self-consistently generated) potential and the spinor structure, which imbues the band-projected potential with momentum-dependence. Truncating this potential at linear-order in momentum, we obtain a three-band model that captures the low-energy band structure and Berry curvature of such topological bands. We analytically find that the Berry curvature strongly depends on momentum-dependence of the potential. Furthermore, we identify that the model can be tuned to have emergent symmetries, including $U(1)$ and anti-unitary symmetries. Emergent-symmetry breaking perturbations drive the system to regimes hosting topological flat bands, quadratic band touchings, and multiple Dirac cones. We recover many of these features when studying an un-truncated model of a $\lambda$-jellium spinor inside of a periodic potential. *Hannes Braun,Dumitru C\u{a}lug\u{a}ru,Pietro M. Bonetti,Haoyu Hu,Yan Zhang,Yi Jiang,Quansheng Wu,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,B. Andrei Bernevig,Michael M. Scherer,Laura Classen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究扭转角3.65°、空穴填充ν=-1的扭转双层WSe2关联电子相，发现近邻谷间相干自旋密度波存在混合d波单态与p波三重态超导，并指出谷间相干自旋涨落是配对胶水；连续模型中的门屏蔽库仑相互作用可使配对延伸至零位移场，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在扭转双层WSe2中观察到超导性，且其可存在于从关联绝缘体到零位移场的较宽位移场范围内，因此需要从理论上分析该体系的关联电子相与超导不稳定性。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock、随机相位近似和泛函重整化群计算；从第一性原理推导含门屏蔽库仑相互作用的连续模型，并与含Hubbard相互作用的现实三轨道Wannier模型及人工单轨道Hubbard模型的失稳行为进行比较。

Result: 在位移场和相互作用强度构成的相图中，两种模型均出现混合d波单态和p波三重态超导不稳定性，且邻近谷间相干自旋密度波序；配对机制分析表明谷间相干自旋涨落起配对胶水作用。配对区域范围与临界温度依赖模型输入，差异可追溯到投影相互作用中的相互作用程。

Conclusion: 在连续模型中，门屏蔽库仑相互作用可实现一直延伸到零位移场的配对，从而使理论与实验观测一致。

Abstract: Motivated by the observation of superconductivity in twisted bilayer WSe$_2$ over an extended range of displacement fields, reaching from the correlated insulator down to zero field, we analyze the correlated electron phases of this system at hole filling $\nu = -1$ and twist angle $3.65^\circ$ using Hartree--Fock, random phase approximation, and functional renormalization group calculations. We derive a continuum model with gate-screened Coulomb interaction from first principles and compare its instabilities to those of a realistic three-orbital Wannier model with a Hubbard interaction and an artificial single-orbital Hubbard model. In both cases, we find a superconducting instability of mixed $d$-wave singlet and $p$-wave triplet character, arising adjacent to inter-valley-coherent spin-density-wave order in the phase diagram spanned by displacement field and interaction strength. Our additional analysis of the pairing mechanism points to inter-valley coherent spin fluctuations as the pairing glue. The extent of the pairing regime and the associated critical temperatures depend on the model input, and we trace differences to the interaction range entering the projected interaction. Within the continuum model, the gate-screened Coulomb interaction yields pairing down to zero displacement field, bringing theory into agreement with experiment.

</details>


### [94] [Conformal soft modes at elastic instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17920)
*Lars Franke,Nick Sander,Markus Garst*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 晶体接近连续弹性相变时，临界涨落在弹性理论中表现为类共形 Killing 方程的位移模式，并与共形变换及极端力学行为相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解接近连续弹性相变时晶体临界涨落的理论结构，并探索其对临界弹性现象和极端机械超材料的意义。

Method: 在线性弹性理论层面分析临界模式的位移矢量，建立其与共形 Killing 方程类似的条件，并区分同构不稳定性与晶体对称破缺不稳定性。

Result: 临界模式位移满足类共形 Killing 方程；同构不稳定性出现四个临界模式，包括一个伸缩和三个特殊共形变换；对称破缺不稳定性可包含连续模式，即使晶体尺寸有限，某些情形还对应二维闵可夫斯基空间的共形变换。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明共形变换结构在临界弹性涨落中起关键作用，并对临界弹性现象及极端机械超材料具有启示。

Abstract: In case a crystal is close to a continuous elastic phase transition, pronounced critical fluctuations emerge. We show that on the level of linear elasticity theory the displacement vector for these critical modes obeys a condition reminiscent of the conformal Killing equation. For an isostructural instability, four critical modes arise corresponding to one dilation and three special conformal transformations. At crystal symmetry-breaking instabilities, the critical fluctuations include a continuum of modes even for crystals with finite size. For certain cases, these comprise the conformal transformations associated with two-dimensional Minkowski space. These results have implications for critical elastic phenomena as well as for extremal mechanical metamaterials.

</details>


### [95] [A hierarchy of exact lower bounds on the Laughlin spectral gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18854)
*Michael G. Scheer,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出 bootstrap/SDP 方法，给出球面上第一 Haldane 赝势哈密顿量谱隙的严格下界，涵盖任意 N_Phi 及热力学极限，并建立将数值 SDP 界转化为严格证明的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 Laughlin 等分数量子霍尔模型波函数对应的赝势哈密顿量提供谱隙下界，特别是热力学极限下的严格证明；并推广到 Moore-Read/Read-Rezayi 等模型。

Method: 基于 Knabe 判据：基态能量为 0 时，谱隙 >= epsilon 等价于 H^2 >= epsilon H。将 H^2 分成可解析界定的部分和可由有限半定规划（SDP）界定的部分；SDP 规模仅由截断参数决定，对 N_Phi 只参数依赖。为严格化数值界，提出从大型有理 SDP 构造近乎最优精确可行点的算法；另提出证明 N_Phi->infty 时 SDP 秩稳定性的算法以认证热力学极限。

Result: 获得任意 N_Phi 及热力学极限下第一 Haldane 赝势（Laughlin 态）谱隙的数值精确（至求解器精度）下界，并可转化为数学严格下界；同时证明所需 SDP 的秩稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了 Laughlin 谱隙，并为其他赝势（如 Moore-Read/Read-Rezayi）提供潜在谱隙下界方法；更一般地，给出将数值半定规划界转化为严格数学证明的通用方法，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We develop a bootstrap approach to deriving lower bounds on the spectral gap of the first Haldane pseudopotential Hamiltonian on the sphere with $N_\Phi$ flux quanta for arbitrary $N_\Phi$, including in the thermodynamic limit $N_\Phi \to \infty$. Our approach can in principle be generalized to pseudopotentials describing other model wavefunctions such as the Moore-Read state or Read-Rezayi sequence. Our approach is based on Knabe's observation that if $H$ is a Hamiltonian with ground state energy $0$ then the spectral gap is at least $\epsilon > 0$ if and only if $H^2 \succeq \epsilon H$. To show this inequality, we split $H^2$ into two parts, one that can be analytically bounded and another that can be bounded using a finite semidefinite program (SDP). Crucially, the size of this program is determined only by cutoff parameters, and the program depends on $N_\Phi$ only parametrically, allowing us to obtain numerically exact (up to solver precision) lower bounds on the gap. In order to turn these numerical bounds into mathematically rigorous bounds, we introduce an algorithm that can produce nearly-optimal exact feasible points for large rational SDPs. We additionally provide an algorithm that establishes the rank stability of the semidefinite program in the limit $N_\Phi \to \infty$ which is needed to certify the bound in the thermodynamic limit. Beyond establishing the Laughlin gap and revealing a potential method for deriving spectral gap lower bounds for other pseudopotentials, our work provides a general method for turning numerical semidefinite programming bounds into rigorous mathematical proofs, which may have wide applicability.

</details>

### [96] [Molecule-intercalated van der Waals Josephson junctions uncover complex multiband superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16483)
*Daniel Margineda,David Caldevilla-Asenjo,Covadonga \'Alvarez-Garc\'ia,Daniel Tezze,Raul Arenal,Maxim Ilyn,Celia Rogero,Luis E. Hueso,Marco Gobbi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过分子插层TaS2/NbSe2异质结构建混合分子约瑟夫森结，观察到非对称Fraunhofer干涉以及场致和自发约瑟夫森二极管效应，表明分子插层可调控超导内部相位并揭示非常规超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子插层是调控范德华超导体电子结构的有效途径，但插层化合物很少被集成到相位敏感量子器件中；本文旨在将分子插层超导体用于约瑟夫森器件并探测其相位结构。

Method: 制备基于TaS2/NbSe2异质结的混合分子约瑟夫森结；对TaS2电极进行选择性插层以诱导单层样Ising超导；测量约瑟夫森干涉、磁输运及二极管效应，并比较电极堆叠反转和下层NbSe2插层后的行为。

Result: 器件表现出非对称Fraunhofer干涉、场致约瑟夫森二极管效应和自发约瑟夫森二极管效应；零场二极管效应在反转电极堆叠后消失，并在插层下层NbSe2电极后重新出现。这些结果与时间反演对称破缺的多带超导态一致，其特征是不同超导凝聚体之间存在非平庸相对相位。

Conclusion: 混合分子约瑟夫森结可作为相位敏感量子器件平台，用于研究和集成分子工程超导体，并为发现与调控非常规超导相位提供机会。

Abstract: Intercalation of molecular species provides a route to tailor the electronic structure in van der Waals superconductors, yet intercalated compounds have rarely been integrated into phase-sensitive quantum devices. Here, we realize hybrid molecular Josephson junctions based on TaS2/NbSe2 heterostructures. Selective intercalation of the TaS2 electrode induces monolayer-like Ising superconductivity, while the junctions exhibit asymmetric Fraunhofer interference, field-induced and spontaneous Josephson diode effects. The zero-field diode disappears upon reversing the electrode stacking and re-emerges after intercalating the lower NbSe2 electrode. These findings indicate that the hybrid superconducting electrode hosts unconventional superconductivity underlying the nonreciprocal transport, while molecular intercalation acts as a tuning knob for its internal phase structure. This behavior is consistent with a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking multiband superconducting state characterized by a nontrivial relative phase between superconducting condensates. Our results establish hybrid molecular Josephson junctions as a quantum-device platform for phase-sensitive investigation and integration of molecularly engineered superconductors, opening opportunities to uncover and engineer unconventional superconducting phases.

</details>


### [97] [Twofold Symmetry Observed in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe Interfacial Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.05904)
*Xinru Han,Hailang Qin,Tianluo Pan,Bin Guo,Kaige Shi,Zijin Huang,Jie Jiang,Hangyu Yin,Hongtao He,Fei Ye,Wei-Qiang Chen,Jia-Wei Mei,Gan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Bi2Te3/FeTe界面超导体中观测到二重超导能隙对称性，且该对称性与FeTe的Te-Te方向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导配对对称性对理解超导微观机理至关重要；本文旨在探究Bi2Te3/FeTe界面超导体的配对对称性及其与拓扑性质的联系。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜中的准粒子干涉(QPI)测量和宏观磁阻测量，分析能隙内外的QPI图样，并进行面内角度依赖磁阻和面内上临界场测量。

Result: QPI图样显示能隙内外的超导能隙明显各向异性；面内磁阻和面内上临界场均表现出清晰的二重对称性；对称方向与FeTe中的Te-Te方向一致，削弱了其由双共线反铁磁序产生的可能性。

Conclusion: 该发现为理解Bi2Te3/FeTe超导体系的拓扑性质提供关键信息，并推动对其配对机制的理论研究。

Abstract: Superconducting pairing symmetry are crucial in understanding the microscopic superconducting mechanism of a superconductor. Here we report the observation of a twofold superconducting gap symmetry in an interfacial superconductor Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe, by employing quasiparticle interference (QPI) technique in scanning tunneling microscopy and macroscopic magnetoresistance measurements. The QPI patterns corresponding to energies inside and outside the gap reveal a clear anisotropic superconducting gap. Furthermore, both the in-plane angle-dependent magnetoresistance and in-plane upper critical field exhibit a clear twofold symmetry. This twofold symmetry align with the Te-Te direction in FeTe, which weakens the possible generation by bi-collinear antiferromagnetism order. Our finding provides key information in further understanding of the topological properties in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe superconducting system and propels further theoretical interests in the paring mechanism in the system.

</details>


### [98] [Superconductivity from altermagnetic fluctuations: the crucial role of sublattice polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16507)
*Mats Barkman,Andreas Kreisel,Brian M. Andersen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在未进入磁有序的交替磁涨落体系中，正常态能带的子格极化（SLP）会禁止跨子格库珀对，从而抑制最近邻反铁磁交换介导的自旋单态配对，并让同子格长程键主导；当次近邻交换为铁磁时产生自旋三重态超导，长程相互作用偏向单态时则产生特征性单态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联体系中的非常规超导常出现在磁有序态附近；许多交替磁候选材料并未跨越磁不稳定性，因此需要理解其涨落（而非长程奈尔序）如何驱动超导。

Method: 研究交替磁涨落诱导的超导，重点考察正常态能带的子格极化（SLP）对配对通道的影响，并分析最近邻、次近邻及更长程交换相互作用下的库珀对形成。

Result: SLP作为交替磁能带的固有属性，即使无磁序也存在，它阻止不同子格间形成库珀对；这抑制最近邻反铁磁交换介导的自旋单态配对，使同子格长程键占优。对于无阻挫奈尔序预期的次近邻铁磁交换，体系倾向自旋三重态超导；该机制不同于长程奈尔序交替磁中由自旋劈裂费米面驱动的等自旋配对。若无序相的长程相互作用偏向单态，SLP则导致具有独特性质的单态配对态。

Conclusion: 子格极化是决定交替磁涨落超导配对对称性的关键因素：它区分了短程涨落与长程奈尔序机制，并可导致自旋三重态或特征性自旋单态超导。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity in correlated systems often appears in the vicinity of a magnetically ordered state. This situation is also relevant for altermagnets, characterized by a nonrelativistic spin-split electronic structure in the presence of N\'eel order. Many proposed altermagnetic candidates, however, never cross the magnetic instability. We study superconductivity arising from altermagnetic fluctuations in such systems and show that the sublattice polarization (SLP) of the normal-state bands plays a pivotal role in determining the superconducting state. SLP, an inherent property of altermagnetic band structures that persists even without magnetic order, prevents Cooper pairs from forming between different sublattices. This suppresses pairing mediated by the nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, which favors spin-singlet superconductivity, and leaves longer-range same-sublattice bonds dominant. When the next-nearest-neighbor exchange is ferromagnetic, as expected for frustration-free N\'eel order, this results in spin-triplet superconductivity. This mechanism is fundamentally different from that in altermagnets with long-range N\'eel order, where the spin-split Fermi surfaces favor equal-spin pairing. Finally, when longer-range interactions in the disordered phase instead favor singlet pairing, SLP leads to singlet states with characteristic properties distinct from nearest-neighbor pairing states.

</details>


### [99] [High-Efficiency Field-Free Superconducting Diodes in Mo$_2$N/FePt Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16972)
*Juan S. Cruz,Emilio De Biasi,Juan C. Zapata,Yeonkyu Lee,Jeehoon Kim,Nestor Haberkorn*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在室温生长的 Mo2N/FePt 双层中实现了近理想、无外场的超导二极管效应，二极管效率最高约 85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用铁磁条状畴的剩余杂散场来调控超导边缘磁通进入与涡旋运动，从而实现高效、可调的无外场超导二极管。

Method: 制备 Mo2N/FePt 双层并进行输运测量；结合微磁模拟分析 FePt 条状畴的周期杂散场调制及桥边缘末端的不对称场增强；通过面内磁化角度和反向磁场扫描调控非互易性。

Result: 观察到极性依赖的磁通进入和涡旋运动阈值：一个电流极性在接近估计去配对电流的较大比例下仍近乎无耗散，相反极性在更低电流下出现耗散；较薄双层中二极管效率可达约 85%；非互易性可随面内磁化角度连续调谐，并可通过 FePt 层磁化反转可逆开关。

Conclusion: 边缘控制的磁通进入与涡旋运动是实现高效率和可调控超导二极管的有效途径。

Abstract: Near-ideal, field-free superconducting diode behavior is demonstrated in room-temperature-grown Mo$_2$N/FePt bilayers. The response is consistent with remanent FePt stripe domains producing polarity-dependent thresholds for flux entry and vortex motion. Micromagnetic simulations reveal a periodic stray-field modulation across the stripes and asymmetric field enhancement near their ends at the bridge edges. One current polarity remains nearly dissipationless up to a sizable fraction of the estimated depairing current, whereas the opposite polarity develops dissipation at much lower current, yielding diode efficiencies up to approximately 85% in the thinner bilayer. The nonreciprocity can be continuously tuned by the in-plane magnetic preparation angle and reversibly switched by reverse-field excursions through the hysteretic reversal of the FePt layer. These results support edge-controlled flux entry and vortex motion as a route to high-efficiency superconducting diodes.

</details>


### [100] [Observation of stacked vortex structure with number controllable Majorana zero mode in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterojunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17701)
*Xinru Han,Zhaokun Luo,Quanxin Hu,Xingjian Zhu,Xiaohong Pan,Dan Mu,Xinyu Liang,Zulin Xu,Qiheng Wang,Ryan L. Kong,Mingyuan Li,Zhengliang Zhang,Fazhi Yang,Mojun Pan,Xin Liu,Qiangqiang Gu,Baiqing Lv,Gan Wang,Hong Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在1QL-Bi2Te3/FeTe异质结中，低温STM/STS显示莫尔调制的超导能隙与堆叠磁涡旋，并观测到数量可由磁场和Bi2Te3岛尺寸调控的马约拉纳零能模，确立了可控Majorana平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固态器件中创建并调控马约拉纳零能模是实现容错拓扑量子计算的关键挑战。

Method: 采用低温扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）研究化学计量比的一个五重层（1QL）Bi2Te3/FeTe异质结及超导岛中的磁涡旋，探测莫尔超晶格、超导能隙调制与涡旋束缚态。

Result: 观测到晶格失配导致的长周期莫尔超晶格及其对超导能隙的纳米尺度调制；磁涡旋具有堆叠核心，并出现两层派生的束缚态分支：远离涡旋中心演化为X形色散分支，以及量子极限分支，表现为空间未分裂的MZMs和离散整数量子化Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon态。所有测量涡旋中均观测到MZMs，其数量可由磁场和Bi2Te3岛尺寸调控。

Conclusion: 化学计量比1QL-Bi2Te3/FeTe可作为图案化超导界面，实现孤立和少涡旋Majorana构型，为融合MZMs及拓扑量子计算提供可控平台。

Abstract: Creation and control of Majorana zero modes (MZMs) in solid devices is the key challenge in realizing fault tolerant topological quantum computation. Here we report spectroscopic evidence for number controllable MZMs in stoichiometric one quintuple layer Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterostructures, together with the observation of stacked vortices in superconducting islands. Low temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy reveal a long period moire superlattice arising from lattice mismatch, which induces nanoscale modulation of superconducting gaps. Magnetic vortices exhibit a stacked core structure with two layer derived bound state branches: a dispersive branch evolving into an X-shaped pattern away from the vortex center, and a quantum-limit branch characterized by a spatially unsplit MZMs together with discrete integer quantized Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon states. Notably, MZMs are observed in all measured vortices, and their number can be tuned by magnetic field and the size of Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$ islands. These results establish stoichiometric 1QL-Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe as a patterned superconducting interface that enables isolated and few-vortex Majorana configurations, providing a controlled platform for fuse MZMs.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Geometry and Pair Breaking in Intra- and Intervalley Superconductivity of Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14480)
*Louis Primeau,Yang Zhang,Shi-Zeng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在菱面体四层石墨烯中，采用DFT拟合多轨道紧束缚模型和Kohn-Luttinger理论，发现Bloch形状因子促进谷内p_x-ip_y超导并提高最大Tc，但三角翘曲导致配对失配，限制其相图范围；主导不稳定性出现在配对Lifshitz转变附近及环形/三瓣费米面区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体石墨烯中从自旋/谷极化正常态涌现超导，但谷内库珀对因三角翘曲而缺乏简并性和粒子-粒子嵌套，常规Cooper不稳定性不适用；已有模型提示能带几何增强手性配对，但其与真实能带诱导配对破缺的竞争尚不清楚。

Method: 使用DFT拟合的多轨道紧束缚模型，研究电子掺杂菱面体四层石墨烯的Kohn-Luttinger超导；在RPA屏蔽和投影Cooper相互作用中保留完整Bloch波函数形状因子，并将超导不稳定性分解为形状因子修饰的对称分辨相互作用、由对称化配对色散决定的Cooper对相空间，以及三角翘曲产生的能量失配三部分。

Result: Bloch形状因子强烈偏好谷内配对，主导为p_x-ip_y不稳定性，最大Tc显著高于谷间参考态；三角翘曲诱导的失配截断Cooper对数，使谷内超导局限于更窄相区；主导不稳定性出现在配对Lifshitz转变附近，主要位于重构的环形或三瓣费米面区域。

Conclusion: 菱面体石墨烯超导由量子几何修饰相互作用、配对费米学和三角翘曲诱导的配对破缺共同控制。

Abstract: The observation of superconductivity emerging from a spin- and valley-polarized normal state in rhombohedral graphene raises a basic puzzle: intravalley Cooper pairs involve states that are not generically degenerate because of trigonal warping, and therefore lack the particle--particle nesting underlying the conventional Cooper instability. While recent model studies suggest that nontrivial band geometry can strongly promote chiral pairing, how this geometric enhancement competes with realistic band-structure-induced pair breaking remains unclear. Here we study Kohn--Luttinger superconductivity in electron-doped rhombohedral tetralayer graphene using a DFT-fitted multiorbital tight-binding model, retaining the full Bloch-wave-function form factors in both RPA screening and the projected Cooper interaction. We separate the superconducting instability into three contributions: the symmetry-resolved form-factor-dressed interaction, the Cooper-pair phase space determined by the symmetrized pair dispersion, and the energy mismatch generated by trigonal warping. We find that the Bloch form factors strongly favor intravalley pairing, with a leading predominantly $p_x-ip_y$ instability and a substantially larger maximum $T_c$ than the intervalley reference state. At the same time, trigonal-warping-induced mismatch cuts off the Cooper logarithm and confines intravalley superconductivity to a much narrower region of the phase diagram. The dominant instability occurs near pair-Lifshitz transitions and primarily in reconstructed annular or three-lobed Fermi-surface regimes. Our results show that superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene is controlled by the combined effects of quantum-geometry-dressed interactions, pair fermiology, and trigonal-warping-induced pair breaking.

</details>


### [102] [Photon echo from Maki-Thompson interference in a fluctuating s-wave superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16462)
*Hui Zhou,Yuan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 理论研究了双太赫兹脉冲驱动下涨落s波超导体的非线性光学响应，发现Maki-Thompson过程会产生光子回波，而Aslamazov-Larkin过程不产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在区分涨落超导体中不同的涨落电导机制，并利用非线性光谱探测其中的量子干涉效应。

Method: 通过理论分析双脉冲激发下的非线性光学响应，比较Maki-Thompson过程与Aslamazov-Larkin过程对光子回波的贡献，并结合太赫兹二维相干光谱进行探测。

Result: 光子回波源于Maki-Thompson过程中时间反演电子与空穴轨迹之间的量子干涉；而Aslamazov-Larkin电导对应的涨落库珀对输运不产生回波。该回波可用太赫兹二维相干光谱直接测量。

Conclusion: 光子回波可作为区分Maki-Thompson与Aslamazov-Larkin等涨落电导机制的有效探针，展示了太赫兹二维相干光谱在涨落超导研究中的潜力。

Abstract: We study theoretically the nonlinear optical response of a fluctuating s-wave superconductor driven by two successive terahertz pulses. We show that the response exhibits a photon echo signal that arises from the quantum interference between the time-reversed electron and hole trajectories in the Maki-Thompson process. By contrast, the transport of the fluctuating Cooper pairs responsible for the Aslamazov-Larkin conductivity does not generate an echo. The photon echo signal can be readily measured using terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy, highlighting its potential to distinguish different fluctuation conductivity mechanisms.

</details>


### [103] [Microscopic Modeling of Interlayer Tunneling in Twisted Bilayer Cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16930)
*Siddhant Panda,Andreas Kreisel,Laura Fanfarillo,P. J. Hirschfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过基于 d_{x^2-y^2} 轨道重叠的微观模型计算扭角方形晶格中的层间隧穿，发现隧穿振幅符号改变且长程项常占主导，导致谱重再分配、TRSB d+id′ 区域缩小，并在 45° 附近强烈抑制约瑟夫森临界电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭角铜氧化物中近 45° 可能出现 d+id′ 态，但实验与理论结果差异大，尤其缺乏对层间隧穿的现实描述，因此需要更准确的微观模型。

Method: 拟合面内跃迁，并通过计算扭角方形晶格每个格点上 d_{x^2-y^2} 波函数的轨道重叠来得到层间隧穿振幅；研究能带、费米面和谱函数并与唯象模型比较；构建随扭角、填充和直接隧穿强度（由 c 轴间距控制）变化的相图，并计算约瑟夫森临界电流。

Result: 观察到符号改变的隧穿振幅、直接隧穿对扭角的强依赖，以及长程隧穿常主导直接隧穿；谱重再分配改变不同超导基态的竞争；相图中时间反演对称破缺的 d+id′ 区域较唯象模型缩小；45° 附近约瑟夫森临界电流被强烈抑制，与实验一致。

Conclusion: 更真实的层间隧穿刻画对扭角铜氧化物相图和输运性质至关重要，可解释实验观测并显著影响 d+id′ 等有序态的预测。

Abstract: The field of twistronics, recently extended to twisted bilayer cuprate systems, has generated considerable interest due to the prediction of $d+id'$ states close to a $45^\circ$ twist, as suggested by multiple experimental probes. Theoretical studies of these systems using a variety of models and many-body theoretical techniques have revealed multiple competing order parameters. The large variability in experimental results and theoretical predictions demands an accurate microscopic model for these systems, in particular a more realistic description of the interlayer tunneling. In this study, we fit the in-plane hopping and compute the interlayer tunneling amplitudes by calculating the orbital overlap between $d_{x^2-y^2}$ wave functions at each site of the twisted square-lattice model. We observe sign-changing tunneling amplitudes, a strong twist-angle dependence of the direct tunneling term, and long-range tunneling amplitudes that often dominate over direct tunneling. We investigate the band structure, Fermi surfaces, and spectral functions in comparison with those obtained from the phenomenological model and find a substantial redistribution of spectral weight that modifies the competition between different superconducting ground states. We determine the phase diagram as a function of twist angle, filling, and direct tunneling strength, which is controlled by the $c$-axis distance between the monolayers, and observe a reduction in the region of time-reversal-symmetry-breaking (TRSB) $d+id'$ states compared with the phenomenological model. Finally, we investigate the Josephson critical current across the phase diagram and find strongly suppressed critical currents close to a $45^\circ$ twist, consistent with experimental observations.

</details>


### [104] [Pre-pairing and polarization of doped charge carriers in unconventional superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14401)
*Lixin Zhang,Mengyan Nie,Yan Cheng,Ran Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，铜氧化物中掺杂剂倾向形成二聚体而非孤立分布；由此提出掺杂空穴/电子在制备时预配对为Z-pair，其周期组装可能导致高温超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战掺杂剂孤立分布的传统观点，并为氧化物高温超导及非常规超导寻找不同于Cooper对和Zhang-Rice单态的预配对机制。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算，比较空穴掺杂La2-xSrxCuO4、Na掺杂Ca2CuO2Cl2、电子型Nd2-xCexCuO4以及氧掺杂等体系，分析掺杂剂构型、电子结构、库仑相互作用与配对对称性。

Result: Sr等掺杂剂偏向形成二聚体；两个掺杂空穴/电子结合为极化的单态Z-pair。其吸引主要来自强反铁磁交换能；掺杂位附近在位库仑排斥U可不连续降低，从而稳定Z-pair。Z-pair是p波样局域态，破坏CuO2面对称性，并沿垂直于极化方向相互吸引。

Conclusion: 提出所有氧化物高温超导体中空穴/电子在制备阶段即可形成Z-pair；Z-pair不同于Cooper对和Zhang-Rice单态，可能普遍存在于非常规超导体，其周期组装可产生相干态与超导性。

Abstract: Based on first principles calculations, we show in hole-doped La2-xSrxCuO4, the Sr dopants prefer to form dimers, rather than be isolated as generally believed. Same trend applies to other superconductors such as Na-doped Ca2CuO2Cl2, and electron type Nd2-xCexCuO4. The dimer structure makes the two doped holes (electrons) bound up and form a singlet which is also polarized. Another type of dopant in cuprates is oxygen which behaves similarly. So, we propose that, for all oxide high TC superconductors, the holes (electrons) are pre-paired (Z-pairs) during the preparation of the superconductor. The spontaneously formed Z-pairs are conceptually different from not only the Cooper pairs, but also the Zhang-Rice singlets. The attraction force in a Z-pair originates basically from the strong antiferromagnetic exchange energy. We also found that the on-site Coulomb repulsion (U) is no longer a constant due to doping. Around a doping site, U can reduce its value discontinuously, which plays a key role in stabilizing the Z-pairs against isolated holes (electrons). A Z-pair is a p-wave like localized state which breaks the symmetry of the CuO2 plain and attracts each other along the direction perpendicular to the polarization. The Z-pairs may be popular in all of the unconventional superconductors and the periodic assembly can result in the emergence of coherence states and superconductivity.

</details>


### [105] [Chemical-Potential-Dependent Superconducting Spectroscopy on Bi2Te3/NbSe2 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14627)
*Pengyu Hu,Chun Yu Wan,Qiaoyan Yu,Chen Qian,Yisheng Gu,Xian Du,Xuhui Ning,Tingwen Miao,Shi Chen,Zihao Ma,Hao Yang,Hemian Yi,Weijiong Chen,Xiaoxue Liu,Liang Liu,Dandan Guan,Yaoyi Li,Shiyong Wang,Canhua Liu,Sangen Zhao,Fulin Wang,Junwei Liu,Hao Zheng,Jinfeng Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在极低温高分辨扫描隧道谱中，Bi2Te3/NbSe2异质结的5QL和7QL薄膜均出现带内扭结或内峰的相干峰，偏离常规单s波谱形；结合化学势依赖测量与模型模拟，表明该特征源于Bi2Te3体态和表面态选择性近邻耦合到NbSe2的单一超导能隙，而非继承NbSe2的复杂序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑绝缘体/超导体异质结中近邻效应诱导的超导性质，理解Bi2Te3体态与表面态如何分别耦合并贡献于隧道谱，以及其谱学特征是否继承NbSe2的复杂超导序参量。

Method: 采用稀释制冷温度下的高分辨率扫描隧道谱（STS），测量5QL和7QL Bi2Te3/NbSe2异质结构；结合化学势依赖的谱学测量与模型模拟分析谱形来源。

Result: 在5QL和7QL Bi2Te3薄膜中均观察到具有内扭结或内峰的相干峰，明显偏离常规单s波谱线形；化学势依赖测量和模拟表明，这些特征来自Bi2Te3体态和表面态选择性近邻耦合到NbSe2的单一超导能隙。

Conclusion: Bi2Te3/NbSe2异质结中的异常隧道谱特征并非源于继承NbSe2的复杂超导序参量，而是Bi2Te3体态和表面态分别与NbSe2的单一超导能隙发生选择性近邻耦合的结果。

Abstract: High-resolution scanning tunneling spectroscopy performed at dilution refrigerator temperatures was employed to investigate the superconducting properties of topological insulator/superconductor (Bi2Te3/NbSe2) heterostructures. Coherence peaks with inner kinks or inner peaks, deviating from the conventional single s-wave spectral line shape, were observed in both 5 quintuple-layer (5QL) and 7QL Bi2Te3 thin films. Based on the combination of chemical-potential-dependent spectroscopic measurements and model simulations, we conclude that these spectral features arise from contributions of bulk and surface states in Bi2Te3 by selectively proximity-coupled to a single superconducting gap from NbSe2, rather than inheriting the complicated superconductor's order parameter of NbSe2.

</details>


### [106] [Kink boundary-induced polarity revealed by nonreciprocal charge transport in FeTe0.7Se0.3 superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14728)
*Shengyao Li,Ya Deng,Han Yan,Dianyi Hu,Zherui Yang,Xueyan Wang,Ming Yue,Qiong Wu,Zheng Liu,X. Renshaw Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在中心对称超导体 FeTe0.7Se0.3 中利用扭结边界诱导的应变工程，实现了破缺反演对称性并产生非互易电荷输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易电荷输运通常受材料本征晶体对称性限制，因此需要一种不依赖本征对称性、可推广到中心对称材料的调控方法。

Method: 在生长过程中通过应力弛豫形成具有非对称曲率的尖点状扭结带边界，产生空间非均匀应变场和面内有效极化，从而破缺反演对称性；并在面外磁场下测量超导转变附近的输运响应。

Result: 在具有扭结边界的 FeTe0.7Se0.3 中观察到显著非互易输运，其对电流和温度有强依赖，并跨越超导转变；而平坦纳米片中的响应可忽略。

Conclusion: 扭结边界诱导的对称性破缺可作为在中心对称体系中工程化非互易电荷输运的通用途径。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport, characterized by direction-dependent electrical response, provides a powerful probe of electronic structure and a platform for rectification. However, its realization is typically governed by intrinsic crystal symmetry, restricting the range of suitable materials. Here, we demonstrate nonreciprocal charge transport in an otherwise centrosymmetric superconductor, FeTe0.7Se0.3 (FTS), through kink boundary-induced strain engineering. The stress relaxation during growth generates a cusp-like kink-band boundary with asymmetric curvature, producing a spatially nonuniform strain field and an effective in-plane polarity that breaks inversion symmetry. Pronounced nonreciprocal transport is observed under out-of-plane magnetic fields, with strong dependence on current and temperature across the superconducting transition, in contrast to the negligible response in flat nanosheets. Our results identify kink boundary-induced symmetry breaking as a general route to engineer nonreciprocal charge transport in centrosymmetric systems.

</details>


### [107] [Driven Vortex Motion in Periodic Channel Potential Embedded in Superconducting Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14775)
*A. Al Luhaibi,A. Glatz,Michael Vogl,Hocine Bahlouli,J. B. Ketterson*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文用 TDGL 数值研究周期宽度调制超导通道中的涡旋动力学：驱动涡旋产生以通道周期决定的基频 f0 及高阶谐波的振荡电场，并通过几何调制抑制高阶谐波，获得近纯正弦输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超导量子电路中，需要低谐波、近单频的微波信号来操控超导量子比特；已有圆形孔周期钉扎中的涡旋晶格动力学会产生丰富谐波，因此希望探索用通道几何调制来主动控制谐波含量，并面向低温片上波形发生器与可扩展 transmon 量子计算架构。

Method: 数值求解含时 Ginzburg-Landau（TDGL）方程，模拟具有正弦宽度调制的超导通道；通道由平均宽度和调制幅度表征；在驱动下研究涡旋运动及其产生的振荡电场频谱，并与 Al Luhaibi 等考虑的圆形孔周期钉扎势中涡旋晶格动力学进行对比。

Result: 驱动涡旋产生基频 f0（由通道周期性决定）及高阶谐波的振荡电场；谐波含量可由通道几何有效调控。适当设计的通道剖面能强烈抑制高阶谐波，使电场响应接近纯正弦波形。

Conclusion: 结果表明，几何调制是工程超导器件中低谐波微波信号的可行途径，可能实现用于控制超导量子比特的低温片上波形发生器，并成为可扩展 transmon 量子计算架构的有用构建模块。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate vortex dynamics in periodically width-modulated superconducting channels by numerically solving the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau (TDGL) equations. The channels are designed with sinusoidally varying widths characterized by their average width and modulation amplitude. The motion of driven vortices generates an oscillatory electric field with a fundamental frequency, $f_0$, set by the channel periodicity, together with higher-order harmonics. In contrast to the situation of vortex lattice dynamics in a periodic pinning landscape of circular holes considered by Al Luhaibi et al. [1], we show that the harmonic content of the generated electric field can be effectively controlled through the channel geometry. In particular, appropriately engineered channel profiles strongly suppress higher-order harmonics, yielding an electric-field response that closely approaches a pure sinusoidal waveform. These results demonstrate that geometric modulation offers a viable route to engineering low-harmonicity microwave signals in superconducting devices and may enable cryogenic on-chip waveform generators for controlling superconducting qubits. Such devices could provide a useful building block for scalable transmon-based quantum computing architectures.

</details>


### [108] [Local Suppression of Superconductivity in Niobium Films by Focused Silicon Ion Implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15200)
*Rochelle Y. Qu,Shunsuke Nishimura,Max Proepper,Joseph J. Barrera,Alexander Brooks,Joshua Vande Berg,Achim Nadzeyka,Aravind R. Sreeja,Jay C. LeFebvre,Roger K. Lake,Shane A. Cybart*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 用聚焦离子束直写硅离子注入，在Nb薄膜中局部抑制超导性，从而无需刻蚀即可制作磁通壕沟；论文测量了不同厚度与注入剂量下注入线的Tc、电阻和临界电流密度，并据此给出100 nm Nb接地平面在4.2 K下的注入参数建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中俘获的磁通会移动附近Josephson结的临界电流并干扰电路工作。传统方法是在接地平面中刻蚀壕沟以将磁通引离逻辑区，本文希望不刻蚀薄膜而实现类似磁通隔离/捕获功能。

Method: 采用聚焦离子束直写硅离子注入Nb薄膜，在三种厚度、跨两个数量级离子剂量下写入单条注入线；测量每条线的超导转变温度、电阻和临界电流密度。用含时Ginzburg-Landau模拟检验注入区能否捕获并保持涡旋，并据此提出100 nm Nb接地平面在4.2 K下的起始离子能量与剂量，以及等效宽束剂量。

Result: 注入线的转变温度随剂量增加而下降，而周围Nb不变；临界电流密度在4.2 K下变为电阻性前可下降约两个数量级。线的临界电流与电阻乘积随约化温度遵循单一幂律，因此可由转变温度和电阻估算任意温度下的临界电流。含时Ginzburg-Landau模拟表明注入区可捕获并保持涡旋。

Conclusion: 硅离子注入可局域抑制Nb超导性，形成无需刻蚀的磁通壕沟/涡旋捕获区；论文给出了用于100 nm Nb接地平面、4.2 K工作的注入参数建议及等效宽束剂量估计，为超导电路磁通管理提供可行途径。

Abstract: Magnetic flux trapped in a superconducting circuit shifts the critical currents of nearby Josephson junctions and disturbs the operation of the circuit. The usual remedy is a moat etched through the ground plane to hold the flux away from the logic. We report direct write silicon ion implantation with a focused ion beam as a way to make moats without etching the film, by locally suppressing superconductivity in niobium (Nb). Single implanted lines were written across Nb electrodes in films of three thicknesses over two orders of magnitude in ion fluence, and the transition temperature, resistance, and critical current density of each line were measured. The transition temperature of the line falls with fluence while the surrounding Nb is unchanged, and the critical current density falls by about two orders of magnitude before the line becomes resistive at 4.2 K. The product of the critical current and the resistance of the lines follows a single power law in reduced temperature. This gives an estimate of the critical current of a line at any temperature from its transition temperature and resistance. A time dependent Ginzburg-Landau simulation indicates that an implanted region can capture and hold a vortex. From these results we suggest a starting ion energy and fluence for implanted moats in a 100 nm Nb ground plane at 4.2 K and estimate the equivalent broad beam fluence.

</details>


### [109] [Spatial interference changes the symmetry class of a Josephson element](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15746)
*Takayuki Kubo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 空间干涉可在横向均匀且局域能量反演对称的约瑟夫森结中打破有效势反演对称性；多谐波空间形状因子可引入立方相互作用和三波混频，且相位反射能在保持电容分流结量子谱不变的情况下翻转奇阶非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破反演对称性对约瑟夫森势奇阶非线性的限制，在均匀结中实现立方相互作用与三波混频，并寻求不改变量子谱的非线性调控方式。

Method: 基于谐波相关的复空间形状因子推导反演对称性破缺判据；分析相位反射对展开系数和量子谱的影响；以中心十字结为例，并在外加场模型中用三分量磁场构造反演不对称有效势。

Result: 单谐波仍为平移余弦；多谐波可获得无共同反演中心的相位，从而允许立方相互作用与三波混频。相位反射使各极小附近的奇阶展开系数反号、偶阶不变；对固定充电能和任意偏置电荷的电容分流结，相位反射还保持完整量子谱。三分量磁场可产生反演不对称势，反转奇数个场分量可精确反射该势且不改变量子谱。

Conclusion: 空间干涉为工程化反演不对称约瑟夫森势和奇阶非线性提供了通用机制；相位反射或反转奇数个磁场分量可作为调控手段，在无需重新调谐谱的情况下翻转奇阶非线性扇区。

Abstract: Spatial interference can break the inversion symmetry of an effective Josephson potential even in a laterally homogeneous junction with inversion-symmetric local energy. We derive a general criterion based on harmonic-dependent complex spatial form factors: a single local harmonic remains a shifted cosine, whereas multiple harmonics can acquire phases incompatible with any common inversion center, permitting cubic interactions and three-wave mixing. Phase reflection reverses all odd-order expansion coefficients about corresponding minima while preserving the even-order ones. For a capacitively shunted junction at fixed charging energy and arbitrary offset charge, it also preserves the complete quantum spectrum, enabling reversal of the odd nonlinear sector without spectral retuning. A centered cross junction with multiple local harmonics is one concrete example. Within the imposed-field model, a three-component magnetic field can generate an inversion-asymmetric effective potential, and reversing an odd number of field components exactly reflects it without changing the quantum spectrum of the capacitively shunted junction.

</details>


### [110] [Pressure Evolution of the Superconducting Order Parameter of Kagome Superconductor CsV3Sb5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16253)
*Haiyan Zuo,Wangju Yang,Zhifan Wu,Yuqing Zhao,Qiuxia Chen,Weiming Lao,Yangpeng Qi,Hechang Lei,Haizi Cong,Cong Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用软定向点接触谱结合电阻测量，研究了静水压下调制的CsV3Sb5超导能隙及各向异性，揭示压力诱导的低能电子结构变化与CDW-SC关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CsV3Sb5中CDW、超导与拓扑态共存，静水压可产生双穹顶超导相图并抑制CDW，但压力调制下超导态的微观本质及其与CDW的关系仍不清楚。

Method: 采用软定向点接触谱（SDPCS）和超导电阻测量，在静水压下对CsV3Sb5进行各向异性点接触Andreev反射（PCAR）测量，并用双带Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk（BTK）模型拟合c轴谱。

Result: c轴方向分辨出两个s波超导能隙：大能隙满足2Δ1/kBTc > 3.52，小能隙满足2Δs/kBTc < 3.52；谱权平均耦合比2Δavg/kBTc在P1至P2压力窗口内增强。ab面结中，超导能隙各向异性在P1以上逐渐被抑制，并出现明显的峰-谷-峰结构，表明超导准粒子与玻色激发耦合增强。

Conclusion: 压力可改变低能电子结构并调控超导能隙及各向异性，结果揭示了kagome超导中CDW与SC之间复杂纠缠的新线索。

Abstract: Kagome superconductors AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs) offer an outstanding platform for exploring the interplay among charge-density-wave (CDW) order, superconductivity (SC), and nontrivial topological electronic states. For CsV3Sb5, moderate hydrostatic pressure induces a double-dome superconducting phase diagram while monotonically suppressing the CDW phase, which signals an unconventional coupling between SC and CDW. Nevertheless, the microscopic nature of the pressure-modulated superconducting state and its relationship with CDW order remain poorly understood. Here we employ soft directional point-contact spectroscopy (SDPCS), combined with superconducting resistive measurements, to probe the superconducting order parameter of CsV3Sb5 under hydrostatic compression. Our anisotropic PCAR measurements resolve two distinct s-wave superconducting gaps along the c-axis and a strongly anisotropic gap within the ab plane. Two-band Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk (BTK) fits to the c-axis spectra yield a large gap satisfying 2Delta1/kBTc > 3.52 and a small gap with 2Deltas/kBTc < 3.52. The spectral-weight-averaged coupling ratio 2Deltaavg/kBTc is enhanced in the pressure window between P1 and P2, suggesting that pressure-induced modifications of the low-energy electronic structure may contribute to the evolution of superconductivity. For ab-plane junctions, the superconducting gap anisotropy is gradually suppressed above P1, accompanied by the emergence of a pronounced peak-dip-hump structure suggestive of enhanced coupling between superconducting quasiparticles and a bosonic excitation. These results shed new light on the intricate entanglement between CDW and SC in kagome superconductivity.

</details>


### [111] [Tailored VxOy Metal--Insulator-Transition Oxides for Temperature-Triggered Smart Insulation in Superconducting Coils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16581)
*Mattia Ortino (GREEN),Davide Uglietti (SPC),Andrea Testino (PSI),Nicola Casati (PSI),Alexander Steppke (PSI),Wiktoria Wnetkowska (PSI),Andrea Masi (ENEA),Kamil Sedlak (SPC)*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 为REBCO磁体开发可在30–70 K窗口工作的智能绝缘层，探索钒氧体系以降低金属-绝缘体转变温度，并验证电流共享与热模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能绝缘可在正常运行时绝缘、仅在热点附近导电，从而兼顾绝缘线圈的快速爬升与非绝缘线圈的被动能量泄放。MIT氧化物是天然候选，但此前唯一在线圈中测试的V2O3转变温度为150–170 K，远高于REBCO磁体有用的30–70 K窗口，因此需要降低转变温度。

Method: 探索钒-氧体系；采用TGA氧化还原处理制备多相粉末；测试水热法V3O7（水合或退火）及V2O3-环氧树脂样品；进行低温测量以表征转变、热滞回和比热；建立首个电-热模型以模拟智能绝缘层中的电流共享。

Result: TGA氧化还原处理得到的多相粉末有效转变温度约127 K，比V2O3低约25 K。水热V3O7（水合或退火）电阻过高且无可用转变。V2O3-环氧树脂样品提供了稳健参考和验收标准。低温测量显示可重复的热滞回；V2O3比热测量将先前模型输入修正近两个数量级。首个电-热模型复现了通过智能绝缘层的电流共享。

Conclusion: 掺杂V2O3和V8O15被确定为下一步目标；该工作为面向REBCO磁体的低温智能绝缘材料提供了降低转变温度和电-热建模的路径。

Abstract: Smart insulation is a turn-to-turn layer that insulates in normal operation and conducts only near a hot spot. It could combine the fast ramping of insulated coils with the passive energy dump of non-insulated coils. Metal-insulator transition (MIT) oxides are natural candidates, but the only one tested in a coil so far, V2O3, switches at 150-170 K, far above the 30-70 K window useful for REBCO magnets. We explore the vanadium-oxygen system to lower the transition. TGA redox processing produced multi-phase powders with an effective transition near 127 K, about 25 K below V2O3. Hydrothermal V3O7, hydrated or annealed, was far too resistive and showed no usable transition. V2O3-epoxy specimens provided a robust reference and an acceptance criterion. Cryogenic measurements revealed a reproducible thermal hysteresis, and the measured specific heat of V2O3 corrects earlier model inputs by close to two orders of magnitude. A first electro-thermal model reproduces current sharing through the smart-insulation layer. Doped V2O3 and V8O15 are identified as the next targets.

</details>


### [112] [H$_2$-Like Units and Pressure-Tunable High-$T_\mathrm{c}$ Superconductivity in NaH$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17148)
*Tianran Chen,Taner Yildirim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 第一性原理预测NaH6的R-3m和Pm-3m相可在中等压力下具有常规超导性：R-3m在10 GPa下Tc=34 K、50 GPa下99 K；Pm-3m在50 GPa下Tc=230 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富氢材料有望实现高温常规超导，但通常需要极端压力；本文旨在寻找并理解可在较温和压力下稳定且具有高Tc的氢化物，并探究含拉长H2单元体系的超导机制。

Method: 结合大规模氢化物搜索与第一性原理计算，比较相稳定性和动力学稳定性，计算电子-声子耦合及Tc；使用分子动力学和声子重正化评估结构稳定性与非谐效应；采用模分辨电子-声子耦合和基于对称性的氢亚晶格振动诱导二阶矩张量模型分析趋势。

Result: R-3m相在低至10 GPa动态稳定，计算Tc从10 GPa的34 K升至50 GPa的99 K；Pm-3m相在更高压热力学竞争，50 GPa下Tc达230 K，继续压缩仅缓慢下降。两相均含拉长H2-like单元；R-3m中拉曼活性氢振动模线宽随H2相对三重轴倾斜而增加，并与Tc上升同步。对称性模型表明垂直三重轴的响应分量有显著贡献。

Conclusion: NaH6是一种反常的分子型氢化物，其氢亚晶格重组可在相对温和压力下促成高温常规超导，表明拉长H2单元未必排除高Tc，并为中等压力高Tc氢化物设计提供线索。

Abstract: Hydrogen-rich materials offer a route to high-temperature conventional superconductivity, but often require extreme pressures. Here we report a first-principles study of NaH$_6$, identified in our large-scale hydride search and independently predicted in a subsequent high-throughput study. Several phases compete, with $R\bar{3}m$ and $Pm\bar{3}m$ dominant over broad pressure ranges. The $R\bar{3}m$ phase remains dynamically stable down to 10 GPa, where its calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ is 34 K, rising to 99 K at 50 GPa. Cubic $Pm\bar{3}m$ becomes thermodynamically competitive at higher pressures and reaches a calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ of 230 K at 50 GPa, decreasing only weakly upon further compression. Molecular dynamics and phonon-renormalization calculations support structural persistence over the sampled timescale and indicate relatively small anharmonic effects. Despite their high $T_\mathrm{c}$, both phases contain elongated H$_2$-like units, often considered unfavorable for high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ hydrides. Mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling links Raman-active hydrogen vibrations to superconductivity. In $R\bar{3}m$, the linewidths of these modes increase with the pressure-induced tilt of H$_2$ units relative to the threefold axis, alongside the rise in $T_\mathrm{c}$. A minimal symmetry-based model of the vibration-induced second-moment tensor of the hydrogen sublattice shows that these trends are consistent with a significant contribution from response components perpendicular to the threefold axis. NaH$_6$ thus represents an unusual molecular-like hydride in which hydrogen-sublattice reorganization may enable high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ superconductivity at comparatively moderate pressures.

</details>


### [113] [Imaging atomic-scale surface structure and superconducting gap of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17879)
*Dongming Zhao,Shamarita Deb,Lichen Ji,Souvik Das,Paul Simon,Ra\'ul Cardoso-Gil,Steffen Wirth,Walter Schnelle,Sushmita Chandra,Jian-Feng Ge,Claudia Felser*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用XRD、截面STEM、电输运和低温STM/STS研究了天然错配层状化合物(SnS)1.17NbS2的表面原子结构与局域超导性，发现表面原子配准不同于体相错配结构，但SnS和NbS2终止面在2 K具有几乎相同的超导能隙，且能隙空间变化未显示与错配晶格对应的调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华耦合二维材料超晶格可调控能带并诱导涌现量子态；错配层状化合物提供天然组装的超晶格，其中共格与非共格晶格方向引入结构调制，可能影响超导等实空间电子序，因此需在原子尺度理解其表面结构与局域超导态。

Method: 结合X射线衍射、截面扫描透射电子显微镜、电输运测量以及低温扫描隧道显微镜/隧道谱，对错配层状化合物(SnS)1.17NbS2进行结构表征与2 K局域超导测量。

Result: XRD与截面STEM确认体相错配结构；STM分辨出大面积SnS和NbS2终止台阶，但表面原子配准与体相错配结构不同；2 K隧道谱在两种终止面上均显示几乎相同的超导能隙，且能隙空间变化未见与错配晶格对应的调制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了该天然组装错配异质结表面结构与超导态的原子尺度图像，表明不同表面终止可保持相近的局域超导能隙，错配晶格调制未在表面超导能隙中显现。

Abstract: Superlattices of van der Waals-coupled two-dimensional materials offer a platform for engineering electronic band structure and fostering emergent quantum states.
