Misfit layered compounds provide naturally assembled superlattices, in which crystallographically distinct two-dimensional subsystems coexist within a single bulk crystal. Therein, the presence of both commensurate and incommensurate lattice directions introduces structural modulation, which might alter real-space electronic orders such as superconductivity. Here, we investigate the atomic-scale surface structure and local superconductivity of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2 using structural characterization, electrical transport, and low-temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM). While X-ray diffraction and cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy confirm the bulk misfit structure, STM resolves large SnS- and NbS2-terminated terraces, where the surface atomic registry differs from the bulk misfit structure. Furthermore, tunnelling spectroscopy at 2 K reveals nearly identical superconducting gaps on both terminations, and the spatial variation of the gap shows no visible modulation that corresponds to the misfit lattice. These results establish an atomic-scale picture of the surface structure and superconducting states of this naturally assembled misfit heterostructure.

</details>


### [114] [Effects of the choice of the exchange-correlation functional on high-temperature superconductivity predictions in covalent-cage M(BN)5 family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18171)
*Zhongyu Wan,Guo-Hua Zhong,Cheng-wei Ju,Ruiqin Zhang,Hai-Qing Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过高通量第一性原理计算，量化了M(BN)5共价笼状超导体中电子-声子耦合对交换关联泛函选择的敏感性，发现PBEsol比PBE更准确，并预测Rb(BN)5在常压和2 GPa下Tc分别达113 K和138 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共价笼状超导体的实验合成激发了常压高温超导希望，但计算发现新材料时准确估计超导性质仍具挑战，尤其是交换关联效应如何影响电子-声子耦合尚不明确。

Method: 采用高通量第一性原理计算，系统比较不同交换关联泛函（主要是PBE与PBEsol）对M(BN)5超导体电子-声子耦合、晶格体积、声子谱、电子结构及压力响应的影响。

Result: PBE系统性高估晶格体积，导致声子软化、电子-声子耦合增强并引发Peierls不稳定性；PBEsol改善短程相互作用描述，消除Rb(BN)5在PBE下出现的虚频，给出常压Tc=113 K、2 GPa下Tc=138 K。共价键中的电子局域化和短程关联通过晶格尺度声子硬化与电子尺度能带展宽双重机制抑制Peierls畸变并使反键态离域。压力分析表明压缩会减弱不同泛函间的声子差异，但费米能级附近精细电子带仍对交换关联扰动敏感。

Conclusion: 共价键介导的超导体对交换关联泛函选择异常敏感，不同于传统金属键超导体；准确处理短程相互作用对预测其超导性至关重要，PBEsol可修正PBE的晶格膨胀和虚频问题，为共价笼状超导体的计算筛选与设计提供依据。

Abstract: The recent experimental synthesis of covalent cage superconductors has ignited hopes for ambient-pressure high-temperature superconductivity, while the computational strategy for discovering new materials poses challenges for accurately estimating superconducting properties. Here, we quantified the response of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) properties in M(BN)5 superconductors to different exchange-correlation (XC) effects using high-throughput first-principles calculations. We found that the metallic covalent bonds responsible for superconductivity exhibit anomalously high sensitivity to functional selection uncertainty, distinguishing them from conventional superconductors mediated by metallic bonding. The widely used PBE functional systematically overestimates lattice volumes, inducing phonon softening, enhanced EPC, and consequent Peierls instability. In contrast, PBEsol, which is more accurate through enhanced treatment of short-range interactions, eliminates imaginary frequencies in Rb(BN)5 that appear at the PBE level, yielding an outstanding superconducting critical temperature (Tc) of 113 K at ambient pressure and 138 K at 2 GPa. Enhanced electron localization and short-range correlation in covalent bonds manifest through dual mechanisms: phonon hardening at the lattice scale and band widening at the electronic scale, which collectively suppress Peierls distortion and delocalize antibonding states. Pressure-dependent analysis reveals that compression attenuates phonon discrepancies between XC functionals, whereas electronic properties remain notably sensitive to XC-induced perturbations of fine electronic bands near the Fermi level.

</details>


### [115] [Quantitative Impact of Occupation Smearing Methods on Conventional Superconductivity: from Electronic Structure through Lattice Dynamics to Electron-Phonon Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18185)
*Zhongyu Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 以CsH6为基准，系统研究了布里渊区展宽参数对金属体系声子介导超导计算的影响；传统展宽可导致显著误差，而优化四面体方法给出CsH6常压动力学稳定且Tc=121 K，并提出展宽宽度的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算金属体系时，布里渊区离散电子网格必须引入展宽函数和展宽宽度，但其对声子介导超导的定量影响长期被忽视，参数常凭惯例选取，可能掩盖真实物理性质。

Method: 以六氢化物CsH6为基准，对比不同展宽函数和展宽宽度（长尾、短尾）与无参数优化四面体方法，分析展宽诱导误差的大小及其传播机制，并从自由电子气模型推导适用于不同展宽函数的展宽宽度标度关系。

Result: 长尾展宽使占据窗口离域，造成费米能级显著移动、态密度平坦化和声子硬化，从而削弱电声耦合；短尾展宽采样不足，产生态密度振荡和增强的费米面嵌套，通过增强电子屏蔽和Peierls失稳软化声学模并产生虚频，使CsH6动力学不稳定而被排除。优化四面体方法表明CsH6在常压下动力学稳定，并具有121 K的显著超导转变温度。

Conclusion: 经验性展宽参数可能遮蔽理论上有前景的高温超导体；所推导的展宽宽度标度关系为不同展宽函数提供了客观、精确且高效的高通量筛选参数选择协议。

Abstract: In first-principles calculations of metallic systems, the discrete electronic mesh in the Brillouin zone necessitates a smearing function and its width (e.g., the widely used 0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton scheme) to enforce continuity of energy and occupation at the Fermi surface. However, the quantitative impact of such empirical parameters on phonon-mediated superconductivity has long been overlooked, leading to their habitual selection by mere convention. Here, using hexahydride CsH6 as a benchmark, we systematically investigate the magnitude and propagation mechanism of smearing-induced errors against the parameter-free optimized tetrahedron method. Long-tailed smearing delocalizes the occupation window, causing substantial Fermi-level shifts, flattening of the density of states, and hardening phonon modes, thereby weakening electron-phonon coupling. Conversely, short-tailed smearing suffers from insufficient sampling, producing density-of-states oscillations and enhanced Fermi-surface nesting; via amplified electronic screening and Peierls instabilities, this softens acoustic modes and generates imaginary frequencies, causing CsH6 to be dynamically unstable and discarded. However, the optimized tetrahedron method reveals that CsH6 is dynamically stable under ambient pressure with a remarkable superconducting transition temperature of 121 K. To prevent subjective empirical parameters from obscuring theoretically promising high-temperature superconductors, we derive a simple scaling relation for the smearing width, applicable to different smearing functions, from the free-electron-gas model, offering an objective, precise, and efficient protocol for parameter selection in high-throughput screening.

</details>


### [116] [Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity near Phase Boundaries in Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18286)
*Konstantinos Sourounis,Peter M. Oppeneer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作用第一性原理结合自洽Migdal-Eliashberg方程，研究菱面体多层石墨烯中声子介导的超导，复现实验多个超导区并预测新相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未扭转多层石墨烯超导的微观机制尚不明确；不同超导区可能对应不同电子相甚至不同配对机制，而纯电子配对已被广泛研究，声子介导机制仍缺乏深入检验。

Method: 以第一性原理计算结果为输入，求解耦合自洽Migdal-Eliashberg方程，并纳入相互作用驱动的电子基态对称性破缺；系统改变载流子密度和位移场。

Result: 复现了实验观测到的多个超导区，得到相近的超导能隙和Tc等性质，并预测了尚未发现的额外超导相。

Conclusion: 菱面体石墨烯中的多个超导区可满意地由声子介导配对机制解释。

Abstract: The recent observation of superconductivity in untwisted multilayer graphene has sparked intense interest in its microscopic origin. As the displacement field and carrier density are varied, superconductivity emerges in several distinct regions with different temperature and magnetic-field responses. This behavior has been attributed to the presence of distinct electronic phases, raising the possibility that different superconducting regions originate from different microscopic mechanisms. While purely electronic pairing mechanisms have been extensively explored, an in-depth examination of phonon-mediated superconductivity is still missing. Here, we solve the coupled selfconsistent Migdal-Eliashberg equations using input from first-principles calculations and incorporating interaction-driven symmetry breaking of the electronic ground state. Varying the carrier density and displacement field, we reproduce the experimentally observed superconducting regions with comparable superconducting properties such as gap and $T_c$ and predict additional, as-yet undiscovered superconducting phases. Our findings exemplify that several superconductivity regions in rhombohedral graphene can be satisfactorily explained by phonon-mediated pairing.

</details>


### [117] [The role of dependency range in thermally coupled superconducting networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18418)
*Nahala Yadid,Bnaya Gross,Shlomo Havlin,Nadav M. Shnerb*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究热耦合非晶超导网络，发现热输运依赖性可重现连续、成核主导和混合级联三种转变区制，且长程热耦合在加热时稳定超导相，级联呈各向异性一维前沿传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互依网络脆弱性理论多基于几何网络模型，依赖范围外生且耦合强度与距离无关，难以说明真实材料中由物理输运产生的互依性与失效级联。

Method: 构建实验校准的电阻分流约瑟夫森结模型，模拟热耦合非晶超导网络中由电流重分布和热扩散介导的相互作用。

Result: 系统再现连续、成核主导和混合级联三种典型转变区制；热驱动依赖随距离同时增宽并减弱，使长程热耦合在加热时稳定超导相，与几何模型中长程耦合的失稳作用相反；级联呈各向异性并表现为一维前沿传播。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了可实验检验的视角，补充了几何框架，表明物理输运驱动的互依性可显著改变级联行为与相稳定性。

Abstract: Interdependent networks can be highly fragile because dependency links amplify local failures into cascades that trigger global collapse. Much of the theoretical understanding comes from geometric network models, where dependency range is imposed externally and coupling strength is distance independent, and it is unclear how these insights apply to real materials, where interdependence arises from physical transport. Here we study thermally coupled amorphous superconducting networks, in which dependencies emerge from current redistribution and heat-mediated interactions governed by thermal diffusivity. Using an experimentally calibrated model of resistively shunted Josephson junctions, we show the system reproduces the three canonical transition regimes: continuous, nucleation-dominated, and mixed-order. However, because heat-driven dependency simultaneously broadens and weakens with distance, long-range thermal coupling can stabilize the superconducting phase upon heating, in contrast with its destabilizing role in geometric models. Cascades are anisotropic, with one-dimensional front propagation. These results offer an experimentally testable perspective complementing the geometric framework.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems for Knowledge-Grounded Material Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14123)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Subhadeep Pal,Wei Lu,Yu-Chuan Hsu,Gayla Lyon,Jiezhu Jin,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出MARS——面向科学与制造约束下材料替代的分层多智能体推理系统，并在PFAS替代案例中验证其可生成有证据支撑的候选材料与实验室级制造路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助科学发现需从生成看似合理的想法，转向可复现、约束感知且能支持实际工程决策的工作流；PFAS替代是同时涉及功能、环境、实验与工艺约束的困难材料设计问题。

Method: MARS将材料替代分解为闭环流程：需求分析、基于知识 grounding 的候选生成、可制造性评估与工艺设计；系统集成检索增强生成、知识图谱推理、实验室可用材料数据库、中间结果持久记忆，以及面向拒绝/失败的迭代反馈。

Result: 在PFAS替代案例中，MARS生成结构化材料需求、识别有证据支撑的替代候选，并制定实验室规模制造路线；多随机种子消融评估显示，MARS在三个子系统中总体性能最高，且在固定主干模型时增益仍存在。

Conclusion: 分层分解、知识 grounding 与可制造性感知的迭代优化，能够提升材料替代推理能力，其效果超出单纯依赖模型能力本身的提升。

Abstract: AI-assisted scientific discovery increasingly seeks to move beyond generating plausible ideas toward reproducible, constraint-aware workflows that can support practical engineering decisions. We present MARS, a hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning System for knowledge-grounded material substitution under scientific and manufacturing constraints. We apply MARS to replace per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS), a demanding materials-design problem in which substitutes must satisfy functional performance, environmental, experimental, and processing requirements. MARS decomposes substitution into a closed-loop workflow comprising requirement analysis, grounded candidate generation, and manufacturability assessment with process design. The system integrates retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge-graph reasoning, a database of lab-available materials, persistent memory of intermediate results, and rejection-aware iterative feedback. In a PFAS-substitution case study, MARS generates structured material requirements, identifies an evidence-grounded substitute candidate, and develops a lab-scale manufacturing route. In a multi-seed ablation evaluation, MARS achieves the highest overall performance across all three subsystems, with gains that persist when the backbone model is held fixed. These results suggest that hierarchical decomposition, knowledge grounding, and manufacturability-aware refinement improve material-substitution reasoning beyond model capability alone.

</details>


### [119] [Anisotropic pressure response of thermal conductivity in crystalline polytetrafluoroethylene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14220)
*Maissa Adi,Jamal Abou Haibeh,Samuel Huberman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 压缩使 Form III PTFE 沿链热导率持续下降，而横向热导率在高压段回升；差异由声子速度与寿命的各向异性变化导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解压缩如何通过改变声子速度和寿命，分别影响聚合物沿链与跨链热输运，并厘清横向与纵向热导率响应的微观机制。

Method: 对正交晶系 Form III PTFE 在 1.503、3.452、5.056 GPa 下，采用第一性原理谐性与立方力常数，在单模弛豫时间近似（SMRTA）中计算 100/200/300 K 热导率，并用精确因子分解为热容-速度因子和加权有效寿命。

Result: 100–300 K 下链轴热导率随每个加压步降低；3.452→5.056 GPa 时，300 K 两个横向分量分别增加 23.7% 和 13.7%，纵向降低 14.5%。横向回升源于更大的热容加权平方速度，与链间回复力变硬一致；沿链则受更短有效寿命主导，300 K 下电导加权 Normal 与 Umklapp 速率均增加。相同立方力常数块比较显示，1.503 GPa 时链间耦合已显著，并随压缩增强。

Conclusion: PTFE 热导率的压力响应高度各向异性：链间硬化可提升横向输运，但沿链声子寿命缩短导致纵向热导率下降；链间耦合在低压已不可忽略并在压缩下增强。

Abstract: Compression can change thermal conductivity differently along polymer chains and across them by altering phonon velocities and lifetimes. We examine this directional response in orthorhombic Form III polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE) at calculated pressures of 1.503, 3.452, and 5.056 GPa, using first-principles harmonic and cubic force constants within the single-mode relaxation-time approximation (SMRTA). At 100, 200, and 300 K, the chain-axis conductivity decreases at every pressure step. Between 3.452 and 5.056 GPa, both transverse components recover: at 300 K they increase by 23.7% and 13.7%, while the longitudinal conductivity decreases by 14.5%. An exact factorization separates the conductivity into a heat-capacity--velocity factor and a weighted effective lifetime. The transverse recovery follows from larger heat-capacity-weighted squared velocities, consistent with stiffer interchain restoring forces. Along the chains, shorter effective lifetimes dominate, and both conductivity-weighted Normal and Umklapp rates increase at 300 K. Comparing the same cubic force-constant blocks at all three pressures, we find that interchain coupling is already substantial at 1.503 GPa and increases under compression.

</details>


### [120] [Emergence of quantum transport in SnTe films upon structural refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14233)
*Shrinkhala Sharma,Rabindra Basnet,Thomas Parker,Afolabi Akinfaderin,Alexander Samokhvalov,Rajeswari Kolagani,Ramesh C. Budhani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文研究了退火对Bi2Te3缓冲c面蓝宝石上脉冲激光烧蚀生长的SnTe薄膜中化学计量比、结晶取向与相干输运的影响，并通过磁输运揭示低温弱反局域化及高温非Kohler标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑晶体绝缘体的表面态受晶体对称性保护，不同于常规Z2拓扑绝缘体；需要定量磁输运测量来提取相位相干长度和独立相干通道数，并理解SnTe薄膜中缺陷、结晶性与输运的关联。

Method: 在Bi2Te3缓冲的c面Al2O3衬底上用脉冲激光烧蚀生长SnTe薄膜，实施沉积后退火控制织构；进行磁阻测量，并在强自旋轨道耦合极限下用Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka理论分析低温弱反局域化，同时检验高温Kohler标度。

Result: 高温退火改善结晶性并增强[111]外延取向；100 oC退火样品载流子密度更高、电阻率更低，暗示Sn空位作为原生受主缺陷，而200 oC退火接近1:1化学计量比。T<10 K时零场附近磁阻尖峰表明弱反局域化；T>20 K时磁阻为正且较小，偏离常规Kohler标度；50-250 K间用经验温度依赖缩放因子可近似重合磁阻曲线，表明仅Hall载流子密度变化不能解释该标度行为。

Conclusion: 沉积后退火强烈影响SnTe薄膜中化学计量比、结晶性与相干输运之间的相互作用，说明缺陷控制和织构调控对拓扑晶体绝缘体薄膜输运性质至关重要。

Abstract: Topological crystalline insulators are distinguished from the conventional Z2 topological insulators by crystalline symmetry protection of the surface states. Quantitative characterization of these states requires magneto-transport measurements from which the phase coherence length and number of independent coherent channels can be extracted. Here we report such measurements on textured thin films of SnTe grown on Bi2Te3 buffered c-plane sapphire (Al2O3) by pulsed laser ablation. A post deposition annealing protocol was implemented to control the crystallographic texture, which reveals improved crystallinity and enhanced epitaxial alignment along the [111] direction in films annealed at elevated temperatures. The higher carrier density and lower resistivity seen in films annealed at 100 oC are suggestive of Sn vacancies which act as native acceptor defects, while films annealed at 200 oC approach the 1:1 stoichiometry. The magnetoresistance of all samples at T < 10 K characterized by a cusp centered around zero-field is suggestive of weak antilocalization and interpreted in the framework of the Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka theory in the strong spin-orbit coupling limit. At T > 20 K, the MR is small and positive, and deviates from conventional Kohler scaling. An approximate collapse of the MR curves between 50 and 250 K is obtained using an empirical temperature-dependent scaling factor, indicating that changes in the Hall-derived carrier density alone cannot account for the observed scaling behavior. These results demonstrate that post growth annealing strongly influences the interplay between stoichiometry, crystallinity, and coherent transport in SnTe thin films.

</details>


### [121] [Crossed Electric Spin Generation in Multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14668)
*Xukun Feng,Wei Du,Jin Cao,Qian Niu,Shengyuan A. Yang,Cong Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种多层结构中的交叉电场自旋生成效应，利用混合贝里曲率实现全电自旋操控，并在Fe3GeTe2与V2Te2O中预测出显著自旋生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合面内电场与栅极电场操控自旋的优势，并揭示准二维体系中由自旋-层-轨道耦合带来的独特量子几何结构。

Method: 构建交叉电场自旋生成理论，引入混合贝里曲率描述其量子几何机制，并结合第一性原理计算进行验证与预测。

Result: 在二维铁磁材料Fe3GeTe2和交替磁材料V2Te2O中预测出显著的自旋生成效应。

Conclusion: 该理论为二维材料中的全电自旋操控开辟了新的贝里曲率路径。

Abstract: We propose a crossed electric spin generation effect in multilayer structures. This effect combines the advantages of in-plane and gate electric fields for manipulating spin, and unveils a nice quantum geometric structure in terms of the underlying hybrid Berry curvatures that are unique to quasi-2D systems and manifest inherent spin-layer-orbital coupling. Combining our theory with first-principles calculations, we predict sizable spin generation in 2D ferromagnetic Fe3GeTe2 and altermagnetic V2Te2O. Our theory opens a new Berry-curvature route for all-electric spin manipulation in 2D materials

</details>


### [122] [Investigation on Thermal Stability of Epi-GeSn Directly on Si (001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14947)
*Tharundev V V,Dushyant Singh,Vigneshswari SR,Subha Maity,Prince Rathor,Krista R Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究直接生长在Si(001)衬底上的亚稳Ge1-xSnx外延层（约4.5 at.% Sn）的结构性质与热稳定性，发现70 nm和140 nm薄膜均完全弛豫，但热稳定性差异显著，归因于外延生长中形成的初始缺陷结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeSn/Si异质结构在GeSn基光电子器件中具有应用潜力，但亚稳GeSn的热稳定性及其与初始缺陷结构的关系尚需阐明，以提供器件制备的设计规则。

Method: 采用高分辨X射线衍射（HRXRD）和拉曼光谱表征Ge1-xSnx/Si(001)外延层的结构与应变；利用温度依赖拉曼光谱和扫描电子显微镜（SEM）在最高813 K评估热稳定性，拉曼激发波长为532 nm，主要探测表面区域。

Result: 70 nm和140 nm薄膜均为完全弛豫，较厚膜存在约+0.09%的小拉伸应变。70 nm薄膜的表面加权拉曼响应和表面形貌保持高度稳定；140 nm薄膜则严重退化，表现为Ge-Ge纵向光学（LO）声子模显著衰减和展宽，并伴随表面Sn偏析增强。

Conclusion: 直接GeSn/Si异质结构的热稳定性由外延生长阶段建立的初始缺陷结构主导，这为理解GeSn基光电子器件的制备与热稳定性设计提供了关键规则。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the structural properties and thermal stability of metastable Ge1-xSnx epilayers (~4.5 at.% Sn) grown directly on Si(001) substrates by high-resolution X-ray diffraction (HRXRD) and Raman spectroscopy. Our results demonstrate that both 70 and 140 nm films are fully relaxed, with the thicker film exhibiting a small tensile strain (+0.09%). The thermal stability of the heterostructures was evaluated up to 813 K using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and scanning electron microscopy. The surface-weighted Raman response (532 nm excitation) and surface morphology of the 70 nm film remain highly stable. In contrast, the 140 nm film undergoes severe degradation, characterized by significant attenuation and broadening of the Ge-Ge longitudinal optical phonon mode, alongside enhanced surface Sn segregation. These findings demonstrate that the thermal stability of direct GeSn/Si heterostructures is governed by the initial defect structure established during epitaxial growth, providing key design rules for understanding the fabrication of GeSn-based optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [123] [Transition-Metal Chalcogenide Nanowires: A First-Principles Study of Stability, Electronic Structure, and Mechanical Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15214)
*Danilo de Ara\'ujo Moura,Flavio Bento de Oliveira,Andreia Luisa da Rosa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合DFT与机器学习力场分子动力学，研究了六种亚纳米过渡金属硫属化物纳米线的稳定性、电子结构、力学性质及其对应变/曲率的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚纳米过渡金属硫属化物纳米线是一维平台，可通过化学组成和机械变形调控电子功能与力学响应，面向柔性纳米电子系统应用。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算与机器学习力场分子动力学（MLFF-MD）研究MoS、MoSe、MoTe、WS、WSe、WTe纳米线，并考察其稳定性、电子结构、轴向刚度、杨氏模量以及轴向应变和曲率响应。

Result: 优化结构在谐波近似下动态稳定；MoS、MoSe、WS、WSe为金属性，MoTe和WTe为半导体，HSE06带隙分别为0.52和0.20 eV；有效轴向刚度从硫化物到硒化物、碲化物降低，对应金属-硫属键长增加，圆形有效截面下杨氏模量为392–718 GPa；六种纳米线相对所选元素参考相均有负形成能；300 K、2.5 ns的MLFF轨迹保持一维形貌和局部原子序，表明有限温度动态稳定性；碲化物中观察到应变诱导带隙调制。

Conclusion: 化学组成和机械变形是调控超薄过渡金属硫属化物纳米线电子与力学性质的互补途径，支持其作为柔性纳米电子系统组件的进一步研究。

Abstract: Subnanometer transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires provide a one-dimensional platform in which electronic functionality and mechanical response can be tuned through chemical composition. Here, density functional theory calculations are combined with machine-learning force-field (MLFF) molecular dynamics to investigate MoS, MoSe, MoTe, WS, WSe, and WTe nanowires. The optimized structures are dynamically stable within the harmonic approximation, and their electronic character evolves systematically across the chalcogen series: MoS, MoSe, WS, and WSe are metallic, whereas MoTe and WTe are semiconducting, with HSE06 band gaps of 0.52 and 0.20 eV, respectively. The effective axial stiffness decreases from sulfides to selenides and tellurides, consistent with the increase in metal--chalcogen bond length; using a circular effective cross section, the corresponding Young moduli range from 392 to 718 GPa. All six nanowires have negative formation energies relative to the selected elemental reference phases. MLFF trajectories extending to 2.5 ns at 300 K preserve the one-dimensional morphology and local atomic order, providing evidence of finite-temperature dynamical stability on the simulated time scale. The corresponding MLFF molecular-dynamics energy traces are shown in. We further examine the response of the nanowires to axial strain and curvature, including strain-induced band-gap modulation in the tellurides. These results establish composition and mechanical deformation as complementary routes for tuning the electronic and mechanical properties of ultrathin transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires and support their further investigation as components of flexible nanoelectronic systems.

</details>


### [124] [A Topological Spin on Racetrack Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18297)
*Benjamin Zingsem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在中空铁磁管中，360°畴壁可集快速运动、抗扰动与可探测信号于一体，无需DMI或维持场即可实现电流驱动的高速赛道存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛道存储器需要同时具备快速运动、抗扰动和可探测信号的局域磁性载体；本文旨在利用单一连续磁壳中的360°畴壁满足这些要求。

Method: 采用解析能量分析与微磁模拟，研究中空铁磁管中由两次连续磁化反转形成的360°畴壁，考察其有限间距、扰动恢复、电流驱动输运、稳定性机制及七比特图案传输。

Result: 该360°畴壁为束缚结构，周围磁畴同向；无需Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用或维持场即可电流驱动。织构缠绕抗解绕，径向旋转有能量代价，提供稳定性控制；电流开启时组成自旋的相对旋转定量解释额外位移。其速度达数百m/s，强驱动下瞬态接近1 km/s。七比特图案经脉冲传输与恢复后仍保持载体和局域读出对比；弯曲轨道与差分电流测试表明径向刚度和驱动均匀性是完整运动控制因素。

Conclusion: 这些结果为快速、可局域区分的管状磁比特奠定物理基础，并指出可通过定制激发靶向的连续翻转路径。

Abstract: Magnetic racetrack memory requires localized carriers that combine rapid motion, resistance to perturbations and a detectable signal. Here we show how a 360{\deg} domain wall in a hollow ferromagnetic tube combines these properties within a single continuous magnetic shell. Two successive magnetization reversals form a bound object whose surrounding domains point in the same direction. Analytical energetics and micromagnetic simulations establish its finite separation, recovery from tested perturbations and current driven transport without microscopic Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction or a maintaining field. The texture's winding resists unwinding while radial rotation carries an energy cost, providing a specific control over stability. Relative rotation of the constituent spins accounts quantitatively for additional displacement during current onset. The wall supports motion at hundreds of metres per second, with transient velocities approaching one kilometre per second at stronger drive. A prepared seven-bit pattern retains its carriers and local readout contrast after pulsed transport and recovery. Curved-track and differential-current tests identify radial stiffness and drive uniformity as controls for intact motion. These results establish a physical basis for fast, locally distinguishable tubular magnetic bits and identify a continuous switching pathway that can be targeted by tailored excitation.

</details>


### [125] [Quantitative Modelling of Solvation in 2D Materials via Alchemical Decoupling and Potential Energy Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18490)
*Lindsey N. Ioos,Duncan A. Peterson,Scott C. Warren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于溶剂化Gibbs自由能连接全脱溶剂与全溶剂化两个势能面的建模策略，用于预测二维材料在溶剂中的稳定性；结果显示完全脱溶剂能垒远高于此前预期，表观聚集的二维材料层间仍可能保留单层溶剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料在溶液中为何能保持悬浮、以及其聚集与剥离机制尚不清楚；相关模拟受长时标和高活化能限制，难以准确建模。

Method: 分别构建完全脱溶剂和完全溶剂化二维材料的两个不连通势能面，利用溶剂化Gibbs自由能对二者进行校准，并通过溶剂化能将其连接，从而预测不同溶剂中二维材料的稳定性。

Result: 该策略给出的稳定性预测与此前模拟定性不同，并与实验结果高度一致；同时预测完全脱溶剂具有显著更高的能垒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，看似已聚集的二维材料实际上可能在相邻层间仍保留单层溶剂；该工作加深了对溶剂-二维材料相互作用的理解，可指导后续实验。

Abstract: Understanding why 2D materials remain suspended in solutions is essential to understanding the mechanisms of aggregation and exfoliation. These dynamics have been difficult to model because of the long timescales and large activation energies involved in these simulations. Here we describe an improved strategy for modelling the solvation and stability of 2D materials that uses the Gibbs free energy of solvation to accurately calibrate two disconnected potential energy surfaces for the fully desolvated and fully solvated 2D materials. By joining these surfaces using solvation energy, we obtain predictions for 2D material stability in various solvents that are qualitatively different than prior simulations and that have excellent agreement with experimental results. Our approach predicts a much higher barrier to full desolvation of the material, suggesting that materials that appear to be aggregated in fact still retain monolayers of solvent between adjacent layers of 2D materials. This work provides much needed understanding on the interactions between the solvent and 2D material that can guide future experimental work.

</details>


### [126] [In-Situ TEM Observation of Solid-State Phase Evolution in High-Entropy Alloy Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14147)
*Nirmal Kumar Katiyar,Noorbasha Bhavani Sai,Guilherme Gurian Dariani,Douglas S. Galvao,Pulickel M. Ajayan,Pedro Alves da Silva Autreto,Sriram Vijayan,Chandra Sekhar Tiwary*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用变温HRTEM和分子动力学模拟，揭示CoCrFeMnNi高熵合金纳米团簇在预熔化温度下发生团簇粗化与成分不均匀化，表明原子迁移由表面扩散和晶格扩散主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金在预熔化温度下的行为尚不清楚；此时原子获得足够迁移能力与时间重排，体系可能从熵主导转变为焓主导。

Method: 在室温、550°C和850°C下对CoCrFeMnNi高熵合金纳米团簇进行高分辨透射电子显微镜观察，并结合分子动力学模拟验证原子迁移机制。

Result: 纳米团簇随温度升高粗化为更大团簇，并伴随成分不均匀性，该现象归因于表面扩散和晶格扩散。

Conclusion: 实验与模拟共同证实，高熵合金纳米团簇在预熔化温区具有显著原子迁移、团簇粗化和成分偏析行为，扩散机制是其微观起源。

Abstract: The emergence of new materials has gone beyond conventional design principles. Among them are high entropy alloys (HEAs), in which multiple metallic elements coexist within a single unit cell. In this case, each atomic position in a solid material has a different chemical environment, leading to configurational entropy as high as that in the fully random or melted state, which has been frozen to a solid solution. However, the behaviour of such alloy systems at pre-melting temperatures is largely unknown, where atoms gain sufficient mobility and time to rearrange, leading to an enthalpic rather than an entropic interplay. This study demonstrated the phase transformation of HEA nanoclusters (CoCrFeMnNi) under high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) at different temperatures, including room temperature (RT), 550 {\deg}C, and 850 {\deg}C. The clustered nanoparticles coarsen into larger clusters, accompanied by compositional heterogeneity due to surface and lattice diffusion. This experimental finding of atomic mobility was further corroborated using molecular dynamics, which provides a detailed mechanistic description.

</details>


### [127] [Precursor stoichiometry controls dopant structure, defect chemistry, and valence in Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14195)
*M\'aty\'as M. Rudolf,G\'abor Bortel,Nikoletta Jegeny\'es,\'Arp\'ad Jakab,L\'aszl\'o Bencs,Ferenc Simon,Adam Gali,D\'avid Beke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Mn掺杂ZnGa2O4纳米晶中，宿主阳离子比例是决定发光的关键设计变量；富Zn组成经高温退火可获得最亮的Mn2+发光，其机制涉及掺杂捕获、本征缺陷库存以及退火控制的Mn价态与晶格有序化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂纳米晶的发光通常通过激活剂优化，但宿主阳离子比例这一同等重要却常被忽视的设计变量尚未被系统研究。本文旨在阐明Zn/Ga比与退火温度如何共同调控Mn掺杂ZnGa2O4纳米晶的缺陷、Mn价态和发光性能。

Method: 以Mn:Zn = 4×10^-4、名义Zn/Ga比0.30–0.75（化学计量比0.50）水热生长Mn掺杂ZnGa2O4纳米晶，并在400–1100°C退火；通过X射线和UV激发发光、EPR、Rietveld精修XRD、Raman光谱和SEM构建并表征组成–温度矩阵。

Result: 所有生长态样品均为相纯尖晶石，非化学计量以内部点缺陷形式容纳，晶格参数变化约0.4%；退火时过量Ga以β-Ga2O3析出，析出量由溶液组成决定。Mn2+发光出现两个最优区：近化学计量比样品在400–600°C退火，以及富Zn样品在1100°C退火，后者在X射线激发下亮度高一倍。X射线和365 nm激发下两最优区之间在800°C出现极小值，同时Mn2+ EPR布居塌缩，但Raman线宽无对应变化，说明源于瞬态、缺陷补偿的Mn氧化而非晶格退化。最富Zn组成在任何退火阶段均无Mn2+或Mn4+发光，而其痕量Cr3+发光很强，表明Mn在生长阶段已被排斥。

Conclusion: 溶液化学计量比在掺杂剂捕获和本征缺陷库存两个层面起作用，退火则控制掺杂剂价态和晶格有序度；因此最亮的高温荧光粉并非名义化学计量比组成，而是其过量组分在趋向化学计量比过程中被消耗的组成。

Abstract: The luminescence of doped nanocrystals is usually optimised through the activator; here we show that the host cation ratio is an equally decisive, and largely overlooked, design variable. Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals (Mn:Zn = $4\times10^{-4}$) were grown hydrothermally at nominal Zn/Ga ratios between 0.30 and 0.75 (stoichiometric: 0.50) and post-annealed at 400--1100$^\circ$C, and the resulting composition--temperature matrix was mapped by X-ray- and UV-excited luminescence, EPR, Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy and SEM. The as-grown crystals are phase-pure spinel at every composition and accommodate their off-stoichiometry internally as a point-defect inventory, reflected in a 0.4\% variation of the lattice parameter; on annealing, the excess gallium is exsolved as $\beta$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ in amounts set by the solution composition. The Mn$^{2+}$ emission separates into two optima, near-stoichiometric crystals annealed at 400--600$^\circ$C and Zn-excess crystals annealed at 1100$^\circ$C, the latter being twice as bright under X-ray excitation. Under X-ray and 365~nm excitation the two optima are separated by a minimum at 800$^\circ$C that coincides with a collapse of the Mn$^{2+}$ EPR population and has no counterpart in the Raman linewidths, identifying transient, defect-compensated oxidation of the dopant rather than lattice degradation as its origin. The most Zn-rich composition shows neither Mn$^{2+}$ nor Mn$^{4+}$ emission at any annealing stage, whereas its trace Cr$^{3+}$ emission is strong, indicating that Mn is rejected already during growth. Solution stoichiometry therefore acts on two levels, dopant capture and native-defect inventory, while annealing controls dopant valence and lattice order. The brightest high-temperature phosphor is thus not the nominally stoichiometric one, but the composition whose excess is consumed on its way to stoichiometry.

</details>


### [128] [Benchmarking machine-learning interatomic potentials using classical force fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14239)
*Johannes Sandberg,Genevi\`eve Dusson,Julien Lam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 系统比较线性原子团簇展开、Behler-Parrinello高维神经网络势与图神经网络MACE势在拟合Lennard-Jones和Stillinger-Weber等经典势时的表现，发现MACE最准确但计算成本显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势模型众多，需从精度、数据效率、噪声鲁棒性、碳足迹及物理观测量再现能力等方面进行公平比较，以指导计算材料科学中的模型选择。

Method: 以Lennard-Jones和Stillinger-Weber势为基准，训练并评估线性ACE、Behler-Parrinello HDNN和基于图神经网络的MACE模型；考察不同训练集规模、噪声鲁棒性、碳足迹以及径向分布函数等观测量的再现能力。

Result: MACE模型整体上最准确地复现势能面，且过拟合迹象较少；但其计算成本显著高于其他模型，其他模型则在精度与效率之间存在不同取舍。

Conclusion: 该比较揭示了机器学习原子间势在精度与计算成本之间的权衡，强调在计算材料科学中选择模型时需结合具体任务需求综合考量。

Abstract: We propose a rational comparison between several well-known machine-learning interatomic potentials (linear atomic cluster expansion, the Behler-Parrinello high-dimensional neural network potential, and the graph neural network-based MACE potential) by testing their performance for fitting classical potentials such as the Lennard-Jones and Stillinger-Weber potential. With this we assess their advantages and limitations regarding several aspects: varying training dataset size, robustness to noise, carbon footprint, as well as the ability to reproduce the radial distribution function as an example observable. Overall the MACE models are shown to most accurately reproduce the potential energy surface, with little sign of overfitting, but at a significant computational cost, highlighting important questions regarding the choice of models in computational materials science.

</details>


### [129] [Mn--Cr dopant association in dilute co-doped $\mathrm{ZnAl_2O_4}$ revealed by combined EPR and optical spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14262)
*Apoko S. Omondi,M\'aty\'as M. Rudolf,Nikoletta Jegeny\'es,\'Arp\'ad Jakab,G\'abor Bortel,Bence G. M\'arkus,Adam Gali,D\'avid Beke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过EPR自旋计数与多种光谱联用，揭示ZnAl2O4中Mn/Cr共掺杂的发光增强源于形成交换耦合的边共享Cr-Mn缺陷复合物，而非能量传递；超过Mn浓度阈值后Mn再分配为EPR沉默构型并导致淬灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共掺杂是获得更亮荧光粉的常用策略，但共掺杂离子的具体存在形式及其对发光的作用机制通常不明确。

Method: 以水热法生长的ZnAl2O4纳米颗粒为对象，制备两个稀Mn/Cr共掺杂系列，结合定量电子顺磁共振自旋计数、光致发光、光致发光激发谱和X射线激发光谱进行表征。

Result: 发现Mn2+/4+可进入Cr3+相邻的八面体位，形成交换耦合、边共享的Cr-Mn缺陷复合物；该八面体Mn降低Cr3+位点的反演对称性，增强其宇称禁阻吸收，从而提升Cr3+发光且不依赖能量传递。缺陷复合物布居遵循质量作用定律；Mn浓度超过阈值后，Mn再分配为EPR沉默的八面体构型，淬灭两种发光。

Conclusion: 共掺杂荧光粉的浓度最优值可能由缺陷物种分布决定，而不仅是能量传递效率；这为通过结构设计获得更亮发光体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Efficient and durable phosphors made from abundant elements underpin energy-saving solid-state lighting, near-infrared light sources for sensing, and X-ray and biomedical imaging. Co-doping is a standard route to brighter phosphors, yet the fate of the co-dopants is rarely established. We combine quantitative electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) spin counting with photoluminescence, photoluminescence excitation, and X-ray-excited spectroscopy across two dilute Mn/Cr co-doping series of hydrothermally grown $\mathrm{ZnAl_2O_4}$ nanoparticles and show that the two ions can form an exchange-coupled, edge-sharing Cr--Mn defect complex, with $\mathrm{Mn^{2+/4+}}$ entering an octahedral site next to $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$. The octahedral Mn lifts the inversion symmetry of the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ site and strengthens its parity-forbidden absorption, enhancing the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ emission without energy transfer. The population of the defect complex follows the law of mass action, and above a threshold Mn concentration, Mn re-partitions into EPR-silent octahedral configurations that quench both emissions. Concentration optima in co-doped phosphors can thus reflect defect speciation, not only energy-transfer efficiency, which opens a structural design handle for brighter emitters.

</details>


### [130] [A Solution-Processed Ti/Pt/NiOx/Ag RRAM Device with Multiple Forming-Free Non-Volatile Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14322)
*Abhrajit Roy,Chopali Chanchal Sahu,Rudraksh Sangore,Raman Hissariya,Poulomi Chakrabarty,Harsh Roniyar,Merlyne De Souza,Debanjan Bhowmik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 报道了一种溶液法制备的NiO基RRAM器件（Ti/Pt底电极、Ag顶电极），无需forming即可实现HRS/LRS二进制开关及脉冲驱动的LTP/LTD，具备约5位、30个中间突触态，并在MNIST/CIFAR-10分类中用实测LTP-LTD校准MLP/CNN权重更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算单元能耗高，ANN交叉阵列需要低能耗非易失突触器件；溶液法过渡金属氧化物RRAM成本低、易制备、稳定且具忆阻特性，但多数此前器件需forming才表现非易失性，限制阵列设计并增加变异性与高压需求。

Method: 制备NiO基RRAM：溶液法沉积NiO，Ti/Pt底电极，Ag顶电极；测试forming-free HRS/LRS二进制开关、脉冲驱动LTP/LTD及中间态；用XPS确认氧空位；将实测LTP-LTD特性用于校准MLP和CNN的突触权重更新，分别在MNIST和CIFAR-10上做分类。

Result: 实现forming-free双稳态与脉冲调制LTP/LTD，HRS-LRS间有30个中间突触态，约5-bit权重分辨率；多数态至少1000 s稳定，显示非易失性；XPS证实氧空位；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上分别用MLP和CNN成功分类。

Conclusion: 该溶液法NiO RRAM无需forming即可提供稳定多态非易失突触行为，可降低变异性、免高压脉冲并简化交叉阵列设计，且经实测突触特性校准后可用于ANN数据分类，适合低能耗神经形态/存内计算硬件。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANN) implemented on crossbar arrays of non-volatile synaptic devices are known to consume much less energy compared to that implemented on conventional computing units. Solution-processed resistive random-access memory (RRAM) devices based on transition-metal oxides are suitable as such synaptic devices because of their cheap and easy fabrication method, stability, and suitable memristive properties. Here, in NiO-based RRAM devices with Ti/Pt as the bottom electrode and Ag as the top electrode (NiO layer deposited through solution processing), we experimentally report forming-free binary switching between a high resistance state (HRS) and a low resistance state (LRS) as well as forming-free pulse-driven modulation (long-term potentiation (LTP), long-term depression (LTD)) through 30 intermediate synaptic states between HRS and LRS (around 5-bit synaptic weight resolution). Many of these states are measured to be stable at least up to 1000 seconds confirming their non-volatility and their utility in storage of synaptic weights for ANN crossbar arrays. On the contrary, most prior experimental reports (including reports both on solution-processing-based and sputtering-based RRAM devices) require forming to exhibit non-volatility. Avoiding forming reduces variability, eliminates the need for high voltage pulses, and makes the crossbar array design simpler. Further, we confirm the presence of oxygen vacancies in our materials stack using X-ray photo-electron spectroscopy (XPS). Finally, we show successful data classification on MNIST and CIFAR-10 data sets using a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) model and a convolution neural network (CNN) model respectively, where the synaptic weight updates of these ANN models are calibrated against the experimentally measured LTP-LTD characteristics of the NiO RRAM devices.

</details>


### [131] [Atoms that Matter: Largest Materials Datasets Are 99% Redundant, and Local Chemistry Can Guide the Required 1%](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14392)
*Evgeny Moerman,Igor Poltavsky,Anton Charkin-Gorbulin,Alexandre Tkatchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在最大材料数据集 OMat24 上，仅用不到 1%（650 万原子）的子集训练 MACE，即可接近全量 MACE-OMAT-0 的性能；决定信息量的是化学环境多样性而非数据规模，剩余 99% 结构高度冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验通用材料机器学习力场是否必须依赖约 1 亿结构才能实现迁移，并探索能否用极小标注子集保留 OMat24 的信息内容。

Method: 在 OMat24 上按三种互补策略选取 650 万原子子集：随机采样、基于预训练 MLFF 隐空间聚类的 DIRECT、以及模型无关的 EnvTok——利用原子位置和种类（可选磁矩）最大化离散、可解释局域环境的多样性；训练 MACE 模型并与全量参考 MACE-OMAT-0 比较。

Result: 三种子集模型在力误差上与全量模型相差仅几十 meV/Å，并在生成能、弹性模量、热容、Li+ 迁移势垒及高达 3000 K、500 GPa 的分子动力学稳定性上接近或超过全量模型；不同选择策略结果相近。

Conclusion: 数据规模不是关键，化学环境多样性才是 OMat24 的信息核心，其余 99% 结构 largely redundant。广泛采样化学空间仍必要，但只需对结构/化学上不同的构型标注；EnvTok 可在标注或训练前识别这些构型，得到紧凑、架构无关的数据集。百倍缩减使杂化 DFT 或更高级别重标注通用训练集变得可行，并释放计算预算用于扩展环境覆盖与多保真学习。

Abstract: General-purpose machine-learning force fields (MLFFs) for materials are now trained on the order of $100$ million structures, assuming transferability requires such scale. We test this premise on OMat24, the largest available materials dataset, by training MACE models on subsets of $6.5$ million atoms, less than $1\%$ of OMat24, selected in three complementary ways: at random, by latent-space clustering of a pretrained MLFF (DIRECT), and by a new model-free method maximizing the diversity of discrete, interpretable local environments (EnvTok). All three models approach the full-data reference MACE-OMAT-0 model to within a few tens of $\mathrm{meV}/\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ in forces, and remain close to or exceed it on formation energies, elastic moduli, heat capacities, Li$^{+}$ migration barriers, and molecular-dynamics stability up to $3000\,$K and $500\,$GPa. This near-independence from the selection strategy shows that chemical environment diversity, not size, defines OMat24's information content: the remaining $99\%$ of structures are largely redundant. Broad sampling of chemical space thus remains essential, but reference labels are needed only for structurally and chemically distinct configurations. Using atomic positions and species, optionally augmented by magnetic moments, EnvTok identifies these before any labelling or training, yielding compact, architecture-independent datasets. This hundredfold reduction makes hybrid-DFT or higher-level relabelling of general-purpose training sets feasible, and frees computational budget for extending environmental coverage and multi-fidelity learning.

</details>


### [132] [Large Out-of-plane Polarization in Two-dimensional Bi2O3 Monolayers Predicted by an AI-driven Inverse-design Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14429)
*Hongmei Tan,Jing Zhang,Shan Gao,Yueyu Zhang,Xiangmei Duan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合逆向结构设计、机器学习原子间势与第一性原理计算，以Bi2O3为模型体系发现两种未报道的极性单层（四方与正交相），具有高面外极化、动力学/热稳定性及约3.8 eV带隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维极性氧化物因内建电场在纳米电子学、光电子学和表面电荷工程中前景广阔，但非层状氧化物网络结构复杂，寻找此类二维极性相仍很困难。

Method: 采用逆向结构设计，联合机器学习原子间势与第一性原理计算，探索非层状氧化物网络的二维构型；以Bi2O3为代表，筛选并验证新单层结构，计算Berry相位极化、稳定性、有限温度结构与电子结构，并分析极化机制。

Result: 发现四方和正交两种Bi2O3极性单层，Bi/O亚层垂直分离产生本征极性，Berry相位二维极化分别为34.12和37.15 pC/m；o-Bi2O3单层具有二维氧化物中最大面外极化之一。二者动力学稳定、有限温度结构完整、带隙约3.8 eV。大极化源于维度诱导的非层状Bi-O网络重构，打破局域配位对称并引起面外电荷重分布，同时Bi 6s2孤对电子促进不对称Bi-O键。

Conclusion: 维度诱导的局域配位对称破缺可作为高极化二维氧化物的设计原则；逆向设计与机器学习辅助筛选相结合可用于探索非层状氧化物网络中的非常规极性相。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) polar oxides with built-in electric fields offer promising opportunities for nanoscale electronics, optoelectronics, and surface charge engineering. However, their discovery remains challenging due to the enormous structural complexity of nonlayered oxide networks. Here, we combine inverse structural design with a machine-learning interatomic potential and first-principles calculations to explore 2D oxide configurations. Using Bi2O3 as a representative system, we identify two previously unreported polar monolayers with tetragonal and orthorhombic symmetries. Both structures exhibit vertically separated Bi and O sublayers, giving rise to pronounced intrinsic polarity with Berry-phase 2D polarizations of 34.12 and 37.15 pC/m, respectively. Notably, the o-Bi2O3 monolayer exhibits one of the largest reported out-of-plane polarizations among 2D oxides. Both monolayers exhibit dynamical stability, finite-temperature structural integrity, and wide band gaps (~3.8 eV). The large polarization originates from dimensionality-induced reconstruction of the nonlayered Bi-O network, which breaks local coordination symmetry and produces out-of-plane charge redistribution, while the stereochemically active Bi 6s2 lone pair further favors asymmetric Bi-O bonding. These findings suggest dimensionality-induced local-coordination symmetry breaking as a promising design motif for high-polarization 2D oxides, and demonstrate the utility of combining inverse design with machine-learning-assisted screening to explore unconventional polar phases in nonlayered oxide networks.

</details>


### [133] [Fluctuation-based evaluation of thermal conductivity using deviational phonon Monte Carlo simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14433)
*Takuma Hori,Yuzuho Oki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于涨落的蒙特卡洛方法，在声子玻尔兹曼输运方程框架下计算热导率，用偏差声子粒子的均方位移表征热导率，无需热库，单次模拟即可获得多方向热导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在发展无需热储、可同时评估多方向热导率的方法，并适用于不同声子输运区间和复杂纳米结构。

Method: 从能量型方差缩减的声子BTE公式出发，推导偏差声子能量空间扩散与宏观热传导方程的关系，将热导率表示为偏差声子粒子的均方位移。

Result: 对室温晶体硅体材料、方形纳米线和各向异性多晶纳米结构进行计算，结果与理论预测一致，包括声子边界散射引起的尺寸依赖和模分辨贡献；对各向异性多晶结构还能同时解析方向热导率并反映晶粒形貌影响。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该基于涨落的方法在不同输运区间和结构构型中评估热导率的有效性。

Abstract: We develop a fluctuation based Monte Carlo method for evaluating thermal conductivity within the phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) framework. Starting from the energy based variance reduced formulation of the BTE, we derive a relation between the spatial spreading of deviational phonon energy and the macroscopic heat conduction equation. The resulting formulation expresses thermal conductivity in terms of the mean square displacement of deviational phonon particles, without imposing thermal reservoirs. Consequently, thermal conductivity along multiple directions can be evaluated from a single simulation. The method is demonstrated for crystalline silicon at room temperature in bulk, square nanowires, and anisotropic polycrystalline nanostructures. The calculated thermal conductivities agree with theoretical predictions, including the size dependence induced by phonon boundary scattering and the mode-resolved contributions. For anisotropic polycrystalline structures, the method simultaneously resolves the directional thermal conductivities and captures their dependence on grain morphology. These results demonstrate the validity of the proposed fluctuation-based approach for evaluating thermal conductivity across different transport regimes and structural configurations.

</details>


### [134] [A Fisher-Geometric Approach to Supercell Pair Distribution Function Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14597)
*Dawei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种面向原子超胞-粉末PDF精修的无矩阵Fisher几何优化方法，将观测似然的信息度量回拉到原子坐标，用自动微分和阻尼线性系统实现直接二阶更新，避免构造完整Jacobian或坐标曲率矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF测量维度远小于待优化原子坐标维度，坐标高度耦合；反向模式微分虽可高效求梯度，但一阶优化器未显式利用耦合PDF敏感度来预条件坐标更新。

Method: 把观测似然的信息度量拉回原子坐标，使更新按集体位移的PDF响应和实验不确定度加权；在各向同性阻尼下，对M个PDF通道求解阻尼线性系统，并用自动微分计算Jacobian-向量和向量-Jacobian乘积，不显式构造完整Jacobian或坐标曲率矩阵。高斯误差时退化为经典Levenberg-Marquardt精修，同时避免稠密坐标空间分解；框架还可扩展到相关高斯误差和稳健似然更新。

Result: 摘要未提供定量结果；所述方法实现无矩阵反向传播与似然感知集体更新，可用于大型原子超胞的直接二阶精修，并兼容相关高斯误差和稳健似然更新。

Conclusion: 该工作将Fisher几何预条件、自动微分与无矩阵线性求解结合，为粉末PDF约束下的大规模原子超胞精修提供了可扩展的二阶优化框架。

Abstract: Refining atomistic supercells against powder pair distribution functions (PDFs) requires optimizing many coupled atomic coordinates using a much smaller set of radial measurements. Reverse-mode differentiation makes gradients accessible for large systems, but standard first-order optimizers do not explicitly use coupled PDF sensitivities to precondition coordinate updates. We introduce a matrix-free, Fisher-geometric approach that pulls the information metric of the observation likelihood back onto atomic coordinates. The resulting update weights collective displacements according to their PDF response and experimental uncertainty. Under isotropic damping, the update is obtained by solving a damped linear system over $M$ PDF channels, using automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobian--vector and vector--Jacobian products without constructing the full Jacobian or coordinate curvature matrix. For Gaussian measurement errors, the update recovers classical Levenberg--Marquardt refinement while avoiding dense coordinate-space factorization. The same computational framework accommodates correlated Gaussian errors and robust likelihood-based updates. This approach combines likelihood-aware collective updates with matrix-free backpropagation for direct second-order refinement of large atomistic supercells.

</details>


### [135] [Enhancing Quasi-Two-Dimensional Electronic Structure in Bi2O2Se via Chlorine Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14619)
*Yong-Zhen Jiang,Qi-Xun Wen,Xiang Xu,Su-Tao Sun,Feng-Fan Ren,Zi-Xuan Zhou,Xing-Yuan You,Yu-Cheng Kan,Zheng Zhang,Jie-Yi Liu,Yu-Zhou Zhao,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Shu-Hua Yao,Jian Zhou,Yan-Bin Chen,Zhong-Kai Liu,Yu-Lin Chen,Yang-Yang Lv,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过氯取代在Bi2O2Se中引入范德华间隙，在保持高迁移率、间接带隙和有效质量等关键电子特性的同时增强二维性，并实现0.8~3.5 eV的宽带隙调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bi2O2Se是下一代电子学和量子现象研究的重要半导体候选，但其Se层间强离子键限制了该准二维材料的潜力，因此需要一种能削弱层间耦合而不破坏面内输运的改性策略。

Method: 以Bi6O6Se2Cl2单晶为最优Cl掺杂化合物，结合输运测量和角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）表征，并辅以第一性原理计算，分析Cl取代对电子结构、层间耦合和二维特性的影响。

Result: Cl取代引入范德华间隙并调节电子结构；关键电子特征如间接带隙、高电子迁移率和载流子有效质量得以保留，同时二维性显著增强。理论计算表明，导电Bi-O层与掺杂Se/Cl层之间的空间分离可实现层间耦合和层内输运的独立优化；理论与实验结合预测Bi2O2Se体系带隙可在0.8~3.5 eV宽范围内工程化。

Conclusion: Cl取代和空间解耦策略可有效调控准二维Bi2O2Se的精细电子结构，拓展其在光电子学、纳米电子学和基础研究中的应用潜力，并为其他准二维量子体系的电子结构改性提供可行思路。

Abstract: Bismuth oxyselenide (Bi2O2Se) has emerged as a leading semiconductor candidate for next-generation electronics and quantum phenomena research. However, its strong ionic interlayer bonding of Se layers fundamentally restricts the full potential of this otherwise promising quasi-two-dimensional (2D) material. In this work, we demonstrate chlorine substitution as an effective strategy to introduce van der Waals gaps and modify the electronic structure of the system. Through comprehensive transport and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements on Bi6O6Se2Cl2 single crystal, an optimal Cl-doping compound, we show that the key electronic features, including indirect band gap, high electron mobility, and carrier effective mass, are well preserved, while the 2D character of the system is sufficiently enhanced. First-principles calculations attribute this selective tunability to the unique spatial separation between conductive Bi-O layers and dopant Se/Cl layers, which enables independent optimization of interlayer coupling and intralayer transport. Remarkably, our theoretical calculations combined with experimental observations project a broad bandgap engineering across a wide range (0.8 ~ 3.5 eV) within the Bi2O2Se system. Our findings not only expand the application potential of Bi2O2Se-based materials in optoelectronics, nanoelectronics, and fundamental research but also highlight spatial decoupling as a viable strategy for modifying detailed electronic structure of quasi-2D quantum systems.

</details>


### [136] [Finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory: a unified framework for i-DMFT and TAO-DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14706)
*Jingang Han,Huanjing Gong,Kai Luo,Hongming Weng,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出有限温度绝热连接约化密度矩阵泛函理论（FT-AC-RDMFT），将 i-DMFT 与 TAO-DFT 统一为同一框架的不同约化，并系统解释有限温度分数占据改善强关联与断键描述的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述化学键断裂需捕获强/静态电子相关，而 Hartree--Fock 和 Kohn--Sham DFT 等单参考自洽场方法难以胜任；尽管 i-DMFT 和 TAO-DFT 通过分数占据有所改善，但二者理论框架不同、偏半经验且关系不清。

Method: 建立 FT-AC-RDMFT：由变分原理导出自然轨道有效本征方程和 Fermi--Dirac 占据数，并通过热绝热连接将 i-DMFT 的经验熵系数与辅助非相互作用体系特征温度联系起来。

Result: i-DMFT 对应精确交换约化，TAO-DFT 对应以密度型交换关联泛函替代精确交换的约化；数值上 i-DMFT 除一常数外与精确交换实现一致，有限温度占据改善解离的行为可推广至杂化和半局域泛函；对强关联 V2O3，有限温度占据显著降低非磁 LDA 严重高估的体模量，使其接近实验。

Conclusion: FT-AC-RDMFT 为分子和固体体系中基于有限温度占据的电子结构方法提供了共同理论基础。

Abstract: Accurately describing chemical bond breaking requires capturing strong/static electron correlation, a major challenge for single-reference self-consistent-field methods such as Hartree--Fock and Kohn--Sham density-functional theory (DFT). Fractional occupations substantially improve this situation, as demonstrated by information-density-matrix-functional theory (i-DMFT) and thermally-assisted-occupation DFT (TAO-DFT). Although useful, they are formulated within rather different theoretical frameworks, are somewhat semi-empirical, and their relationship remains unclear. Here we introduce finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory (FT-AC-RDMFT), in which i-DMFT and TAO-DFT emerge as different reductions of a unified framework. The effective eigenvalue equation for natural orbitals and the Fermi--Dirac occupation numbers follow directly from the variational principle, while a thermal adiabatic connection relates the empirical entropy coefficient of i-DMFT to a characteristic temperature of the auxiliary non-interacting system. Within this framework, i-DMFT is the exact-exchange-based reduction, whereas TAO-DFT results when exact exchange is replaced by a density-based exchange-correlation functional. Numerical calculations confirm that i-DMFT, apart from a constant, coincides with the exact-exchange realization of the framework, and show that the improved dissociation behavior from finite-temperature occupations extends beyond exact exchange to hybrid and semilocal density functionals. For strongly correlated V2O3, finite-temperature occupations substantially reduce the severely overestimated non-magnetic LDA bulk modulus, bringing it close to experiment. These results establish FT-AC-RDMFT as a common theoretical foundation for finite-temperature occupation-based electronic-structure methods in molecular and solid-state systems.

</details>


### [137] [Inverse Designing Reconfigurable Piezoelectric Tensors via Architectural and Modal Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14761)
*Anish Satpati (Rayne),Marco Maurizi (Rayne),Hyunmoon Cho (Rayne),Victor Couedel (Rayne),Nicolas Mule (Rayne),Xiaoyu (Rayne),Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于图表示的通用框架，通过结构架构调控完整有效压电张量，并实现频率选择性张量重构；演示了单块多自由度致动器，体积较传统多叠层组件减少90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然晶体中原子周期性和能量稳定性限制晶体对称性，使压电张量系数仅能呈现20种各向异性，难以按需调控全部有效压电系数。

Method: 建立图表示框架，将宏观对称性、局部极化和结构单元变形协同起来，把每个有效压电系数追溯到增强或抵消它的结构成员，并通过定向几何修改进行控制；进一步利用激励频率切换主导压电系数。

Result: 实现频率选择性张量重构，使单一架构的主导压电系数随激励频率改变；可在多个方向和变形状态下实现可编程传感、致动和模式转换；制备出集成多种致动模式的单块多自由度致动器，体积比传统多叠层组件减少90%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了通过架构设计调控有效压电张量的通用原理，拓展了压电器件设计空间，可用单一结构实现可编程、多方向、多模式功能并显著降低多自由度系统体积。

Abstract: Piezoelectricity arises from the coupling between electric polarization and mechanical deformation, described by a third-rank tensor whose coefficients are constrained by crystal symmetry. In natural crystals, atomic periodicity and energetic stability restrict accessible symmetries, limited to 20 distinct anisotropies that can exist. Here we establish a general framework, based on graph representation, for controlling the full effective piezoelectric tensor through architecture by resolving how macroscopic symmetry, local polarization and deformation of structural elements act together. This enables each coefficient to be traced to the members that reinforce or cancel it and be controlled through targeted geometric changes. We further uncover frequency-selective tensor reconfiguration, in which excitation frequency switches the dominant piezoelectric coefficients of a single architecture. These principles open a broader class of applications in which a single piezoelectric architecture can deliver programmable sensing, actuation and mode conversion across multiple directions and deformation states. We realize a multi-degree-of-freedom actuator that integrates distinct actuation modes in one block and reduces volume by 90% relative to conventional multi-stack assemblies.

</details>


### [138] [Selective faceting induced by Hexagonal Boron Nitride on curved Platinum substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14825)
*Alaa Mohammed Idris Bakhit,Muhammad Saad Mahmood,Khadiza Ali,Anna A. Makarova,Igor Pi\v{s},Federica Bondino,Frederik Schiller,Maria Peressi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合实验与第一性原理计算，研究了六方氮化硼（hBN）在弯曲Pt(331)衬底上的生长，揭示hBN诱导的选择性刻面及界面相互作用差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解hBN在金属衬底上的生长机制与界面相互作用，特别是在复杂曲面/台阶表面上刻面形成与稳定性的来源。

Method: 采用LEED和STM表征结构，XPS和NEXAFS分析电子性质，并用DFT计算hBN粘附能与清洁表面自由能，比较刻面稳定性。

Result: hBN生长使Pt(331)形成Pt(111)、Pt(221)、Pt(441)、Pt(881)和Pt(110)等稳定刻面；hBN-衬底相互作用从Pt(111)最弱增强至Pt(110)最强；DFT证实hBN改变未覆盖刻面的相对稳定性，部分刻面因强界面相互作用稳定，部分则主要受清洁表面能控制。

Conclusion: hBN诱导的选择性刻面不能仅用热力学解释，需综合界面粘附能与清洁表面自由能；该工作为金属衬底上hBN生长及界面调控提供了微观机制认识。

Abstract: This work combines experimental investigations and first-principles calculations to study hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) growth on a curved Pt(331) substrate. Low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM) were employed to investigate the structural properties, observing that hBN growth led to faceting with several stable facets: Pt(111), Pt(221), Pt(441), Pt(881), and Pt(110). Additionally, the electronic properties were examined by X-ray photoemission spectroscopy (XPS) and near-edge X-ray absorption fine structure (NEXAFS), revealing an increase in the hBN-substrate interaction strength, from the weakest at the Pt(111) to the strongest at the Pt(110). Density functional theory (DFT) calculations confirm that the selective faceting mechanism is induced by hBN, which changes the order of relative stability of the uncovered facets. Although a complete understanding of the process is not achievable using only thermodynamical arguments, the comparison between hBN adhesion energies and clean surface free energies clearly indicates that some facets are stabilized with respect to others by stronger overlayer-substrate interaction, whereas for others the clean surface energetics plays the dominant role.

</details>


### [139] [Pressure-transferable universal machine-learning potentials: the WBM-MatPES benchmark and Tuga-FF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14891)
*Pierre-Paul De Breuck,Thalis H. B. da Silva,Antoine Loew,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 发布WBM-MatPES高压材料数据集与基准，并提出Tuga-FF图变换器势函数，在常压和150 GPa高压下均取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势有望替代材料科学工作流中昂贵的从头计算，但现有模型的精度与迁移性受训练数据不足限制，尤其缺乏系统基准和高压力数据。

Method: 构建并发布WBM-MatPES：包含18.2万个结构，按一致的MatPES设置重算，提供两个不同深度的参考凸包以及最高150 GPa、六个压力点的测试集，模拟真实发现流程；在此基准上评估五个已发布MatPES势，并训练覆盖常压与高压构型的图变换器模型Tuga-FF。

Result: 五个已发布MatPES势在25 GPa下能量误差一致在12 meV/atom以内，但在150 GPa下误差急剧增至0.4–5 eV/atom；Tuga-FF在常压基准中最准确，能量误差13.6 meV/atom，在150 GPa下仍保持21 meV/atom。

Conclusion: 该基准可系统排名MLIP并推动社区训练更高精度模型、生成更丰富数据集，从而整体推进机器学习原子间势的发展。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials hold the promise to replace costly ab initio calculations in materials science workflows. However, the accuracy and transferability of current models are limited by the availability of training data. We release WBM-MatPES, a dataset of 182 thousand structures recomputed with consistent MatPES settings, together with two reference convex hulls of different depths and test sets at six pressures up to 150 GPa. These datasets reproduce a realistic discovery workflow and allow potentials to be ranked systematically at this level of theory, similar to the Matbench Discovery leaderboard at the Materials Project settings. In this benchmark, five published MatPES potentials agree within 12 meV/atom at 25 GPa, yet their errors increase dramatically to 0.4-5 eV/atom at 150 GPa. To address this limitation, we develop Tuga-FF, a graph transformer trained on datasets spanning ambient and high-pressure configurations alike. Tuga-FF is the most accurate model in the benchmark at ambient pressure, with an energy error of 13.6 meV/atom, and maintains an error of just 21 meV/atom at 150 GPa. We believe this benchmark will encourage the community to train higher-accuracy models and generate richer datasets, advancing machine-learned interatomic potentials as a whole.

</details>


### [140] [Spiral Edge States in Topological 2D Sb2Te3 Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14905)
*Timothy Carlson,Daniel Lazega,Scott McGuire,Mihirangi Medahinne,Motahhare Mirhosseini,Gabriel Marcus,ChaoChao Dun,Maria Kolesnik-Gray,Vojislav Krstic,David Carroll*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究孪晶/螺旋状二维 Sb2Te3 拓扑绝缘体晶体的边缘态导电，发现由拓扑缺陷连接的螺旋及打结边缘通道可连续导电，并在螺旋结构中观测到 0.155 eV 的边缘局域激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维拓扑绝缘体中几何结构、边界条件与拓扑缺陷如何改变边缘态色散，并尝试通过调控系统量子几何来操纵边缘态能带。

Method: 结合低损耗电子能量损失谱（EELS）、磁力显微镜（MFM）和传统载流子输运测量。

Result: 晶体支持两条空间连续的螺旋且打结边缘导电通道，通道通过一个拓扑缺陷相连；该几何引入的边界条件修改了边缘态色散，并在螺旋中识别出 0.155 eV 的特征激发，似乎与带隙内两个孤立态相关且局域于晶体边缘。

Conclusion: 通过操纵系统量子几何来修改边缘态能带，可能为二维拓扑体系的应用开辟新途径。

Abstract: This work explores the edge state conduction properties of twinned and spiraled, 2D Sb2Te3 topological insulator crystals. We show the structure supports spatially continuous edge states within two spiral and knotted edge conduction channels. The channels are connected through a topological defect. This geometry introduces boundary conditions into the 2D crystal that modifies the edge state dispersion. Using a combination of low loss Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy, Magnetic Force Microscopy and traditional carrier transport measurements we identify a distinct excitation at 0.155 eV in the spiral that seems to be related to two isolated states within the gap. This state is localized to the crystal edges. The modification of edge state bands using manipulation of a systems quantum geometry may open new avenues for the use of 2D topological systems.

</details>


### [141] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of the Physical Properties of Layered Y2GaB and Y2InB MAB Phases: A Comparative First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14908)
*M. Rabi Us Sani Shapon,Md. Asif Afzal,Jubair Hossan Abir,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算比较Y2GaB与Y2InB在0–20 GPa下的压力响应，发现Y2GaB在0–10 GPa力学稳定且性能随压缩增强，而Y2InB仅在常压稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAB相层状三元硼化物由面内强M–B键和较弱层间作用决定其稳定性与功能，需理解静水压力及Ga/In取代如何调控层间/面内相互作用与结构-性能关系。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，在0–20 GPa静水压力下系统比较Y2GaB和Y2InB的结构、弹性、热物理、电子、成键、光学和振动性质，并结合弹性判据、SOC、费米面、电荷密度/重叠及声子谱分析。

Result: Y2GaB在0、5、10 GPa满足弹性稳定，Y2InB仅常压稳定；在Y2GaB稳定区间，加压提高弹性模量、声速、德拜温度和晶格热导率并降低弹性各向异性。两者为多带金属，SOC改变局部色散但不打开能隙；Y2GaB在0–10 GPa保持9个费米面片，Y2InB常压为8个，未发生压力诱导拓扑重构。Y–B键为混合离子-共价并在离域金属背景中，光学参数各向异性；原胞声子谱在0和10 GPa为正频，但扩胞计算显示低频敏感。

Conclusion: 研究确定了两种硼化物机械可行的压力窗口，并阐明Ga/In取代驱动的结构-性能关系，为压力调控MAB相功能提供依据。

Abstract: MAB phases are nanolaminated ternary borides in which strong in-plane M-B bonds and weaker interlayer interactions drive their distinctive stability and functionality. Hydrostatic pressure offers a direct route to tune the in-plane and interlayer interactions that govern the stability and functionality of layered Y2GaB and Y2InB borides. Here, first-principles calculations are used to compare their structural, elastic, thermophysical, electronic, bonding, optical, and vibrational responses from 0 to 20 GPa. Elastic criteria show that Y2GaB is stable at 0, 5, and 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB is stable only at ambient pressure. Within the stable Y2GaB range, compression increases the elastic moduli, acoustic velocities, Debye temperature, and lattice thermal conductivity while reducing the elastic anisotropy. Both compounds are multiband metals and spin-orbit coupling modifies local dispersions without opening a gap. Y2GaB retains nine Fermi-surface sheets from 0 to 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB has eight at ambient pressure, with no pressure-induced topological reconstruction. Charge-density and overlap analyses indicate mixed ionic-covalent Y-B bonding within a delocalized metallic background. Optical parameters are anisotropic. Unit-cell phonon spectra contain positive frequencies at 0 and 10 GPa, although expanded-supercell calculations reveal low-frequency sensitivity. These results establish mechanically viable pressure windows and clarify substitution-driven structure-property relationships for these borides.

</details>


### [142] [EpNet-Si: Atomic Form Factors Learned from Band Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14992)
*Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 EpNet-Si：一种用于硅的经验赝势方法（EPM），用等变网络从局域环境预测原子平面波形状因子，并由少量 DFT 数据训练，实现快速且可迁移的能带计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 EPM 每个 k 点只需一次对角化且无需自洽，但固定的原子形状因子难以在不同晶体结构间迁移，限制了其通用性。

Method: EpNet-Si 对每个原子预测平面波形状因子，包括元素势以及单极、偶极、四极修正，并通过学习到的路由器按原子密度混合，同时加入 s/p 非局域投影子。模型仅 6,195 个参数，通过本征求解器端到端训练，参考数据仅为 77 个晶胞的 DFT 能带能量。

Result: 在训练结构类型的 231 个畸变中，142 个有带隙晶胞的平均能带和带隙误差分别为 15 meV 和 8 meV；在 192 个未见结构中，165 个有带隙晶胞的平均误差分别为 38 meV 和 49 meV。由 EpNet-Si 得到的有效质量、带隙压力系数、形变势和费米能级态密度与参考 DFT 结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，经典 EPM 在少量参考数据上拟合后，可扩展为现代电子能带结构计算的快速替代方法。

Abstract: The empirical pseudopotential method (EPM) computes band structures with one diagonalization per $k$ point and no self-consistency, but its fixed atomic form factors do not transfer between crystal structures. To address this limitation, we introduce EpNet-Si, an EPM for silicon in which an equivariant network predicts each atom's plane-wave form factor from its local environment: a species potential plus monopole, dipole and quadrupole corrections, mixed by a learned router over atomic densities, together with an $s/p$ nonlocal projector. With 6{,}195 parameters, the model is trained through the eigensolver on reference density functional theory (DFT) band energies of only 77 cells. Among 231 distortions of the trained structure types, the 142 gapped cells have mean band and gap errors of 15 and 8\,meV; among 192 unseen structures, the 165 gapped cells have mean errors of 38 and 49\,meV. Physical properties derived from EpNet-Si, including effective masses, gap pressure coefficients, deformation potentials and the density of states at the Fermi level, agree with those obtained from the reference DFT calculations. These results suggest that the classical EPM, fitted to a small set of reference data, can be extended into a fast alternative for modern electronic band-structure calculations.

</details>


### [143] [DiffGA: Steering Frozen Diffusion Models for Materials Design via Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14999)
*Shivang Agarwal,Rodrigo Wang,Kevin Ryczko,Aayush R. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DiffGA 是一种免重训练的反向材料设计框架，将进化搜索注入预训练扩散模型的采样轨迹，并用贝叶斯代理加速黑盒目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒、不可微或动态变化的目标函数使生成模型难以引导，现有方法需重训练/微调或依赖对梯度噪声脆弱的可微引导。

Method: 在扩散采样中演化部分去噪状态，用任意黑盒 oracle 评估；利用 MLFF 图嵌入在线拟合高斯过程代理，并按不确定性感知采集规则替代昂贵性质评估。

Result: 在三个晶体设计任务（冻结 MatterGen 骨干）上，DiffGA 在 oracle 评估的稳定、独特、新颖命中率上优于扩散、算力匹配、纯进化和微调基线；DFT 表明 MLFF oracle 在部分区域可靠但有系统偏差；顶级命中通过第一性原理验证。

Conclusion: DiffGA 实现了免重训练、低成本的黑盒目标导向生成，代理加速流程在保持物理真实性的同时显著降低 oracle 成本，但需关注代理保真度。

Abstract: Steering candidate generation toward user-defined objectives is central to inverse materials design, yet remains difficult when objectives are black-box, changing, or non-differentiable, as in many simulation- and experiment-derived properties. Generative models offer a solution but typically require retraining or fine-tuning for each new objective, demanding labeled data and substantial compute, or rely on differentiable guidance that is brittle to gradient noise and undefined for many practical targets. We present DiffGA, a retraining-free framework that injects evolutionary search into the sampling trajectory of a retrained diffusion model, evolving partially denoised states under an arbitrary black-box oracle. Because oracle evaluation often dominates campaign cost, we couple DiffGA with an on-the-fly Bayesian surrogate that reuses representations from state-of-the-art machine-learned force fields (MLFFs). A Gaussian process is fit during the campaign on the force fields's graph embeddings and, once it demonstrably ranks an unseen generation, replaces expensive property evaluations under an uncertainty-aware acquisition rule, reducing oracle cost with negligible loss of accuracy. Across three crystal-design tasks using a frozen MatterGen backbone, DiffGA outperforms diffusion-only, compute-matched, purely evolutionary, and fine-tuned baselines in oracle-assessed rates of stable, unique, and novel hits. Rather than proposing scientific compounds, we use density functional theory to quantify MLFF oracle fidelity, showing that it is a faithful proxy in key regimes but systematically biased in others, delineating when proxy-driven generative discovery can be trusted. On a representative sample, DiffGA's top hits survive first principles re-evaluation as physically realistic hits, while the surrogate-accelerated pipeline preserves this fidelity at a fraction of the cost.

</details>


### [144] [Graphene-Confined Growth of Few-Layer PtSe2 via One-Step Selenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15022)
*Timea Ema Kraj\v{c}ovi\v{c}ov\'a,Peter Kotrusz,Viera Sk\'akalov\'a,Ag\'ata Lauren\v{c}\'ikov\'a,Igor P\'i\v{s},Martin Hulman,Kimmo Mustonen,Michaela Sojkov\'a*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用氧化石墨烯涂覆 Pt 层并在硒气氛中退火，通过石墨烯介导的物理限域抑制 PtSe2 的垂直团簇生长，在厘米级基底上实现少层、结构有序的 PtSe2 薄膜及石墨烯限域异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PtSe2 具有厚度依赖性质，从体相半金属转变为少层半导体，在下一代电子和光电子器件中前景广阔；但大面积超薄 PtSe2 的可扩展制备仍困难，常规预沉积 Pt 层硒化易导致不期望的三维团簇生长。

Method: 在 Pt 层上涂覆氧化石墨烯（GO），随后在硒气氛中退火，使 PtSe2 形成与 GO 还原为石墨烯同步发生；石墨烯覆盖层作为物理限域层抑制垂直生长，促进横向连接，并在厘米级基底上合成石墨烯限域 PtSe2 异质结构；采用 STEM 及温度分辨原位原子分辨率测量研究硒化温度对生长机制和结构演化的影响。

Result: 石墨烯限域可稳定横向生长，促进少层 PtSe2 薄膜形成并改善结构有序性；STEM 观察到 PtSe2 区域的横向连接，并在厘米级基底上实现石墨烯限域 PtSe2 异质结构。

Conclusion: 该石墨烯辅助限域策略不仅适用于 PtSe2，也为制备和稳定超薄过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）层提供了通用方法，尤其有利于易环境降解材料，为研究其本征性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Platinum diselenide (PtSe$_2$), a member of the transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) family, is a promising material for next-generation electronic and optoelectronic applications due to its thickness-dependent properties, transitioning from semimetallic in bulk to semiconducting behavior at the few-layer limit. However, the scalable fabrication of large-area ultrathin PtSe$_2$ films remains challenging, as conventional selenization of pre-deposited Pt layers often leads to undesired three-dimensional clustered growth. This work explores a novel strategy for promoting two-dimensional growth of PtSe$_2$ by employing a graphene-mediated confinement approach. The method involves coating a Pt layer with graphene oxide (GO), followed by annealing in a selenium atmosphere, which results in the simultaneous formation of PtSe$_2$ and the reduction of GO to graphene. The graphene overlayer acts as a physical confinement layer, suppressing vertical growth and enabling the synthesis of graphene-confined PtSe$_2$ heterostructures on centimetre-scale substrates and promoting lateral connectivity of PtSe$_2$ regions observed by STEM. Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM), including temperature-resolved in situ atomic-resolution measurements, was used to investigate the influence of selenization temperature on the growth mechanism and structural evolution of PtSe$_2$ films. The results show that graphene confinement stabilizes lateral growth and promotes the formation of few-layer films with improved structural ordering. Beyond PtSe$_2$, this graphene-assisted approach provides a general strategy for fabricating and stabilizing ultrathin TMD layers, including those prone to environmental degradation, providing a new outlook for enabling detailed studies of their intrinsic properties.

</details>


### [145] [Role of defect trapping in the enhanced corrosion resistance of Ni-Mo alloys with yttrium and yttrium oxide additions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15089)
*Jia-Hong Ke,Xinchang Zhang,Jingfan Yang,Qiufeng Yang,Ruchi Gakhar,Rongjie Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出通过稀Y溶质与Y2O3弥散相捕获空位、抑制空位介导原子扩散，从而降低Ni合金在氟化物熔盐中的腐蚀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进熔盐堆中氟化物盐会持续溶解结构合金，腐蚀是关键挑战；现有缓解策略多强调热力学稳定性，对原子输运的动力学控制关注较少。

Method: 结合DFT、自洽平均场扩散建模和杂质捕获模型，量化缺陷捕获对有效空位迁移率的影响；并在750°C氟化物熔盐中测试Ni-Mo（NM）、Y改性（NMY）和含Y2O3（NMYO）合金。

Result: 强第一近邻空位-Y结合、离格空位构型以及升高的旋转和解离势垒显著抑制空位扩散率；Y2O3相关位点作为固定陷阱进一步减少可移动空位。腐蚀穿透深度按NM→NMY→NMYO顺序降低，净失重从NM的0.52%降至NMY的0.23%和NMYO的0.09%。

Conclusion: 结果与缺陷捕获导致合金侧输运减弱一致，表明点缺陷工程是面向极端核环境的一种有前景的合金设计策略。

Abstract: Corrosion of structural alloys remains a critical challenge for advanced molten salt reactors, where aggressive fluoride salts promote continuous dissolution at the salt-metal interface. While most mitigation strategies emphasize thermodynamic stability, kinetic control of atomic transport has received comparatively less attention. Here, we propose a corrosion mitigation strategy based on diffusion control and vacancy trapping by dilute yttrium (Y) solutes and yttrium oxide (Y$_2$O$_3$) dispersions in Ni alloys. A combined computational and experimental approach integrates density functional theory (DFT), self-consistent mean-field (SCMF) diffusion modeling, and an impurity trapping model to quantify the impact of defect trapping on effective vacancy mobility. Strong first-nearest-neighbor vacancy-Y binding, off-lattice vacancy configurations, and elevated rotational and dissociation barriers result in pronounced suppression of vacancy diffusivity, while Y$_2$O$_3$-associated sites treated as fixed traps in the model further reduce the mobile vacancy population. Ni-Mo (NM), Y-modified (NMY), and Y$_2$O$_3$-containing (NMYO) alloys were tested in molten fluoride salt at 750{\deg}C. Corrosion penetration decreases systematically from NM to NMY to NMYO, consistent with the predicted reduction in vacancy-mediated transport. The same trend is observed in mass change, with net weight loss decreasing from 0.52% for NM to 0.23% for NMY and 0.09% for NMYO. These results are consistent with an alloy-side transport contribution from defect trapping and reduced atomic transport. This study therefore suggests point defect engineering as a promising alloy design strategy for extreme nuclear environments.

</details>


### [146] [Interfacial Coupling Reshapes Nonlinear Polarization in a Spin-Defect van der Waals Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15189)
*Lena Dalifoski,Eveline Mayner,Poonam Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用偏振分辨SHG成像研究hBN/PbI2异质界面，发现PbI2使VB-缺陷的SHG各向异性转变为四重对称并增强六倍，将自旋缺陷敏化与界面修饰的有效二阶非线性极化率关联起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结为将光学活性层与二维自旋传感器耦合提供了直接途径，但其界面处的相干非线性响应仍缺乏解析。

Method: 采用偏振分辨二次谐波产生（SHG）成像，区分天然hBN、含负电硼空位（VB-）的中子辐照hBN、PbI2及其完全重叠区域，并测量SHG的功率依赖与偏振依赖。

Result: SHG强度随入射功率二次增长，表明以二阶光学过程为主。加入PbI2后，VB-原本畸变的SHG各向异性转变为以四重对称为主，且SHG比天然hBN单独强六倍，呈现非加和非线性响应；同一层状结构还表现出缺陷光致发光增强。

Conclusion: 该非加和界面非线性响应将自旋缺陷敏化与界面修饰的有效二阶非线性极化率联系起来，为异质界面非线性光学与量子传感耦合提供了依据。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures offer a direct route to couple an optically active layer to a two-dimensional spin sensor, yet their coherent nonlinear response at interfaces remains poorly resolved. Here, we use polarization-resolved second harmonic-generation imaging to resolve four distinct regions of a hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) -based heterostructure, native hBN, neutron-irradiated hBN containing negatively charged boron vacancies (VB-), PbI2 and their complete overlap. The detected intensity scales quadratically with incident power, consistent with a dominant second-order optical process. Polarization scans show that adding PbI2 transforms the distorted VB- SHG anisotropy into a predominantly fourfold response with a sixfold stronger SHG than native hBN alone. This non-additive nonlinear response occurs in the same layer architecture also shown to enhance defect photoluminescence, linking spin-defect sensitization to an interface-modified effective second-order susceptibility.

</details>


### [147] [Nucleation at the GST--oxide interface and beam-driven ordering in amorphous Ge--Sb--Te: HAADF, EDS and electron pair distribution functions across a 4D-STEM dose series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15196)
*Mridul Kumar,Kirill Sobolev,Alexander Upcher,Yevgeny Rakita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对磁控溅射GST薄膜进行连续4D-STEM、HAADF与EDS联用分析，发现晶核并非成分或密度异常区，而是优先出现在GST/氧化物界面附近；电子辐照在损伤薄膜的同时使其短程结构轻微有序化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶相变材料中晶核形成位置决定其开关行为，但晶核是否具有不同于基体的成分或密度，以及界面在成核中的作用仍不明确。

Method: 对GST层及其与下方氧化物界面进行6次连续4D-STEM扫描，同步采集HAADF和EDS。采用HAADF强度、I(q)相映射、全衍射图PCA和自监督SimCLR编码器四种方式分类；以Au帽层校准倒空间后比较电子PDF。分析在作者开发的PySTEMLab交互式4D-STEM环境中完成。

Result: 晶核与基体遵循同一HAADF-EDS关系，固定HAADF时EDS信号散布等于泊松计数噪声（比值1.03±0.05），说明成核位点无成分或数密度差异。晶核为晶态，在4D-STEM最大强度虚拟图中显著（中位z=11.0），但在HAADF（z=1.3）和EDS（z=0.3）中不显著。所有体相类别共享同一短程结构，第一壳2.70–2.74 Å；靠近界面时FSDP移向低q，各方法均分离出薄界面边缘环境，其第一壳更短或缺失。I(q)分类在每次扫描中都识别出含62–100%晶核像素的界面环境（比深度高1.2–2.5倍），且去除晶核像素后仍存在。剂量达4.7e3 e/Å²时，HAADF信号下降17%，第一壳缩短0.03 Å，FSDP窄化9%，表现为损伤伴随有序化。

Conclusion: 晶核形成位置由界面环境而非成核体积的独特成分或密度决定；电子辐照在损伤GST薄膜的同时会使其短程结构趋于有序。

Abstract: Where crystal nuclei form in amorphous phase-change materials decides how they switch. We study six successive 4D-STEM scans, with simultaneous HAADF and EDS data, of a magnetron-sputtered Ge-Sb-Te (GST) film, restricted to the GST layer and its interface with the oxide beneath it. Nucleation sites do not differ in composition or number density: the nuclei lie on the same robust HAADF-EDS relation as the matrix, and at fixed HAADF the spread of the summed EDS signal equals its Poisson counting noise (ratio 1.03 +- 0.05). The nuclei are nevertheless crystalline and stand out in the 4D-STEM max-intensity virtual image (median z = 11.0) but not in HAADF (z = 1.3) or EDS (z = 0.3). We classify the film in four ways (HAADF intensity, I(q) phase mapping, PCA of the whole diffraction patterns and a self-supervised SimCLR encoder) and compare the electron pair distribution functions (PDFs) of all classes after calibrating reciprocal space on the Au cap. All bulk classes share one short-range structure (first shell 2.70-2.74 {\AA}); towards the interface the first sharp diffraction peak (FSDP) moves to lower q, and every method isolates a thin interface-edge environment with a shorter or missing first shell. The I(q) classification isolates, in every scan, an interface environment that holds 62-100% of the nucleus pixels (1.2-2.5-fold above depth) and persists when the nucleus pixels are removed before classification. The interface, rather than a distinct composition or density of the nucleating volume, therefore marks where nuclei form. Over 4.7e3 e/{\AA}^2 the HAADF signal falls by 17%, the first shell shortens by 0.03 {\AA} and the FSDP narrows by 9%, apparently ordering the film as it damages it. All analyses used PySTEMLab, an interactive 4D-STEM analysis environment developed for this work.

</details>


### [148] [Framework for an AI-Ready ARPES Community Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15236)
*Zhi-Xun Shen,Jin Gene Wong,Karen Lei,Leon Garcia,Felix Baumberger,Sergey Borisenko,Aaron Bostwick,Ting Cao,Tanny Chavez,Yulin Chen,Monika Choudhary,Andrea Damascelli,Dan Dessau,Thomas P. Devereaux,Sandy A. Ekahana,Alexei Fedorov,Atsushi Fujimori,Yucheng Guo,M. Zahid Hasan,Makoto Hashimoto,Yu He,Philip Hofmann,Zahid Hussain,Harold Y. Hwang,Zhurun Ji,Chunjing Jia,Timur K. Kim,Phil D. C. King,Takeshi Kondo,Alessandra Lanzara,Chun Lin,Donghui Lu,Matteo Michiardi,Sung-Kwan Mo,Brian Moritz,Laurenz Rettig,Eli Rotenberg,Takafumi Sato,Kyle M. Shen,Jonathan A. Sobota,Inna Vishik,Shuolong Yang,Ming Yi,Timothy Zaklama*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该Perspective基于一次社区研讨会，提出为ARPES构建共享数据生态系统，把四十年分散的电子结构谱学数据转化为持久、集体的科学记忆，以支持可复现性、数据复用和AI驱动的量子材料发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理可从AI中深度受益，但当前以论文发表为中心的工作流仅保留了高度筛选的ARPES实验记录，导致数据碎片化且缺乏上下文，阻碍了数据驱动与AI应用。

Method: 报告近期社区研讨会成果，借鉴已有科学数据库经验，评估ARPES中AI应用现状，并围绕数据组织、访问、署名、治理及分阶段试点计划提出实践路径。

Result: 提出共享数据生态系统的三阶段收益：近期建立标准化、可搜索、可引用数据集以强化分析流程；中期发展社区验证工具，实现跨实验室复用与更闭环的自动化实验；长期构建多样化电子谱语料，支撑量子物质基础模型。

Conclusion: 社区应在数据组织、访问、信用、治理和试点项目上形成共识并推进共享ARPES数据生态，从而建立持久科学记忆，促进可复现、可复用和AI赋能的新发现。

Abstract: Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure underlying material properties, Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) has accumulated over four decades of spectra, yet the community's publication-centered workflow preserves only a highly selected fraction of its experimental record.
