A shared data ecosystem could transform these fragmented measurements and their contexts into a persistent, collective scientific memory, creating immediate value for reproducibility and reuse while laying the foundation for AI-enabled discovery. In this Perspective, we report outcomes of a recent community workshop and propose a practical path toward such an ecosystem. Its benefits unfold along three timescales. In the near term, standardized, searchable, and citable datasets strengthen analysis pipelines independently of AI. In the intermediate future, community-validated tools operating across laboratories could enable broader reuse and increasingly closed-loop automated experiments. In the long term, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter. Looking forward, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter and new discoveries. We discuss lessons from existing scientific databases, the current state of AI in ARPES, as well as areas of community convergence on data organization, access, credit, governance, and a staged pilot program.

</details>


### [149] [Three-dimensional reconstruction of weakly scattering objects from single-defocus 4D-STEM data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15303)
*Kousuke Ooe,Yufan Shen,Yuichi Shimakawa,Daisuke Kan,Scott D. Findlay*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出3D结构线性反演（3D LINV）STEM算法，可从单离焦4D-STEM数据快速、直接地重建三维原子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D STEM重建方法中，倾转/离焦序列ADF更适合较重原子，而基于动力学散射模型反演的单离焦4D-STEM数据后处理计算成本高。

Method: 在厚弱相位物体近似下，建立测量的4D-STEM数据与未知三维样品势之间的线性方程，并通过求解该线性方程实现单离焦4D-STEM的3D结构线性反演。

Result: 通过系统4D-STEM仿真考察样品厚度、部分相干性和散粒噪声等观测参数，验证方法性能；并实验重建叠层自支撑膜，获得原子级横向分辨率和纳米级深度分辨率。

Conclusion: 3D LINV STEM为单离焦4D-STEM提供了一种快速、直接的三维结构重建途径，具备原子级横向分辨率和纳米级深度分辨能力。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) atomic-structure analysis is of central importance in (scanning) transmission electron microscopy (STEM). STEM techniques to reconstruct 3D structure include tilt-series or defocus-series annular dark-field, best suited to heavier atomic species, and dynamical-scattering-model inversion applied to single-defocus four-dimensional (4D) STEM data, requiring computationally expensive post-experiment analysis. In this study, we propose a 3D-structure linear inversion (3D LINV) STEM algorithm, which enables a fast and direct reconstruction of 3D structures from a single-defocus 4D-STEM dataset. This method is based on solving linear equations relating the measured 4D-STEM data and the unknown 3D specimen potential under the thick weak phase object approximation. We show the capabilities of 3D LINV STEM through systematic 4D-STEM simulations across various observation parameters such as specimen thickness, partial coherence, and shot noise, and through an experimental demonstration of 3D structure reconstruction of a stacked freestanding membrane with atomic-scale lateral resolution and nanometre-scale depth resolution.

</details>


### [150] [Programmable memristive circuits for reconfigurable collective pseudomagnetic ordering, transport, and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15346)
*Sichang Qiu,Yuxin Jiang,Yongchao Wu,Peng Han,Xinxin Gao,Zekun Huang,Ce Shang,Ronny Thomale,Tie Jun Cui,Shuo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作用可编程连接的相同忆阻单元构建模块化电路，将二值电导态视为Ising型赝自旋，通过带符号反馈调控集体序，并实现铁磁、反铁磁与交错磁输运对称性及储备池计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程硬件通常架构固定，而复杂物理系统通过可复用局域单元的不同互连规则产生多样行为；因此需要把忆阻器的可写状态与非线性动力学转化为可重构集体序和对称性主导的输运。

Method: 采用相同忆阻单元及可编程连接构成模块化电路；实验中将二值电导态编码为Ising型赝自旋，并用单元间带符号反馈选择均匀或交错赝自旋序；结合电路模型模拟畴壁运动与图案恢复；利用对称编码器将测得电导图案、子晶格标签和选定对称规则配置固定输运层；并通过器件校准模拟评估储备池计算。

Result: 实验表明带符号反馈可选择均匀或交错赝自旋序；模拟展示可控畴壁运动和图案恢复；对称编码器实现铁磁、反铁磁和交错磁输运对称性的电路类比，其中反铁磁与交错磁编码共享补偿图案但电压扩散不同，说明可编程电路对称性影响输运；器件校准模拟显示忆阻动力学可产生可用于储备池计算的历史依赖特征。

Conclusion: 通过分离局域记忆、可编程相互作用、对称编码和任务特定读出，该架构提供了一条从器件动力学到集体序、对称依赖输运及学习的模块化路径。

Abstract: Programmable hardware typically uses fixed architectures, whereas complex physical systems obtain diverse behavior by connecting reusable local elements through different interaction rules. Memristive devices combine writable states with nonlinear dynamics, but translating these local properties into reconfigurable collective order and symmetry-governed transport remains challenging. Here we develop modular circuits configured through programmable connections between identical memristive units. Experiments establish binary conductance states as Ising-type pseudospins and show that signed feedback between units selects uniform or staggered pseudospin order. Circuit-model simulations then examine controllable domain-wall motion and pattern recovery. A symmetry encoder combines measured conductance patterns with sublattice labels and a selected symmetry rule to configure a fixed transport layer, implementing circuit analogs of ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and altermagnetic transport symmetries. Although the antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic encodings share a compensated pattern, their distinct voltage spreading illustrates how programmed circuit symmetry affects transport. Device-calibrated simulations show that memristive dynamics generates history-dependent features for reservoir computing. By separating local memory from programmable interactions, symmetry encoding and task-specific readout, this architecture provides a modular route from device dynamics to collective order, symmetry-dependent transport, and learning.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancement of local magnetic ordering temperature due to non-magnetic disorder in anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15622)
*Soumya Bhowmik,Suvayan Saha,Saikat Debnath,Arka Patra,Biswarup Satpati,Maxim Avdeev,Shishir Kumar Pandey,Chandan Mazumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在反Heusler合金Al2MnCu中，非磁性原子的小幅位点无序可通过增强交换相互作用提高居里温度，而大量磁性Mn原子无序对Tc影响很小，揭示了无序对磁性材料的反直觉作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构无序通常被认为不利于磁性，但磁性原子排布与非磁性原子介导的交换环境共同决定磁性质，因此磁性无序与非磁性无序的各自作用尚不清楚；Al2MnCu同时具有约50%涉及Mn的B2型同对称位点无序和约5%涉及Al/Cu的异对称位点无序，为解耦二者作用提供了平台。

Method: 围绕居里温度Tc进行磁临界分析，并结合直流磁测量与高阶交流磁化率测量；同时通过理论分析区分磁性原子无序和非磁性原子无序对交换相互作用及Tc的影响。

Result: 临界分析未得到标准铁磁体的单一普适类，有效临界指数随温度非单调变化，表明存在类似非均匀铁磁体的复杂相互作用；直流磁测量和高阶交流磁化率证实Tc以上仍存在局域有序磁团簇；理论表明，少量非磁性无序增强交换相互作用并提高局域磁有序温度，而更大程度的磁性原子无序几乎不影响Tc。

Conclusion: Al2MnCu的实验与理论结合揭示了非磁性无序诱导Tc升高的反常现象，说明无序在某些磁性材料中可以产生反直觉的增强作用，并为理解无序在磁性中的角色提供了范例。

Abstract: Structural disorder is generally regarded as detrimental to magnetism. Since magnetic properties are governed by both the arrangement of magnetic atoms and the crystal environment, including non-magnetic atoms that mediate exchange interactions, the distinct role of magnetic and non-magnetic disorder remains less understood. The recently reported anti-Heusler alloy, Al$_2$MnCu, forming with $\sim$50\% intra-symmetry site disorder (B2) involving the magnetic Mn atom and $\sim$5\% inter-symmetry site disorder associated with non-magnetic Al and Cu atoms, offers a good platform to disentangle the individual roles of magnetic and non-magnetic atomic disorder. Our magnetic critical analysis, in the vicinity of the Curie temperature ($T_{\rm C}$), does not lead to any conventional single universality class expected for a standard ferromagnet; instead, the non-monotonic temperature dependence of effective critical exponents points towards more complex magnetic interactions, akin to inhomogeneous ferromagnets. Dc magnetometry complemented by higher-harmonic ac magnetic susceptibility data, further confirms the persistence of locally ordered magnetic clusters above $T_{\rm C}$. Theoretical analysis establishes that the enhancement of the local magnetic ordering temperature arises directly from the strengthening of exchange interactions due to the small degree of non-magnetic disorder, whereas $T_{\rm C}$ is hardly influenced by the much larger level of magnetic-atom disorder. The combined experimental and theoretical investigations on the anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu reveal and explain the unusual observation of nonmagnetic disorder-induced elevation of $T_{\rm C}$, providing a paradigm for the counterintuitive role of disorder in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [152] [Hidden Spin-Valley Hall Effect and Gate-Switchable Electric Hall Effect in 2D Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15630)
*Jiajun Lu,Mu Tian,Chaoxi Cui,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao,Run-Wu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在具有自旋-谷-层耦合（SVLC）的二维交错磁体中，门电场可打破层极化相反谷间的对称补偿，把隐藏的自旋谷霍尔响应转化为可开关的净电荷霍尔信号（EHE），并给出候选材料与第一性原理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体的电荷霍尔响应因自旋向上/向下霍尔电流符号相反而相互抵消；这一隐藏响应若与不同谷锁定，则可形成自旋谷霍尔效应，但通常被对称性掩蔽，需要寻找可电控揭示该响应的机制与材料。

Method: 提出并利用自旋-谷-层耦合（SVLC）机制与共线自旋层群（cSLG）理论，对全部158个交错磁cSLG进行分类，筛选支持SVLC的32个群，并结合第一性原理计算研究候选单层Mg2Os2(SO5)2。

Result: 门电场可解除相反层极化谷间的对称补偿，产生门可切换的EHE；分类得到32个支持SVLC的cSLG，预测Mg2Os2(SO5)2和Ca(CoN)2族为候选材料；第一性原理显示Mg2Os2(SO5)2具有可逆谷分裂、门可切换霍尔响应，以及超过1 mrad且随EHE信号反号的Kerr旋转。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了通过对称性实现全电控谷-自旋操控的路径，并表明交错磁体是高性能谷输运与电可切换磁光器件的潜在平台。

Abstract: Hidden beneath the vanishing charge Hall response of altermagnets are spin-up and spin-down Hall currents of opposite sign---concealed, not absent---whose cancellation is enforced by symmetry. When these spin states are locked to distinct valleys, the hidden currents constitute a spin-valley Hall effect. Here we show that in 2D altermagnets with spin-valley-layer coupling (SVLC), a gate field unveils this hidden spin-valley Hall response.~By lifting the symmetry-enforced compensation between valleys with opposite layer polarizations, the field converts the hidden response into a net charge Hall signal, yielding a gate-switchable electric Hall effect (EHE) enabled by SVLC. Using collinear spin layer group (cSLG) theory, we classify all 158 altermagnetic cSLGs, identify 32 that support SVLC, and predict monolayer Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ and the Ca(CoN)$_2$-family as candidate materials. First-principles calculations for Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ reveal reversible valley splitting, a gate-switchable Hall response, and a Kerr rotation exceeding 1 mrad whose sign reverses together with the EHE signal. These results establish a symmetry-based route to all-electrical valley-spin control and identify altermagnets as promising platforms for high-performance valley transport and electrically switchable magneto-optics.

</details>


### [153] [Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15683)
*Regina Burganova,Irina Piyanzina,Ruslan Akhmerov,Alexander Sigov,Maxim Fedorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种机器学习工作流，通过分别预测Curie-Weiss温度和Neel温度来间接预测磁挫败指数，并筛选潜在受挫磁化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁挫败通常用frustration index量化，但多数材料无法同时获得Curie-Weiss和Neel温度，因此直接预测磁挫败指数仍具挑战。

Method: 构建基于成分的CatBoost模型，使用实验数据训练，并通过化学信息驱动的数据集划分优化；分别预测Neel和Curie-Weiss温度，利用集成不确定性构建保守frustration index以提高候选筛选可靠性，并筛选约67000个Materials Project化合物。

Result: 稀土氧化物模型对Neel温度预测最优，稀土模型对Curie-Weiss温度预测最优；筛选出20个高置信度受挫磁候选，包括若干已知受挫磁体和此前未探索的氧化物体系。

Conclusion: 该间接机器学习策略可有效预测磁挫败指数并筛选潜在受挫磁材料，为后续实验研究提供候选体系。

Abstract: Magnetic frustration is commonly quantified by the frustration index, calculated from the Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. Because both quantities are not simultaneously available for most materials, direct prediction of the magnetic frustration index remains challenging. Here, we propose a machine learning workflow for indirect prediction of the frustration index and consequent screening of potential frustrated magnetic compounds through independent prediction of Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. In our work, composition-based models implemented via the CatBoost algorithm, trained on experimental data were optimized using chemically informed dataset subdivision. The highest predictive accuracy was achieved for the rare earth-oxide model in predicting Neel and for the rare earth model in predicting Curie-Weiss. Ensemble uncertainty was incorporated through a conservative frustration index to improve the reliability of candidate selection. Screening of approximately 67000 Materials Project compounds identified 20 high-confidence frustrated magnetic candidates, including several experimentally known frustrated magnets and previously unexplored oxide systems potential for further investigation.

</details>


### [154] [Reversible intrinsic-extrinsic crossover of the anomalous Hall effect in PrAlSi via Al/Si ratio tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15726)
*Huiyang Yang,Xiaohua Luo,Hongwei You,Wei Ren,Yuntao Gao,Changcai Chen,Zhishuo Zhang,Chunsheng Fang,Shengcan Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在PrAlSi单晶中，通过偏离化学计量比调控Al/Si比例，可逆地实现反常霍尔效应从内禀到外禀再到内禀的机制交叉，并伴随非单调的巨大反常霍尔电导率变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性Weyl半金属中的反常霍尔效应可由内禀Berry曲率或外禀散射机制主导，但在同一材料平台内可逆切换这两种机制仍难以实现。

Method: 通过改变Al/Si比例（约1.05至0.75）制备四组偏离化学计量比的PrAlSi单晶，并测量其反常霍尔电导率、载流子迁移率和输运类型。

Result: Al/Si变化跨越PrAl0.93Si0.89至PrAl0.79Si1.06时，反常霍尔电导率呈非单调拱形：170、1413、507、164 Ω^-1cm^-1；中间组分PrAl0.90Si0.94（Al/Si约0.96）具有高迁移率和多载流子输运，外禀skew-scattering主导并产生1413 Ω^-1cm^-1的巨反常霍尔电导率；富Si和贫Si组分则表现为较低的反常霍尔电导率和内禀Berry曲率主导机制。偏离中间组分还会抑制迁移率并使载流子由空穴型转为电子型。

Conclusion: Al/Si比可作为化学调控旋钮，主要通过组分带填充调节费米能级，而化学无序作用次要；偏离化学计量比的PrAlSi因此成为可通过细微组分变化可逆切换反常霍尔效应机制的可调平台。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in magnetic Weyl semimetals arises from distinct microscopic origins-intrinsic Berry curvature and extrinsic scattering mechanisms-yet reversible switching between these regimes within a single material platform remains elusive. Here, we demonstrate that off-stoichiometric tuning of the Al/Si ratio (from ~1.05 to ~0.75) reversibly drives an intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover of the AHE in PrAlSi single crystals. Across four compositions spanning a narrow Si variation (PrAl0.93Si0.89 to PrAl0.79Si1.06), the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) exhibits a non monotonic arch (170-1413- 507-164 {\Omega}-1cm-1). The intermediate composition PrAl0.90Si0.94 (Al/Si ~0.96) exhibits high carrier mobility, multi carrier transport, yielding a giant AHC of 1413 {\Omega}-1cm-1 dominated by extrinsic skew-scattering. The Si-poor and Si-rich compositions exhibit lower AHC values and intrinsic Berry-curvature-dominated AHE, establishing a composition-driven intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover. Deviating from the intermediate composition suppresses mobility and reverses carrier type from hole-like to electron-like. The Al/Si ratio thus serves as a chemical knob that primarily modulates the Fermi level via compositional band filling, while off-stoichiometry-induced chemical disorder plays a subordinate role in determining the AHE mechanism. These results position off-stoichiometric PrAlSi as a tunable platform where subtle compositional variation reversibly switches the AHE mechanism.

</details>


### [155] [Ionomer-free Iridium Anodes with Low Loading for Zero-gap Electrolyzers via Direct Atomic Layer Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15798)
*Peter M. Piechulla,Iris Blommestein,Anne van Gerven,Henri Pelzer,Ezgi Demir\"oz,Jonas Englhard,Mingliang Chen,Julien Bachmann,J. Ruud van Ommen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过定制微孔基底和两种原子层沉积（ALD）工艺制备无离聚物Ir基阳极，显著降低PEMWE贵金属载量，同时保持性能与耐久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子交换膜水电解（PEMWE）适合利用间歇性可再生电力制氢，但阳极Ir供应有限，成为规模化瓶颈，因此需降低Ir载量并提升利用率。

Method: 以Ir(EtCp)(CHD)为前驱体，在定制微孔基底上采用热ALD（O3为共反应物）和等离子体增强ALD直接沉积无离聚物催化剂层；热ALD生成主要为金属Ir/IrOx，PEALD生成IrO2，并同时制备低载量ALD阴极。

Result: 热ALD阳极Ir载量为0.21 mg/cm2，尽管比商业催化剂涂层膜阳极少7倍，PEMWE性能仍相当，并通过等效600 h间歇运行的加速耐久测试；使用低载量ALD阴极后总铂族金属载量仅0.24 mg/cm2。PEALD通过调控催化剂分布将Ir载量降至0.04 mg/cm2，但所得IrO2活性较低。直接ALD沉积无需含离聚物油墨，阳极兼容多种膜材料，并在阴离子交换膜的CO2电解实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 直接ALD催化剂沉积为低Ir载量、无离聚物PEMWE阳极提供了可行路径，并具跨膜材料与跨电解体系应用潜力，但需权衡超低载量与催化活性。

Abstract: Proton exchange membrane water electrolysis (PEMWE) is among the most promising techniques to provide hydrogen as both energy carrier and feedstock for the chemical industry, due to its compatibility with intermittent renewable electricity sources. However, a major bottleneck in scaling up PEMWE is the limited supply of the anode catalyst Ir. This work shows a novel approach to tailoring ionomer-free catalyst layers based on custom-made microporous substrates and two different atomic layer deposition (ALD) processes, both using Ir(EtCp)(CHD) precursor. Thermal ALD using O3 as counter-reactant yields mostly metallic Ir/IrOx and an electrode Ir loading of 0.21 mg/cm2. The PEMWE cell-level performance is comparable to a commercial catalyst-coated membrane anode despite 7x lower loading, and is maintained over an accelerated durability test equivalent to 600 h of intermittent operation. Moreover, using a low-loading ALD-made cathode, the total platinum group metal loading is only 0.24 mg/cm2. Plasma-enhanced ALD reduces the Ir loading to 0.04 mg/cm2 by tailoring the catalyst distribution, however, yields lower-activity IrO2. Finally, direct ALD catalyst deposition needs no ionomer-bearing ink. The anodes are therefore compatible with various membrane materials, as demonstrated in a CO2 electrolysis experiment with an anion exchange membrane.

</details>


### [156] [Latent Heat and Raman Spectrum Change on Phase Transition of Water Including Calcium Chloride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15821)
*Dongha Kim,Changyoun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用拉曼O-H伸缩振动信号追踪水相变过程中与潜热相关的能量学，并考察CaCl2杂质掺杂的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜热和相变热力学演化难以直接定量探测；O-H频移与氢键强度相关，因此拉曼光谱有望关联熵/焓变化与温度依赖的谱学特征。

Method: 在既有将O-H频移与氢键强度联系的理论框架上，将熵/焓变化与峰位和积分强度的温度依赖关系关联；在控温条件下测量纯水及CaCl2掺杂样品，并对光谱进行解卷积和指定波段积分。

Result: 拉曼指标表现出可重复的温度依赖性，可捕捉相变窗口；CaCl2诱导系统性频移，并造成表观潜热特征的可测改变。

Conclusion: 拉曼光谱可作为定量探针，用于研究水及相关体系中的热力学演化和杂质效应。

Abstract: This study investigates how Raman O-H stretching signatures may be able to track latent-heat-related energetics during water's phase transitions. Prior work on latent heat and Raman analysis of phase change is reviewed to establish theoretical approach. Building on a framework that links O-H frequency shifts to hydrogen-bond strength, we relate entropy/enthalpy variations to temperature-dependent changes in peak position and integrated intensity. Experiments on pure water and impurity-doped samples, specifically CaCl$_2$, were performed under controlled temperatures, and spectra were deconvoluted and integrated over defined bands. The Raman metrics exhibit reproducible temperature dependence that captures the transition window, while CaCl$_2$ induces systematic shifts and measurable modifications in the apparent latent-heat signature. These results highlight Raman spectroscopy as a quantitative probe of thermodynamic evolution and impurity effects in water and related systems.

</details>


### [157] [Epitaxial Oxide Thin Films with High Entropy and Anomalous Crystallinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15861)
*Joseph Petruska,Christina M. Rost,Jon-Paul Maria,Saeed S. I. Almishal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述讨论PLD如何通过非平衡生长与动力学冻结拓展高熵氧化物薄膜的组成与结构空间，重点分析岩盐型MgCoNiCuZnO及其衍生体系，并指出其可兼具化学无序与高结晶/外延质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵氧化物薄膜的多阳离子组成和亚稳结构难以通过近平衡方法实现，需要理解PLD生长动力学与外延约束如何调控各阳离子角色，以拓展缺陷、组成和功能构型空间。

Method: 综述PLD与高熵氧化物薄膜研究，以岩盐型MgCoNiCuZnO为主，结合萤石、尖晶石和钙钛矿示例；将生长动力学和外延约束与Co价态、Cu配位、异价及尺寸失配添加等关联分析。

Result: 在MgCoNiCuZnO中，化学与生长条件共同实现晶格调控和共格纳米结构；异价与尺寸失配添加扩展缺陷和组成空间；这些耦合自由度导致异常结晶性，使广泛化学无序与优异结晶质量和外延有序共存，部分体系尤其MgO上岩盐薄膜实现完全共格。

Conclusion: PLD为高熵氧化物薄膜中功能构型的发现与工程化提供机会，可用于高质量单层薄膜和异质结构。

Abstract: Pulsed laser deposition (PLD) expands the compositional and structural possibilities of high-entropy oxide (HEO) thin films through far-from-equilibrium growth and kinetic arrest of metastable configurations. This review connects growth kinetics and epitaxial constraints to the roles of individual cations in shaping the resulting films. We focus primarily on rock salt MgCoNiCuZnO and its derivatives, with selected fluorite, spinel, and perovskite examples illustrating broader applicability. In MgCoNiCuZnO, Co valence and Cu coordination illustrate how chemistry and growth conditions jointly enable lattice tuning and coherent nanostructure formation, while aliovalent and size-misfit additions expand the accessible defect and compositional landscapes. These coupled degrees of freedom underpin anomalous crystallinity, the coexistence of extensive chemical disorder with exceptional crystalline quality and epitaxial order, including full coherence in selected systems, particularly rock salts grown on MgO. This combination opens opportunities to discover and engineer functional configurations in high-quality single-layer films and heterostructures.

</details>


### [158] [Extreme anharmonicity determines the superionic transition in the prototypical antifluorite Li$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15986)
*Lorenzo Bastonero,Chiara Cignarella,Nicola Marzari,Lorenzo Monacelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合非微扰晶格动力学与机器学习势，研究反萤石结构Li2O中高温快离子相变的微观机制，指出强非谐性而非单纯体积膨胀主导该相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超离子导体具有类液态离子迁移率，是固态电池电解质的重要候选；但典型反萤石型锂离子氧化物超离子导体中快离子相变长期被归因于体积驱动的谐声子软化，忽略了快离子区固有的强晶格非谐性，相变微观起源仍未解决。

Method: 采用非微扰晶格动力学，结合第一性原理精度的机器学习原子间势，研究Li2O相变；并用含时自洽谐近似分析谱性质。

Result: 发现非谐性而非体积膨胀在决定相变温度及机制中起决定性作用；谱分析揭示该材料中准粒子图像失效，谱函数和红外响应给出相变特征，可通过非弹性中子或X射线散射及红外光谱实验检验。

Conclusion: 结果确立Li2O在超离子区附近的极端非谐性，并为理解和调控此类材料的快离子相变提供晶格动力学框架。

Abstract: Superionic conductors are promising electrolyte candidates for all-solid-state batteries, thanks to their liquid-like ionic mobility. In the prototypical lithium-ion oxide superionic conductor with antifluorite structure, the high-temperature transition to the fast-ion regime has long been attributed to volume-driven harmonic phonon softening. However, this quasi-harmonic picture neglects the strong lattice anharmonicity inherent to the fast-ion regime, leaving the microscopic origin of the transition unresolved. Here, we apply non-perturbative lattice dynamics combined with a first-principles-accurate machine-learning interatomic potential to study the phase transition in Li$_2$O. We demonstrate that anharmonicity, rather than volume expansion alone as commonly assumed, plays the decisive role in determining the transition temperature and the underlying mechanism. Finally, we analyze the spectral properties using the time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation, revealing the breakdown of the quasi-particle picture in this material. The spectral function and the infrared response provide signtures of the phase transition that can be investigated experimentally using inelastic neutron or X-ray scattering and infrared spectroscopy. All these results establish the extreme anharmonicity of Li$_2$O near the superionic regime and provide a lattice-dynamic framework for understanding and engineering the fast-ion transition in this type of materials.

</details>


### [159] [An empirical model for deriving information on ion dynamics from $\mu$SR data via the conventional Kubo-Toyabe function analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16006)
*Ryosuke Kadono*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种经验解析模型，将描述离子跳跃扩散μSR的扩展Kubo-Toyabe函数G_z^EA(t;Δ,ν_i,Q)与常规KT函数G_z^KT(t;Δ_KT,ν_KT)的参数关联，用于解释并校正常规KT拟合中出现的异常温度依赖，并从电池材料文献数据中可靠提取离子扩散的活化能和指前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用常规KT函数G_z^KT分析离子扩散μSR数据时，线宽Δ_KT(T)和涨落率ν_KT(T)会出现反常温度依赖，这些通常是近似导致的伪像，需建立模型将其与真实离子扩散参数联系起来。

Method: 推导扩展KT函数（含Edwards-Anderson参数Q）与常规KT函数之间的经验解析关系；将该关系应用于文献中电池材料的Δ_KT和ν_KT数据，并按Arrhenius方程ν_i(T)=ν_i0 e^{-E_i/kT}提取离子扩散参数。

Result: 所提公式能成功复现常规KT分析中观察到的Δ_KT(T)和ν_KT(T)反常温度依赖；由文献电池材料数据得到的活化能E_i和指前因子ν_i0与其他技术所得结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该模型把常规KT拟合中的异常伪像转化为提取离子扩散动力学的有力诊断工具，为μSR离子扩散分析提供了更可靠的参数解释方法。

Abstract: We present an empirical model that analytically relates the parameters of the extended Kubo-Toyabe (KT) function $G_z^{\rm EA}(t;\Delta,\nu_{\rm i},Q)$, which describes muon spin relaxation ($\mu$SR) induced by jump diffusion of ions using the Edwards-Anderson parameter $Q$, to those of the conventional KT function $G_z^{\rm KT}(t;\Delta_{\rm KT},\nu_{\rm KT})$ that serves as an approximation of $G_z^{\rm EA}(t)$. The proposed formulae successfully reproduce the anomalous temperature dependences of the linewidth $\Delta_{\rm KT}(T)$ and the fluctuation rate $\nu_{\rm KT}(T)$ which are artifacts observed upon analyzing $\mu$SR data on ion diffusion using $G_z^{\rm KT}(t)$. Applying this model to $\Delta_{\rm KT}$ and $\nu_{\rm KT}$ published in the literature of battery materials yields activation energy $E_{\rm i}$ and pre-exponential factor $\nu_{\rm i0}$ in the Arrhenius equation for ion diffusion, $\nu_{\rm i}(T)=\nu_{\rm i0} e^{-E_{\rm i}/kT}$, that are in excellent agreement with those deduced using other techniques, transforming peculiar artifacts into a powerful diagnostic tool.

</details>


### [160] [Nonrelativistic Optically-Induced Spin Torque in Noncollinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16052)
*Zhiyuan Li,Jian Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过非共线磁构型和非相对论自旋劈裂，实现了光诱导的更强自旋力矩，将光自旋电子学从相对论极限拓展到非相对论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光诱导自旋重取向通常依赖自旋轨道耦合强度，因此受限于含重元素材料；作者希望突破这一限制，探索非相对论区域的光控自旋机制。

Method: 采用普适的自旋群论分析，研究自旋空间与晶格空间中的对称算符如何控制自旋力矩以及Bloch波函数的量子几何，包括量子度规和Berry曲率，并给出位点分辨自旋对称性与选择定则；随后用Kagomé晶格模型和真实材料家族展示光照下的局域自旋力矩。

Result: 揭示了对称性对自旋力矩和量子几何的选择定则；在Kagomé模型及真实材料中验证了光诱导局域自旋力矩；由于非相对论自旋劈裂通常可达电子伏特量级，远大于自旋轨道耦合引起的劈裂，因此产生的光诱导自旋力矩显著更强。

Conclusion: 该研究将光自旋电子学从相对论极限推广到非相对论极限，显著拓宽了其材料平台与实现途径。

Abstract: Manipulating spin using contactless and non-invasive optical approaches is one of the forefront topic in the modern spintronic science and technology. Prior studies on optically-induced spin reorientation usually scales with spin-orbit coupling strength, setting a limitation on composing heavy elements. In this work, we extend such a limit into nonrelativistic regime by exploring noncollinear magnetic configuration. Using a general spin group-theoretical investigation, we show how the symmetric operators in both spin and lattice spaces control the spin torque and quantum geometry of Bloch wavefunction--including both quantum metric and Berry curvature. We describe their site-resolved spin symmetries and unravel their selection rules. Furthermore, we adopt a Kagom\'e lattice model and a realistic material family to showcase the local spin torque under light irradiation. Since the nonrelativistic spin splitting usually spans on the order of electron-volt, significantly larger than typical spin-orbit induced splitting, the nonrelativistic light-induced spin torque is concomitantly much stronger. This work extends the previous opto-spintronics from relativistic to nonrelativistic limit, significantly broadening its realization platform.

</details>


### [161] [Vibrational Fingerprints of Competing Structural and Polar Phases in CuInP2S6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16091)
*Pegah Mohammadi,Sobhit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理DFT计算系统研究CuInP2S6多种结构相的振动性质，建立用于识别其顺电、铁电和反铁电相的拉曼/红外光谱指纹及选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CuInP2S6在温度和压力驱动下发生系列相变，产生多种结构相，但因其结构相似、振动响应复杂，实验上难以明确区分这些相，亟需系统的振动光谱理论指导。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论计算，研究顺电C2/c和P-31c、铁电Cc和P31c、反铁电P-1和P-3等候选相的声子谱与对称性；确定各相的拉曼和红外活性模式；利用Porto记法建立不同背散射几何下偏振和晶体取向相关的拉曼选择定则。

Result: 计算得到各候选结构的声子谱、拉曼/红外活性模式及特征振动指纹；建立了依赖于偏振和晶体取向的拉曼选择定则，并指出在特定实验构型下预期出现或消失的模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为解释CuInP2S6的拉曼和红外测量、可靠识别其在温度/压力驱动相变中竞争的结构相、铁电相和反铁电相提供了全面的理论框架。

Abstract: Layered van der Waals CuInP2S6 crystals exhibit a rich sequence of temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions, giving rise to multiple structural phases with distinct physical properties. Despite extensive experimental and theoretical efforts, unambiguous identification of these phases remains challenging because of their structural similarities and complex vibrational responses. In this work, we employ first-principles density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate the vibrational properties of the reference paraelectric C2/c and P-31c phases and their corresponding dynamically stable ferroelectric Cc and P31c phases, as well as the antiferroelectric P-1 and P-3 phases. From calculated phonon spectra and symmetry analysis, we determine the Raman- and infrared-active modes of each candidate structure and identify characteristic vibrational fingerprints that enable their phase determination through vibrational spectroscopy. Furthermore, using Porto notation, we establish polarization- and crystal-orientation-dependent Raman selection rules for different backscattering geometries and identify the modes that are expected to emerge or vanish under specific experimental configurations. Thus, our results provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for interpreting Raman and infrared measurements of CuInP2S6 and for reliably identifying its competing structural, ferroelectric, and antiferroelectric phases across temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions.

</details>


### [162] [Angle-Resolved Photoemission as a Sensitive Probe of Surface Quality: Sputtered versus Annealed Cu(001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16421)
*Hyeonhee Na,Jeonghwan Oh,Jinju Lee,Hanjun Sim,Myeongun Go,Jimin Sohn,Jeonggu Kang,Byungmin Sohn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Cu(001)表面在Ar+溅射后仍显示清晰的(1×1) LEED图案和干净的Cu 2p芯能级谱，但ARPES谱失去动量依赖且能带色散消失；退火后色散恢复。表明LEED和XPS不足以判定表面是否适合ARPES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES对表面有序性要求极高，而表面质量通常仅由LEED和XPS判断；本文检验这些常规判据在溅射诱导缺陷存在时是否可靠。

Method: 对Cu(001)单晶原位测量四个表面制备阶段的LEED、XPS和ARPES：初始制备、6 K老化24 h、Ar+溅射以及退火。

Result: 溅射与退火表面的LEED均呈近乎相同的(1×1)图案，Cu 2p芯能级也几乎一致；但溅射表面给出与动量无关的ARPES谱，退火后能带色散恢复。作者将此差异归因于探针响应不同：LEED反映平均晶格，仍保持周期性；ARPES反映电子态，其晶体动量在缺陷存在时不再良定义。

Conclusion: 尖锐的LEED图案和干净的芯能级谱并不能保证表面适合ARPES；ARPES测量前需直接验证动量分辨的电子结构或表面质量。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) requires a well-ordered surface, whose quality is routinely judged from low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS). We compare LEED, XPS, and ARPES of a Cu(001) single crystal measured in situ at four stages of surface preparation: as-prepared, after aging for 24 h at 6 K, after Ar$^+$ sputtering, and after annealing. The sputtered and annealed surfaces show nearly identical $(1\times1)$ LEED patterns and Cu 2$p$ core levels, but the sputtered surface yields a momentum-independent ARPES spectrum, and the band dispersion recovers after annealing. We attribute this contrast to how each probe responds to sputtering-induced defects: LEED reflects the average lattice, which remains periodic, whereas ARPES reflects the electronic states, whose crystal momentum is no longer well defined. Our results indicate that a sharp LEED pattern and clean core-level spectra do not guarantee a surface suitable for ARPES.

</details>


### [163] [Decoupling Trap States in Amorphous Oxide Semiconductor Transistor Using a Conductive-Semiconducting-Conductive MOS Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16438)
*Kai Chen,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong,Shengman Li,Yunlong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种非晶氧化物半导体（AOS）基导电-半导体-导电（CSC）MOS测试结构，可有效感知、区分并分析AOS晶体管中由不同陷阱引起的电导损失，获得偏压分辨的陷阱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试结构中非理想金属-AOS接触会卷积多种陷阱态产生的电导损失，难以区分界面态与浅态；因此需要一种能抑制接触伪影、解耦陷阱贡献并校准漏电、串联电阻和器件几何效应的表征平台。

Method: 设计CSC MOS测试结构以缓解接触相关伪影，解耦界面态和浅态，并识别与校准漏电、串联电阻及器件几何效应；通过偏压分辨测量提取陷阱密度与时间常数分布。

Result: 获得偏压分辨陷阱谱并揭示三种区间：(1) 耗尽区由界面态主导，时间常数达亚微秒，密度随耗尽加深略增，呈施主样态；(2) 弱至中等积累区由浅态主导，体密度呈高斯样分布，响应时间从微秒到纳秒；(3) 强积累区界面态重新主导，态数量增加但时间常数不变。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明不同陷阱群在AOS晶体管不同工作条件下对电导损失和可靠性退化的影响，并提供直观指标；CSC结构简单、可扩展且稳健，适合AOS晶体管的陷阱表征。

Abstract: We propose a novel amorphous oxide semiconductor (AOS)-based conductive-semiconducting-conductive (CSC) MOS test structure to effectively sense, discriminate, and analyze distinct trap-induced conductance losses in AOS transistors. In conventional test structures, non-ideal metal-AOS contacts convolute the conductance loss generated by various trap states. The CSC architecture mitigates these contact-related artifacts, enabling the decoupling of interface states and shallow states. Furthermore, the contributions from leakage, series resistance, and device-geometry effects are identified and calibrated. Our results yield bias-resolved trap profiles and reveal three distinct regimes: (1) In the depletion region, interface states dominate the conductance loss. These states exhibit sub-microsecond time constants and their density increases slightly with deeper depletion, which is associated with donor-like states. (2) From weak to moderate accumulation, shallow states become the primary contributor, displaying a Gaussian-like volumetric density-of-states distribution with response times spanning from microseconds to nanoseconds. (3) In the strong accumulation regime, interface states regain dominance with an increased state quantity, while their time constants remain invariant. These bias-resolved profiles clarify how different trap populations impact AOS transistors under distinct operating conditions, providing intuitive metrics for understanding reliability degradation. Consequently, the CSC structure offers a simple, scalable, and robust platform for trap characterization in AOS transistors.

</details>


### [164] [Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni$_2$MnGa with machine-learning force-fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16467)
*Markus E. Gruner,Yulia Klunnikova,Mike J. Bruckhoff,Konstatin P. Skokov,Olga N. Miroshkina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 构建了基于DFT数据训练的机器学习力场，用于Ni2MnGa等Heusler合金的马氏体相变模拟，重现软TA2声子和纳米孪晶马氏体，并揭示自适应纳米孪晶促进正、逆相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT虽能刻画调制马氏体的能量景观，但难以在相关空间和时间尺度上模拟有限温度动力学，因此需要兼具近DFT精度与大规模模拟效率的方法。

Method: 从从头算分子动力学数据训练机器学习力场，并用于经典分子动力学模拟，研究Ni2MnGa中自适应纳米孪晶对奥氏体-马氏体相变的影响。

Result: ML-FF可重现Ni2MnGa的关键特征，包括软TA2声子和纳米孪晶马氏体偏好；自适应纳米孪晶促进正向与反向相变；T=0时DFT预测的负孪晶界能沿整个相变路径支持有限温度下有序调制马氏体的形成。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁性形状记忆合金提供了近DFT精度的大尺度模拟路径，有助于连接电子结构、磁性与晶格动力学以理解马氏体相变。

Abstract: The martensitic transformations in stoichiometric Ni$_2$MnGa and related Heusler alloys are governed by a delicate interplay between electronic structure, magnetism, and lattice dynamics. While density functional theory (DFT) has successfully mapped the energy landscape of modulated martensites, it remains computationally prohibitive for capturing finite-temperature dynamics across relevant length and time scales. Here, we bridge this gap by constructing DFT-trained machine-learning force-fields (ML-FF) interpolated from ab initio molecular dynamics. We demonstrate that ML-FF trained on DFT data can reproduce key features of Ni$_2$MnGa, including the soft TA$_2$ phonon and a preference for nanotwinned martensites. Using classical molecular dynamics simulations, we explore the impact of adaptive nanotwinning on the austenite-martensite transition, which facilitates the dynamics of both, forward and backward transformation. We demonstrate, that the negative twin boundary energy along the entire transformations path predicted by DFT for $T=0$, supports the formation of ordered modulated martensites at finite temperatures. Our results pave the way for large-scale simulations of magnetic shape memory alloys with near-DFT accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO$_3$ using Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16521)
*Kazutaka Nishiguchi,Ryota Yamamoto,Seiichiro Ten-no*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于AIMD数据训练了用于完整及缺氧SrTiO3的神经网络势，能准确复现DFT能量、力和维里，并用于大尺度长时间氧空位扩散模拟，得到迁移焓0.64 eV，接近实验值0.67 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位的形成与扩散显著影响SrTiO3的电子和原子输运性质；但基于DFT的AIMD受计算成本限制，难以研究大体系、长时间尺度的氧空位动力学。

Method: 利用AIMD/DFT生成参考数据集，训练神经网络势NNP，覆盖完整STO和含氧空位STO；验证其对能量、原子力、维里及超胞尺寸依赖和稀释极限形成能的复现能力；随后用NNP进行大尺度、长时间分子动力学模拟以研究氧空位扩散。

Result: NNP准确复现DFT参考能量、力和维里，并再现总能量与氧空位形成能的超胞尺寸依赖性；NNP-MD给出的氧空位扩散系数比传统经典MD更符合实验；Arrhenius分析得到氧空位迁移焓0.64 eV，与实验值0.67 eV接近。

Conclusion: 基于第一性原理数据训练的神经网络势为研究钙钛矿氧化物中的缺陷形成和长时间空位输运提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Oxygen-vacancy formation and diffusion play important roles in determining the electronic and atomic transport properties of SrTiO$_3$ (STO). Accurate analysis of such point defects requires sufficiently large-scale and long-time molecular dynamics (MD) simulations; however, direct investigations of oxygen-vacancy dynamics using {\it ab initio} MD (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) are limited to relatively small systems and short time scales because of their high computational cost. In this study, we develop a neural network potential (NNP) for pristine and oxygen-deficient STO using a reference dataset generated from AIMD simulations. The trained NNP model accurately reproduces the DFT reference energies, atomic forces, and virials for both pristine STO and STO containing an oxygen vacancy. The NNP also reproduces the supercell-size dependence of the total energies and oxygen-vacancy formation energies, including the formation energy extrapolated to the dilute limit. Large-scale and long-time MD simulations using the NNP (NNP-MD) are then performed to investigate oxygen-vacancy diffusion. The resulting oxygen-vacancy diffusion coefficients show substantially better agreement with experimental data than those obtained from conventional classical MD simulations. An Arrhenius analysis yields an oxygen-vacancy migration enthalpy of 0.64 eV, in close agreement with the experimental value of 0.67 eV. These results demonstrate that NNPs trained on first-principles data provide an accurate and computationally efficient framework for investigating defect formation and long-time vacancy transport in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [166] [Interlayer Registry Controls Polar Topology in Twisted Bilayer h-BN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16528)
*Di Fan,Changming Ke,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作用第一性原理训练的深势分子动力学模拟，揭示扭转双层h-BN中空间变化的层间配准决定莫尔极化织构，并系统阐明其随扭角、温度和机械应变的演化，指出层间配准是调控莫尔极性拓扑的核心参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德华双层会产生层间配准周期性变化的莫尔堆叠场，从而实现空间图案化极化；但配准控制的极性拓扑如何随扭角、温度和机械应变演化，仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用第一性原理训练深势分子动力学模拟，研究扭角和相对层形变对层间配准及极化织构的调控，并建立失配矩阵配准映射模型解释机械响应。

Result: 随扭角减小，莫尔极化织构从紧凑近圆形涡旋状图案演变为扩展三角形AB/BA畴；该转变源于小扭角下增强的晶格弛豫，它扩大低能反极化AB/BA区域并将面内极化主要限制在畴界。局域面内绕数仍与层间配准关联，并可保持至1000 K，表明拓扑对热涨落稳健。均匀双层形变仅产生弱畸变，而层选择性形变强烈重构莫尔极化织构；失配矩阵模型可解释大扭角下涡旋半径变化和小扭角下三角畴几何。

Conclusion: 层间配准是扭转双层h-BN中形成和调控莫尔极性拓扑的中心控制参数。

Abstract: Twisting van der Waals bilayers creates a moir'e stacking field with periodically varying local interlayer registry, enabling spatially patterned polarization. However, a systematic understanding of how this registry-controlled polar topology evolves with twist angle, temperature, and mechanical strain is still lacking. Here, using first-principles-trained deep-potential molecular dynamics simulations, we show that spatially varying interlayer registry governs the polarization texture and can be directly tuned by twist and relative layer deformation. As the twist angle decreases, the moir'e polarization texture evolves from compact, nearly circular vortex-like patterns to extended triangular AB/BA domains. This crossover arises from enhanced lattice relaxation at small twist angles, which expands low-energy, oppositely polarized AB/BA regions and confines in-plane polarization mainly to domain boundaries. Despite this reconstruction, the local in-plane winding remains tied to interlayer registry and persists up to 1000 K, indicating robust topology against thermal fluctuations. We further show that the mechanical response is dictated by whether deformation changes the relative registry: homogeneous deformation of both layers produces only weak distortions, whereas layer-selective deformation strongly reconstructs the moir\'e polarization texture. A mismatch-matrix registry-mapping model is developed to capture this response, explaining deformation-induced changes in vortex radii at larger twist angles and triangular-domain geometry at smaller twist angles. These results identify interlayer registry as the central control parameter for forming and tuning moir'e polar topology in twisted bilayer h-BN.

</details>


### [167] [Real-space obstruction control of the shift current in two-dimensional Quantum Spin Hall insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16544)
*Michele Bagaglini,Mattia Iannetti,Jonas Erhardt,J\"org Sch\"afer,Ralph Claessen,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Gianni Profeta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在三角晶格量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型中，受阻拓扑相在能带结构相似的情况下表现出显著增强的位移电流；其起源于不同Wannier中心扇区之间的光学跃迁带来大实空间位移，并可调控位移电流符号，作者进一步指出indenene是实现该效应的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找可在红外波段工作、具有大体光伏效应的低带隙材料，以推动光电子应用；位移电流作为主要微观机制，因其几何起源不仅依赖光学跃迁概率，还依赖激发对应的实空间位移，因此非中心对称且电子波函数受阻的材料特别有前景。

Method: 采用三角晶格量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型，研究平凡相、量子自旋霍尔相和高阶拓扑绝缘相中的非线性光伏电流，重点分析位移电流及其与Wannier中心扇区、光学跃迁和实空间位移的关系。

Result: 发现受阻拓扑相尽管电子能带结构与其它相相似，却具有显著增强的位移电流响应；增强机制来自属于不同Wannier中心扇区的态之间的光学跃迁，这类跃迁可产生大实空间位移，并且位移电流符号随拓扑相变化而受控。

Conclusion: 研究将可调控体光伏效应的探索拓展到具有实空间电子受阻特征的材料，并识别出SiC(0001)上In单层形成的indenene作为实现该现象的潜力候选材料。

Abstract: The search for materials exhibiting a large bulk photovoltaic effect has recently expanded to low-band-gap systems capable of operating in the infrared regime, with potential applications in optoelectronics. Among the microscopic mechanisms contributing to this phenomenon, the shift current is one of the dominant sources of photocurrent generation. Owing to its geometric origin, the shift current is not only determined by the optical transition probability but also by the real-space displacement associated with the optical excitation, making non-centrosymmetric materials with obstructed electronic wave functions particularly promising candidates. In this work, using a tight-binding model of a quantum spin Hall insulator on a triangular lattice, we investigate the nonlinear photocurrent across trivial, quantum spin Hall, and higher-order topological insulating phases. We show that obstructed topological phases exhibit a substantially enhanced shift-current response, despite the similar electronic band structure. We identify the origin of this enhancement in optical transitions between states belonging to distinct Wannier-center sectors, which allow for a large real-space displacement upon photoexcitation and, depending on the topological phase, control the sign of the shift current. Our results extend the search for controllable bulk photovoltaic effects to materials characterized by real-space electronic obstruction and identify indenene, a monolayer of In on SiC(0001), as a promising candidate for realizing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [168] [Domain-wall-to-domain interconversion in a polar conducting oxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16681)
*Martin F. Sarott,Majid Ahmadi,Johanna van Gent Gonz\'{a}lez,Ewout van der Veer,Mads C. Weber,Jacopo Pinna,Daniel A. Chaney,Arijit Sinha,Hamideh Hassani,Horatio R. J. Cox,Thomas Perrault,Amir Sabet Ghorabaei,Maria A. Loi,Philippe Ghosez,Bart J. Kooi,Beatriz Noheda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在极性外延WO3中，通过扫描探针施加交变电场产生局域加热，使纳米尺度畴壁可逆扩展为三维功能区域，从而实现畴壁与畴之间的可逆转换，并在极性/导电性质之间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁是铁性晶体中对称性不同于畴内部的三维拓扑缺陷，可呈现母体材料中禁戒的功能，但其通常严格局限于二维界面，限制了对其本质的揭示和实际应用。

Method: 利用导电、非极性的畴壁可 adopted 母相结构这一特性，在极性外延WO3中通过扫描探针针尖施加交变电场，诱导局域加热，使畴壁相扩展进入相邻极性畴，实现畴壁到畴的相互转换。

Result: 实现了可逆的维度控制，将纳米尺度畴壁扩展为三维功能区域，并可空间可逆地切换畴的极性性质与畴壁的导电性质。

Conclusion: 该策略为在氧化物中可逆调控畴/畴壁功能提供了新途径，并为可重构氧化物电子学提供了新范式。

Abstract: Domain walls in ferroic crystals are topological defects with a symmetry distinct from the domain interior, allowing them to display functionalities that are otherwise forbidden in the host material. Despite their unique properties, domain walls are strictly confined to two-dimensional interfaces, which hinders unveiling their true nature and limits their applicability. Here, we demonstrate reversible dimensionality control by expanding nanoscale domain walls into three-dimensional functional regions in polar epitaxial tungsten trioxide ($\text{WO}_3$). This domain-wall-to-domain interconversion is enabled by the conducting, non-polar domain walls adopting the parent-phase structure. An alternating electric field applied through a scanning probe tip induces localized heating, which expands this domain-wall phase into the adjacent polar domains. This approach enables reversible spatial control to switch between mutually exclusive properties of domains (polarity) and domain walls (conductivity), offering a new paradigm for reconfigurable oxide electronics.

</details>


### [169] [Engineering Ferroelectric Phases in BaTiO$_3$ Films via Buffer-Layer Induced Misfit Strain Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16766)
*A. S. Mikheykin (Southern Federal University),N. V. Lyanguzov (Southern Federal University),A. S. Konstantinov (Southern Federal University),V. B. Shirokov (Southern Federal University,Federal Research Centre The Southern Scientific Centre of the Russian Academy of Sciences),E. D. Linnik (Southern Federal University),T. Bondar (Southern Federal University),A. G. Razumnaya (Jo\v{z}ef Stefan Institute)*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在MgO衬底上的BaTiO3薄膜中插入LSMO导电缓冲层可反转有效应变，从而调控铁电相序与极化织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变工程控制铁电相对称性对极化拓扑和氧化物器件功能至关重要，但衬底失配、热失配与界面效应如何共同决定有效应变和相变仍需明确。

Method: 制备500 nm BaTiO3/MgO薄膜及插入50 nm La0.67Sr0.33MnO3缓冲层的异质结构，结合温度依赖偏振拉曼光谱、X射线衍射和压电力显微镜研究应变状态、相变序列和极化行为。

Result: MgO(001)晶格本应诱导张应变，但热膨胀失配主导使500 nm BaTiO3薄膜在冷却时呈有效压缩应力；插入LSMO后有效应变由压缩反转为拉伸。冷却相序为：顺电→正交aa相（约407 K）→单斜r相（约300-345 K），并具有面内和倾斜极化分量。LSMO既作底电极又改变应变，界面电荷重分布产生内建电场，表现为自极化和非对称PFM回线。

Conclusion: 导电缓冲层可主导衬底诱导应变并控制铁电对称性与极化织构，为低功耗自旋电子学、可调微波器件和神经形态计算用氧化物异质结构提供途径。

Abstract: Controlling ferroelectric phase symmetry through strain engineering is crucial for tailoring polarization topology and functional properties in oxide-based devices. Here, we show that a thin conductive buffer layer can override substrate-induced strain in BaTiO$_3$ (BTO) films on MgO. Although the lattice parameter of MgO(001) would be expected to induce tensile misfit strain, in 500~nm BaTiO$_3$ films the thermal-expansion mismatch dominates, resulting in effective compressive stress upon cooling. Inserting a 50-nm La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$ (LSMO) buffer layer reverses the effective strain state from compressive to tensile. Using temperature-dependent polarized Raman spectroscopy, X-ray diffraction, and piezoresponse force microscopy, we reveal an unconventional phase sequence upon cooling: paraelectric $\rightarrow$ orthorhombic ($\textit{aa}$-phase, $\sim$407 K) $\rightarrow$ monoclinic ($\textit{r}$-phase, $\sim$300-345 K), characterized in-plane and tilted polarization components. The LSMO layer not only serves as a bottom electrode but also modifies the strain state, while interfacial charge redistribution induces a built-in electric field, evidenced by self-polarization and asymmetric PFM hysteresis. These findings highlight the critical role of conductive buffer layers in controlling ferroelectric symmetry and enabling oxide heterostructures with tailored polarization textures for low-power spintronics, tunable microwave devices, and neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [170] [Molecular Precursor Chemistry Shapes Early-Stage Reaction Pathways in Nitrogen-Doped Carbon Nanotube Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16831)
*Narayan N. Som,Lukasz Nowicki,Tomasz Gizewski,Karolina Z. Milowska,Anna Wroblewska,Caghan Tuncer,Maciej Trzaskowski,Olga Iwasinska-Kowalska,Tomasz Plocinski,Mariusz Zdrojek,Krzysztof K. Koziol,Malgorzata Jakubowska,Agnieszka Lekawa-Raus*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 碳纳米管的结构特征不仅由成核和后续生长决定，还可追溯至前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最初纳秒；前驱体化学通过改变早期Fe-C/Fe-SiO2相互作用和分解路径，显著影响CNT形貌与无序度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统讨论多聚焦于已成形的催化剂纳米颗粒上的CNT成核与生长，忽视前驱体分解和催化剂形成早期阶段；本文旨在检验前驱体化学是否在最初纳秒就决定CNT的结构轨迹。

Method: 结合实验与反应分子动力学模拟，对比无氮CNT合成与两种芳香氮前驱体（吡啶、吡嗪，每分子分别含1和2个氮原子）的CNT合成，分析CNT结构、氮掺入以及早期Fe-C、Fe-SiO2相互作用和分解路径。

Result: 含氮前驱体均促进竹节状CNT形成，并产生比无氮参照显著更多结构无序的CNT；氮掺入并不简单随供氮量增加，表明前驱体化学贡献强。反应分子动力学显示，含氮前驱体显著改变催化剂演化中的局部Fe-C和Fe-SiO2相互作用；吡啶与吡嗪分解路径不同，吡嗪在模拟时间尺度内分解显著更多。

Conclusion: 前驱体化学通过影响局部化学环境和早期催化剂演化，在CNT生长初始阶段发挥重要作用；CNT结构轨迹可始于前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最初纳秒。

Abstract: The structural characteristics of carbon nanotubes are usually discussed in terms of processes occurring during nanotube nucleation and subsequent growth from an already formed catalyst nanoparticle. Here, we show that their structural trajectory can begin much earlier, within the first nanoseconds of precursor decomposition and catalyst formation. We combine experiment and reactive molecular dynamics to compare nitrogen-free CNT synthesis with two closely related aromatic nitrogen precursors, pyridine and pyrazine, containing one and two nitrogen atoms per molecule, respectively. Experimentally, both nitrogen-containing precursors promote bamboo-like CNT formation and produce substantially more structurally disordered CNTs than the nitrogen-free reference. Notably, nitrogen incorporation does not scale simply with the amount of nitrogen supplied, demonstrating a strong contribution from precursor chemistry. Reactive molecular dynamics reveals that nitrogen-containing precursors significantly modify local Fe-carbon and Fe-SiO2 interactions during catalyst evolution, while pyridine and pyrazine exhibit distinct decomposition pathways, with pyrazine showing significantly greater decomposition within the simulated timescale. These results demonstrate that precursor chemistry influences the local chemical environment and early catalyst evolution, highlighting its potential role in the initial stages of CNT growth.

</details>


### [171] [Trust-Limited Machine-Learning-Accelerated Nudged Elastic Band Calculations for Small-Polaron Hopping in Rutile TiO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16850)
*Ashish Kumar Singh,Irina V. Lebedeva*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种高斯过程辅助的NEB工作流，用于DFT+U下金红石TiO2中小极化子跳跃计算，可在电荷转移区获得可重复势垒与全带力收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子跳跃的NEB计算因核运动与自洽局域电子态耦合而困难；在电荷转移区附近，SCF收敛慢、局域化切换和竞争电子解使代理辅助优化复杂。

Method: 开发高斯过程辅助NEB工作流；使用SIESTA的DFT+U自旋极化计算；采用联合能量-力GP与Matérn协方差推进弹性带，并用活性原子RMS与最大原子位移限制约束代理运动相对于已评估构型；达到限制即触发第一性原理采样，无需代理完全收敛；15图像算例中重复中心图像采样会激活单独校准的局部GP；最终进行全带DFT验证。

Result: 三个独立DFT验证的96原子[001]计算（11、13、15图像，不同代理设置与GP参数化）给出势垒0.1645、0.1637、0.1640 eV，跨度0.793 meV；15图像算例中重复中心图像采样激活局部GP；最终全带DFT验证最大投影NEB力为0.0346 eV/Å；324原子计算给出更低势垒0.1302 eV，表明超胞敏感性并证明更大GP观测维度可行。

Conclusion: 该工作流结合受控代理外推、面向电荷转移区的插值和独立DFT验证来获得极化子迁移路径，强调可重复势垒和全带力收敛。

Abstract: Nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations of small-polaron hopping are demanding because nuclear motion is coupled to a self-consistent localized electronic state. Near the charge-transfer region, slow self-consistent-field convergence, localization switching, and competing electronic solutions complicate surrogate-assisted optimization. We develop a Gaussian-process-assisted NEB workflow for this regime and demonstrate electron small-polaron hopping in rutile TiO2 using spin-polarized density functional theory with a Hubbard U correction (DFT+U ) with SIESTA. A joint energy-force Gaussian process (GP) with a Mat\'ern covariance model advances the elastic band, while active-atom root-mean-square and maximum-atom displacement limits constrain surrogate motion relative to evaluated configurations. Reaching these limits triggers first-principles sampling without requiring full surrogate convergence. Three independently DFT-verified 96-atom [001] calculations using 11, 13, and 15 images and different surrogate settings and GP parameterizations yield barriers of 0.1645, 0.1637, and 0.1640 eV, respectively, spanning 0.793 meV. In the 15-image calculation, repeated central-image sampling activates a separately calibrated local GP during surrogate optimization. Final full-band DFT verification gives a maximum projected NEB force of 0.0346 eV/{\AA}. A 324-atom calculation yields a lower barrier of 0.1302 eV, indicating supercell sensitivity and demonstrating feasibility at larger GP observation dimension. The workflow combines controlled surrogate extrapolation, targeted charge-transfer-region interpolation, and independent DFT verification to obtain polaron migration pathways, emphasizing reproducible barriers and full-band force convergence.

</details>


### [172] [First-Principles Calculations of the Electron-Hole-Pair Creation Energy in Semiconductors and Dielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16949)
*Dallin O. Nielsen,Massimo V. Fischetti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理方法计算 AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si 和 SiO2 中电子-空穴对产生能；对 Si 进一步用 GW/BSE 修正，得到约 3.50 eV/pair，并与实验符合更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确获得半导体和介电材料中的电子-空穴对产生能，并解释理论值与实验值之间的差异，尤其是 Si 中约 14.9% 的偏差。

Method: 使用 DFT 计算能带结构，DFPT 获得声子色散，TDDFT 计算介电函数和电子能量损失函数；进行能量至约 100 eV 的全带 Monte Carlo 电子热化模拟，计算激发生成电子-空穴对所需平均能量。针对 Si，采用 GW 方法计算准粒子能带修正，并用 Bethe-Salpeter 方程（BSE）计算介电函数。

Result: 计算得到 AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si、SiO2 的对产生能分别约为 12.2、6.5、7.3、3.10、16.7 eV/pair，与实验数据总体吻合；最大偏差为 Si，比约 3.6 eV/pair 的实验值低约 14.9%。使用 GW/BSE 数据后，Si 的对产生能为约 3.50 eV/pair。

Conclusion: GW/BSE 方法显著提高 Si 对产生能计算精度，主要归因于在低于约 10–15 eV 时电子能量损失函数幅度略有降低。总体而言，声子发射与电离在声子散射率和电离率交点附近的竞争，是决定对产生能的主要因素；另一关键因素是能量损失函数中等离激元峰的位置和形状。

Abstract: We present first-principles calculations of the electron-hole-pair creation energy in semiconductors and dielectrics, focusing in particular on AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2. We have employed density functional theory (DFT) to calculate the band structure, density-functional perturbation theory to obtain the phonon dispersion, and time-dependent DFT (TDDFT) to calculate the electron energy loss function (via the dielectric function). We performed full-band Monte Carlo simulations of electron thermalization for energies up to ~100 eV and calculated the average energy required to excite an electron-hole pair. We calculated pair-creation energies of approximately 12.2, 6.5, 7.3, 3.10, and 16.7 eV/pair for AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2, respectively. These values are in good agreement with measured data, with the largest discrepancy being that of Si (about 14.9% lower than the accepted experimental value of ~3.6 eV/pair). To improve the agreement for Si, we used the GW approach and the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) to calculate the quasiparticle shifts of the electronic band structure and the dielectric function, respectively. Finally, using the GW/BSE data, we calculated a creation energy of ~3.50 eV/pair. We find that the marked improvement in the accuracy of the calculated creation energy, when using the GW/BSE approach, is primarily attributable to the slight decrease in the magnitude of the electron energy-loss function when using the GW/BSE approach for energies below ~10-15 eV. Overall, it is clear that the competition between phonon emission and ionization near the intersection of the phonon scattering and ionization rates is largely responsible for determining the value of the creation energy, with the other major factor being the location and shape of the plasmon peak in the energy-loss function.

</details>


### [173] [Capturing Coordination Core Topology in Metal--Organic Frameworks Using Persistent Mayer Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17056)
*Caleb Simiyu Khaemba,Chun-Long Chen,Guo-Wei Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出持久Mayer同调(PMH)以量化表征MOFs/POMs的局部配位核心，并与ERPH结合预测吸附和自扩散性质，其中第三阶PMH表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如元素分辨持久同调ERPH）难以将局部配位核心转化为可量化、可扩展且可解释的信息，且经典拓扑无法捕捉某些局部几何变异。

Method: 构建PMH，聚焦周期性金属中心重原子、碳和给体通道形成的配位核心，区分局部配位环境、连接基框架及潜在配位原子的排列；考察N=3和N=5的PMH，并分别与ERPH结合，用ERPH描述扩展框架中化学定义元素组的多尺度组织，预测8种氮/氧相关吸附与自扩散性质。

Result: 配位核心PMH表征在预测性能上一致优于单独ERPH，其中第三阶Mayer表示总体性能最强。

Conclusion: PMH捕获的内部配位构型包含传统元素分辨拓扑描述符未充分表达的关键结构信息，为理解局部配位环境提供了定量、可解释的拓扑框架，并可能促进MOFs和POM基材料的理性设计。

Abstract: Local coordination cores are critically important in metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) and polyoxometalates (POMs), as they govern the fundamental chemical and structural properties of these materials. Existing methods, including element-resolved persistent homology (ERPH), have limited ability to translate local coordination cores into quantitative, scalable, and interpretable information. Here, we introduce persistent Mayer homology (PMH) to enable the detection of the geometric variability invisible by classical topology. By focusing on coordination cores formed by periodic metal-centered heavy-atom, carbon, and donor channels, PMH distinguishes different arrangements of the local coordination environment, linker framework, and potential coordinating atoms. We examine explainable PMH of orders $N=3$ and $N=5$ and separately combine each with ERPH, which characterizes the multiscale organization of chemically defined element groups across the extended framework, to predict eight nitrogen- and oxygen-related adsorption and self-diffusion properties. The resulting coordination-core PMH representations consistently improve predictive performance over ERPH alone, with the third-order Mayer representation providing the strongest overall performance. These results demonstrate that the inner coordination configurations captured by PMH contain essential structural information that is not fully represented by conventional element-resolved topological descriptors. This finding provides a quantitative interpretable topological framework for understanding local coordination environments and may facilitate the rational design of MOFs and POM-based materials.

</details>


### [174] [Symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl points in nonmagnetic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17071)
*Ze-Xin Xue,Ke-Xin Pang,Yun-Yun Bai,Yanfeng Ge,Yan Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究非磁性晶体中三荷Weyl点（C-3 WPs）与单荷Weyl点（C-1 WPs）共存的八节点Weyl构型；通过对称性分类和第一性原理预测硼同素异形体TBR-B48与手性OBD-B48，并分析表面费米弧、手性对应及对称性破缺效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高荷Weyl半金属及C-3 WPs具有非常规拓扑性质和增强边界现象，但理想C-3 WSM仍稀少，且非磁性晶体中受对称性约束的C-3与C-1 Weyl点共存问题尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 结合拓扑电荷守恒与空间群对称性分析，系统分类全部230个空间群，并分别在无自旋轨道耦合（SOC）与有SOC条件下筛选候选群；对候选硼结构进行第一性原理计算和表面态计算；通过破坏C6对称性和反转结构手性研究能带、Weyl荷及表面费米弧演化。

Result: 该八节点构型由两个C-3 WPs和六个相反手性的C-1 WPs组成，等价于两个相同的{3,-1,-1,-1}不可约Weyl分子。无SOC时识别出12个候选群，有SOC时为23个。TBR-B48和手性OBD-B48均实现该构型，分别具有hourglass型与非hourglass型C-3 WPs。表面态计算显示，在C-3对的共享投影处有6条费米弧分支，在各自投影处各有3条。反转OBD-B48的结构手性会反转所有Weyl荷符号，建立晶体手性与动量空间手性的直接对应。破坏TBR-B48中起保护作用的C6对称性会使每个C-3 WP分裂为三个C-1 WPs，并重构表面费米弧。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了设计与搜索非磁性Weyl半金属中C-3与C-1共存体系的对称性准则，并为高荷Weyl拓扑、手性及表面态调控提供了候选材料平台。

Abstract: High-charge Weyl semimetals (WSMs), particularly those hosting charge-three Weyl point (C-3 WPs), have attracted considerable interest owing to their unconventional topological properties and enhanced boundary phenomena. However, ideal C-3 WSMs remain rare, and the symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl point (C-1 WPs) in nonmagnetic crystals remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a representative eight-node Weyl configuration, consisting of two C-3 WPs and six C-1 WPs with opposite chirality, corresponding to two identical $\{3,-1,-1,-1\}$ irreducible Weyl molecules. By combining topological charge conservation with space-group symmetry analysis, we systematically classify all 230 space groups and identify 12 and 23 candidate groups without and with spin--orbit coupling (SOC), respectively. First-principles calculations without SOC reveal that the boron allotropes TBR-B$_{48}$ and chiral OBD-B$_{48}$ realize this configuration, hosting hourglass-type and non-hourglass-type C-3 WPs, respectively. Surface-state calculations reveal six Fermi-arc branches at the shared projection of the charge-three pair and three at each individual projection. Reversing the structural chirality of OBD-B$_{48}$ reverses the signs of all Weyl charges, establishing a direct correspondence between crystal and momentum-space chirality. Furthermore, breaking the protecting $C_6$ symmetry in TBR-B$_{48}$ splits each C-3 WP into three C-1 WPs and reconstructs the surface Fermi arcs. Our work establishes symmetry-based guidelines for designing and searching for nonmagnetic WSMs hosting charge-three and charge-one Weyl-point coexistence.

</details>


### [175] [Spin-orbit torque effects in all-electrical spin-wave spectroscopy experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17095)
*Jos\'e Solano C\'ordova,Jone Mencos,Richard Schlitz,Quentin Rossi,Denise Reustlen,Javed Ali,Sebastian Sailler,Michaela Lammel,Matthieu Bailleul,Daniel Stoeffler,Felix Casanova,Sebastian T. B. Goennenwein,Helena Reichlova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了 Pt 天线在 105 nm 厚外延 YIG 薄膜中激发相干自旋波，发现扣除刻蚀及 Pt 界面不均匀性影响后，剩余传播信号与 Oersted 场和自旋轨道矩（SOT）共同激发一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电激发与探测传播自旋波是磁子器件的关键，Pt 天线可通过自旋霍尔效应产生 SOT；理论上 RF-SOT 可作为不同于传统感应激发的相干自旋波激发机制，因此需要实验验证。

Method: 使用 Pt 天线在 105 nm 厚外延 Y3Fe5O12（YIG）薄膜中激发相干自旋波，考察振荡 Oersted 场与振荡自旋流两种机制；分析原始传输谱，并去除刻蚀工艺导致的 YIG 薄膜改性和 Pt 诱导的空间不均匀界面贡献。

Result: 原始传输谱强烈受制于刻蚀相关 YIG 改性和 Pt 界面空间不均匀性；扣除这些贡献后，剩余的两个传播波与 Oersted 场和 SOT 的共同激发相符。

Conclusion: Pt 天线激发相干自旋波不能仅用传统 RF Oersted 场解释，实验结果支持 Oersted 场与 SOT 共同参与激发。

Abstract: Electrical excitation and detection of propagating spin waves are key elements of magnonic devices. While conventional antennas consisting of non-magnetic metals such as Au or Cu excite spin waves predominantly through radio-frequency (RF) Oersted fields, antennas comprising Pt can additionally generate spin-orbit torques (SOTs) via the spin Hall effect. Recent theoretical work has proposed RF-SOT as an alternative mechanism for coherent spin-wave excitation, predicting distinct characteristics compared with conventional inductive excitation. Here, we investigate Pt antennas for the excitation of coherent spin waves in 105-nm-thick epitaxial $\text{Y}_3\text{Fe}_5\text{O}_{12}$ (YIG) films through two potential mechanisms: an oscillating Oersted field and an oscillating spin current. The raw experimental transmission spectra are strongly affected by fabrication-induced modifications to the YIG film associated with the etching process together with spatially inhomogeneous interface conditions induced by Pt. After removing these contributions, we find the remaining two wave propagation consistent with a combined excitation from Oersted fields and SOTs.

</details>


### [176] [Comprehensive Workflow for Uncertainty-Aware Ionic Liquid Solvent Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17171)
*Alexander Lobo,Urvi Awasthi,Leonid Zhukov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一个将数据处理、深度学习建模、不确定性估计与物理筛选耦合的离子液体性质预测和最优候选筛选流程，用于分布外可靠排序并优选出面向CO2捕集的新颖离子液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子液体具有可调性，但现有数据驱动性质模型在数据稀缺、噪声大且分布不均时性能下降，化学空间和热力学条件覆盖有限，导致分布外筛选脆弱：即使点预测看似合理，也难以一致比较候选、量化下游影响并信任排序。

Method: 将数据处理、深度学习建模和物理计算视为耦合系统，把性质预测与不确定性估计及物理过滤相连接，并收紧预测性质与降维筛选准则之间的联系，以支持可靠的分布外排序和候选优先级排序。

Result: 以烟气CO2捕集为例，设计新型离子液体，预测其性质，并基于CO2溶解度、离子液体粘度和工作容量筛选Pareto最优候选，从而优先考虑此前未测试且预测权衡有利的离子液体。

Conclusion: 该工作流通过预测、不确定性和物理筛选的耦合，提高了分布外候选排序的可靠性，可指导数据稀缺场景下目标性质离子液体的发现与筛选。

Abstract: Designing synthesizable materials with targeted properties remains one of the central objectives in materials sciences. Ionic liquids (ILs) are well suited to this goal due to their "tunability", where physical and chemical properties can be precisely customized by modifying their molecular structure, specifically by varying the combination of cations and anions. At the same time, current data-driven property models for ionic liquids often degrade in scarce, noisy, and uneven data regimes, where measurements cover only a small fraction of chemical space and a limited range of thermodynamic conditions. This makes out-of-distribution screening brittle: even when point predictions look plausible, it is difficult to compare candidates consistently, quantify downstream implications, or trust rankings enough to guide molecular discovery. We present a property-prediction and optimal candidate selection workflow that treats data processing, deep learning modeling, and physics-based calculations as a coupled system, linking property prediction with uncertainty estimation and physics-based filtering. By tightening the link between predicted properties and the criteria used for down-selection, the workflow supports reliable out-of-distribution ranking and enables prioritization of previously untested ionic liquid candidates with favorable predicted trade-offs. We demonstrate the application of our workflow by designing novel ionic liquids for CO2 capture from flue gas, predicting their properties and selecting Pareto-optimal candidates based on CO2 solubility, IL viscosity and working capacity.

</details>


### [177] [Evidence of an anisotropic Griffiths phase in a two-dimensional van der Waals magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17315)
*Anupam K. Singh,Dhananjay Nair,Holger Meyerheim,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Banabir Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在范德华磁体Fe5GeTe2中，自插层诱导出高度各向异性的Griffiths相：顺磁基体中嵌入短程有序铁磁团簇，且在面内磁场下表现出非解析磁响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年二维范德华磁性主要关注长程有序磁相，而无序磁相仍研究不足；本文旨在探索自插层范德华磁体中的无序驱动磁性。

Method: 通过磁化率测量、Lee-Yang标度分析及熵异常等手段，研究Fe5GeTe2在居里温度与更高Griffiths温度之间的磁响应，并比较面内与面外磁场下的行为。

Result: 观察到短程有序铁磁团簇嵌入顺磁基体，磁化率呈反常幂律发散，Lee-Yang标度普适性和明显熵异常支持Griffiths相；该非解析响应仅在面内磁场出现，面外磁场会突然改变行为，表明纳米团簇内局域磁相互作用与层状晶格几何紧密耦合。

Conclusion: 研究确立了维度受限、无序驱动的磁性区域，并表明自插层范德华磁体是研究磁无序、各向异性与降低维度相互作用的平台。

Abstract: Over the last decade, the emerging field of two-dimensional van der Waals magnetism has focused mainly on long-range-ordered magnetic phases. Disordered magnetic phases, however, remain largely unexplored. Here, we report a highly anisotropic Griffiths phase driven by self-intercalation in the van der Waals magnet Fe5GeTe2. We find evidence of short-range-ordered ferromagnetic clusters embedded in a paramagnetic matrix, which dominate the magnetic response between the Curie temperature and a higher characteristic Griffiths temperature. An anomalous power-law divergence in the magnetic susceptibility, universal Lee Yang scaling, and distinct entropy anomalies provide further evidence for this phase. Strikingly, the non-analytic Griffiths response emerges exclusively under in-plane magnetic fields, whereas out-of-plane fields abruptly alter this behavior. This directional dependence indicates that the local magnetic interactions within nanoscale clusters are closely coupled to the layered lattice geometry. Our findings establish a regime of dimensionally constrained, disorder-driven magnetism and identify self-intercalated van der Waals magnets as a platform for investigating the interplay between magnetic disorder, anisotropy, and reduced dimensionality.

</details>


### [178] [Can Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials Capture Small Polarons? A Geometry-Based Benchmark in Transition-Metal Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17352)
*Vladislav Korostelev,Pjotrs \v{Z}guns,Konstantin Klyukin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作检验通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）能否描述过渡金属氧化物中的小极化子，发现其表现依赖训练数据与电子关联处理，且均无法稳定空间分离的极化子-施主构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子在过渡金属氧化物电荷输运、催化和降解过程中至关重要，但缺乏显式电子自由度的uMLIPs能否正确描述其局域化尚不清楚。

Method: 利用基于TM-O键长膨胀Z-score的几何描述符识别极化子畸变，研究一系列过渡金属氧化物中H诱导的电子局域化，并比较不同uMLIPs与DFT+U。

Result: uMLIPs表现出从离域态到亚稳小极化子的不同局域化程度，强烈依赖训练数据及电子关联处理；训练数据中缺少特定TM阳离子的Hubbard U修正会导致电子离域，与DFT+U相反。部分uMLIPs能重现耦合质子-极化子转移中的极化子跳跃势垒，但所有模型均不能稳定空间分离的极化子-施主构型，而倾向于在H掺杂附近局域。

Conclusion: 当前uMLIPs尚不能可靠地统一描述过渡金属氧化物中的小极化子，尤其难以捕捉电子关联和供体-极化子空间分离，需在训练数据与电子关联处理上改进。

Abstract: Small polarons, formed when excess electrons become localized through lattice distortions, play a critical role in charge transport, catalysis, and degradation processes in transition-metal (TM) oxides. Whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), which lack explicit electronic degrees of freedom, can describe them remains unclear. Here, we investigate H-induced electron localization across a range of TM oxides using a geometric descriptor based on TM-O bond-length expansion (Z-score) to identify polaronic distortions. We find that uMLIPs exhibit varying degrees of electron localization, from delocalized states to metastable small polarons, with their behavior strongly dependent on the training data and treatment of electronic correlations. In particular, the absence of Hubbard U corrections for specific TM cations in the training data leads to electron delocalization, in contrast to DFT+U predictions. Although some uMLIPs reproduce polaron hopping barriers during coupled proton-polaron transfer, all of them fail to stabilize spatially separated polaron-donor configurations, instead favoring localization near the H dopant.

</details>


### [179] [Collision Cascade Morphology and Defect Nucleation in Pure Tungsten and an Equimolar Refractory W-Ta-Cr-V Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17361)
*Mark Fedorov,Francisco Javier Dominguez-Gutierrez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用机器学习势的分子动力学模拟对比纯W与WTaCrV难熔高熵合金的辐照损伤，发现WTaCrV因更频繁的亚级联分裂及Cr/V轻元素以点缺陷形式容纳损伤而具有更优的抗初级辐照损伤能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解纯W和WTaCrV难熔高熵合金中碰撞级联与缺陷形成机制，特别是亚级联形成和轻元素对辐照损伤的影响，以评估合金的抗辐照性能。

Method: 使用分子动力学模拟，PKA能量0.1–100 keV，采用机器学习表格化高斯近似势（tabGAP）并补充短程ZBL相互作用；分析热峰寿命、影响区域、原子重定位和存活缺陷。

Result: 亚级联形成和轻元素存在至关重要。级联分裂将PKA能量分布到更大区域，产生更少位错和更小空洞；空洞仅出现在W中，WTaCrV中无空洞。WTaCrV中级联分裂更频繁。间隙原子分析表明Cr及少量V作为轻小元素以点缺陷形式容纳损伤，抑制位错环形成。

Conclusion: 与纯W相比，WTaCrV难熔高熵合金表现出更好的抗初级辐照损伤能力，这归因于更频繁的级联分裂以及Cr/V轻元素对点缺陷的容纳。

Abstract: Collision cascades and defect-formation mechanisms in pure W and the WTaCrV refractory high-entropy alloy were investigated using molecular dynamics simulations over primary knock-on atom (PKA) energies of 0.1-100 keV. Machine-learning tabulated Gaussian approximation potentials (tabGAP), complemented by short-range Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) interactions, were employed. Analysis of the heat spike lifetime, affected region, atomic relocations and surviving defects reveals an importance of subcascade formation and presence of light elements in the alloy. Cascade splitting into subcascades contributes to the distribution of the PKA energy over a larger region in the material, nucleating less dislocations and smaller voids - latter being present only in W and absent in WTaCrV. Since cascade splitting is more frequently observed in WTaCrV, the alloy shows better resistance to primary irradiation damage compared to pure W. Apart from cascade splitting, analysis of interstitials shows that Cr and to lesser extent V, being lighter and smaller elements, accommodate the damage in form of point defects, contributing to the mitigation of formation of the dislocation loops.

</details>


### [180] [High-Resolution X-ray Spectro-Microscopy of Au Microcrystallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17417)
*Chaitali Sow,Martin Seyrich,Johannes Hagemann,Giridhar U. Kulkarni,Thomas F. Keller,Christian G. Schroer,Andreas Schropp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用X射线spectro-ptychography在Au L3边（约12 keV）对Au微晶进行多模态振幅/相位XANES成像，在150 nm至1.71 μm厚晶体上实现45 nm空间分辨率，并定量获取约12 keV的异常相位变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异相催化剂功能理解需要高分辨率纳米尺度化学成像；但高于10 keV（如Au L边）的X射线spectro-ptychography受弱吸收截面和低信号限制，相关演示仍有限。

Method: 对同时具有fcc和非常规非立方晶格的Au微晶开展多模态spectro-microscopy；在L3边采集振幅与相位衬度，覆盖150 nm至1.71 μm厚度，空间分辨率45 nm，并将XANES谱与标准透射和荧光衬度对比，同时定量反演约12 keV的异常相位变化。

Result: 在Au薄晶体上分辨出L3边的振幅和相位衬度，获得45 nm空间分辨率；XANES谱与透射/荧光结果高度一致，并提供更高空间分辨率的互补化学信息；约12 keV异常相位变化可定量反演。

Conclusion: 该方法证明X射线spectro-ptychography可在高能Au L边实现高分辨化学成像，适用于广泛功能材料，尤其有利于催化剂纳米尺度化学成像。

Abstract: Understanding the functionalities of heterogeneous catalysts requires high-resolution nanoscale chemical imaging tools. X-ray spectro-ptychography offers this capability by enabling X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) based amplitude and phase contrasts imaging with high spatial resolution. However, such demonstrations at energies above 10 keV, such as the Au L-edges, remain limited due to weak X-ray absorption cross-sections and correspondingly, low signal levels. Here, multimodal spectro-microscopy has been performed on Au microcrystallites that host both conventional face-centered cubic (fcc) and unconventional non-cubic lattices, particularly important for catalysis. Amplitude and phase contrasts across the $\mathrm{L}_3$ edge are resolved on thin Au crystallites (150 nm to 1.71 $\mu\mathrm{m}$) with 45 nm spatial resolution. The resulting XANES spectra exhibit excellent agreement with standard transmission and fluorescence contrasts, while providing complementary chemical insights with improved spatial resolution. Quantitative retrieval of anomalous phase variations ~12 keV further demonstrates the suitability of this method for high-resolution chemical imaging across a wide range of functional materials.

</details>


### [181] [Crystal growth, magnetic, and transport properties of the candidate altermagnet Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17452)
*Nashra Pistawala,Martin R. Lees,Geetha Balakrishnan,Daniel A. Mayoh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次成功生长出高质量Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$单晶，并确认其六方结构、约141 K的反铁磁奈尔温度及非平庸磁输运行为，为交替磁性研究提供实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性是凝聚态磁性新范式，但实验确认的候选材料极少；Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$虽被理论预言为潜在交替磁体，却尚无单晶实验研究。

Method: 采用单晶生长、单晶X射线衍射、电阻率与热容测量以及磁输运测量，系统表征其结构、热学、磁学和输运性质。

Result: 单晶XRD确认六方晶系空间群$P6_3/mmc$，满足交替磁性判据之一；电阻率和热容明确给出反铁磁奈尔温度$T_N \sim 141$ K；磁输运显示磁阻和霍尔响应中的非平庸特征，指示量子扩散输运现象。

Conclusion: 该工作首次以单晶形式合成Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$，使其成为探测交替磁性材料丰富物理现象的实验平台。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a rapidly emerging paradigm in condensed matter magnetism, yet the number of experimentally confirmed altermagnetic candidates remains remarkably limited. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ has long been theoretically proposed as a promising altermagnet, but there are no reports on experimental investigation of single crystals. Here, we report the successful growth of high-quality single crystals of Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ and present its structural, thermal, magnetic, and transport properties. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction provides detailed crystallographic information and confirms the hexagonal crystal symmetry ($P6_3/mmc$), one of the criteria for altermagnetic behavior. Resistivity and heat capacity measurements unambiguously identify an antiferromagnetic N\'{e}el temperature of $T_N \sim 141$ K. Magnetotransport measurements reveal non-trivial features in the magnetoresistance and Hall response, indicative of quantum diffusive transport phenomena. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$, synthesized here for the first time in single-crystalline form, therefore serves as an experimental platform for probing the rich spectrum of phenomena predicted for altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [182] [Electric Edelstein Effect in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17535)
*Xiaoran Lv,Tianqi Zhang,Chaoxi Cui,Yaling Li,Yi Shen,Weikang Wu,Ziming Zhu,Yugui Yao,Zhi-Ming Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出二维体系中的电场Edelstein效应（EEE）：面外栅极电场与面内电场共同产生自旋极化，并可由栅压调控；该效应在广泛磁性与非磁性体系中对称性允许，并在MnSe2等材料中第一性原理预测显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性Edelstein效应（EE）的自旋极化由材料对称性固定，调控维度有限；作者希望发展一种对称性允许、栅极可调的电致自旋极化机制，用于多态自旋电子学。

Method: 提出EEE概念；对全部磁性层点群进行对称性分析；建立微观理论，将EEE归因于能带几何量并指出其在小带隙附近增强；对中心对称铁磁体MnSe2和非磁SnSe开展第一性原理计算。

Result: EEE在铁磁、反铁磁、交错磁及非磁等广泛体系中允许；在线性EE被对称禁戒时（如中心对称MnSe2）可产生自旋极化；在EE已存在时，EEE增加一个通常与传统分量非共线的栅极可调分量，使两个电场成为独立旋钮，将自旋极化控制维度从一维提升到二维；SnSe中两旋钮可覆盖扩展的自旋球区域。

Conclusion: EEE是一种对称性导向、栅压可调的电学自旋操控途径，为二维量子材料中的多态自旋电子学开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We propose the electric Edelstein effect (EEE) in two-dimensional (2D) systems, where crossed out-of-plane and in-plane electric fields generate a spin polarization. The gate field converts the Edelstein response from a material-fixed spin polarization into a gate-tunable one. A symmetry analysis over all magnetic layer point groups shows that the EEE is allowed in a wide range of magnetic and nonmagnetic systems, including ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and altermagnets. The EEE plays two distinct roles: it generates spin polarization where the linear Edelstein effect (EE) is symmetry-forbidden, as in centrosymmetric materials; and it adds a new gate-tunable component, generally non-collinear with the conventional one, where the EE is already present. The two fields then act as independent knobs and raise the control dimension of the spin polarization from one to two. Our microscopic theory traces the EEE to band-geometric quantities, which are strongly enhanced near small band gaps. First-principles calculations predict pronounced effects in the centrosymmetric ferromagnet MnSe2, where the linear EE is symmetry-forbidden. In the nonmagnet SnSe where the EE and EEE coexist, the two knobs cover an extended region of the spin sphere. Our findings establish the EEE as a symmetry-guided, gate-tunable route to electrical spin manipulation and open avenues for multistate spintronics in 2D quantum materials.

</details>


### [183] [A grand potential for molten-salt electrochemical phase diagrams from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17611)
*Lorenzo Bastonero,Lucio Colombi Ciacchi,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于第一性原理的多组分电化学相图构建框架，结合机器学习原子间势，用于熔盐电解低碳金属提取的电极、电解质与工况筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清洁能源转型推高金属需求，而金属提取是重要碳排放源；熔盐电解有望无碳生产铝、铁、钛和关键金属，但腐蚀性操作条件和复杂多组分原料使实验开发昂贵且困难，因此需要可预测相图以加速材料与条件设计。

Method: 建立多组分体系电化学相图的第一性原理框架；推导可规模化生成相图的大势，并利用微调的基础机器学习原子间势提供自由能；在熔融CaCl2中的硅体系验证，并筛选十种碱金属/碱土金属卤化物电解质用于铝电解。

Result: 硅-熔融CaCl2体系在有限结构下可重现实验相图，扩展更大晶体结构集后还能预测新相区；铝电解筛选中，三项热力学判据识别出NaF，其中冰晶石成为稳定含铝相，重现了Hall和Héroult于1886年确立的化学体系。

Conclusion: 该框架为从复杂原料出发选择兼容电极、电解质和操作条件，实现低碳金属生产提供了第一性原理依据。

Abstract: The clean-energy transition is driving unprecedented demand for metals, whose extraction is among the largest industrial sources of carbon emissions. Molten-salt electrolysis offers a path toward carbon-free production of aluminum, iron, titanium, and critical metals. However, corrosive operating conditions and complex, multicomponent feedstocks make experimental development costly and challenging. Here, we introduce a first-principles framework for constructing electrochemical phase diagrams of multicomponent systems to accelerate electrode and electrolyte design and guide the selection of compatible materials and operating conditions for electrochemical cells. We derive a grand potential that generates these diagrams at scale, and supply free energies using first-principles data obtained through a fine-tuned foundation machine-learned interatomic potential. For silicon in molten CaCl$_2$, the construction not only recovers the experimental diagram when using the first-principles framework on a limited set of structures, but also predicts further domains when extended to a larger set of crystal structures. Finally, we demonstrate the predictive power of the approach by screening ten alkali and alkaline-earth halide electrolytes for aluminum electrowinning. Three thermodynamic criteria identify NaF, where cryolite emerges as the stable aluminum-bearing phase, recovering the chemistry that Hall and H\'eroult established empirically in 1886. The framework provides a first-principles basis for selecting compatible electrodes, electrolytes, and operating conditions toward low-carbon metal production from complex source materials.

</details>


### [184] [A Parameter-Free Reference Hamiltonian for Tight-Binding Vanadium Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17687)
*Jun-qiang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 针对钒氧化物DFTB缺乏可迁移描述的问题，作者构建了一个无经验可调项的紧束缚模型：参考哈密顿量来自叠加中性原子、完整PBE交换关联势与冻结核正交性，电荷响应用PBE线性响应得到的三项屏蔽因子和未屏蔽库仑范围描述。该模型复现了VO2的PBE/PAW能量差和V 3d自洽势移，但无自旋模型不能自发区分对称等价位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二阶DFTB对钒氧化物不可迁移：中性双中心参考忽略eV量级环境项，Mulliken电荷模型将紧凑V 3d壳层的自洽势移低估到不足PBE的一半；二者在V 3d与O 2p带质心分离上各有eV量级偏差并相互抵消，因此需要更可靠且无拟合参数的紧束缚描述。
