Method: 建立紧束缚模型：参考哈密顿量由叠加中性原子、完整PBE交换关联势和冻结核正交性导出，不含可调项；轨道分辨电荷响应采用三个来自PBE线性响应的屏蔽因子及未屏蔽库仑范围。以PAW为主要参考，检验VO2的固定几何参考能、M1相对金红石的能量差、体积压缩/膨胀响应，以及拟合与留出的PBE电荷响应。

Result: 固定几何和给定数值设置下，参考能与平面波Harris-Foulkes能量差匹配：M1相对金红石的有符号偏差为+2.6 meV/atom，对应能量差92 meV/atom；3%体积压缩和膨胀下分别为+6.2和-5.2 meV/atom。电荷响应在容差内捕获22个拟合PBE响应中的21个以及全部8个留出响应；金红石中V 3d自洽势移为-3.89 eV，接近PBE的-3.92 eV。无自旋模型在固定几何下保持凸性，在其实现算符和采样尊重的对称性下不能自发区分对称等价位点。

Conclusion: 该无参数紧束缚模型能较好重现PBE/PAW的VO2能量差与电荷响应，特别是V 3d自洽势移。但仅凭该无自旋模型不足以解释Magnéli相中对称等价位点的分化；这种分化必须来自电子-晶格耦合或显式自旋/关联项。

Abstract: Density-functional tight binding has yet to yield a transferable description of vanadium oxides; for the standard second-order construction and the tested cells, we demonstrate that two of its approximations deviate by electron volts and offset each other in the band-index centroid separation between the V 3d and O 2p bands: the neutral two-centre reference omits electron-volt environmental terms, whereas the Mulliken charge model assigns the compact V 3d shell less than half of the self-consistent potential shift observed in PBE. Instead, we build a tight-binding model where the reference Hamiltonian derives from superposed neutral atoms, the full PBE exchange-correlation potential and frozen-core orthogonality, without adjustable terms, and where the orbital-resolved charge response employs three screening factors obtained from PBE linear response and an unscreened Coulomb range. Against the primary PAW reference, the reference energy at fixed geometry and the stated numerical settings matches the plane-wave Harris-Foulkes energy differences of VO2, exhibiting signed deviations of +2.6 meV/atom for M1 relative to rutile (an energy difference of 92 meV/atom) and +6.2 and -5.2 meV/atom under 3% volume compression and expansion; meanwhile, the charge response captures 21 of 22 fitted and all 8 held-out matched PBE responses within tolerance, providing the V 3d shell with nearly the same self-consistent shift as PBE (-3.89 versus -3.92 eV in rutile). The convexity of the spinless model at fixed geometry prevents it from spontaneously differentiating symmetry-equivalent sites under the symmetries respected by its implemented operators and sampling, so within this model such differentiation in the Magn\'eli phases must arise from electron-lattice coupling or an explicit spin or correlation term.

</details>


### [185] [Hydrogen-Bond Density Drives the Order-Disorder Phase Transition from Orientational to Vibrational in Polyalcohol Plastic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17774)
*Ares Sanuy,Carlos Escorihuela-Sayalero,Pol Lloveras,Mar\'ia del Barrio,Hassan Allouchi,Josep-Llu\'is Tamarit,Luis Carlos Pardo,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过经典分子动力学模拟 NPG、PG、PE，揭示新戊烷基塑料晶体的相变熵随氢键连通性增加发生从取向无序主导到晶格振动主导的完全反转，并指出氢键密度可调控热效应大小及其压力稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新戊烷基塑料晶体的有序-无序相变伴随巨大熵变，是热能存储和压卡冷却的候选固态材料，但其熵变的微观本质是各化合物固有属性，还是可通过分子设计普遍理解并系统调控，尚不明确。

Method: 采用经典分子动力学模拟新戊基二醇（NPG）、五甘油（PG）和季戊四醇（PE），并用实验相变温度、焓变和相图进行验证，分析不同氢键连通性下相变熵的取向、构象和振动贡献。

Result: 随着分子氢键连通性增加，相变熵特征完全反转：NPG 中取向无序主导；PE 中晶格振动主导且对压力异常稳健；PG 处于平衡中间区，取向、构象和振动通道贡献相当。该反转源于两个耦合微观机制：氢键网络致密化使有序与无序相声子态密度间低频间隙增大，以及分子对称性提高逐渐抑制取向无序。

Conclusion: 由于振动通道对压力的敏感性显著低于取向通道，氢键密度成为化学上可及的设计杠杆，可同时调控氢键塑料晶体热效应的大小和压力稳健性，对寻找新型压卡与热能存储材料具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Neopentane-derived plastic crystals undergo order-disorder phase transitions accompanied by exceptionally large entropy changes, making them attractive solid-state materials for thermal energy storage and barocaloric cooling. Yet it remains unclear whether the microscopic nature of this entropy change is an intrinsic property of each compound or can be understood generally and tuned systematically through molecular design. Here, we use classical molecular dynamics simulations of neopentyl glycol (NPG), pentaglycerine (PG), and pentaerythritol (PE), validated against experimental transition temperatures, enthalpy changes, and phase diagrams, to show that the character of the phase-transition entropy undergoes a complete inversion as the hydrogen-bond connectivity of the molecule increases: orientational disordering dominates in NPG, whereas lattice vibrations dominate in PE and become remarkably robust against pressure. PG occupies a balanced intermediate regime in which the orientational, conformational, and vibrational channels contribute comparably. This inversion reflects two coupled microscopic mechanisms: a growing low-frequency gap between the phonon densities of states of the ordered and disordered phases as the hydrogen-bond network densifies, and an increase in molecular symmetry that progressively suppresses orientational disordering. Because the vibrational channel is substantially less sensitive to pressure than the orientational one, hydrogen-bond density emerges as a practical, chemically accessible design handle for tailoring not only the magnitude but also the pressure robustness of the caloric response in hydrogen-bonded plastic crystals, with direct implications for the search for new barocaloric and thermal-energy-storage materials.

</details>


### [186] [Learning from fracture: Teaching language models to design through physical experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17804)
*Bo Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FracLearn 提出一个“经验—抽象—泛化”框架，让语言模型在不更新权重的情况下，通过模拟断裂实验学习并优化物理设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型如何从经验中学习优化物理设计，尤其是将计算实验转化为可复用的材料与工程知识。

Method: 在受俄罗斯方块启发的断裂游戏中，模型先提出结构方案并观察模拟断裂响应以积累经验；再将观察抽象为连接增强相放置、裂纹演化与载荷传递的条件性原则，并附支持证据与失效条件；最后在泛化阶段组合这些原则设计新结构，挑战并超越先前设计记录。

Result: 多轮设计活动显示模型可通过修正假设逐步改进并恢复性能，最佳综合得分比经验阶段记录高 11.0%；断裂分析表明性能提升与裂纹偏转、临时捕获等增韧机制及更高残余承载能力相关。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条通向科学智能体的路径，使其能把计算实验转化为可复用的物理知识，用于材料发现与工程设计。

Abstract: How can language models learn to optimize physical designs through experience? We introduce FracLearn, an experience-abstraction-generalization framework evaluated through a deliberately unfamiliar, Tetris-inspired fracture game. The model first proposes architectures and examines simulated fracture responses to accumulate experience. Abstraction organizes these observations into conditional principles linking reinforcement placement, crack evolution and load transfer, with supporting evidence and failure conditions. Generalization challenges the model to combine these principles in new architectures and surpass the earlier design record. Campaigns demonstrate progressive refinement and recovery through hypothesis revision, yielding a best composite score 11.0% above the experience-stage record. Fracture analyses connect improved performance to crack deflection and temporary trapping as toughening mechanisms, alongside greater residual load capacity. Learning occurs through external knowledge and decisions, without model-weight updates. This approach offers a route to scientific agents that turn computational experiments into reusable physical knowledge for materials discovery and engineering design.

</details>


### [187] [Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17809)
*Inhee Maeng,Young Mi Lee,Jinwoo Park,Seung Jae Oh,Min-Cherl Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 近期三方面证据质疑铅卤钙钛矿中光生载流子以大型极化子形式运动并借此屏蔽缺陷的经典图像；作者主张需区分极化子形成与缺陷容忍机制，并提出通过测量晶格畸变随激发密度的变化来澄清载流子行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铅卤钙钛矿普遍被认为形成大型极化子，晶格畸变可屏蔽带电缺陷，从而解释缺陷容忍性；但新近结构、理论、二维材料与输运/缺陷容忍实验对此提出挑战，需检验该图像是否成立。

Method: 综述并整合三类近期证据：时间分辨结构探测与计入热运动的有效质量计算；三维至二维及RP/DJ二维钙钛矿中载流子-晶格耦合比较；缺陷容忍直接测试与输运数据。在此基础上提出关键实验：测量晶格畸变作为激发密度的函数，覆盖太阳光低密度至超快实验高密度。

Result: 热运动本身即可提高有效质量，使极化子证据含糊；3D→2D耦合增强，RP与DJ通过不同通道耦合，行为由主导耦合通道而非仅晶格刚度决定；首个缺陷容忍直接测试不支持极化子屏蔽，作者将缺陷容忍归因于柔软无序晶格。输运数据显示极化子屏蔽降低载流子间散射，但不证明保护载流子免受缺陷影响。

Conclusion: 即使证实极化子形成，也不等于证明极化子是缺陷容忍的原因；需在宽激发密度范围内测量晶格畸变，以分离极化子形成与缺陷容忍机制，并据此设计实验澄清铅卤钙钛矿中光生载流子的真实行为。

Abstract: Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call this picture into question. First, time-resolved structural probes now measure the photoinduced lattice distortion directly; yet calculations show that thermal motion of the lattice alone can raise the carrier effective mass-an increase previously taken as evidence for polarons-so that it remains unclear whether the lattice distortion around a carrier is really a polaron. Second, the carrier-lattice coupling strengthens as the material is thinned from 3D to 2D. In the two main families of 2D perovskites, Ruddlesden-Popper and Dion-Jacobson, the carrier couples to the lattice through different channels, and their contrasting behavior is set by which coupling channel dominates rather than by lattice stiffness alone. Third, the first direct test of defect tolerance does not point to polaron screening; its authors attribute defect tolerance to the soft, disordered lattice. New transport data show that polaron screening reduces scattering between carriers, but not that it protects carriers from defects. Polaron formation, even if established, therefore does not establish polarons as the cause of defect tolerance. Separating the two requires measuring the lattice distortion as a function of excitation density, over a range spanning the low densities of a solar cell under sunlight and the much higher densities used in most ultrafast experiments. On this basis, we propose experiments that can clarify how photogenerated carriers behave in lead-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [188] [Orthoexciton-to-paraexciton conversion rate in Cu$_2$O at sub-Kelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17812)
*Yusuke Morita,Yukihiro Akiyama,Yuki Haita,Yuga Hirota,Kosuke Yoshioka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在稀释制冷机中测量了低于1 K时Cu2O中1s正激子到1s仲激子的转化率；通过应变陷阱中激子的时间分辨发光谱发现，该转化率在64 mK、2.2 kbar下为5.9×10^7 s^-1，且在64–800 mK间仅弱依赖温度，低于2 K时仍继续下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前预期转化率在约1.7 K以下几乎变为常数，但低于1 K的实验数据缺乏，需要检验该预期并理解低温下正激子—仲激子转化的机制。

Method: 使用稀释制冷机，对应变诱导陷阱势中受限的1s正激子和1s仲激子进行时间分辨发光光谱测量；通过1s正激子的Γ3^-–LO声子辅助发光时间演化提取转化率，温度范围64–800 mK，施加应力2.2 kbar。

Result: 在混合室温度64 mK、2.2 kbar下得到转化率为5.9×10^7 s^-1；64–800 mK之间转化率随温度变化很弱，且不到相同应力下2 K时报道值1.6×10^8 s^-1的一半。因此转化率在2 K以下继续下降，与约1.7 K以下近乎恒定的先前预期相反。

Conclusion: 作者用正激子动量分布在低温转化过程中未与晶格达到平衡来解释结果，并指出需要新理论同时说明低于1 K时转化率较小及其弱温度依赖性。

Abstract: We measured the conversion rate from 1s orthoexcitons to 1s paraexcitons in Cu$_2$O below 1 K using a dilution refrigerator. To determine the conversion rate, we performed time-resolved luminescence spectroscopy of 1s orthoexcitons and 1s paraexcitons confined in a strain-induced trap potential. From the time evolution of the ${\Gamma}$$_3$$^{-}$-LO-phonon-assisted luminescence of 1s orthoexcitons, we obtained a conversion rate of $5.9 \times 10^7$ s${^{-1}}$ at a mixing-chamber temperature of 64 mK under an applied stress of 2.2 kbar. Between 64 and 800 mK, the conversion rate depends only weakly on temperature, and it is less than half of the value of $1.6 \times 10^8$ s${^{-1}}$ reported at 2 K under the same applied stress. The conversion rate therefore continues to decrease below 2 K, contrary to the previous expectation that it becomes nearly constant below about 1.7 K. We discuss these results in terms of the momentum distribution of orthoexcitons, which does not reach equilibrium with the lattice during the conversion at these low temperatures. These findings call for a theory that accounts for both the small magnitude of the conversion rate and its weak temperature dependence below 1 K.

</details>


### [189] [Colossal Spin--Lattice--Charge Coupling in Noncollinear Ferrimagnetic NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17849)
*Peng Wang,Nanshu Liu,Hongkuan Yuan,Xue Jiang,Jijun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文预测非共线亚铁磁 NbMnX2（X=S、Se、Te）单层具有室温附近居里温度，并揭示其磁-力-热-电多功能耦合，包括巨各向异性磁阻、磁致弹性模量变化和增强磁热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线亚铁磁体兼具净磁矩与内部自旋自由度，可耦合磁、力、热和电子响应；作者旨在寻找稳定的二维非共线亚铁磁平台并探索其多功能应用。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算、对称性分析、原子自旋模拟和输运建模。

Result: 发现 NbMnX2 单层具有稳定的非共线亚铁磁基态，居里温度最高达 313 K；交换作用、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用和磁各向异性竞争产生场响应倾斜态。NbMnTe2 中磁化旋转使弹性模量变化达 36%；NbMnS2 和 NbMnSe2 中晶格熵显著增强近室温磁热响应；NbMnSe2 中磁化旋转恢复镜像保护交叉、连续关闭约 0.13 eV 带隙，并在 100 K 产生高达 10000% 的电导型各向异性磁阻比。

Conclusion: 非共线 NbMnX2 单层可作为二维自旋电子学、纳米力学和热控制的多功能平台。

Abstract: Noncollinear ferrimagnets combine a net magnetic moment with internal spin degrees of freedom, creating opportunities to couple magnetic, mechanical, thermal, and electronic responses. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, atomistic spin simulations, and transport modeling, we identify stable NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) monolayers with noncollinear ferrimagnetic ground states and Curie temperatures up to 313 K. Competing exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, and magnetic anisotropy produce field-responsive canted states that couple strongly to the lattice and charge carriers. Magnetization rotation changes the elastic modulus of NbMnTe$_2$ by up to 36\%, while lattice entropy substantially enhances the near-room-temperature magnetocaloric response of NbMnS$_2$ and NbMnSe$_2$. In NbMnSe$_2$, rotation restores a mirror-protected crossing, continuously closes an approximately 0.13 eV gap, and produces a conductivity-based anisotropic magnetoresistance ratio up to 10,000\% at 100 K. The combined results establish noncollinear NbMnX$_2$ monolayers as a multifunctional platform for two-dimensional spintronics, nanomechanics, and thermal control.

</details>


### [190] [ARES: a plane-wave density-functional-theory code in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17854)
*Zehan Liu,Qian Wang,Xiangyu Zheng,Jiaxiang Li,Chuntian Wu,Yongshuo Chen,Xuecheng Shao,Qiang Xu,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ARES 是 Rust 编写的平面波 DFT 软件，采用 22 个 crate 的分层工作区，支持 NC 赝势与 PAW、LDA/PBE、k 点采样、力/应力/弛豫、能带、DOS 和色散校正，可串行或 MPI 并行；其突出特点是面向 Quantum ESPRESSO 的系统化验证体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发从头算原子材料模拟软件，并通过可复现、可再生成的验证夹具来严格检验数值正确性，使各数值组件能与 Quantum ESPRESSO 在相同物理和数值输入下对标。

Method: 以 Rust 实现平面波 DFT，采用分层 22-crate workspace；支持 norm-conserving 赝势和 PAW，LDA 与 PBE 泛函，Gamma-only 和显式 k 点采样，smearing 与 mixing，力、应力、弛豫、能带结构、态密度和色散校正；串行或 MPI 按 k 点池、能带组和平面波行分布执行。验证使用编号 fixtures 仓库，参考数据由脚本重新生成而非一次性保存，并与 Quantum ESPRESSO 逐项比较；赝势还与全电子参考数据对标。

Result: 在六种材料（一种半导体、三种金属、两种二维体系）上，费米能级 1 eV 内的能带能量与参考值中位偏差低于 0.4 meV；所读取赝势在全部 65 个配对元素上的 delta factor 中位数为 0.696 meV/atom。

Conclusion: ARES 已具备多种平面波 DFT 功能并建立了较严格的验证流程，但限制明确：仅支持两种泛函，无自旋极化或自旋轨道路径，应力实现有明确适用范围，且不支持加速器执行。

Abstract: ARES (Ab initio atomic mateRial modEling Software at JLU) is a plane-wave density-functional-theory (DFT) code written entirely in Rust as a layered 22-crate workspace. It supports norm-conserving pseudopotentials and the projector augmented-wave (PAW) method with the local-density approximation (LDA) and Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) functionals, {\Gamma}-only and explicit k-point sampling, smearing and mixing, forces, stress, relaxation, band structures, densities of states (DOS) and dispersion corrections; execution is serial or MPI-distributed over k-point pools, band groups and plane-wave rows. The code's distinguishing engineering choice is its validation apparatus. Every numerical component is checked against Quantum ESPRESSO driven from the same physical and numerical inputs, and the comparison is organized as numbered fixtures kept in the repository whose reference data is regenerated by a script instead of being captured once. On six materials spanning a semiconductor, three metals and two two-dimensional systems, band energies within one electronvolt of the Fermi level agree with the reference to a median below 0.4 meV. The pseudopotentials the code reads are validated against all-electron reference data as well: the median delta factor is 0.696 meV/atom over all 65 paired elements. Restrictions are stated with the capabilities: two functionals, no spin-polarized or spin-orbit path, a stress implementation with an explicit scope, and no accelerator execution.

</details>


### [191] [Thermal stability and structural ordering of thick Co$_2$MnAl films grown on MgO and Si substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17955)
*David Robles-Cuenca,Juan Antonio Gonz\'alez,Juan Pedro Andr\'es,Miguel \'Angel Arranz,Mohamed Salaheldeen,Ricardo L\'opez Ant\'on*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作研究750 nm厚Co2MnAl（CMA）薄膜在MgO(001)和Si衬底上的结构与热稳定性，旨在为需要较大磁体积的ANF能量收集器、集成电感器和磁MEMS等应用建立热集成路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝大多数CMA研究集中于超薄自旋电子学结，但ANF能量收集、集成功率电感和磁MEMS等按磁体积缩放的应用需要显著更厚的薄膜，因此需要明确厚CMA薄膜的加工窗口和热集成兼容性。

Method: 在MgO(001)和Si衬底上生长750 nm厚CMA薄膜，并通过结构分析、热处理和磁性测量，研究其有序化、界面扩散、相变及饱和磁化行为。

Result: MgO上600 °C可获得优化的B2有序；Si上高于500 °C时发生Si互扩散，形成界面硅化物和Kirkendall空洞，导致微裂纹、电阻率突跳并最终转变为完全无序A2相。尽管成分为Mn贫化的Co2.25Mn0.7Al1.05，薄膜仍保持约600 emu/cm^3的饱和磁化强度，与离化学计量Heusler合金的理论模型一致。

Conclusion: 研究界定了将厚CMA层集成到多功能CMOS兼容平台中的热处理加工窗口，并表明MgO衬底更适合保持B2有序，而Si衬底在高温下存在严重扩散退化风险。

Abstract: While most studies on Co$2$MnAl (CMA) focus on the ultrathin regime for spintronic junctions, applications scaling with magnetic volume--such as Anomalous Nernst Effect (ANE) energy harvesters, integrated power inductors, and magnetic MEMS--require significantly thicker films. In this work, 750 nm thick CMA films were grown on MgO (001) and Si substrates to establish a roadmap for their thermal integration. Structural analysis reveals that B2 ordering is optimized on MgO at 600 $^\circ$C, which is important for functional devices. In contrast, samples on Si substrates exhibit a disorder transition toward the A2 phase, closely linked to diffusion-driven degradation processes. Above 500 $^\circ$C, Si interdiffusion into the Heusler layer triggers the formation of interfacial silicides and Kirkendall voids, leading to microcracking, electrical resistivity jumps, and a final fully disordered A2 phase. Despite the Mn-poor stoichiometry (Co$2.25$Mn$0.7$Al$1.05$), the films maintain a robust saturation magnetization of ~600 emu/cm$^3$, consistent with recent theoretical models for off-stoichiometric Heusler alloys. These results define the processing windows for integrating thick CMA layers into multifunctional CMOS-compatible platforms.

</details>


### [192] [Field-free Magnetoacoustic Nonreciprocity via Exchange-bias Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17979)
*Weiqi Du,Mingyuan Ma,Zheng Sun,Chong Chen,Shixuan Liang,Deqi Tao,Shihai An,Yifan Fu,Sulei Fu,Feng Pan,Cheng Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层结构与剪切水平波延迟线集成的器件中，通过交换偏置工程实现零场磁声非互易传播，归一化隔离达21 dB/mm且符号可由磁场扫描历史编程，数值优化预测可达91 dB/mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁声系统适合小型化、无源非互易器件，但强非互易通常依赖外部偏置磁场，限制器件微型化与集成。因此需要无需外场的磁声非互易方案。

Method: 构建IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层并集成剪切水平波延迟线；利用交换偏置稳定具有时间反演与空间反演对称性破缺的剩磁态，调控自旋波色散，在零场下实现共振磁声耦合；并通过数值优化增强动态磁不对称性。

Result: 实验实现零场归一化隔离21 dB/mm，隔离符号可由场扫描历史编程；数值优化预测通过提高动态磁不对称性可将零场归一化隔离提升至91 dB/mm。

Conclusion: 交换偏置工程为紧凑型射频隔离器和环形器提供可行路径，也可作为无需外部磁场的磁声器件通用策略。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal wave propagation is essential for directional signal processing and isolation. Magnetoacoustic systems are attractive platforms for nonreciprocal devices because of their small footprint and passive operation. However, pronounced nonreciprocity typically requires an external bias field,limiting device miniaturization and integration. Here, we demonstrate field-free magnetoacoustic nonreciprocity in an IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB multilayer integrated with a shear-horizontal-wave delay line. Exchange-bias engineering stabilizes a remanent magnetic state with broken time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetries while tailoring the spin-wave dispersion to achieve resonant magnetoacoustic coupling at zero field. The normalized isolation reaches 21 dB/mm, with its sign programmable by the field-sweep history. Further numerical optimization predicts a zero-field normalized isolation of 91 dB/mm by increasing the dynamical magnetic asymmetry. Our work establishes exchange-bias engineering as a promising route toward compact RF isolators and circulators and as a general strategy for realizing magnetoacoustic devices without external magnetic fields.

</details>


### [193] [Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17987)
*Guanchen Dong,Huanrong Liu,Lin Wang,Yuxuan Chen,Zhengyu Tu,Qing-an Li,Bin Xu,Jian Li,Shan Zhang,Xiaotong Li,Linghua Tan,Guangcheng Yang,Jun Kang,Rui Su,Pengfei Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出改进的NNAP势函数与主动学习策略，用仅13,680个构型可靠描述含能材料，并揭示DAAzF热解与冲击响应机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料涉及多体复杂相互作用，传统机器学习势通常依赖大量数据，难以兼顾效率与可靠性。

Method: 引入sphmulti球谐基组，在每个径向通道融合额外径向基和元素特异化学权重；结合超活跃学习、最远点采样、迭代充分性准则及知识引导关键事件训练NNAP。

Result: 对3,3'-二氨基-4,4'-偶氮氧化呋咱，仅用13,680个构型即复现异构化势垒、热解活化能和冲击Hugoniot行为；MD模拟表明热解经呋咱环开环后链式反应进行，冲击加载诱导层间结构变形并驱动各向异性氢转移。

Conclusion: 该工作为含能材料构建高效、可靠的机器学习势提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Energetic materials involve multiple interactions, making reliable machine-learning potentials (MLPs) difficult to construct without extensive datasets. Here, we enhance the ability of the neural network interatomic potential (NNAP) to describe molecular systems using sphmulti, a refined spherical-harmonic basis set that combines extra radial basis with element-specific chemical weighting in each radial channel. We further integrate hyperactive learning, farthest-point sampling, and iterative sufficiency criteria with knowledge-guided key events, enabling the NNAP to retain essential information for describing the material system. For 3,3'-diamino-4,4'-azoxyfurazan, the resulting NNAP is trained on only 13,680 configurations yet captures key properties, including the isomerization barrier, pyrolysis activation energy, and shock Hugoniot behavior. NNAP-driven molecular dynamics simulations further reveal that pyrolysis proceeds through furazan-ring opening followed by chain reactions, whereas shock loading induces anisotropic hydrogen transfer driven by interlayer structural deformations. This work enables efficient and reliable MLPs for energetic materials.

</details>


### [194] [Hydrogen partial pressure as a key parameter in the reactive sputtering of photochromic YHO thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18068)
*E. Strods,M. Zubkins,E. Letko,V. Vibornijs,A. Sarakovskis,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过调节反应磁控溅射中 H2/Ar 比并经空气后氧化制备 YHO 薄膜，发现低 H2/Ar（低氢分压）可提升透明度、光致变色对比度和漂白速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氢分压/H2/Ar 比及氧掺入对含氧氢化钇薄膜的织构、光学透过率和光致变色性能的影响，并检验氧含量与光致变色响应之间的常见关联。

Method: 采用反应磁控溅射在不同 H2/Ar 气流比下沉积 YHO 薄膜，随后空气后氧化；原位等离子体发射光谱监测沉积过程；XRD 分析织构与晶粒尺寸；光学透射率表征；太阳模拟器光照 10 min 测光致变色对比；双指数模型分析漂白动力学；XPS 表征氧掺入。

Result: H2/Ar 增加使薄膜从强 <100> 织构转为多晶且晶粒略减小；透射率约从 85% 降至 70%（H2/Ar 至 0.6），更高氢含量时急剧下降。690–820 nm 厚膜光致变色对比从 14% 降至 7%（H2/Ar 0.08→0.60），360–490 nm 薄膜趋势类似但较弱。H2/Ar<0.25 时漂白时间常数约 1–15 min。XPS 显示低 H2/Ar 薄膜氧掺入更高，偏离常见氧含量—光致变色响应关联。

Conclusion: 反应溅射中低氢分压有利于 YHO 薄膜获得更高光学透明度、更强光致变色对比和更快漂白动力学。

Abstract: Oxygen-containing yttrium hydride (YHO) thin films were synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering over a wide range of hydrogen-to-argon gas flow ratios (H2/Ar) during yttrium hydride deposition, followed by post-oxidation in air. The deposition process was monitored in situ by plasma optical emission spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals a strong dependence of crystallographic texture on the H2/Ar ratio: highly <100>-textured films formed at low H2/Ar ratios progressively evolve into more polycrystalline microstructures with slightly reduced crystallite size as the hydrogen content increases. The optical transmittance gradually decreases from approximately 85 % to 70 % as the H2/Ar ratio is raised to 0.6, followed by a sharp drop at higher hydrogen contents. The photochromic contrast of 690-820 nm thick YHO films, measured after 10 min of illumination with a solar simulator, decreases from 14 % to 7 % as the H2/Ar ratio increases from 0.08 to 0.60. Thinner films (360-490 nm) exhibit a similar but less pronounced trend. Bleaching kinetics, analysed using a double-exponential model, indicate faster recovery, with effective bleaching time constants of approximately 1-15 min for films deposited at H2/Ar < 0.25. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals higher oxygen incorporation in films deposited at lower H2/Ar ratios, deviating from the commonly reported correlation between oxygen content and photochromic response in YHO. These results demonstrate that low hydrogen partial pressure during reactive sputtering is advantageous for achieving higher optical transparency, enhanced photochromic contrast, and faster bleaching kinetics in YHO thin films.

</details>


### [195] [Tellurium trioxide as an n-type wide-gap semiconductor with ultrahigh work functions from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18076)
*Tianwei Wang,Teruya Nagafuji,Kota Hanzawa,Hidenori Hiramatsu,Fumiyasu Oba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，宽禁带β-TeO3可实现约8 eV的超高功函数，并通过杂质掺杂和表面O空位调控其电子性质，有望用于空穴注入/提取/传输层及透明电极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有极端功函数的下一代宽禁带半导体，对电子和光电器件的持续发展具有重要意义。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，研究β-TeO3的能带结构、点缺陷（间隙H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn，置换F，O空位）以及一系列低指数非极性表面的功函数与缺陷行为。

Result: β-TeO3带隙超过3 eV，表现为n型半导体，功函数可高达约8 eV；间隙H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn和置换F为浅施主，O空位是次要的载流子补偿缺陷，会在邻近Te位点局域俘获电子；有序O空位在低指数非极性表面能量上有利并倾向于降低功函数。

Conclusion: 需精确控制O化学势以抑制表面O空位形成，从而保持β-TeO3因超高功函数而具备的优异电子性能；该材料在空穴注入/提取/传输层以及发光器件和光伏电池的透明电极方面具有潜力，并为载流子掺杂和表面调控提供指导。

Abstract: The exploration of next-generation wide-gap semiconductors with extreme work functions is highly desirable for the continued advancement of electronic and optoelectronic devices. By using first principles calculations, we show that \b{eta}-TeO3, with a wide band gap exceeding 3 eV, emerges as a promising n-type semiconductor exhibiting ultrahigh work functions of up to ~8 eV. Intentionally or unintentionally incorporated interstitial H, Li, Na, Cu, Mg, and Zn impurities and substitutional F impurities are identified as shallow donors, whereas O vacancies act as minor carrier-compensating defects that locally trap electrons at neighbouring Te sites. The work function is evaluated across a series of low-index nonpolar surfaces. The presence of ordered O vacancies is energetically favoured on these surfaces and tends to reduce the corresponding work functions. These findings underscore the significance of precise control over the O chemical potential to suppress surface O-vacancy formation, thereby preserving the exceptional electronic performance of \b{eta}-TeO3 associated with ultrahigh work functions. Collectively, the results highlight the potential of \b{eta}-TeO3 for hole injection/extraction/transport layers as well as transparent electrodes for applications such as light-emitting devices and photovoltaic cells, while providing useful guidance on carrier doping and surface tuning for these practical applications through elucidating the effects of the point-defect and surface configurations on the electronic properties.

</details>


### [196] [Local-resolvent formulation and assessment of linear-scaling Kohn-Sham calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18137)
*Albert Linda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出局域预解式密度泛函理论（LRes-DFT）：用若干复能量处单电子预解式对角元代替全系统轨道，实现无需轨道的绝缘体Kohn-Sham DFT，并给出三维分治实现与基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论因需要延展于整个体系的轨道而计算昂贵；对绝缘体，可利用单电子预解式对角元避免显式轨道与动能泛函。

Method: 使用 g(r,z)=<r|(z-ĥ)^(-1)|r>，在包围占据能级的复能量圆上取 M 个等距点，并偏移半个间距使点不在实轴上；对预解式求和等价于占据数 1/(1+u^M)，形成平滑阶跃。密度和带能量对 g 线性，故无需轨道或动能泛函。若圆随化学势移动，固定电子数下的力精确。小带隙时可用 Gauss-Legendre 与 Zolotarev 规则减少取点。三维实现用每个分子周围小窗口的本征对替代预解式，属于采用不同占据函数的 divide-and-conquer（DC）。

Result: 对最多1024个电子的 H2 分子晶体，与 Kohn-Sham 结果一致到每电子 2.5×10^-7 Ha；单核计算在约500电子以下比稠密对角化慢，在1024电子时快3.5倍。该方法对无序或压缩体系以及弥散基函数会损失精度。

Conclusion: LRes-DFT 是一种无需轨道的绝缘体 DFT 方案，具有线性标度潜力，并在分子晶体基准中达到高精度；但其精度受体系无序、压缩和基组弥散性限制。

Abstract: Kohn-Sham density-functional theory is expensive because it needs orbitals that extend over the whole system. For insulators this can be avoided by using only the diagonal of the one-electron resolvent, $g(\mathbf{r},z)=\langle\mathbf{r}|(z-\hat h)^{-1}|\mathbf{r}\rangle$, at a few complex energies $z$. We call the scheme local-resolvent density-functional theory (LRes-DFT). The density and the band energy are linear in $g$, so neither orbitals nor a kinetic-energy functional are needed. The complex energies are $M$ equally spaced points on a circle that encloses the occupied levels, offset by half a spacing so that no point lies on the real axis. Summing the resolvent over them is equivalent to occupying each level with $1/(1+u^M)$, where $u$ is the level's energy measured from the circle's center in units of its radius. This is a smooth step that is near 1 inside the circle and near 0 outside, and it stays between 0 and 1 for every $M$. If the circle moves with the chemical potential, forces at fixed electron number are exact. Gauss-Legendre and Zolotarev rules need fewer points when the gap is small. In three dimensions we use eigenpairs of small windows around each molecule instead of resolvents. This is divide-and-conquer (DC) with a different occupation function, and it is as accurate as DC with Fermi-Dirac occupations. For H$_2$ molecular crystals of up to 1024 electrons it agrees with Kohn-Sham to $2.5\times10^{-7}$ Ha per electron. On one core it is slower than dense diagonalization below about 500 electrons and 3.5 times faster at 1024 electrons. It loses accuracy for disordered or compressed systems and for diffuse basis functions.

</details>


### [197] [High-throughput screening of Heusler alloys for thermoelectrics combining machine-learned densities of states and first-principles transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18179)
*Shivam Randive,Ayana Ghosh,Rahul Dixit,Himanshu Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在DXMag的106,235个Heusler结构中，用DFT或神经网络预测的DOS筛选热电候选，经3,625次能带与输运计算验证；DOS-Seebeck指标能高效排序，但预测DOS会丢失带隙，最终仍需能带结构确认带隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热电材料可将废热转化为电能，但全数据库输运计算成本过高；因此搜索通常依据电子态密度（DOS）选择后续计算对象，且越来越多使用机器学习预测DOS，但这种筛选策略此前很少被系统验证。

Method: 对DXMag数据库中全部106,235个Heusler结构进行测试：分别基于DFT谱筛选51,257个、基于神经网络预测谱筛选54,978个，并以3,625个能带结构与输运计算作为判据；采用DOS导出的Seebeck指标排序，并检验稳定性、输运性能及已知half-Heusler热电材料。

Result: DOS导出的Seebeck指标将大多数通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排在其总体前1%；11个已确立的half-Heusler热电材料中有10个通过计算筛选。神经网络预测谱丢失了大多数带隙，且谱相似性指标未察觉，但通过筛选的化合物仍排名靠前。最终筛出80个计算候选，其中61个尚无实验测量报道。

Conclusion: 无论由计算还是预测得到，DOS都可用于决定“计算什么”；但要判断带隙是否存在，只能依赖能带结构。

Abstract: Thermoelectrics turn waste heat into electricity, but finding new ones requires transport calculations too costly for whole databases. Searches therefore choose what to calculate from the electronic density of states (DOS), increasingly predicted by machine learning, yet such choices are rarely tested. We test them on all 106,235 Heusler structures in the DXMag database, screening 51,257 on density-functional spectra and 54,978 on neural-network predictions, and judging both by 3,625 band-structure and transport calculations. A DOS-derived Seebeck indicator ranks most compounds passing every transport and stability test within the top 1% of their population, and ten of the eleven established half-Heusler thermoelectrics calculated pass. Predicted spectra lose most band gaps, unnoticed by spectral-similarity metrics, yet still rank passing compounds near the top. The screen certifies 80 computational candidates, 61 without reported measurements. Computed or predicted, the DOS can choose what to calculate; only a band structure shows whether a gap exists.

</details>


### [198] [Orientation-Resolved Nanoindentation of Single Crystal Ti-15Mo: Inverted Elastic Anisotropy and Yield-Onset Behavior Beyond Simple CRSS Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18181)
*Kidus Abebe,David Gaetano,Aurelia Moriyama-Gurish,Jana \v{S}milauerov\'a,Yanfei Gao,Udo D. Schwarz,Amit Datye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合EBSD与纳米压痕量化了单晶Ti-15Mo的纳米力学各向异性，发现近<111>取向模量最低、屈服起始最软，且屈服行为偏离简单Schmid因子模型，并归因于残余无热ω相的可逆相变/滞弹性柔量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ti-15Mo作为β稳定合金，其力学响应受相稳定性和单晶尺度变形机制控制；这些晶内机制具有各向异性，因此理解弹性与塑性响应的取向依赖性对织构化锻造部件的加工至关重要。

Method: 采用EBSD与纳米压痕联用，将硬度、压痕模量、应变速率敏感性和压痕屈服响应与近<111>、<124>、<011>、<023>、<013>等晶面法向取向关联；并进行应变速率跳跃实验获取激活体积V*，同时用CRSS/Schmid因子模型进行对比评估。

Result: 模量各向异性较小（约10%），但与A>1立方晶体的预测相反，近<111>取向模量最低；屈服起始也在近<111>最软，跨度达41%，而硬度跨度仅6.6%，且不遵循模量或屈服的排序。屈服行为无法用所评估的简单CRSS/Schmid因子模型解释。应变速率跳跃得到V*=18–31b^3，符合扭折对形核控制的塑性；按V*取向分为两组，提示滑移主导与孪生/相变参与两种响应。

Conclusion: 研究揭示Ti-15Mo单晶中均匀立方β相预期弹性各向异性的反转，以及简单Schmid因子模型无法再现的取向依赖屈服起始；提出残余无热ω相是贡献者，因为β→ω塌缩发生在{111}_β面，并可通过可逆ω→β转变提供滞弹性柔量。

Abstract: Ti-15Mo (wt%) is a well-established $\beta$-stabilized alloy whose mechanical response is strongly governed by phase stability and deformation mechanisms that operate at the single-grain scale. Because these intragranular mechanisms are intrinsically anisotropic, understanding the orientation dependence of the elastic and plastic response is critical to the texture-engineered processing of wrought components. Here, we quantify the nanomechanical anisotropy of single-crystal Ti-15Mo by combining EBSD with nanoindentation to correlate hardness, indentation modulus, strain rate sensitivity, and indentation yield response with the crystallographic surface normal near $\langle111\rangle,\langle124\rangle,\langle011\rangle,\langle023\rangle$, and $\langle013\rangle$ directions. While the modulus anisotropy is small (~10%), contrary to the prediction for a cubic crystal with A>1, orientations near $\langle111\rangle$ exhibit the lowest moduli. Yield onset is likewise softest near $\langle111\rangle$ and spans 41%, while hardness spans only 6.6% and follows neither the modulus nor the yield ordering. The alloy also exhibits yield behavior not captured by the simple CRSS/Schmid-factor models evaluated here. Strain-rate-jump tests yield V*=18-31b$^3$, consistent with kink-pair-nucleation-controlled plasticity; based on V*, the orientations separate into two groups suggestive of glide-dominated versus twin/transformation-participating response. We reveal an inversion of the elastic anisotropy expected for a homogeneous cubic $\beta$-phase and an orientation-dependent yield-onset response that is not reproduced by the simple Schmid-factor models examined here. Retained athermal $\omega$ is proposed as the contributor because $\beta\rightarrow\omega$ collapse occurs on {111}$_\beta$ planes and may provide anelastic compliance through the reversible $\omega\rightarrow\beta$ transformation.

</details>


### [199] [MINFF: A Family of Classical Force Fields for Minerals with Tunable Angle Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18220)
*Michael Holmboe,Nathan Lavauzelle,Rogers E. Swai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 MINFF 力场家族，用于 Si、Al、Fe、Mg、Ca、Ti、Li、F 的层状与非层状氧化物/氢氧化物；其无 M-O 键项，通过静电、色散和统一 M-O 三元组角项控制配位，兼顾精度与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物原子模拟受力场精度和可迁移性限制，且需兼容水模型与离子对势，并覆盖多种金属及 basal、apical、hydroxyl、edge 和同晶替代邻位氧。

Method: MINFF 不设 M-O 键项，M-O 距离由静电和色散决定，所有 M-O 三元组用单一谐波角项控制配位几何；金属电荷取参考矿物 DDEC6 电荷，氧电荷按最近邻电荷涂抹分配；氧 Lennard-Jones 参数基于 OPC3，金属 LJ 参数拟合晶胞指标、键长和键角。分全局 GMINFF 与按矿物定制 TMINFF，并在 0、250、500、1500 kJ/mol/rad^2 四个角力常数下拟合 >30 种参考矿物；与 OPC3 及相关离子对势的兼容性经测量而非假定。拓扑可由 atom（MATLAB）、atomipy（Python）及 www.atomipy.io 生成。

Result: 含角项时，MINFF 将实验晶胞指标误差控制在 0.5% 内，原子类型距离和角度误差分别约 0.7-1.4% 和 0.1-0.4%，Global Instability Index 低至 0.11，优于 CLAYFF 的 0.34 和 INTERFACE 的 0.21；适中角常数可再现层状硅酸盐硬面内/软剪切弹性并改善高岭石 librational 带；水化 Na-蒙脱石中，MINFF 对 2W 00l 图谱的残差因子在测试模型中最低。

Conclusion: MINFF 提供通用与定制两套参数及四种角刚度，可直接影响可迁移性-保真度权衡，是一个可调、可迁移、与 OPC3 兼容的矿物力场框架，适用于层状和非层状氧化物/氢氧化物模拟，并配套拓扑生成工具。

Abstract: Atomistic simulations of minerals are limited by the accuracy and transferability of the force field. We introduce MINFF, a force field family for layered and non-layered oxides and hydroxides of M being Si, Al, Fe, Mg, Ca, Ti, Li and F. MINFF has no M-O bond terms. Instead M-O distances follow from electrostatics and dispersion, while a single harmonic angle term on all M-O triplets controls coordination geometry. Metal charges are DDEC6 charges from reference mineral structures, and oxygen charges are assigned by nearest-neighbour charge smearing, covering basal, apical, hydroxyl and edge sites and sites adjacent to isomorphous substitutions. The oxygen Lennard-Jones parameters are based on the OPC3 water model and change by less than 5% when re-optimised per mineral, so compatibility with OPC3 and related ion-pair potentials is measured rather than assumed. Metal Lennard-Jones parameters were fitted to unit-cell metrics, bond distances and angles, both synchronously across more than 30 reference minerals (general, GMINFF) and for individual minerals (tailored, TMINFF), each at four angle force constants (0, 250, 500 and 1500 kJ/mol/rad^2). With angle terms, MINFF reproduces experimental unit-cells metrics to within 0.5% and atom-type distances and angles to within 0.7-1.4% and 0.1-0.4%, with Global Instability Index values as low as 0.11, below those of CLAYFF (0.34) and INTERFACE (0.21). A moderate angle constant reproduces the stiff in-plane and soft shear elastic constants of phyllosilicates and improves the Kaolinite librational bands, and in hydrated Na-Montmorillonite MINFF gives the lowest residual factor against the 2W 00l pattern of the models tested. The general and tailored sets at four angle stiffnesses give direct control over the transferability-fidelity trade-off. Topologies can be generated with the atom (MATLAB) and atomipy (Python) libraries and at www.atomipy.io.

</details>


### [200] [Coherent Phonon Generation from Prethermal Carrier Populations in a Semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18279)
*Mingyu Shi,Matteo Savoini,Patrick Friebel,Andrea Annunziata,Thomas Metzger,Yunpei Deng,Laura Cattaneo,Steven L. Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在碲中测量带隙附近飞秒激光驱动相干声子的光子能量依赖，发现完全对称A1模振幅随波长变化很弱；结果支持由跃迁选择性的预热化力控制，而非热化载流子密度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，半导体中全对称相干光学声子的产生常归因于热化瞬态载流子布居；本文检验这一理解，并探究带隙附近激发时控制相干声子产生的关键因素。

Method: 对碲进行系统测量和第一性原理计算，扫描带隙附近的激发波长；将测量的光谱依赖与考虑光学矩阵元、能带和动量依赖A1形变势的跃迁分辨预热化力模型进行拟合。

Result: 与热化载流子布居模型预期相反，完全对称A1模的相干振幅在跨带隙调谐时仅微弱变化；跃迁分辨预热化力模型可重现实验光谱依赖，拟合得到激发跃迁态寿命为几飞秒。

Conclusion: 在带隙附近激发半导体时，跃迁选择性而非热化总载流子密度是控制相干声子产生的关键因素。

Abstract: Ultrafast laser excitation of carriers in semiconductors often results in the creation of fully symmetric coherent optical phonons, typically understood in terms of a thermalized transient carrier population. We test this understanding with systematic measurements and first-principles calculations of the photon-energy dependence of ultrafast-laser-driven coherent phonon generation in tellurium, using excitation wavelengths around the electronic band gap. We observe that, contrary to the expectations of models assuming a thermalized carrier population, the coherent amplitude of the fully symmetric $A_1$ mode changes only weakly when tuning wavelength across the band gap. The measured spectral dependence is instead reproduced by a transition-resolved prethermal force weighted by optical matrix elements and band- and momentum-dependent $A_1$ deformation potentials. Fitting this model to the data yields an excited transition state lifetime of a few femtoseconds. These results identify transition selectivity, rather than a thermalized total carrier density, as a key factor controlling coherent-phonon generation in semiconductors excited near a band gap.

</details>


### [201] [Transition-Metal-Induced Uniaxial Anisotropy in YFe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18303)
*M. Nur Hasan,Bruno L. Fertig,Santa Pile,Rafael M. Vieira,Alena Vishina,Alexander Kovacs,Gotthard Rieger,Stefan Denneler,Samuel J. R. Holt,Martin Lang,Swapneel Pathak,Andrea Petrocchi,Hans Fangohr,Thomas George Woodcock,Thomas Schrefl,Heike C. Herper*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理筛选和实验验证，选择性过渡金属（V、Nb、Ta）替代可将YFe3的易面磁晶各向异性调控为单轴易轴，同时保持高居里温度和热稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-rich永磁体需兼顾单轴磁晶各向异性、高磁化强度与热稳定性；YFe3磁化强度和居里温度有优势，但本征易面各向异性限制其永磁应用。

Method: 系统第一性原理筛选过渡金属掺杂，结合交换相互作用计算、原子尺度Monte Carlo和微磁模拟；并对V合金化YFe3进行实验合成、热处理、EBSD和Kerr显微镜表征，以及浓度依赖MAE计算。

Result: 筛选出V、Nb、Ta有效；YFe2.33Ta0.67单轴磁晶各向异性能达2.3 MJ/m3，并保持铁磁序和热稳定性，微磁模拟中矫顽力最高、抗不可逆退磁最强；YFe2.33V0.67在300 K具有相当的最大能积。实验上低V（1-3 at.%）可保留YFe3相，高V导致多相；MAE约在11-14 at.% V发生符号反转，低V区EBSD/Kerr显示面内磁畴。

Conclusion: 选择性过渡金属替代可有效调控YFe3磁晶各向异性，实现易面到易轴转变并保持高居里温度，为Fe-rich永磁材料设计提供可行途径。

Abstract: Achieving uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy while preserving high magnetization and thermal stability remains a central challenge in the development of Fe-rich permanent magnets. YFe$_3$ is a promising candidate owing to its saturation magnetization and Curie temperature, but its intrinsic easy-plane anisotropy limits its potential for permanent-magnet applications. Here, we show that selective transition-metal substitution drives an easy-plane-to-easy-axis transition in YFe$_3$. Systematic first-principles screening identifies V, Nb, and Ta as effective dopants, with YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibiting a large uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy energy of 2.3~MJ/m$^{3}$. Calculated exchange interactions and atomistic Monte Carlo simulations show that the enhanced anisotropy is achieved while preserving robust ferromagnetic order and high thermal stability. Micromagnetic simulations further reveal that YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibits the highest coercivity and strongest resistance to irreversible demagnetization, whereas YFe$_{2.33}$V$_{0.67}$ achieves a comparable maximum energy product at 300~K. Experimental characterization of V-alloyed YFe$_3$ shows that the YFe$_3$ phase can be retained at low nominal V concentrations (1-3 at.\%) under the employed synthesis and heat-treatment conditions, whereas higher V contents lead to increasingly multiphase samples. Concentration-dependent MAE calculations reveal a sign reversal between approximately 11 and 14~at.\% V, marking the transition from easy-plane to uniaxial easy-axis anisotropy. Consistent with this concentration dependence, electron backscatter diffraction and Kerr microscopy reveal in-plane magnetic domain structures in the experimentally accessible low-V regime. Therefore, our work offers a promising approach to tuning the magnetocrystalline anisotropy of YFe$_3$ while retaining high Curie temperatures.

</details>


### [202] [Accurately Generalizing Deep-Learning Hamiltonian Methods to Plane-Wave Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18317)
*Chuntian Wu,Jiaxiang Li,Qiang Xu,Xuecheng Shao,Congwei Xie,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种可投影性引导方法，从少量选定平面波本征态和本征值构建非正交原子轨道哈密顿量，使混合泛函平面波DFT计算可用于紧凑AO哈密顿量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将局域原子轨道哈密顿量学习扩展到平面波混合泛函DFT时，需要紧凑的AO表示来保留非局域算符效应；传统方法依赖经验位移，会给学习目标引入任意性。

Method: 选取高可投影性PW本征态，并用选定高能态补全AO子空间，构建非正交AO哈密顿量；利用PW本征态和本征值隐式包含非局域贡献，避免显式重建其AO矩阵元；随后用等变神经网络学习所得PBE和HSE哈密顿量。

Result: 在MoS2和钠相基准中，相比实空间重构，以更小AO基准确再现PW能带；所得PBE和HSE哈密顿量可由等变神经网络学习。

Conclusion: 该方法连接了PW参考计算与电子结构预测，拓宽了可用于AO哈密顿量学习的PW DFT范围，并为开发更广泛适用的电子结构模型提供了紧凑、准确的训练目标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonians from material structures offer a route to efficient electronic-structure calculations. However, extending localized atomic-orbital (AO) Hamiltonian learning to plane-wave (PW) calculations with hybrid functionals requires a compact AO representation that preserves the effects of nonlocal operators. Here we propose a projectability-guided construction of nonorthogonal AO Hamiltonians from a small, selected set of PW eigenstates and eigenvalues. High-projectability states are retained, and selected higher-energy states complete the AO subspace, avoiding an empirical shift and its arbitrary contribution to the learning targets. Because the construction uses PW eigenstates and eigenvalues, it incorporates nonlocal contributions present in the reference calculation without explicitly reconstructing their AO matrix elements. Benchmarks for MoS2 and sodium phases show accurate reproduction of PW bands with smaller AO bases than real-space reconstruction. The resulting PBE and HSE Hamiltonians can be learned by equivariant neural networks, connecting PW reference calculations with electronic-structure prediction. This approach broadens the range of PW DFT calculations available for AO Hamiltonian learning and enables compact, accurate training targets for developing more widely applicable electronic-structure models.

</details>


### [203] [Reversible Magneto-ionic Modification of Metallic Magnetic Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18396)
*Md Golam Hafiz,Hari Babu Vasili,Philippa Shepley,Mannan Ali,Andrew J. Britton,Oscar Cespedes,Weibin Li,Manuel Valvidares,Rohit Pachat,Wilfried Fotso,Mourad Cherif,Shimpei Ono,Yves Roussigne,Mohamed Belmeguenai,Gavin Burnell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用离子液体门控在金属磁性多层膜中电驱动氧迁移，可逆且非易失地调控HM/FM界面氧化态，并显著改变PMA、DMI、磁畴成核与畴壁钉扎等磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有结构反演不对称、垂直磁各向异性和大DMI的磁性多层膜广泛用于基于磁畴壁和skyrmion的数据处理，因此需要对其界面与磁性进行可控、局部、可逆的调控。

Method: 采用离子液体门控，通过电场驱动氧离子穿过全金属薄膜结构，在重金属/铁磁体界面发生氧化还原迁移；施加正或负栅压以抽出或驱入氧离子。

Result: 获得HM/FM界面氧化态改变的直接证据；氧化使有效各向异性常数Keff和矫顽场Hc增加，氧离子还原使其降低。正栅压从磁性层抽出氧离子，显著降低磁畴成核场；负栅压将氧离子驱入磁性层，使近饱和区畴壁钉扎程度可逆增加。负电压还降低磁矩和DMI。

Conclusion: 这种全金属结构中的磁离子调控有利于自旋电子器件应用，尤其适用于可现场编程的磁畴壁和skyrmion器件。

Abstract: Magnetic multilayers with structural inversion asymmetry, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA), and large Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) are widely studied for magnetic domain wall- and skyrmion-based data processing applications. In this work, we report a controlled, nonvolatile, but reversible local modification of such a system by electrically driven oxygen migration through the metallic film structure by using ionic liquid gating. Our findings provide direct evidence for the modification of the oxidation state at the heavy metal (HM)/ferromagnet (FM) interface. As a result, we observed changes in fundamental magnetic properties, such as an increase in the effective anisotropy constant (Keff) and coercive field (Hc) with oxidation, and a decrease with the reduction of oxygen ions. Positive gate voltages relative to the thin film extract oxygen ions from the magnetic layer, significantly reducing the domain nucleation field. In contrast, negative voltages drive oxygen ions into the magnetic layer, leading to a reversible increase in the extent of domain wall pinning near saturation. We observe a corresponding decrease in the magnetic moment and DMI, with negative voltage reducing both. This magneto-ionic modulation in fully metallic structures is beneficial for spintronic device applications, particularly in field-programmable domain wall and skyrmion devices.

</details>


### [204] [Substrate-controlled epitaxial-like growth and photochromic properties of yttrium oxyhydride thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18448)
*M. Zubkins,E. Strods,E. Letko,H. M\"andar,D. Moldarev,K. Mizohata,K. Sarakinos,V. Vibornijs,K. Kundzins,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过反应磁控溅射β-YHx前驱体并空气后氧化制备YHO薄膜，研究衬底取向、沉积温度与成分对结构及光致变色性能的影响；光致变色主要受成分和局部O/H构型控制，外延织构未带来系统性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究YHO薄膜中衬底取向、结晶织构、氧含量与光致变色性能之间的关系，并厘清类外延生长或长程阳离子有序是否能提升光学响应，为光学与光电子应用提供依据。

Method: 在YSZ(111)/(100)单晶及非晶SiO2、钠钙玻璃衬底上，以200°C或室温反应磁控溅射β-YHx前驱体，随后空气后氧化；采用XRD、SEM、ERDA及光学测试系统表征结构、成分和光致变色行为。

Result: 衬底取向显著影响微结构与结晶织构，获得马赛克展宽约3-4°的类外延生长；YSZ(100)上晶粒为10-20 nm，大于YSZ(111)的约8 nm，并存在面内残余压应变但整体与衬底基本松弛。200°C沉积薄膜氧含量较低（O/Y=0.71-0.82），室温沉积较高（O/Y=0.92-1.26）。所有薄膜在太阳模拟器光照10 min后相对透射率变化为7-22%。

Conclusion: 类外延薄膜未系统性改善光致变色响应，表明该效应主要由成分和局部O/H构型而非长程阳离子有序决定。研究揭示了织构、成分与光致变色性能的关系，有助于理解YHO在光学和光电子领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Yttrium oxyhydride (YHO) thin films are synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering of \b{eta}-YHx precursor films onto single-crystalline yttria-stabilised zirconia (YSZ) (111) and (100) substrates at 200 {\deg}C, followed by post-deposition oxidation in air. For comparison, reference films are also prepared on amorphous SiO2 and soda-lime glass substrates at both 200 {\deg}C and room temperature (RT). The structural (X-ray diffraction and scanning electron microscopy), compositional (elastic recoil detection analysis), and optical properties, including photochromic behaviour, of the films are systematically investigated. The results reveal a strong influence of substrate orientation on the film microstructure and crystallographic texture, leading to epitaxial-like growth with a mosaic spread of ~3-4{\deg}. YHO deposited on YSZ(100) exhibits larger crystallite sizes (10-20 nm) than YHO grown on YSZ(111) (~8 nm) and exhibits residual in-plane compressive strain while remaining largely relaxed with respect to the substrate. Films deposited at 200 {\deg}C exhibit lower oxygen concentrations (O/Y = 0.71-0.82) than films deposited at RT (O/Y = 0.92-1.26). All films exhibit photochromic behaviour under solar simulator illumination, with relative transmittance changes ranging from 7 to 22 % after 10 min of illumination. Despite the pronounced structural differences, the epitaxial-like films show no systematic improvement in photochromic response, indicating that the effect is governed primarily by composition and the local O/H configuration rather than by long-range cation ordering. These findings provide new insights into the relationship between crystallographic texture, composition, and photochromic properties in YHO thin films, contributing to the understanding of this material for optical and optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [205] [Flexoelectricity and Flexomagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18525)
*Alexander Edstr\"om,Massimiliano Stengel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 综述应变梯度和曲率作为反演对称破缺场，如何诱导柔性电性与柔性磁性，并梳理其几何关系、微观机制、二维材料表现和第一性原理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均一应变保持反演对称，而应变梯度为奇宇称，可解锁母晶格中原本禁戒的耦合；在纳米尺度和弯曲低维体系中梯度易得且常不可避免，曲率因而可视为写入晶格的对称破缺场。

Method: 先建立应变梯度与曲率的几何联系；以柔性电性为较成熟参照，综述其体、表面和动态贡献、定义困难、微观机制、二维材料表现及第一性原理计算；再以磁电效应为概念桥梁，将柔性磁性广义定义为磁性与应变梯度的耦合，重点讨论曲率线性磁化响应和曲率诱导Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并概述非线性、非均匀磁电、Rashba型效应及与交错磁性的联系。

Result: 指出柔性电性已形成包含微观机制和第一性原理方法的研究框架，但在体、表面和动态贡献下其定义仍复杂；柔性磁性可拓展至曲率驱动的DM相互作用等，并涵盖多种非线性、非均匀磁电、Rashba型现象及交错磁性关联。

Conclusion: 该综述为曲率和应变梯度驱动的电极化与磁性耦合提供统一视角，强调曲率作为晶格内对称破缺场的重要性，并连接柔性电性、柔性磁性及相关新兴效应。

Abstract: Strain gradients, unlike homogeneous strain, are odd under space inversion and unlock couplings that are strictly forbidden in the parent crystal. At the nanoscale, and in curved low-dimensional systems in particular, such gradients are readily attainable and often unavoidable, so that curvature acts as a symmetry-breaking field written into the lattice itself. This chapter reviews the resulting couplings to electric polarization and magnetism. After establishing the geometric relation between strain gradients and curvature, we review flexoelectricity as the better-established reference case: the bulk, surface and dynamic contributions that complicate its definition, its microscopic mechanisms, its manifestation in two-dimensional materials, and the first-principles methodology now available for its calculation. Using the magnetoelectric effect as a conceptual bridge, we then develop flexomagnetism in a broad sense, covering any coupling phenomena between magnetism and strain gradients, and specifically discuss the linear magnetization response to curvature and curvature-induced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, beyond the established curvilinear treatment. Finally, we briefly describe some additional effects fitting under this broad umbrella of flexomagnetism, including nonlinear ones, inhomogeneous magnetoelectric and Rashba-type phenomena, and connections to altermagnetism.

</details>


### [206] [Imaging Gate-Tunable Single-Layer Molecular Kagome Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18539)
*Ziyi Wang,Rafal Zuzak,Weichen Tang,Haowen Pang,Yutang Qiao,Dhanvanth Balakrishnan,Yuqiao Yan,Aining Hu,Andrew Sun,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Feng Wang,Steven G. Louie,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在范德华衬底及石墨烯基场效应器件上外延生长单层Ni3(HITP)2金属有机框架，观测到kagome平带与van Hove奇点谱特征，并实现栅压静电调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统kagome材料中的新奇电子态常嵌于化学复杂的三维晶体中，难以隔离或调控；分子基kagome网络虽具结构精确性和可调性，但多局限于与二维器件不兼容的金属衬底。

Method: 采用金属盐分子束外延在石墨烯等范德华衬底上生长单层Ni3(HITP)2 MOF；利用低温扫描隧道谱表征电子结构，并结合第一性原理计算；进一步构建混合二维MOF场效应器件进行栅压调控。

Result: 低温扫描隧道谱分辨出与kagome衍生平带和van Hove奇点相关的谱特征，且与第一性原理计算一致；栅压可使其kagome谱特征发生能量位移。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在二维器件兼容衬底上对化学可编程kagome电子晶格的静电控制，为可调kagome量子态及其器件化研究提供了新平台。

Abstract: Kagome lattices provide a simple geometry for engineering electronic states that exhibit novel quantum behavior. In conventional kagome materials, however, the relevant states are often embedded in chemically complex three-dimensional crystals and are difficult to isolate or tune. Molecule-based kagome networks offer structural precision and tunability, but have largely been confined to metallic substrates incompatible with 2D devices. Here we report growth of single-layer Ni$_3$(HITP)$_2$ metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) on van der Waals substrates including graphene-based field-effect devices using metal-salt molecular beam epitaxy. Low-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy resolves kagome-derived flat-band and van Hove singularity-based spectral features consistent with first-principles calculations. Gating hybrid 2D MOF devices allows us to energetically shift kagome spectral features, thus establishing electrostatic control of chemically programmable 2D kagome electronic lattices.

</details>


### [207] [A Transferable Machine-Learned Potential Enabling Large-Scale Copper-Carbon Simulations from Equilibrium to Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18545)
*Feiyu Zhou,Wei Qiu,Yan Chen,Jiahui Liu,Yilun Liu,Shengying Yue,Penghua Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出可迁移的神经演化势 NEP-CuC，用于统一描述金属 Cu、多种碳成键、异质 Cu-C 相互作用及近距原子碰撞，并验证其在 Cu-C 体系中的物理保真度与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu-C 材料与界面在金刚石热管理、金属化、石墨烯生长和碳涂层等技术中至关重要，但相关原子尺度模拟需要同时准确处理金属 Cu、多样碳结构、异质 Cu-C 相互作用和短程强排斥。

Method: 从异质 Cu-C 候选池中选取 2,727 个均匀重标注构型训练 NEP-CuC；用 5 个独立模型集成的不一致度作为敏感性指标；引入 ZBL 修正描述短程核排斥，并在运行时加入 D3(BJ) 色散修正；通过源数据留出集、独立外部数据集以及结构、弹性、缺陷、界面、振动和有限温度输运基准测试，并开展非晶碳形成、取向相关冲击压缩和 Cu/金刚石界面沉积-退火-拉伸模拟。

Result: 基准测试表明 NEP-CuC 在元素 Cu、多种碳环境和异质 Cu-C 构型中具有广泛但依赖性质的物理保真度，并能在强非平衡条件下保持可迁移性。对 Cu/金刚石界面，制备历史会留下持久界面相结构差异；初始沉积阶段入射 Cu 能量从 1 eV 增至 50 eV 时，500 K 下平均峰值名义拉伸应力由 12.35±0.71 GPa 降至 6.65±0.98 GPa。

Conclusion: NEP-CuC 提供了连接加工过程、界面结构与物理响应的原子尺度框架，适用于 Cu-C 体系，并直接服务于 Cu/金刚石热管理界面、金刚石金属化和非平衡 Cu-C 加工等应用。

Abstract: Cu-C materials and interfaces are central to diamond thermal management, metallization, graphene growth and carbon-coating technologies, but atomistic simulations across these applications require a potential that describes metallic Cu, diverse carbon bonding, heterogeneous Cu-C interactions and close atomic encounters. Here we develop NEP-CuC, a transferable neuroevolution potential trained on a dataset of 2,727 uniformly relabelled configurations selected from a heterogeneous Cu-C candidate pool. Disagreement within an ensemble of five independently trained models provides an internal indicator of model sensitivity. A Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) correction describes short-range nuclear repulsion, while a runtime D3(BJ) correction supplements dispersion-sensitive calculations. Tests on source-derived held-out and independent external datasets, together with structural, elastic, defect, interfacial, vibrational and finite-temperature transport benchmarks, establish broad but property-dependent physical fidelity across elemental Cu, diverse carbon environments and heterogeneous Cu-C configurations. Large-scale simulations of amorphous-carbon formation and orientation-dependent shock compression of Cu further test transferability under strongly non-equilibrium conditions. For Cu/diamond interfaces, the same potential describes deposition, annealing and tensile response: preparation history leaves persistent differences in interphase structure, while the mean peak nominal tensile stress at 500 K decreases from $12.35\pm0.71$ to $6.65\pm0.98$ GPa as the incident Cu energy during the initial deposition stage increases from 1 to 50 eV. NEP-CuC provides an atomistic framework for linking processing, interfacial structure and physical response across Cu-C systems, with direct relevance to Cu/diamond thermal-management interfaces, diamond metallization and non-equilibrium Cu-C processing.

</details>


### [208] [Direct growth of crystalline spinel GaON films: A wide-bandgap mixed-anion semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18550)
*Baikui Li,Zecheng Yang,Wenhao Yin,Zijing Wu,Haitao Zhao,Yunwei Sheng,Honglei Wu,Jiannong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积直接生长出结晶尖晶石相γ-GaON薄膜，并确认其室温带边跃迁约在4.3 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合阴离子半导体可实现超越传统多阳离子合金的带隙工程，但竞争相与无序性使其合成及电子性质识别困难；GaON有望连接GaN与Ga2O3体系，但其晶体结构、组分与带隙关系尚不清晰。

Method: 采用氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积生长GaON薄膜，通过调控氮氧供给获得不同相；结合衍射、原子分辨率显微术和元素分析确认尖晶石结构与氮 incorporation；利用变温光电流谱研究带边响应。

Result: 调控氮氧供给可得到单斜β-Ga2O3、以无序为主的薄膜以及结晶尖晶石GaON；衍射、显微和元素分析确立了尖晶石结构及氮 incorporation；变温光电流谱显示结晶γ-GaON响应明显红移，支持室温下约4.3 eV的带边跃迁。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究混合阴离子宽禁带半导体物理及开发相关异质结构提供了机会。

Abstract: Mixed-anion semiconductors offer opportunities for bandgap engineering beyond conventional multication alloys, but competing phases and disorder complicate their synthesis and the identification of their electronic properties. Gallium oxynitride (GaON) could connect the technologically important GaN and Ga2O3 systems, yet establishing a clear relation between its crystal structure, composition and bandgap remains challenging. Here we report the direct growth of crystalline spinel gamma-GaON films by nitrogen-plasma-assisted pulsed-laser deposition. Varying the nitrogen and oxygen supply yields monoclinic beta-Ga2O3, predominantly disordered films and crystalline spinel GaON. Complementary diffraction, atomic-resolution microscopy and elemental analysis establish the spinel structure and nitrogen incorporation. Temperature-dependent photocurrent spectroscopy reveals a clear redshift of the crystalline gamma-GaON response, supporting assignment of a band-edge transition near 4.3 eV at room temperature. These results open opportunities to investigate mixed-anion wide-bandgap semiconductor physics and develop heterostructures.

</details>


### [209] [Time-Resolved Phonon Dispersions of Thermally Excited Graphite at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18561)
*Mairi McCauley (Department of Physics,Center for the Science of Materials Berlin,Humboldt-Universit\"at zu Berlin,Berlin,Germany),Christoph T. Koch (Department of Physics,Center for the Science of Materials Berlin,Humboldt-Universit\"at zu Berlin,Berlin,Germany),Benedikt Haas (Department of Physics,Center for the Science of Materials Berlin,Humboldt-Universit\"at zu Berlin,Berlin,Germany)*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将动量分辨振动电子能量损失谱（EELS）与脉冲激光加热和同步门控电子探测结合，首次在微秒时间尺度上实现石墨声子色散的时间分辨测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子决定材料热传播，但此前缺乏在纳米尺度直接分辨声子色散的时间分辨测量手段；因此需要发展兼具能量、动量与时间分辨的声子谱学方法。

Method: 利用动量分辨振动EELS获取声子能量与动量，并结合脉冲激光加热和同步门控电子探测，在相对于激光激发的特定延迟记录石墨的增益与损失谱，从而确定各延迟温度并追踪声子支随温度的变化。

Result: 随温度升高，石墨光学声子支能量降低且峰宽增加；这些变化与温度依赖的声子重整化和非谐相互作用引起的散射一致。该方法兼容低至50 ns的探测门控。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了基于振动EELS的时间分辨声子色散谱学，为在纳米尺度同时实现能量和动量分辨、探测温度依赖声子动力学提供了途径，并可进一步拓展至更快动力学过程。

Abstract: Lattice vibrations govern the propagation of heat in materials, and investigating their dynamics through time-resolved phonon dispersion measurements would provide insight into these excitations and their interactions. However, time-resolved measurements that directly resolve the phonon dispersion at the nanoscale have not been previously demonstrated. Momentum-resolved vibrational electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) provides access to phonon energies and momenta with nanoscale spatial resolution. Here, we combine momentum-resolved vibrational EELS with pulsed laser heating and synchronised gated electron detection to observe the phonon dispersion of graphite during controlled heating and cooling on the microsecond timescale. Recording both the gain and loss spectra at defined times relative to laser excitation, we determine the temperature at each time delay and follow changes to the phonon branches as a function of temperature. The momentum-resolved spectra reveal a decrease in energy of the optical phonon branch with increasing temperature accompanied by an increase in peak width. These changes are consistent with temperature-dependent phonon renormalisation and scattering associated with anharmonic interactions. The approach is compatible with detector gating on timescales down to 50 ns, providing a route towards extending these measurements to faster dynamics. This work demonstrates time-resolved phonon dispersion spectroscopy using vibrational EELS and provides a route towards probing temperature-dependent phonon dynamics at the nanoscale with simultaneous energy and momentum resolution.

</details>


### [210] [Composition-controlled optical fingerprints and anisotropy of self-trapped excitons in double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18665)
*Simone Grillo,Offir Zachs Maishlos,Liya Koren,Rotem Strassberg,Rachel Lifer,Edi Maizenberg,Umberto De Giovannini,Angel Rubio,Yehonadav Bekenstein,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以Sb掺杂无铅双钙钛矿Cs2BInCl6纳米晶为对象，通过Na→K的B位合金化结合光谱与从头算，揭示自陷激子（STE）主导发光红移，并证明偏振分辨光谱可解析STE波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双钙钛矿组成可调，适用于光催化和光电子，但软晶格导致强激子-声子耦合和STEs，产生宽红移PL；需厘清掺杂与合金化对发光和吸收的作用。

Method: 制备Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6纳米晶并调控B位Na/K合金化；结合稳态/时间分辨光学光谱与从头算计算，分析PL能量、吸收、STE畸变、带隙内态和偏振各向异性。

Result: 合金化使PL能量从约2.48 eV调至2.70 eV，而约3.8 eV吸收几乎不变；Sb掺杂提供明亮辐射复合通道并离域激子波函数。STE导致大PL红移，与掺杂剂杂化带隙内态上移相关；Na→K选择性改变STE晶格畸变。理论预测强PL偏振各向异性且依赖B位组成，时间分辨各向异性测量与预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究解耦了Sb掺杂与B位合金化在无铅双钙钛矿中的作用，表明STE波函数及其演化可通过偏振分辨光谱探测，为调控DPs发光和光电性质提供依据。

Abstract: Double perovskites (DPs) offer a compositionally versatile platform for photocatalysis and optoelectronics, but their soft lattices favor strong exciton-phonon coupling and self-trapped excitons (STEs), which produce broad, red-shifted photoluminescence (PL). Here, we combine optical spectroscopy and ab initio calculations to study lead-free, Sb-doped Cs2BInCl6 nanocrystals with B-site alloying from Na to K. Alloying tunes the PL energy from about 2.48 to 2.70 eV, while absorption features near 3.8 eV remain nearly unchanged. Calculations reveal that Sb doping enables bright radiative recombination channels and delocalizes the excitonic wavefunction. We identify STEs responsible for the large PL red shift, associated with an upward shift of dopant-hybridized in-gap states. Na-to-K substitution selectively changes the STE lattice distortion, explaining why PL varies strongly with composition while absorption does not. Theory further predicts pronounced PL polarization anisotropy that reflects the STE wavefunction's spatial structure and depends on B-site composition. Time-resolved anisotropy measurements closely agree with these predictions, providing experimental fingerprints of the excitonic wavefunction and its evolution with alloying. Together, these results disentangle the roles of doping and alloying in lead-free DPs and establish polarization-resolved spectroscopy as a probe of STE wavefunctions.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Tersoff-NN: Hybrid Machine-Learned Bond-Order Extension of the Tersoff Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15591)
*Yusuke Nishimura,Sho Kubota,Kotaro Takematsu,Kentaro Hirai,Takanobu Watanabe*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出了 Tersoff-NN：一种保留 Tersoff 势径向相互作用与截断结构、仅用紧凑神经网络学习环境依赖键序因子的硅混合机器学习势，在精度、效率和动力学稳定性间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分子动力学需要准确、鲁棒且高效的原位势；机器学习势虽能高精度复现第一性原理势能面，但计算成本通常高于经验势，且在其训练域外可靠性不足。

Method: 针对 Si 构建 Tersoff-NN，保留 Tersoff 势的解析径向相互作用和截断结构，用紧凑神经网络表示其环境依赖的键序因子；在 32768 原子体系中进行基准测试，并与 Behler-Parrinello 型神经网络势及另一种原子中心键序神经网络进行比较。

Result: Tersoff-NN 比所测试的 BP 型神经网络势快 4–12 倍；相较传统 Tersoff 势改善了液态 Si 的短程结构，并较好复现从头算径向与角向分布、金刚石 Si 平衡结构及若干晶相间的定性能量趋势；熔淬模拟得到以四配位、类四面体局域环境为主的无序固体；即使训练集未包含 Si 表面，仍保留特征重构基元。与替代原子中心键序神经网络相比，尽管验证误差相当，基于三体/三重态架构的模型保持稳定，而替代模型出现非物理键序增长和原子坍缩。

Conclusion: 仅学习键序项即可改善对不同 Si 环境的描述，同时保持显著计算效率并增强动力学稳定性；物理约束对确保机器学习势的动态稳定性至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate, robust, and efficient interatomic potentials are highly desirable for large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Although machine-learning interatomic potentials can reproduce first-principles potential-energy surfaces with high accuracy, they typically incur higher computational costs than conventional empirical potentials and may be less reliable outside their training domains. We developed Tersoff-NN, a hybrid machine-learning potential for Si, which retains the analytical radial interactions and cutoff structure of the Tersoff potential while representing its environment-dependent bond-order factor using compact neural networks (NNs). Benchmarks for 32,768-atom systems show that Tersoff-NN is 4-12 times faster than the Behler-Parrinello-type NN potentials tested in this study. Tersoff-NN improves the short-range structure of liquid Si relative to the conventional Tersoff potential, closely reproduces the ab initio radial and angular distributions, and reproduces the equilibrium structure of diamond Si and qualitative energetic trends among several crystalline phases. Furthermore, melt-quench simulations reveal a disordered solid with predominantly four-coordinated, tetrahedral-like local environments. Additionally, characteristic reconstruction motifs are retained on Si surfaces despite their exclusion from the training dataset. A direct comparison with an alternative atom-centered bond-order NN highlights the critical role of physical constraints in ensuring dynamic stability. Despite exhibiting comparable validation errors, the selected triplet-based architecture remains stable, while an alternative model exhibits unphysical bond-order growth and atomic collapse. These results confirm that learning only the bond-order term improves the description of diverse Si environments while retaining substantial computational efficiency and enhancing dynamical stability.

</details>


### [212] [All-electron machine-learned orbital-free density functional for periodic CHNO systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17975)
*Marc K. Ickler,Andreas Hermann,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 STRUCTURES-P：面向周期 CHNO 体系的机器学习全电子轨道自由密度泛函；从原子密度叠加出发做变分优化即可恢复 Kohn-Sham 基态，并显著降低密度与能量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器学习全电子轨道自由 DFT 是否可用于固定几何固体中的变分密度优化与能量评估，并统一处理孤立分子与周期体系。

Method: 静电项在原子中心高斯基组中解析计算；等变神经网络从非基态密度的能量与梯度学习动能及交换关联能。通过最小化由原子密度叠加得到的泛函恢复 Kohn-Sham 基态。单一泛函可同时训练于孤立分子和周期体系，并可泛化到更大晶胞。

Result: 在留出晶体上，密度误差从电子数的 6.7% 降至 0.4%，能量误差达每原子 0.25 mHa，为同架构原子间势的一半；模型可泛化到更大晶胞，基本保持相对能量排序，并在测试的 H-N 组成切片上复现凸包顶点。

Conclusion: 这些结果证明机器学习全电子轨道自由 DFT 在固定几何固体中进行变分密度优化和能量评估是可行的。

Abstract: We introduce STRUCTURES-P, a machine-learned all-electron orbital-free density functional for periodic CHNO systems. Electrostatics are evaluated analytically in an atom-centered Gaussian basis, while an equivariant network learns the kinetic and exchange-correlation energy from energies and gradients of non-ground-state densities. Minimizing the functional from a superposition of atomic densities recovers the Kohn-Sham ground state: on held-out crystals, the density error falls from 6.7% to 0.4% of the electron count and the energy error reaches 0.25 mHa per atom, half that of a same-architecture interatomic potential. A single functional can be trained for both isolated molecules and periodic systems, and the model generalizes to larger unit cells. It largely preserves relative-energy rankings and reproduces convex-hull vertices on the tested H-N composition slice. Together, these results demonstrate the viability of machine-learned all-electron orbital-free DFT for variational density optimization and energy evaluation in fixed-geometry solids.

</details>


### [213] [UGME: A python package for non-Markovian protein folding dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14234)
*Anthony J. Dominic III,Bertram Cham,Andr\'es Montoya-Castillo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 ugme：一个模块化、开源的 Python 包，用于构建和验证广义主方程（GME）模型，以显式处理生物分子动力学降维中的非马尔可夫记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级分子动力学（MD）难以直接模拟蛋白折叠、蛋白-蛋白结合、蛋白-DNA 识别等长时程过程；常用马尔可夫状态模型（MSM）整合大量短轨迹，但 MSM 假设投影动力学在足够长滞后时间后无记忆。将高维生物分子动力学投影到少数状态会丢失溶剂重组、侧链重排和中间构象等未解析自由度信息，这些运动导致后续动力学保留记忆，破坏马尔可夫描述。

Method: 开发 ugme 软件包，采用模块化开源 Python 实现，用于构建和验证广义主方程（GME）模型，显式纳入非马尔可夫（记忆）效应。

Result: 摘要报告了 ugme 的提出，可作为构建和验证 GME 模型的工具；未提供具体定量性能或基准测试结果。

Conclusion: ugme 为长时程生物分子过程的动力学降维建模提供了显式处理记忆效应的开源框架，有望弥补 MSM 忽略非马尔可夫性的不足。

Abstract: Biomolecular processes, including protein folding, protein-protein association, and protein-DNA recognition, often occur on timescales beyond what individual atomistic molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can reach. To access these longer-timescale dynamics, practitioners commonly combine many parallelized, short trajectories into reduced kinetic models, such as Markov state models (MSMs), that describe how the biomolecules interconvert between a handful of biomolecular configurations (e.g., folded and unfolded states of a protein). Central to MSM construction is the assumption that, at sufficiently long time lags, these projected dynamics become memoryless, such that the future evolution of the system depends only on its current state. Yet, projecting high-dimensional biomolecular dynamics onto a small set of states necessarily discards information about unresolved degrees of freedom, including solvent reorganization, side-chain rearrangements, and intermediate configurations. Because these unresolved motions can continue to influence subsequent dynamics, the resulting dynamics retain memory that is absent from a Markovian description. Here, we present ugme, a modular, open-source Python package for constructing and validating generalized master equations (GME) models that explicitly account for these non-Markovian (memory) effects.

</details>


### [214] [Learning Electron Density with Virtual Atoms for Transferable Atomistic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14521)
*Yuzhi Liu,Xinzijian Liu,Chengqian Zhang,Anyang Peng,Duo Zhang,Han Wang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将电子密度查询点建模为 DPA3 中的虚拟原子，与真实原子共享编码器，并通过非对称邻域构建和任务特定读出实现密度、能量与力的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子密度是电子结构的基本量，但现有多数机器学习密度模型仅将其作为重构目标，且与机器学习原子间势分开开发，未充分利用密度作为监督信号。

Method: 在 DPA3 框架中引入虚拟原子：将密度查询点表示为虚拟节点，由与真实原子相同的参数化编码器处理；采用非对称邻居构造，使虚拟原子可从邻近真实原子接收信息而不改变真实原子交互图；通过任务特定读出头支持密度、能量和力预测。

Result: 在 Materials Project 基准上达到最先进密度重构精度，在镍锰钴电池正极材料上匹配最佳已报道性能，在 QM9 上与专用密度模型具有竞争力；密度与能量/力联合监督可提升域内精度、增强线性探测下局部环境信息可访问性，并降低 QM40 大分子零样本力误差；仅密度检查点也可在固定微调协议下为下游能量-力学习提供有效初始化。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明电子密度可作为原子间势表示的一种电子结构监督来源。

Abstract: Electron density is a fundamental quantity of electronic structure, yet most machine learning density models treat it solely as a reconstruction target and are developed separately from machine learning interatomic potentials. Here, we introduce a virtual-atom formulation within the DPA3 framework, in which density query points are represented as virtual nodes and processed by the same parameterized encoder used for real atoms. An asymmetric neighbor construction allows virtual atoms to receive information from nearby real atoms without altering the real-atom interaction graph. The resulting framework supports density, energy, and force prediction through task-specific readout heads. The proposed model achieves state-of-the-art density reconstruction accuracy on the Materials Project benchmark, matches the best reported performance on nickel-manganese-cobalt battery cathode materials, and is competitive with dedicated density models on QM9. When density labels are used jointly with energy and force supervision, the model improves in-domain accuracy, makes local environment information more accessible under linear probing, and reduces zero-shot force errors on larger QM40 molecules. Density-only checkpoints also provide useful initializations for downstream energy-force learning under the fixed fine-tuning protocol. Together, these results support electron density as a source of electronic-structure supervision for interatomic-potential representations.

</details>


### [215] [Kernel-Level Constant-Potential Molecular Dynamics in GROMACS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15733)
*Sheng Bi,Mathieu Salanne,Jun Cheng,Guang Feng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作开源地在GROMACS中实现了恒电势方法，将电极原子电荷的自适应计算集成到CPU/GPU优化例程中，从而高效模拟电极的电化学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电极内部电荷重排会影响电池、超级电容器等电化学器件的行为。分子模拟需让每个电极原子的电荷随环境调整，但反复计算这些电荷会带来显著计算开销。

Method: 在GROMACS中实现开源恒电势方法，并与程序优化的CPU和GPU例程集成；通过已有代码验证电极电荷，并利用充电、伏安和阻抗模拟展示对电学条件变化的响应；还支持电极移动或形变、有限金属屏蔽和电子溢出模型。

Result: 在与已建立代码对比中验证了电极电荷。对一对带电碳纳米管，允许电荷远离相对表面可在中等电荷下将接触排斥力降低约一半，并在均匀电荷模型于模拟时间内分离的情况下仍保持结合。

Conclusion: 该实现使电极电学响应可在GROMACS中研究，适用于界面结构、充电动力学和电极运动等研究。

Abstract: How charge rearranges within an electrode shapes the behavior of batteries, supercapacitors, and other electrochemical devices. Molecular simulations can capture this response by allowing the charge on each electrode atom to adjust to its surroundings, but repeatedly calculating these charges adds substantial computational cost. We present an open-source implementation of the constant-potential method in GROMACS that integrates these calculations into the program's optimized CPU and GPU routines. Comparisons with established codes validate the electrode charges, while charging, voltammetry, and impedance simulations demonstrate the response to changing electrical conditions. The implementation also supports electrodes that move or deform and models of finite metallic screening and electron spillover. In a pair of charged carbon nanotubes, allowing charge to move away from the facing surfaces reduces the repulsive force at contact by about half at an intermediate charge and keeps the pair bound where a uniform-charge model opens within the simulated time. These capabilities make the electrical response of electrodes accessible within GROMACS for studies of interfacial structure, charging dynamics, and electrode motion.

</details>


### [216] [The RAS-CCSD model, with applications for open-shell molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15848)
*Sarai Dery Folkestad*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出并初步实现了限制性活性空间耦合簇单双激发（RAS-CCSD）模型，用于开壳层与多参考体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开壳层和多参考体系提供一种简单、实用的耦合簇理论，并解决非限制性CCSD难以保持自旋适配、无法方便地处理多行列式参考态的问题。

Method: 详细构建RAS-CCSD模型并给出试点实现；在限制性活性空间框架下进行耦合簇单双激发计算。

Result: 基态能量相对FCI的误差与非限制（自旋轨道）CCSD相近；激发态误差通常更大，需调整方法以平衡基态和激发态表现。

Conclusion: RAS-CCSD具有自旋适配优势，不同于非限制CCSD，可用于向多行列式参考态加入动力学相关；但激发态精度仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: The restricted active space coupled cluster singles and doubles (RAS-CCSD) model, and its pilot implementation is detailed. The approach offers a simple, pragmatic way to formulate a coupled cluster theory for open-shell and multireference systems. For ground state energies, errors (compared to FCI) are shown to be similar to those of unrestricted (spin-orbital) CCSD. For excited states, the errors of RAS-CCSD are generally larger, and adaptations to the methodology are needed to achieve an approach which is balanced for both ground and excited states. However, unlike unrestricted CCSD, the approach is spin-adapted and can be used to add dynamical correlation to a multideterminantal reference state.

</details>


### [217] [Single-electron Fluorine Bonding. I. The SEFB-15 and CSFB-14 Benchmark Datasets, Reference Binding Energies and Intermolecular Bond Distances: Assessment of M06-2X density functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16629)
*Govinda Prasad Khanal,Elangannan Arunan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 论文构建了开壳单电子氟键（SEFB-15，15个DF···CH3复合物）和闭壳氟键（CSFB-14，14个DF···NH3复合物）数据集，并给出高精度从头算参考；评估发现M06-2X对闭壳氟键可靠，但对开壳单电子氟键系统性过结合且不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共价结合氟作为卤键供体的F-bonding本身罕见，而以自由基为电子供体的开壳变体此前未被探索，因此需要建立基准数据并评估常用DFT方法。

Method: 构建SEFB-15和CSFB-14数据集，DF包括F2、NCF、CNF、NH3F+和NH2F2+。以DF-RMP2-F12/CBS//DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12计算参考结合能，并与CCSD(T)、CCSD(T)-F12b、W1-F12、W2-F12验证；F键距离以DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12为参考，并与CCSD(T)-F12b、CCSD(T)、CCSD、DCSD比较。重点评估M06-2X/AVTZ，考察基组与BSSE校正影响。

Result: 参考结合能相对CCSD(T)-F12b/CBS//CCSD(T)-F12b/VDZ-F12的MA%D为4.5%（SEFB）和3.4%（CSFB）；参考F键距离的MA%D为0.9%（SEFB）和0.4%（CSFB）。M06-2X/AVTZ系统性过结合全部SEFB-15复合物并低估其F键距离，结合能MA%D为75.8%，距离MA%D为8.9%；它预测F2···CH3与F2···NH3结合能相当，与所有从头算结果矛盾，且在16个基组中持续存在。更换基组或BSSE校正均不能解决过结合，BSSE校正反而使DF···NH3结合能更差。对CSFB-14，M06-2X/AVTZ的结合能MA%D为16.0%，距离MA%D为3.3%。

Conclusion: M06-2X对闭壳氟键（CSFB）可靠，但对开壳单电子氟键（SEFB）不可靠，且与基组选择无关；伴随论文II将报告其他Minnesota泛函和从头算方法在这些数据集上的表现。

Abstract: Halogen bonding in which a covalently bound fluorine acts as halogen-bond donor (F-bonding) is rare, and its open-shell variant with radical electron donors remains unexplored. We introduce SEFB-15 dataset (15 open-shell DF$\cdots$CH$_3$ complexes) exemplifying single-electron F-bonding (SEFB) and CSFB-14 dataset (14 closed-shell DF$\cdots$NH$_3$ complexes) representing closed-shell F-bonding (CSFB). Reference binding energies (BEs) at DF-RMP2-F12/CBS//DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12 are validated against CCSD(T), CCSD(T)-F12b, W1-F12 and W2-F12 for selected systems (DF = F$_2$, NCF, CNF, NH$_3$F$^+$ and NH$_2$F$_2^+$), with MA%D of 4.5% (DF$\cdots$CH$_3$) and 3.4% (DF$\cdots$NH$_3$) against CCSD(T)-F12b/CBS//CCSD(T)-F12b/VDZ-F12. BEs from W1-F12 and W2-F12 are identical. Reference F-bond distances at DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12 are validated against CCSD(T)-F12b, CCSD(T), CCSD, and DCSD for the same systems, with MA%D of 0.9% (SEFB) and 0.4% (CSFB) against CCSD(T)-F12b/VDZ-F12. M06-2X is recommended for closed-shell halogen bonding with heavier halogens but has never been assessed for open-shell F-bonding. M06-2X/AVTZ systematically overbinds all SEFB-15 complexes and underestimates their F-bond distances, with MA%Ds of 75.8% on BEs and 8.9% on F-bond distances. M06-2X predicts comparable BEs for F$_2\cdots$CH$_3$ and F$_2\cdots$NH$_3$, contrary to all ab initio results, an anomaly persisting across all 16 basis sets. Neither basis set nor BSSE correction resolves the overbinding. BSSE correction worsens BEs for DF$\cdots$NH$_3$ complexes. For CSFB-14, M06-2X/AVTZ yields MA%Ds of 16.0% for BEs and 3.3% for F-bond distances. M06-2X is reliable for CSFB but unreliable for SEFB regardless of basis set. Companion paper II reports performance of other Minnesota functionals and ab initio methods for these datasets.

</details>


### [218] [Exact Gaussian--Hermite closure of the mapping quantum--classical Liouville equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16864)
*Sahand Nikzat Ali Nassimi,Rony Modak Parai*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过Hermite函数展开映射密度，证明Liouville方程中的额外耦合项与背向反作用力的升阶贡献精确抵消，使精确动力学在有限稀疏系数方程上闭合，并在物理子空间约化为子系统量子-经典Liouville方程；相比之下PBME因保留该升阶贡献而不断产生高阶Hermite项且精度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liouville方程除Poisson括号外包含额外耦合项；忽略该项得到PBME。需要厘清该额外项的动力学作用，并评估PBME相对于量子-经典描述的精度。

Method: 将映射密度对映射变量的依赖，围绕所有物理映射密度共有的高斯因子，用Hermite函数展开；在此基下分析额外耦合项与背向反作用力产生的升算符贡献，并推导系数场方程。对Tully双避免交叉模型直接传播系数方程，并与子系统方程独立解及收敛全量子动力学比较。

Result: 在该Hermite基下，额外耦合项表现为纯升算符，且与背向反作用力产生的升阶贡献精确抵消；因此精确动力学不生成更高Hermite阶，闭合为有限、稀疏的系数场方程组。物理扇区内这些方程约化为子系统量子-经典Liouville方程。相反，PBME保留力的升阶贡献，修改物理扇区内的背向反作用，并持续生成更高Hermite阶。Tully双避免交叉模型中，系数方程的直接传播证实有限闭合，并与子系统方程独立解一致；与收敛全量子动力学比较，PBME在布居、相干和动量分布上均不如量子-经典描述准确。
