Conclusion: Hermite展开揭示了额外耦合项与背向反作用力升阶贡献的精确抵消机制，给出了有限闭合的精确映射动力学；其在物理扇区约化为子系统量子-经典Liouville方程。PBME因未抵消该升阶贡献而引入对背向反作用的修改并产生高阶项，因而在所考察范围内精度低于量子-经典描述。

Abstract: Liouville equation contains an excess coupling term in addition to the Poisson bracket. Neglecting this term yields the Poisson bracket mapping equation (PBME). We expand the mapping dependence of the density in Hermite functions about the Gaussian factor common to all physical mapping densities. In this basis, the excess coupling term acts purely as a raising operator and exactly cancels the raising contribution generated by the back--reaction force. Consequently, the exact dynamics does not generate higher Hermite orders and closes on a finite, sparse system of equations for the coefficient fields. Within the physical sector, these equations reduce to the subsystem quantum--classical Liouville equation. In contrast, the PBME retains the raising contribution of the force, modifying the back reaction within the physical sector while continuously generating higher Hermite orders. For Tully's dual avoided crossing model, direct propagation of the coefficient equations confirms this finite closure and agrees with independent solutions of the subsystem equation. Comparison with converged full quantum dynamics further shows that, across all regimes examined, the PBME is less accurate than the quantum--classical description for populations, coherences, and momentum distributions.

</details>


### [219] [Solvent Fluctuations Enable Photoionization of Aqueous Nitrite Microdroplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17021)
*Brandon J. Wallace,Eric R. Heller,Alex Prophet,Pranav Ballapuram,Ashok Ajoy,David T. Limmer,Kevin R. Wilson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 可见光可在微米级单个液滴中直接电离水相亚硝酸盐，这一被忽视的途径可产生硝酸盐、羟基自由基和溶剂化电子，并可能是大气气溶胶、雪包和天然水体中氮氧化物、溶剂化电子及氧化剂的重要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚硝酸盐光化学是大气气溶胶和天然水体中羟基自由基与活性氮氧化物的重要来源；既有观点认为水相亚硝酸盐光化学仅通过光激发后均裂发生，因此需要检验是否存在其他光化学路径。

Method: 在单个微米级液滴中开展可见光照射实验，观测亚硝酸盐的光电离；结合分子模拟解释吸收光谱长波长拖尾；并通过水相反应机理的动力学模拟定量重现观测到的化学过程。

Result: 实验表明可见光可直接电离水相亚硝酸盐，尽管光离子化截面极小，但在单液滴实验的高光子通量密度下可观测；分子模拟显示其原因是水溶液中较大的溶剂重组能导致光离子化吸收具有异常长的波长拖尾；动力学模拟预测生成硝酸盐、羟基自由基和溶剂化电子，且其宽光离子化连续谱与可见太阳光谱重叠。

Conclusion: 可见光光离子化可能是大气气溶胶、雪包和天然水体中被忽视的氮氧化物、溶剂化电子和氧化剂来源；在稀释条件下，溶解氧是溶剂化电子的主要汇，此时光离子化预计成为亚硝酸盐的主导光化学损失途径。

Abstract: Nitrite photochemistry in atmospheric aerosols and natural waters is a major source of hydroxyl radicals and reactive nitrogen oxides. Contrary to the prevailing view that in the environment aqueous nitrite photochemistry proceeds exclusively through photoexcitation followed by homolytic bond cleavage, we show that visible light directly ionizes aqueous nitrite in individual micron-sized droplets. Although the photoionization cross section is exceptionally small, this pathway is observable at the high photon flux densities accessible in single-droplet experiments. Molecular simulations reveal that this is because photoionization absorption has an exceptionally long wavelength tail due to the large solvent reorganization energy in aqueous solutions. Kinetic modeling of an aqueous-phase reaction mechanism quantitatively accounts for the observed chemistry and predicts the formation of nitrate, hydroxyl radicals, and solvated electrons. The overlap of the predicted broad photoionization continuum with the visible solar spectrum further suggests that visible-light photoionization is an overlooked source of nitrogen oxides, solvated electrons, and oxidants in atmospheric aerosols, snowpack, and natural waters. Under dilute conditions, where dissolved oxygen is the main sink for solvated electrons, photoionization is expected to be the dominant photochemical loss pathway for nitrite.

</details>


### [220] [Solvating machine-learning interatomic potentials for solution-phase chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17226)
*Anton Z. Ni,Joonho Lee*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: AnySolv 是一种可迁移的隐式溶剂化势，可为现有气相机器学习原子间势提供溶剂依赖的能量与力修正，无需重新训练，从而支持溶液相反应与分子动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数机器学习原子间势基于气相数据训练，难以用于溶液化学，因此需要一种可迁移、高效且准确的溶剂化修正方法。

Method: AnySolv 作为隐式溶剂化势，对现有气相势添加溶剂依赖的能量和力修正；使用 DFT 与 SMD 在 36 种溶剂中计算的 140 万条溶剂化能量和力训练，涵盖带电、开壳层和过渡态结构；溶剂用连续物理描述符表示，可外推至 179 种溶剂。

Result: 在外部基准上，AnySolv 以低于三个数量级以上的成本将 DFT-SMD 溶剂化能复现至 2–4 meV 平均绝对误差，并达到与 DFT-SMD 相对实验溶剂化自由能相当的精度；可用于筛选六相中 120 个亲核芳香取代反应机理，并重现丙氨酸二肽实验观察到的溶剂依赖构象偏好。

Conclusion: 通过为现有 MLIP 提供可迁移的溶剂化修正，AnySolv 使溶剂依赖的化学反应性与分子动力学模拟能够规模化进行。

Abstract: Most machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on gas-phase data, which limits their use for chemistry in solution. Here, we introduce AnySolv, a transferable implicit solvation potential that provides solvent-dependent energy and force corrections to existing gas-phase potentials without retraining them. It is trained on 1.4 million solvation energies and forces computed with density functional theory (DFT) and SMD in 36 solvents, spanning charged, open-shell, and transition-state structures. Because solvents are represented by continuous physical descriptors, AnySolv supports 179 solvents, including those absent from training. On external benchmarks, AnySolv reproduces DFT-SMD solvation energies to 2-4 meV mean absolute error at more than three orders of magnitude lower cost, and it matches the accuracy of DFT-SMD against experimental solvation free energies. This enables screening of the mechanisms of 120 nucleophilic aromatic substitution reactions in six phases as well as molecular dynamics simulations that reproduce the experimentally observed solvent-dependent conformational preferences of alanine dipeptide. By providing transferable solvation corrections to existing MLIPs, AnySolv enables scalable simulations of solvent-dependent chemical reactivity and molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [221] [Chromatographic Peak Shape beyond the High-P\'eclet Approximation: Analytic Time-Domain Expression and Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17297)
*Hern\'an R. S\'anchez*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对线性等度色谱随机模型，提出用高斯混合近似正态逆高斯密度，将洗脱时间概率密度及其参数导数表示为高斯-伽马级数，并给出快速、稳定、高精度的数值评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有模型可解析给出洗脱时间密度及参数导数，但涉及高斯-伽马卷积，直接计算在截断阶数上代价高且可能因有限精度和相消导致数值不稳定，因此需要更快速、可靠的评估方案。

Method: 采用求积规则将正态逆高斯密度近似为有限高斯混合；洗脱时间PDF写成高斯-伽马形式级数的加权和，并推导其参数导数；新递推将系数及其导数计算复杂度从关于截断阶数的二次降为线性；通过系数概率分析确定所需项数；推导相对舍入误差估计器；在易相消区域，利用由抛物柱函数表示的高斯-伽马相邻项精确比值初始化后向重建，并给出该比值的高精度快速近似。

Result: 新递推显著降低计算成本；概率分析表明通常色谱条件下所需级数项数很少，可实现快速评估；舍入误差估计器可量化前向递推的有限精度损失；后向重建及比值近似在易相消情况下保持高精度，独立高精度计算验证了双精度评估在测试数值案例中的高准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法为色谱随机模型中的洗脱时间密度及其参数导数提供了快速、准确且数值稳健的计算框架，尤其改善了高斯-伽马卷积在相消敏感区域的双精度评估可靠性。

Abstract: Analytic time-domain expressions for the elution-time density and its parameter derivatives were recently derived from a stochastic model of linear isocratic chromatography incorporating advective-diffusive transport, injection dispersion, and fast and slow retention mechanisms. A Gaussian approximation, justified asymptotically at high column-scale P\'eclet numbers, was adopted for the distribution of the combined mobile-phase and fast-retention time, while slow retention was incorporated through a series of Gaussian-Gamma convolutions. Here, the underlying normal-inverse-Gaussian density is approximated by a finite Gaussian mixture using an existing quadrature rule. The elution-time PDF is a weighted sum of series of the previously derived Gaussian-Gamma form. Its parameter derivatives are also obtained. Newly derived recurrences reduce the cost of evaluating the coefficients of that series and their derivatives from quadratic to linear in truncation order for a fixed number of mechanisms. Through a probabilistic analysis of the series coefficients, the required number of terms is found to be small enough for rapid evaluation under usual chromatographic conditions. A relative roundoff-error estimator is derived to quantify finite-precision loss in the inherited forward recurrence for Gaussian-Gamma convolutions. For the cancellation-prone regime, a backward reconstruction is developed, initialized from an exact ratio of consecutive Gaussian-Gamma terms expressed through parabolic-cylinder functions. A highly accurate approximation is derived for rapid evaluation of this ratio. Independent high-precision calculations confirm the high accuracy of the resulting double-precision evaluation in the tested numerical case.

</details>


### [222] [Development of a Minimal Auxiliary Basis for the Particle--Particle Random Phase Approximation (ppRPA): Accelerating Excited-State Calculations in Diradical Nanoparticles and Periodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18020)
*Yuchen Wang,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 ppRPA-as 方法，通过将辅助基组缩减为自由原子半径和单一全局缩放因子决定的最小原子中心高斯壳层，在保持精度的同时大幅加速 ppRPA 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单-三重态能隙决定分子量子信息材料中双自旋能级尺度，这类体系常具双自由基特征；TDDFT 对多参考体系描述差，闭壳层参考不适合多组态基态，绝热核排除双激发，而 ppRPA 改变粒子数而非自旋。

Method: 发展 ppRPA-as：用最小原子中心高斯辅助基组替代常规辅助基，指数由自由原子半径和一个全局缩放因子确定；近似仅进入激发态步骤，单重态和三重态从同一自洽闭壳层参考出发，双激发与单激发同等处理。测试多达 286 个原子的分子和团簇，含重元素，以及四种周期性宿主中的七个点缺陷。

Result: 辅助基组缩小约一个数量级，计算加速最高达 8 倍，同时与常规 ppRPA 的偏差在 0.03 eV 以内；无需对称性破缺或自旋投影即可获得多重度和能级分裂，并优化了缩放因子并给出选择指南。

Conclusion: ppRPA-as 使双自由基纳米团簇和周期性体系的单-三重态能隙计算变得可行。

Abstract: Singlet--triplet gaps set the energy scale of the two-spin manifold in molecular quantum information materials, and such systems frequently carry diradical character: metal nanoclusters, metal--organic frameworks with radical linkers, and the nitrogen--vacancy center in diamond. Time-dependent density functional theory is the standard tool at this size but describes multireference systems poorly. A closed-shell reference is inadequate for a multiconfigurational ground state, and the adiabatic kernel excludes doubly excited configurations. The particle--particle random phase approximation (\pprpa{}) changes the particle number rather than the spin. Singlet and triplet states descend from one closed-shell reference, and double excitations enter on the same footing as single ones. Computational cost has confined \pprpa{} to small systems, and the two-electron integrals that drive it are factorized through an auxiliary basis. We develop \pprpa{}--as, which reduces that basis to a minimal set of atom-centered Gaussian shells whose exponents follow from free-atom radii and one global scaling factor. The approximation enters only the excited-state step, so both calculations proceed from the same self-consistent reference. We test molecules and clusters of up to 286 atoms, including heavy elements, and seven point defects in four periodic hosts, obtaining both multiplicities and their splitting without symmetry breaking or spin projection. We optimize the scaling factor and give guidance on choosing it. An order-of-magnitude smaller auxiliary basis delivers a speed-up of up to eightfold while staying within $0.03$~eV of conventional \pprpa{}, bringing diradical nanoclusters and periodic systems within reach.

</details>


### [223] [Can Machine Learning Potentials Be More Accurate Than the Methods They Are Trained On?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18027)
*Pavlo O. Dral,Yi-Fan Hou,Yuxinxin Chen,Hassan Nawaz,Mateusz Witkowski,Szymon \'Smiga,Ireneusz Grabowski*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该 Perspective 提出，机器学习势（MLP）除了在构型空间和化合物空间泛化外，还可通过向量子化学方法空间泛化，即构建“方法可迁移 MLP”，从而以非经验方式超越其训练所用方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLP 通常被认为精度受限于训练方法，若想突破该限制，以往只能通过拟合实验观测量。作者主张可利用方法空间泛化，在无需实验拟合的情况下突破训练方法精度上限。

Method: 提出方法可迁移 MLP 的构建路线，使其能对未见过的量子化学方法进行预测；并在密度泛函理论和波函数理论中给出可行实现，实现跨量子化学方法的泛化。

Result: 这些方法可迁移 MLP 能比其任一训练方法更准确；即使其精度仍低于相应量子化学方法，以超越训练方法精度为目标也可让通用 MLP 比单方法模型更准确。

Conclusion: 方法可迁移 MLP 将影响训练数据集构建与模型评估，并推动化学模拟的精度极限。

Abstract: Although machine learning potentials (MLPs) generalize across configurational and chemical compound spaces, their accuracy is commonly believed to be limited by their training methods, with improvements beyond this limit achieved so far only by refitting to experimental observables. In this Perspective, we argue that MLPs can also be made more accurate than their training methods non-empirically by generalizing across a third space, that of quantum-chemical methods. We propose routes to method-transferable MLPs that predict at unseen methods, with working implementations in both density-functional and wavefunction-based theories showing that they can be more accurate than any of their training methods. Aiming beyond the accuracy of the training methods could also make universal MLPs more accurate than single-method models, even when the method-transferable MLPs remain less accurate than those QM methods. Method-transferable MLPs will shape the construction of training datasets and the evaluation of models, pushing the accuracy limits of chemical simulations.

</details>


### [224] [QUPEX: A Streamlit-Based Framework for Visualizing Quantum-Mechanical Property Spaces of Organic Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18100)
*Mirtha Pillaca Quispe,Alejandra Hinostroza Caldas,Leonardo Medrano Sandonas*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出开源 Streamlit 框架 QUPEX，用于交互式探索分子量子力学性质空间，并集成了分子可视化、统计分析、聚类、XGBoost 回归与 SHAP 可解释性，且在 QM7-X 子集上演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学数据集日益丰富，为数据驱动研究类药分子的结构-性质和性质-性质关系提供了机会，但整合分子可视化、QM 性质统计分析、聚类和机器学习的交互式工具仍然有限。

Method: 开发基于 Streamlit 的开源框架 QUPEX；在 QM7-X 数据集子集上演示，该子集包含 41,537 个平衡分子结构，最多 7 个非氢原子（C、N、O、S、Cl）及其 24 个 QM 性质；支持性质空间分析，并构建 XGBoost 回归流程，从紧束缚电子描述符预测 PBE0 偶极矩，同时提供模型评估和基于 SHAP 的可解释性功能。

Result: QUPEX 将交互式分子可视化、数据分析和预测建模结合，形成可访问且可扩展的环境，并成功应用于 QM7-X 子集的 QM 性质探索与偶极矩预测建模。

Conclusion: QUPEX 有助于研究分子数据集、识别结构与电子性质模式，支持化学空间探索，并为具有目标 QM 性质的类药分子理性设计提供参考。

Abstract: The growing availability of quantum-mechanical (QM) datasets offers new opportunities to investigate structure-property and property-property relationships in drug-like molecules using data-driven methods. However, interactive tools that integrate molecular visualization, statistical analysis of QM properties, clustering, and machine learning (ML) remain limited, despite their value in facilitating dataset exploration and interpretation. To address this gap, we introduce QUPEX (QUantum Property EXplorer), an open-source Streamlit-based framework for the interactive exploration of QM property spaces in molecular systems. As a demonstration, we apply QUPEX to a subset of the QM7-X dataset comprising 41,537 equilibrium molecular structures containing up to seven non-hydrogen atoms (C, N, O, S, and Cl) and their 24 associated QM properties. In addition to property-space analysis, QUPEX incorporates an XGBoost regression pipeline for predicting PBE0 dipole moments from tight-binding-derived electronic descriptors, together with model evaluation and SHAP-based interpretability functionality. By combining interactive molecular visualization, data analysis, and predictive modeling, QUPEX provides an accessible and extensible environment for investigating molecular datasets and identifying patterns in their structural and electronic properties. These capabilities can support the exploration of chemical space and inform the rational design of drug-like molecules with targeted QM properties.

</details>


### [225] [Photoinduced Modulation of Rectification and Charge Transport in Diarylethene-Viologen Molecular Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18251)
*Sofyan Tahri,Samuel Decorps,Stephane Lenfant,Denis Frath,Imen Hnid*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究了12–16 nm电接枝二芳基乙烯-紫精（DAEVIO）的Au/DAEVIO/Ti/Au分子结，发现385 nm光照可大幅调控电荷传输并产生光伏响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光致变色与氧化还原活性功能集成到大面积分子结中，有望通过光控制电荷传输，这是一个有吸引力的平台。

Method: 制备Au/DAEVIO/Ti/Au分子结，进行变温电学测量，并在原位385 nm连续光照下测试电流密度、整流比、短路电流和开路电压。

Result: OFF态电流密度低且整流强，1 V下整流比300；高温存在热辅助贡献，160–180 K以下温度依赖很弱，表明是多步分子/界面态传输而非直接相干隧穿。光照后+1 V电流密度增大200倍，负偏压电流仅弱改变，整流比从300降至2；同时产生Jsc=+0.006 mA cm-2、Voc=-0.45 V。

Conclusion: 光照同时改变分子电荷传输并产生可测光电响应，机制涉及DAE光致环化引起的电子耦合变化，以及紫精单元和非对称电极界面参与的光生电荷产生与场依赖电荷分离。

Abstract: Large-area molecular junctions combining photochromic and redox-active functionalities provide an attractive platform for controlling charge transport by light. In this work, we study Au/DAEVIO/Ti/Au molecular junctions incorporated with electro-grafted layers of diarylethene-viologen with thicknesses of 12-16 nm. In the initial OFF state, the junctions exhibit low current densities and strong rectification, with a rectification ratio of 300 at 1V. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal a thermally assisted contribution at higher temperatures and a much weaker temperature dependence below 160-180K, consistent with multistep charge transport through molecular and/or interfacial states rather than direct coherent tunneling across the entire molecular layer. Under continuous in situ 385 nm irradiation, the electrical response is strongly and asymmetrically modified. The current density at +1V increases by a factor of 200, while the negative-bias current is only weakly modified, reducing the rectification ratio from 300 to 2. Illumination also produces a nonzero short-circuit current density of + 0.006mA cm-2 and an open-circuit voltage of -0.45V. These results indicate that irradiation simultaneously modifies molecular charge transport and generates a measurable photoelectric response. This behavior is consistent with light-induced changes in electronic coupling associated with DAE photocyclization, combined with photoinduced charge generation and field-dependent charge separation involving the redox-active viologen units and asymmetric electrode interfaces.

</details>


### [226] [Extrapolating Collisional Rate Coefficients for Molecules of Astrophysical Interest Using Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18278)
*Yigit Ergen,J\'er\^ome Loreau*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作用人工神经网络从已有数据外推分子碰撞激发速率系数，避免额外量子散射计算；对多种分子测试表明，未见转动能级预测误差因子多为1.1–1.5，温度外推效果最好，温度与转动能级联合外推多数在因子2以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部热动平衡辐射转移模型需要态-态非弹性碰撞速率系数，但用量子散射方法计算大分子和高激发转动态的速率系数代价极高，因此需要一种能扩展现有碰撞数据集的实用方法。

Method: 训练多层感知机，以分子量子数、能级能量、能隙和动力学温度作为描述符，并在对数速率系数上优化；测试体系包括CO、SiO、SO、H2O、SO2、苯甲腈和乙烯基氰等与H2或He的碰撞，并外推至更高转动能级、更高温度及二者组合。

Result: 对多数体系，涉及未见转动态的速率系数预测平均误差因子为1.1–1.5，主速率系数通常比弱跃迁更准确；温度外推尤其成功，温度与转动能级联合外推多数可保持在因子2以内；预测质量受数据集大小、温度范围、分子复杂度和碰撞倾向规则影响。

Conclusion: 机器学习可成为扩展天体物理碰撞数据集的实用途径，尤其适用于额外量子散射计算计算量很大的大分子体系。

Abstract: State-to-state rate coefficients for inelastic molecular collisions are essential inputs for non-local thermodynamic equilibrium radiative transfer models, yet their computation using quantum scattering methods is prohibitive for large molecules and highly excited rotational states. We investigate the ability of artificial neural networks to extend existing datasets of collisional excitation rate coefficients without performing additional scattering calculations. A multilayer perceptron model was trained using molecular quantum numbers, level energies, energy gaps, and kinetic temperature as descriptors, and optimized on logarithmic rate coefficients. The model was tested on diverse collision systems ranging from simple diatomic molecules (CO, SiO) to open-shell species (SO), small asymmetric tops (H$_2$O, SO$_2$), and complex organic molecules (benzonitrile and vinyl cyanide) colliding with H$_2$ molecules or He atoms. Extrapolations were performed toward higher rotational levels, higher temperatures, and combinations of both. For most systems, predictions of rate coefficients involving previously unseen rotational states yielded mean error factors between 1.1 and 1.5, with the dominant rate coefficients generally reproduced more accurately than weaker transitions. Temperature extrapolations proved particularly successful, while combined extrapolations in temperature and rotational levels remained accurate to within a factor of two in most cases. The prediction quality depends on the interplay between dataset size, temperature range, molecular complexity, and the presence of clear collisional propensity rules. Overall, the approach demonstrates that machine learning can provide a practical route to extending collisional datasets for astrophysical applications, especially for large molecules for which additional quantum-scattering calculations are computationally demanding.

</details>


### [227] [Ensuring Complete Consistency between Hops and Wavefunction Propagation in Fewest-Switches Surface Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18743)
*Jonathan R. Mannouch*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种与电子波函数时间演化完全一致的跳跃概率公式，并结合态追踪算法，解决FSSH在trivial crossing处的跳跃不一致问题，实现大步长下的高精度非绝热动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSSH的随机跳跃与电子波函数演化不完全一致，导致显著误差且需极小时间步长；在非绝热耦合局域于交叉点的trivial crossing处问题尤为严重。

Method: 推导完全一致的hopping-probability formula，使尝试跳跃在任意时间步长下均匹配波函数时间演化；并采用state-tracking algorithm保证trivial crossing处尝试跳跃总被接受。

Result: 在包含极端困难trivial crossings的体系测试中，该跳跃方案即使使用大传播时间步长也能获得高精度结果。

Conclusion: 该方法可解决FSSH的trivial-crossing问题，提升非绝热动力学模拟的准确性与计算效率，允许使用大步长。

Abstract: Fewest-switches surface hopping (FSSH) is a popular and versatile approach for performing dynamical simulations beyond the Born-Oppenheimer approximation, in which nonadiabatic effects are incorporated through stochastic hops of the active potential energy surface. However, the FSSH algorithm does not ensure that hops are completely consistent with the underlying electronic wavefunction dynamics. This can lead to significant errors as well as requiring small propagation time steps, which can significantly compromise the computational efficiency of the method. These problems are known to be particularly severe at so-called trivial crossings, for which the nonadiabatic coupling is entirely localized at the crossing point. Here, we solve the trivial-crossing problem by developing a fully-consistent hopping-probability formula, which generates attempted hops that match the time-evolution of the wavefunction across any size time step by construction. A state-tracking algorithm is also utilized to ensure that attempted hops are always accepted at trivial crossings. Through application to systems that contain extremely challenging trivial crossings, we demonstrate that this hopping scheme is capable of obtaining highly accurate results, even when large propagation time steps are used.

</details>


### [228] [Embossing of Tapered, Isometric Micro-Interdigitated Flow Fields for Enhanced Desalination by Faradaic Deionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18791)
*Md Habibur Rahman,Sadia Afrin,Kyle C. Smith*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出集成压印-压延方法，在普鲁士蓝类似物多孔电极中嵌入微叉指流场，实现可规模化电化学脱盐并提升能量效率与水回收率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化传输结构可改善多孔电极内电解质分布与活性材料利用率，但现有制备方法在精度、通量、活性材料保护或可扩展性方面受限。

Method: 采用非减材、卷对卷兼容的集成压印-压延法，通过控制塑性变形在压延的同时形成通道；结合压痕实验与闭式模型分析压印力；设计连续宽度渐变、等长流路的嵌入式微叉指流场（eμ-IDFFs）。

Result: 该方法保留活性材料，图案化时间缩短至机械铣削的1/100；压印所需力仅为冲压的1/15，且压印通道下方电极微结构出现局部致密化。新型eμ-IDFFs促进电解质均匀分布、提高倍率性能并降低极化；相比可扩展性较差且非等长流路的eμ-IDFFs，可将海水盐度进料淡化至接近饮用水水平，热力学能量效率和水回收率均达到对照的1.5倍，苦咸水淡化同样受益。

Conclusion: 渐缩、等长的eμ-IDFFs及其压印制备方法可推广至其他电化学储能与分离系统。

Abstract: Engineered transport architectures can improve electrolyte distribution and active-material utilization in porous electrodes, yet their implementation remains limited by fabrication methods that compromise precision, throughput, active-material preservation, or scalability. Here, an integrated embossing-calendering method is introduced to pattern Prussian blue analog (PBA) porous electrodes with embedded, micro-interdigitated flow fields (e$\mu$-IDFFs) for electrochemical desalination. This non-subtractive, roll-to-roll compatible method forms channels by controlling plastic deformation while simultaneously calendering the electrode, preserving active material and reducing patterning time by 100-fold relative to mechanical milling. Indentation experiments and a closed-form model further show that embossing requires 15-fold less force than stamping, while electrode microstructures show local densification beneath embossed channels. PBA electrodes are embossed with novel e$\mu$-IDFFs that use continuous width-tapering with isometric flow paths that promote uniform electrolyte distribution, increase rate capability, and reduce polarization. Compared with e$\mu$-IDFFs patterned using less scalable means and non-isometric flow paths, these e$\mu$-IDFFs desalinate seawater-salinity feeds near to potable levels with 1.5-fold of its thermodynamic energy efficiency and water recovery. Brackish desalination benefits similarly. Tapered, isometric e$\mu$-IDFFs and the embossing method introduced to pattern them are readily translated other electrochemical energy storage and separations systems.

</details>
